FILE: bin/windows/installer/mpv-install.bat

@echo off
setlocal enableextensions enabledelayedexpansion
path %SystemRoot%\System32;%SystemRoot%;%SystemRoot%\System32\Wbem;%SystemRoot%\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\

:: Unattended install flag. When set, the script will not require user input.
set unattended=no
if "%1"=="/u" set unattended=yes

:: Make sure this is Windows Vista or later
call :ensure_vista

:: Make sure the script is running as admin
call :ensure_admin

:: Command line arguments to use when launching mpv from a file association
set mpv_args=

:: Get mpv.exe location
cd /D %~dp0\..
set mpv_path=%cd%\mpv.exe
if not exist "%mpv_path%" call :die "mpv.exe not found"

:: Get mpv-icon.ico location
set icon_path=%~dp0mpv-icon.ico
if not exist "%icon_path%" call :die "mpv-icon.ico not found"

:: Register mpv.exe under the "App Paths" key, so it can be found by
:: ShellExecute, the run command, the start menu, etc.
set app_paths_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\App Paths\mpv.exe
call :reg add "%app_paths_key%" /d "%mpv_path%" /f
call :reg add "%app_paths_key%" /v "UseUrl" /t REG_DWORD /d 1 /f

:: Register mpv.exe under the "Applications" key to add some default verbs for
:: when mpv is used from the "Open with" menu
set classes_root_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Classes
set app_key=%classes_root_key%\Applications\mpv.exe
call :reg add "%app_key%" /v "FriendlyAppName" /d "mpv" /f
call :add_verbs "%app_key%"

:: Add mpv to the "Open with" list for all video and audio file types
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\SystemFileAssociations\video\OpenWithList\mpv.exe" /d "" /f
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\SystemFileAssociations\audio\OpenWithList\mpv.exe" /d "" /f

:: Add DVD AutoPlay handler
set autoplay_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\AutoplayHandlers
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\io.mpv.dvd\shell\play" /d "&Play" /f
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\io.mpv.dvd\shell\play\command" /d "\"%mpv_path%\" %mpv_args% dvd:// --dvd-device=\"%%%%L\"" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "Action" /d "Play DVD movie" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "DefaultIcon" /d "%mpv_path%,0" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "InvokeProgID" /d "io.mpv.dvd" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "InvokeVerb" /d "play" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "Provider" /d "mpv" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\EventHandlers\PlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /f

:: Add Blu-ray AutoPlay handler
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\io.mpv.bluray\shell\play" /d "&Play" /f
call :reg add "%classes_root_key%\io.mpv.bluray\shell\play\command" /d "\"%mpv_path%\" %mpv_args% bd:// --bluray-device=\"%%%%L\"" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "Action" /d "Play Blu-ray movie" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "DefaultIcon" /d "%mpv_path%,0" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "InvokeProgID" /d "io.mpv.bluray" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "InvokeVerb" /d "play" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "Provider" /d "mpv" /f
call :reg add "%autoplay_key%\EventHandlers\PlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /f

:: Add a capabilities key for mpv, which is registered later on for use in the
:: "Default Programs" control panel
set capabilities_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Clients\Media\mpv\Capabilities
call :reg add "%capabilities_key%" /v "ApplicationName" /d "mpv" /f
call :reg add "%capabilities_key%" /v "ApplicationDescription" /d "mpv media player" /f

:: Add file types
set supported_types_key=%app_key%\SupportedTypes
set file_associations_key=%capabilities_key%\FileAssociations
:: DVD/Blu-ray audio formats
call :add_type "audio/ac3"                        "audio" "AC-3 Audio"                 ".ac3" ".a52"
call :add_type "audio/eac3"                       "audio" "E-AC-3 Audio"               ".eac3"
call :add_type "audio/vnd.dolby.mlp"              "audio" "MLP Audio"                  ".mlp"
call :add_type "audio/vnd.dts"                    "audio" "DTS Audio"                  ".dts"
call :add_type "audio/vnd.dts.hd"                 "audio" "DTS-HD Audio"               ".dts-hd" ".dtshd"
call :add_type ""                                 "audio" "TrueHD Audio"               ".true-hd" ".thd" ".truehd" ".thd+ac3"
call :add_type ""                                 "audio" "True Audio"                 ".tta"
:: Uncompressed formats
call :add_type ""                                 "audio" "PCM Audio"                  ".pcm"
call :add_type "audio/wav"                        "audio" "Wave Audio"                 ".wav"
call :add_type "audio/aiff"                       "audio" "AIFF Audio"                 ".aiff" ".aif" ".aifc"

call :add_type "video/x-matroska"                 "video" "Matroska Video"             ".mkv"

call :add_type "audio/x-mpegurl"                  "audio" "M3U Playlist"               ".m3u" ".m3u8"

call :add_type ""                                 "audio" "CUE Sheet"                  ".cue"

:: Register "Default Programs" entry
call :reg add "HKLM\SOFTWARE\RegisteredApplications" /v "mpv" /d "SOFTWARE\Clients\Media\mpv\Capabilities" /f

:: Enable long paths in Windows 10
call :reg add "HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\FileSystem" /v "LongPathsEnabled" /t REG_DWORD /d 1 /f

:: Add start menu link
powershell "$s=(New-Object -COM WScript.Shell).CreateShortcut('%ProgramData%\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\mpv.lnk');$s.TargetPath='%mpv_path%';$s.Save()"

echo.
echo Installed successfully^^! You can now configure mpv's file associations in the
echo Default Programs control panel.
echo.
if [%unattended%] == [yes] exit 0
<nul set /p =Press any key to open the Default Programs control panel . . .
pause >nul
control /name Microsoft.DefaultPrograms
exit 0

:die
	if not [%1] == [] echo %~1
	if [%unattended%] == [yes] exit 1
	pause
	exit 1

:ensure_admin
	:: 'openfiles' is just a commmand that is present on all supported Windows
	:: versions, requires admin privileges and has no side effects, see:
	:: https://stackoverflow.com/questions/4051883/batch-script-how-to-check-for-admin-rights
	openfiles >nul 2>&1
	if errorlevel 1 (
		echo This batch script requires administrator privileges. Right-click on
		echo mpv-install.bat and select "Run as administrator".
		call :die
	)
	goto :EOF

:ensure_vista
	ver | find "XP" >nul
	if not errorlevel 1 (
		echo This batch script only works on Windows Vista and later. To create file
		echo associations on Windows XP, right click on a video file and use "Open with...".
		call :die
	)
	goto :EOF

:reg
	:: Wrap the reg command to check for errors
	>nul reg %*
	if errorlevel 1 set error=yes
	if [%error%] == [yes] echo Error in command: reg %*
	if [%error%] == [yes] call :die
	goto :EOF

:reg_set_opt
	:: Set a value in the registry if it doesn't already exist
	set key=%~1
	set value=%~2
	set data=%~3

	reg query "%key%" /v "%value%" >nul 2>&1
	if errorlevel 1 call :reg add "%key%" /v "%value%" /d "%data%"
	goto :EOF

:add_verbs
	set key=%~1

	:: Set the default verb to "play"
	call :reg add "%key%\shell" /d "play" /f

	:: Hide the "open" verb from the context menu, since it's the same as "play"
	call :reg add "%key%\shell\open" /v "LegacyDisable" /f

	:: Set open command
	call :reg add "%key%\shell\open\command" /d "\"%mpv_path%\" %mpv_args% -- \"%%%%L\"" /f

	:: Add "play" verb
	call :reg add "%key%\shell\play" /d "&Play" /f
	call :reg add "%key%\shell\play\command" /d "\"%mpv_path%\" %mpv_args% -- \"%%%%L\"" /f

	goto :EOF

:add_progid
	set prog_id=%~1
	set friendly_name=%~2

	:: Add ProgId, edit flags are FTA_OpenIsSafe
	set prog_id_key=%classes_root_key%\%prog_id%
	call :reg add "%prog_id_key%" /d "%friendly_name%" /f
	call :reg add "%prog_id_key%" /v "EditFlags" /t REG_DWORD /d 65536 /f
	call :reg add "%prog_id_key%" /v "FriendlyTypeName" /d "%friendly_name%" /f
	call :reg add "%prog_id_key%\DefaultIcon" /d "%icon_path%" /f
	call :add_verbs "%prog_id_key%"

	goto :EOF

:update_extension
	set extension=%~1
	set prog_id=%~2
	set mime_type=%~3
	set perceived_type=%~4

	:: Add information about the file extension, if not already present
	set extension_key=%classes_root_key%\%extension%
	if not [%mime_type%] == [] call :reg_set_opt "%extension_key%" "Content Type" "%mime_type%"
	if not [%perceived_type%] == [] call :reg_set_opt "%extension_key%" "PerceivedType" "%perceived_type%"
	call :reg add "%extension_key%\OpenWithProgIds" /v "%prog_id%" /f

	:: Add type to SupportedTypes
	call :reg add "%supported_types_key%" /v "%extension%" /f

	:: Add type to the Default Programs control panel
	call :reg add "%file_associations_key%" /v "%extension%" /d "%prog_id%" /f

	goto :EOF

:add_type
	set mime_type=%~1
	set perceived_type=%~2
	set friendly_name=%~3
	set extension=%~4

	echo Adding "%extension%" file type

	:: Add ProgId
	set prog_id=io.mpv%extension%
	call :add_progid "%prog_id%" "%friendly_name%"

	:: Add extensions
	:extension_loop
		call :update_extension "%extension%" "%prog_id%" "%mime_type%" "%perceived_type%"

		:: Trailing parameters are additional extensions
		shift /4
		set extension=%~4
		if not [%extension%] == [] goto extension_loop

	goto :EOF



--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: bin/windows/installer/mpv-uninstall.bat
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@echo off
setlocal enableextensions enabledelayedexpansion
path %SystemRoot%\System32;%SystemRoot%;%SystemRoot%\System32\Wbem

:: Unattended install flag. When set, the script will not require user input.
set unattended=no
if "%1"=="/u" set unattended=yes

:: Make sure the script is running as admin
call :ensure_admin

:: Delete "App Paths" entry
reg delete "HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\App Paths\mpv.exe" /f >nul

:: Delete HKCR subkeys
set classes_root_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Classes
reg delete "%classes_root_key%\Applications\mpv.exe" /f >nul
reg delete "%classes_root_key%\SystemFileAssociations\video\OpenWithList\mpv.exe" /f >nul
reg delete "%classes_root_key%\SystemFileAssociations\audio\OpenWithList\mpv.exe" /f >nul

:: Delete AutoPlay handlers
set autoplay_key=HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\AutoplayHandlers
reg delete "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /f >nul
reg delete "%autoplay_key%\EventHandlers\PlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /v "MpvPlayDVDMovieOnArrival" /f >nul
reg delete "%autoplay_key%\Handlers\MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /f >nul
reg delete "%autoplay_key%\EventHandlers\PlayBluRayOnArrival" /v "MpvPlayBluRayOnArrival" /f >nul

:: Delete "Default Programs" entry
reg delete "HKLM\SOFTWARE\RegisteredApplications" /v "mpv" /f >nul
reg delete "HKLM\SOFTWARE\Clients\Media\mpv\Capabilities" /f >nul

:: Delete all OpenWithProgIds referencing ProgIds that start with io.mpv.
for /f "usebackq eol= delims=" %%k in (`reg query "%classes_root_key%" /f "io.mpv.*" /s /v /c`) do (
	set "key=%%k"
	echo !key!| findstr /r /i "^HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\\SOFTWARE\\Classes\\\.[^\\][^\\]*\\OpenWithProgIds$" >nul
	if not errorlevel 1 (
		for /f "usebackq eol= tokens=1" %%v in (`reg query "!key!" /f "io.mpv.*" /v /c`) do (
			set "value=%%v"
			echo !value!| findstr /r /i "^io\.mpv\.[^\\][^\\]*$" >nul
			if not errorlevel 1 (
				echo Deleting !key!\!value!
				reg delete "!key!" /v "!value!" /f >nul
			)
		)
	)
)

:: Delete all ProgIds starting with io.mpv.
for /f "usebackq eol= delims=" %%k in (`reg query "%classes_root_key%" /f "io.mpv.*" /k /c`) do (
	set "key=%%k"
	echo !key!| findstr /r /i "^HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\\SOFTWARE\\Classes\\io\.mpv\.[^\\][^\\]*$" >nul
	if not errorlevel 1 (
		echo Deleting !key!
		reg delete "!key!" /f >nul
	)
)

:: Delete start menu link
del "%ProgramData%\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\mpv.lnk"

echo Uninstalled successfully
if [%unattended%] == [yes] exit 0
pause
exit 0

:die
	if not [%1] == [] echo %~1
	if [%unattended%] == [yes] exit 1
	pause
	exit 1

:ensure_admin
	:: 'openfiles' is just a commmand that is present on all supported Windows
	:: versions, requires admin privileges and has no side effects, see:
	:: https://stackoverflow.com/questions/4051883/batch-script-how-to-check-for-admin-rights
	openfiles >nul 2>&1
	if errorlevel 1 (
		echo This batch script requires administrator privileges. Right-click on
		echo mpv-uninstall.bat and select "Run as administrator".
		call :die
	)
	goto :EOF



--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: bin/windows/installer/updater.ps1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
$fallback7z = Join-Path (Get-Location) "\7z\7zr.exe";
$useragent = "mpv-win-updater"

function Get-7z {
    $7z_command = Get-Command -CommandType Application -ErrorAction Ignore 7z.exe | Select-Object -Last 1
    if ($7z_command) {
        return $7z_command.Source
    }
    $7zdir = Get-ItemPropertyValue -ErrorAction Ignore "HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\7-Zip" "InstallLocation"
    if ($7zdir -and (Test-Path (Join-Path $7zdir "7z.exe"))) {
        return Join-Path $7zdir "7z.exe"
    }
    if (Test-Path $fallback7z) {
        return $fallback7z
    }
    return $null
}

function Check-7z {
    if (-not (Get-7z))
    {
        $null = New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $fallback7z)
        $download_file = $fallback7z
        Write-Host "Downloading 7zr.exe" -ForegroundColor Green
        Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "https://www.7-zip.org/a/7zr.exe" -UserAgent $useragent -OutFile $download_file
    }
    else
    {
        Write-Host "7z already exist. Skipped download" -ForegroundColor Green
    }
}

function Check-PowershellVersion {
    $version = $PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major
    Write-Host "Checking Windows PowerShell version -- $version" -ForegroundColor Green
    if ($version -le 2)
    {
        Write-Host "Using Windows PowerShell $version is unsupported. Upgrade your Windows PowerShell." -ForegroundColor Red
        throw
    }
}

function Check-Ytplugin {
    $ytdlp = Get-ChildItem "yt-dlp*.exe" -ErrorAction Ignore
    $youtubedl = Get-ChildItem "youtube-dl.exe" -ErrorAction Ignore
    if ($ytdlp) {
        return $ytdlp.ToString()
    }
    elseif ($youtubedl) {
        return $youtubedl.ToString()
    }
    else {
        return $null
    }
}

function Check-Ytplugin-In-System {
    $ytp = Get-Command -CommandType Application -ErrorAction Ignore yt-dlp.exe | Select-Object -Last 1
    if (-not $ytp) {
        $ytp = Get-Command -CommandType Application -ErrorAction Ignore youtube-dl.exe | Select-Object -Last 1
    }
    return [bool]($ytp -and ((Split-Path $ytp.Source) -ne (Get-Location)))
}

function Check-Mpv {
    $mpv = (Get-Location).Path + "\mpv.exe"
    $is_exist = Test-Path $mpv
    return $is_exist
}

function Download-Archive ($filename, $link) {
    Write-Host "Downloading" $filename -ForegroundColor Green
    Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $link -UserAgent $useragent -OutFile $filename
}

function Download-Ytplugin ($plugin, $version) {
    $link = ""
    $plugin_exe = ""
    switch -wildcard ($plugin) {
        "yt-dlp*" {
            Write-Host "Downloading $plugin ($version)" -ForegroundColor Green
            $32bit = ""
            if (-Not (Test-Path (Join-Path $env:windir "SysWow64"))) {
                $32bit = "_x86"
            }
            $link = -join("https://github.com/yt-dlp/yt-dlp/releases/download/", $version, "/", $plugin, $32bit, ".exe")
            $plugin_exe = -join($plugin, $32bit, ".exe")
        }
        "youtube-dl" {
            Write-Host "Downloading $plugin ($version)" -ForegroundColor Green
            $link = -join("https://yt-dl.org/downloads/", $version, "/youtube-dl.exe")
            $plugin_exe = "youtube-dl.exe"
        }
    }
    Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $link -UserAgent $useragent -OutFile $plugin_exe
}

function Extract-Archive ($file) {
    $7z = Get-7z
    Write-Host "Extracting" $file -ForegroundColor Green
    & $7z x -y $file
}

function Get-Latest-Mpv($Arch, $channel) {
    $filename = ""
    $download_link = ""
    switch -wildcard ($channel) {
        "daily" {
            $api_gh = "https://api.github.com/repos/shinchiro/mpv-winbuild-cmake/releases/latest"
            $json = Invoke-WebRequest $api_gh -MaximumRedirection 0 -ErrorAction Ignore -UseBasicParsing | ConvertFrom-Json
            $filename = $json.assets | where { $_.name -Match "mpv-$Arch" } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty name
            $download_link = $json.assets | where { $_.name -Match "mpv-$Arch" } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty browser_download_url
        }
        "weekly" {
            $i686_link = "https://sourceforge.net/projects/mpv-player-windows/rss?path=/32bit"
            $x86_64_link = "https://sourceforge.net/projects/mpv-player-windows/rss?path=/64bit"
            $x86_64v3_link = "https://sourceforge.net/projects/mpv-player-windows/rss?path=/64bit-v3"
            $rss_link = ''
            switch ($Arch)
            {
                i686 { $rss_link = $i686_link}
                x86_64 { $rss_link = $x86_64_link }
                x86_64-v3 { $rss_link = $x86_64v3_link }
            }
            Write-Host "Fetching RSS feed for mpv" -ForegroundColor Green
            $result = [xml](New-Object System.Net.WebClient).DownloadString($rss_link)
            $latest = $result.rss.channel.item.link[0]
            $tempname = $latest.split("/")[-2]
            $filename = [System.Uri]::UnescapeDataString($tempname)
            $download_link = "https://download.sourceforge.net/mpv-player-windows/" + $filename
        }
    }
    if ($filename -is [array]) {
        return $filename[0], $download_link[0]
    }
    else {
        return $filename, $download_link
    }
}

function Get-Latest-Ytplugin ($plugin) {
    switch -wildcard ($plugin) {
        "yt-dlp*" {
            $link = "https://github.com/yt-dlp/yt-dlp/releases.atom"
            Write-Host "Fetching RSS feed for ytp-dlp" -ForegroundColor Green
            $resp = [xml](Invoke-WebRequest $link -MaximumRedirection 0 -ErrorAction Ignore -UseBasicParsing).Content
            $link = $resp.feed.entry[0].link.href
            $version = $link.split("/")[-1]
            return $version
        }
        "youtube-dl" {
            $link = "https://yt-dl.org/downloads/latest/youtube-dl.exe"
            Write-Host "Fetching RSS feed for youtube-dl" -ForegroundColor Green
            $resp = Invoke-WebRequest $link -MaximumRedirection 0 -ErrorAction Ignore -UseBasicParsing
            $redirect_link = $resp.Headers.Location
            $version = $redirect_link.split("/")[4]
            return $version
        }
    }
}

function Get-Latest-FFmpeg ($Arch) {
    $api_gh = "https://api.github.com/repos/shinchiro/mpv-winbuild-cmake/releases/latest"
    $json = Invoke-WebRequest $api_gh -MaximumRedirection 0 -ErrorAction Ignore -UseBasicParsing | ConvertFrom-Json
    $filename = $json.assets | where { $_.name -Match "ffmpeg-$Arch" } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty name
    $download_link = $json.assets | where { $_.name -Match "ffmpeg-$Arch" } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty browser_download_url
    if ($filename -is [array]) {
        return $filename[0], $download_link[0]
    }
    else {
        return $filename, $download_link
    }
}

function Get-Arch {
    # Reference: http://superuser.com/a/891443
    $FilePath = [System.IO.Path]::Combine((Get-Location).Path, 'mpv.exe')
    [int32]$MACHINE_OFFSET = 4
    [int32]$PE_POINTER_OFFSET = 60

    [byte[]]$data = New-Object -TypeName System.Byte[] -ArgumentList 4096
    $stream = New-Object -TypeName System.IO.FileStream -ArgumentList ($FilePath, 'Open', 'Read')
    $stream.Read($data, 0, 4096) | Out-Null

    # DOS header is 64 bytes, last element, long (4 bytes) is the address of the PE header
    [int32]$PE_HEADER_ADDR = [System.BitConverter]::ToInt32($data, $PE_POINTER_OFFSET)
    [int32]$machineUint = [System.BitConverter]::ToUInt16($data, $PE_HEADER_ADDR + $MACHINE_OFFSET)

    $result = "" | select FilePath, FileType
    $result.FilePath = $FilePath

    switch ($machineUint)
    {
        0      { $result.FileType = 'Native' }
        0x014c { $result.FileType = 'i686' } # 32bit
        0x0200 { $result.FileType = 'Itanium' }
        0x8664 { $result.FileType = 'x86_64' } # 64bit
    }

    $stream.Close()
    $result
}

function ExtractGitFromFile {
    $stripped = .\mpv --no-config | select-string "mpv" | select-object -First 1
    $pattern = "-g([a-z0-9-]{7})"
    $bool = $stripped -match $pattern
    return $matches[1]
}

function ExtractGitFromURL($filename) {
    $pattern = "-git-([a-z0-9-]{7})"
    $bool = $filename -match $pattern
    return $matches[1]
}

function ExtractDateFromFile {
    $date = (Get-Item ./mpv.exe).LastWriteTimeUtc
    $day = $date.Day.ToString("00")
    $month = $date.Month.ToString("00")
    $year = $date.Year.ToString("0000")
    return "$year$month$day"
}

function ExtractDateFromURL($filename) {
    $pattern = "mpv-[xi864_].*-([0-9]{8})-git-([a-z0-9-]{7})"
    $bool = $filename -match $pattern
    return $matches[1]
}

function Test-Admin
{
    $user = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent();
    (New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal $user).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltinRole]::Administrator)
}

function Create-XML {
@"
<settings>
  <channel>unset</channel>
  <arch>unset</arch>
  <autodelete>unset</autodelete>
  <getffmpeg>unset</getffmpeg>
</settings>
"@ | Set-Content "settings.xml" -Encoding UTF8
}

function Check-ChannelRelease {
    $channel = ""
    $file = "settings.xml"

    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) {
        $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Choose mpv updates frequency, weekly or daily? [1=weekly/2=daily] (default=1)" "D1"
        Write-Host ""
        if ($result -eq 'D1') {
            $channel = "weekly"
        }
        elseif ($result -eq 'D2') {
            $channel = "daily"
        }
        else {
            throw "Please enter valid input key."
        }
        Create-XML
        [xml]$doc = Get-Content $file
        $doc.settings.channel = $channel
        $doc.Save($file)
    }
    else {
        [xml]$doc = Get-Content $file
        $channel = $doc.settings.channel
    }
    return $channel
}

function Check-Arch($arch) {
    $get_arch = ""
    $file = "settings.xml"

    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { exit }
    [xml]$doc = Get-Content $file
    if ($doc.settings.arch -eq "unset") {
        if ($arch -eq "i686") {
            $get_arch = "i686"
        }
        else {
            $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Choose variant for 64bit builds: x86_64 or x86_64-v3 (for cpu with AVX2 support) [1=x86_64 / 2=x86_64-v3 (default=1)" "D1"
            Write-Host ""
            if ($result -eq 'D1') {
                $get_arch = "x86_64"
            }
            elseif ($result -eq 'D2') {
                $get_arch = "x86_64-v3"
            }
            else {
                throw "Please enter valid input key."
            }
        }
        $doc.settings.arch = $get_arch
        $doc.Save($file)
    }
    else {
        $get_arch = $doc.settings.arch
    }
    return $get_arch
}

function Check-Autodelete($archive) {
    $autodelete = ""
    $file = "settings.xml"

    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { exit }
    [xml]$doc = Get-Content $file
    if ($doc.settings.autodelete -eq "unset") {
        $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Delete archives after extract? [Y/n] (default=Y)" "Y"
        Write-Host ""
        if ($result -eq 'Y') {
            $autodelete = "true"
        }
        elseif ($result -eq 'N') {
            $autodelete = "false"
        }
        else {
            throw "Please enter valid input key."
        }
        $doc.settings.autodelete = $autodelete
        $doc.Save($file)
    }
    if ($doc.settings.autodelete -eq "true") {
        if (Test-Path $archive)
        {
            Remove-Item -Force $archive
        }
    }
}

function Check-GetFFmpeg() {
    $get_ffmpeg = ""
    $file = "settings.xml"

    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { exit }
    [xml]$doc = Get-Content $file
    if ($doc.settings.getffmpeg -eq "unset") {
        Write-Host "FFmpeg doesn't exist. " -ForegroundColor Green -NoNewline
        $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Proceed with downloading? [Y/n] (default=n)" "N"
        Write-Host ""
        if ($result -eq 'Y') {
            $get_ffmpeg = "true"
        }
        elseif ($result -eq 'N') {
            $get_ffmpeg = "false"
        }
        else {
            throw "Please enter valid input key."
        }
        $doc.settings.getffmpeg = $get_ffmpeg
        $doc.Save($file)
    }
    else {
        $get_ffmpeg = $doc.settings.getffmpeg
    }
    return $get_ffmpeg
}

function Upgrade-Mpv {
    $need_download = $false
    $remoteName = ""
    $download_link = ""
    $arch = ""
    $channel = ""

    if (Check-Mpv) {
        $channel = Check-ChannelRelease
        $file_arch = (Get-Arch).FileType
        $arch = Check-Arch $file_arch
        $remoteName, $download_link = Get-Latest-Mpv $arch $channel
        $localgit = ExtractGitFromFile
        $localdate = ExtractDateFromFile
        $remotegit = ExtractGitFromURL $remoteName
        $remotedate = ExtractDateFromURL $remoteName
        if ($localgit -match $remotegit)
        {
            if ($localdate -match $remotedate)
            {
                Write-Host "You are already using latest mpv build -- $remoteName" -ForegroundColor Green
                $need_download = $false
            }
            else {
                Write-Host "Newer mpv build available" -ForegroundColor Green
                $need_download = $true
            }
        }
        else {
            Write-Host "Newer mpv build available" -ForegroundColor Green
            $need_download = $true
        }
    }
    else {
        Write-Host "mpv doesn't exist. " -ForegroundColor Green -NoNewline
        $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Proceed with downloading? [Y/n] (default=y)" "Y"
        Write-Host ""

        if ($result -eq 'Y') {
            $need_download = $true
            if (Test-Path (Join-Path $env:windir "SysWow64")) {
                Write-Host "Detecting System Type is 64-bit" -ForegroundColor Green
                $original_arch = "x86_64"
            }
            else {
                Write-Host "Detecting System Type is 32-bit" -ForegroundColor Green
                $original_arch = "i686"
            }
            $channel = Check-ChannelRelease
            $arch = Check-Arch $original_arch
            $remoteName, $download_link = Get-Latest-Mpv $arch $channel
        }
        elseif ($result -eq 'N') {
            $need_download = $false
        }
        else {
            throw "Please enter valid input key."
        }
    }

    if ($need_download) {
        Download-Archive $remoteName $download_link
        Check-7z
        Extract-Archive $remoteName
    }
    Check-Autodelete $remoteName
}

function Upgrade-Ytplugin {
    if (Check-Ytplugin-In-System) {
        Write-Host "yt-dlp.exe or youtube-dl.exe already exists in your system, skip the update check." -ForegroundColor Green
        return
    }
    $yt = Check-Ytplugin
    if ($yt) {
        $latest_release = Get-Latest-Ytplugin((Get-Item $yt).BaseName)
        if ((& $yt --version) -match ($latest_release)) {
            Write-Host "You are already using latest" (Get-Item $yt).BaseName "-- $latest_release" -ForegroundColor Green
        }
        else {
            Write-Host "Newer" (Get-Item $yt).BaseName "build available" -ForegroundColor Green
            & $yt --update
        }
    }
    else {
        Write-Host "ytdlp or youtube-dl doesn't exist. " -ForegroundColor Green -NoNewline
        $result = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Proceed with downloading? [Y/n] (default=n)" "N"
        Write-Host ""

        if ($result -eq 'Y') {
            $result_exe = Read-KeyOrTimeout "Download ytdlp or youtubedl? [1=ytdlp/2=youtubedl] (default=1)" "D1"
            Write-Host ""
            if ($result_exe -eq 'D1') {
                $latest_release = Get-Latest-Ytplugin "yt-dlp"
                Download-Ytplugin "yt-dlp" $latest_release
            }
            elseif ($result_exe -eq 'D2') {
                $latest_release = Get-Latest-Ytplugin "youtube-dl"
                Download-Ytplugin "youtube-dl" $latest_release
            }
            else {
                throw "Please enter valid input key."
            }
        }
    }
}

function Upgrade-FFmpeg {
    $get_ffmpeg = Check-GetFFmpeg
    if ($get_ffmpeg -eq "false") {
        return
    }

    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $env:windir "SysWow64")) {
        $original_arch = "x86_64"
        $arch = Check-Arch $original_arch
    }
    else {
        $arch = "i686"
    }

    $need_download = $false
    $remote_name, $download_link = Get-Latest-FFmpeg $arch
    $ffmpeg = (Get-Location).Path + "\ffmpeg.exe"
    $ffmpeg_exist = Test-Path $ffmpeg

    if ($ffmpeg_exist) {
        $ffmpeg_file = .\ffmpeg -version | select-string "ffmpeg" | select-object -First 1
        $file_pattern_1 = "git-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-(?<commit>[a-z0-9]+)" # git-2023-01-02-cc2b1a325
        $file_pattern_2 = "N-\d+-g(?<commit>[a-z0-9]+)"                         # N-109751-g9a820ec8b
        $file_pattern = $file_pattern_1, $file_pattern_2 -join '|'
        $url_pattern = "git-([a-z0-9]+)"
        $file_match= [Regex]::Matches($ffmpeg_file, $file_pattern)
        $remote_match = [Regex]::Matches($remote_name, $url_pattern)
        $local_git = $file_match[0].groups['commit'].value
        $remote_git = $remote_match[0].groups[1].value

        if ($local_git -match $remote_git) {
            Write-Host "You are already using latest ffmpeg build -- $remote_name" -ForegroundColor Green
            $need_download = $false
        }
        else {
            Write-Host "Newer ffmpeg build available" -ForegroundColor Green
            $need_download = $true
        }
    }
    else {
        $need_download = $true
    }

    if ($need_download) {
        Download-Archive $remote_name $download_link
        Check-7z
        Extract-Archive $remote_name
    }
    Check-Autodelete $remote_name
}

function Read-KeyOrTimeout ($prompt, $key){
    $seconds = 9
    $startTime = Get-Date
    $timeOut = New-TimeSpan -Seconds $seconds

    Write-Host "$prompt " -ForegroundColor Green

    # Basic progress bar
    [Console]::CursorLeft = 0
    [Console]::Write("[")
    [Console]::CursorLeft = $seconds + 2
    [Console]::Write("]")
    [Console]::CursorLeft = 1

    while (-not [System.Console]::KeyAvailable) {
        $currentTime = Get-Date
        Start-Sleep -s 1
        Write-Host "#" -ForegroundColor Green -NoNewline
        if ($currentTime -gt $startTime + $timeOut) {
            Break
        }
    }
    if ([System.Console]::KeyAvailable) {
        $response = [System.Console]::ReadKey($true).Key
    }
    else {
        $response = $key
    }
    return $response.ToString()
}

#
# Main script entry point
#
if (Test-Admin) {
    Write-Host "Running script with administrator privileges" -ForegroundColor Yellow
}
else {
    Write-Host "Running script without administrator privileges" -ForegroundColor Red
}

try {
    Check-PowershellVersion
    # Sourceforge only support TLS 1.2
    [Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12
    $global:progressPreference = 'silentlyContinue'
    Upgrade-Mpv
    Upgrade-Ytplugin
    Upgrade-FFmpeg
    Write-Host "Operation completed" -ForegroundColor Magenta
}
catch [System.Exception] {
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}



--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: bin/windows/updater.bat
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@echo OFF
:: This batch file exists to run updater.ps1 without hassle
pushd %~dp0
if exist "%~dp0\installer\updater.ps1" (
    set updater_script="%~dp0\installer\updater.ps1"
) else (
    set updater_script="%~dp0\updater.ps1"
)

:: Check if pwsh is in the system's PATH
where pwsh >nul 2>nul
if %errorlevel% equ 0 (
    :: pwsh is in PATH, so run the script using Windows Powershell
    pwsh -NoProfile -NoLogo -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File %updater_script%
) else (
    :: pwsh is not in PATH, run the script using PowerShell Core
    powershell -NoProfile -NoLogo -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File %updater_script%
)

:: After update, updater.ps1 should not in same folder as mpv.exe
if exist "%~dp0\installer\updater.ps1" if exist "%~dp0\updater.ps1" (
    del "%~dp0\updater.ps1"
)
timeout 5

# Subtitle Studio v5.0 - Build Notes

Current status: **v5.0 ship candidate**  
Current branch: **v5-finalization**  
Current confirmed commit before this BUILD_NOTES update: **99963d44**  
Previous packaging cleanup commit: **edffeebb**

---

## Build Summary

Subtitle Studio v5.0 currently has a working Windows standalone build using Nuitka.

The final working build strategy is intentionally conservative. Several runtime packages are handled manually because compiling or relying on automatic dependency detection caused packaged-app failures.

The current authoritative build script is:

```text
build_nuitka.ps1
```

Do not simplify or refactor this script unless the packaged app is fully retested afterward.

---

## Confirmed Working Runtime Areas

The current ship candidate has confirmed working support for:

- App launch from packaged executable
- Bundled FFmpeg / FFprobe lookup
- Bundled MPV runtime lookup
- Video loading
- Preview/proxy generation
- Subtitle import/edit workflow
- Audio extraction
- Audio enhancement using `pyrnnoise`
- PyAV runtime loading inside the packaged app
- `audiolab` runtime import inside the packaged app
- Exporting subtitle files
- Exporting audio
- Exporting video with subtitles
- Runtime verification checks in the build script

---

## Primary Build Command

From the project root:

```powershell
.\build_nuitka.ps1
```

Expected final executable:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\SubtitleStudio.exe
```

The build output folder is generated and should not be committed to Git.

---

## Required Build Environment

Recommended:

```text
Windows
Python 3.12
PowerShell
Nuitka
MSVC / Visual Studio Build Tools compatible with Nuitka
```

Known working dependency reference:

```text
requirements_WORKING_SHIP.txt
```

Development requirements:

```text
requirements.txt
```

Use `requirements_WORKING_SHIP.txt` as the best reference when reproducing the ship-candidate packaging environment.

---

## Corporate Network Note

Some environments may block package downloads or SSL verification because of corporate SSL inspection.

Do not permanently disable SSL verification.

Recommended approaches:

- Use the approved corporate Python package index
- Use an approved internal wheel cache
- Download wheels through approved channels
- Keep dependency versions pinned once a build is confirmed working

---

## Pre-Build Compile Checks

The build script currently runs key compile checks before Nuitka packaging.

Useful manual checks:

```powershell
python -m py_compile core\audio_enhance.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_edit.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_export.py
```

Additional useful checks before committing:

```powershell
python -m py_compile launcher.py
python -m py_compile main.py
python -m py_compile core\ffmpeg.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_logs.py
```

---

## Nuitka Strategy

The build uses Nuitka standalone mode:

```text
--standalone
--windows-console-mode=disable
--enable-plugin=tk-inter
--output-dir=build_nuitka
--output-filename=SubtitleStudio.exe
```

Important included app modules include:

```text
tabs.tab_edit
tabs.tab_export
tabs.tab_logs
core.player_mpv
core.audio_enhance
```

Important included runtime packages/modules include:

```text
customtkinter

mimetypes
```

Important no-follow exclusions include:

```text
av
audiolab

moviepy
matplotlib
```

---

## Why PyAV Is Manually Copied

PyAV is intentionally excluded from Nuitka compilation:

```text
--nofollow-import-to=av
```

Manual copies are performed for:

```text
av\
av.libs\
av-*.dist-info
python3.dll
```

Reason:

- PyAV native extensions require native FFmpeg-related DLLs from `av.libs`
- `av._core.pyd` imports `python3.dll` directly
- Letting Nuitka handle PyAV automatically was not sufficient for the packaged runtime

Required packaged paths include:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\_core.pyd
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av.libs
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\python3.dll
```

The build script also copies PyAV DLLs into multiple locations to improve runtime resolution reliability:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\
```

---

## Why audiolab Is Manually Copied

`audiolab` is intentionally excluded from Nuitka compilation:

```text
--nofollow-import-to=audiolab
```

Manual copies are performed for:

```text
audiolab\
audiolab-*.dist-info
```

Reason:

Compiling `audiolab` with Nuitka caused a runtime resource-reader failure similar to:

```text
TypeError: iter() returned non-iterator of type 'nuitka_resource_reader_files'
```

The working solution is:

- Do not compile `audiolab`
- Manually copy the `audiolab` package
- Include its pure-Python dependencies through Nuitka

Important: do not manually copy the entire dependency closure from site-packages. That previously caused dependency and stdlib import issues.

---

## audiolab Dependency Strategy

The dependencies needed by `audiolab` are included through Nuitka rather than copied raw.

Important included dependency areas:

```text
requests
urllib3
certifi
idna
charset_normalizer
jinja2
markupsafe
smart_open
humanize
wrapt
http
email
netrc
mimetypes
ssl
socket
```

Reason:

Manual dependency copying previously caused missing stdlib/runtime issues such as:

```text
No module named 'http.cookies'
```

The current hybrid strategy avoids that problem.

---

## Why NumPy Is Not Manually Copied

NumPy is handled by Nuitka:

```text
--include-package=numpy
```

Only native libraries are manually copied:

```text
numpy.libs\
```

Do not manually copy the full `site-packages\numpy` folder into the distribution.

Reason:

Manual full-package copying caused package collisions and unstable runtime behavior.

---

## SoundFile Runtime Handling

SoundFile native data is manually copied when available:

```text
_soundfile_data\
```

The build script also copies any DLLs from that folder into the dist root.

This supports packaged audio read/write behavior used by the enhancement/export pipeline.

Manual copies include:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise\rnnoise.dll
```

The RNNoise model file is copied when available:


## Build Script Runtime Verification

At the end of the build, the script verifies required runtime paths.

Required paths include:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\ffprobe.exe
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\mpv-2.dll
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\mpv.exe
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\python3.dll
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\_core.pyd
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av.libs
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise\rnnoise.dll
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\audiolab
```

If any required path is missing, the build script exits with an error.

---

## Known Good Logs

The ship-candidate package previously produced successful logs for premium audio enhancement:

```text
[OK] pyrnnoise stage complete using denoise_chunk.
[OK] pyrnnoise light polish complete.
[OK] Enhanced audio ready: subtitle_studio_enhanced_pyrnnoise_*.wav
```

Successful export logs included:

```text
[OK] Audio: *_merged_audio.mp3
[OK] Exported: *_merged.srt
[OK] Subtitle export complete
[OK] Export complete.
```

These log patterns should be treated as known-good reference output during clean-machine QA.

---

## Clean-Machine QA Required Before Wider Distribution

Before distributing beyond internal/beta testing, verify on a clean Windows machine that does not have the development environment installed.

Minimum QA checklist:

1. Copy the ship package to the clean machine
2. Launch `SubtitleStudio.exe`
3. Confirm the app opens without a console or missing DLL error
4. Load a test video
5. Confirm preview playback works
6. Import a subtitle file
7. Edit at least one subtitle line
8. Mark an IN/OUT section
9. Run audio enhancement
10. Confirm `pyrnnoise` success appears in the Log tab
11. Export video with burned subtitles
12. Export audio
13. Export subtitle file
14. Manually play exported video
15. Manually play exported audio
16. Manually open exported subtitle file
17. Confirm no runtime DLL errors
18. Confirm no missing FFmpeg/MPV errors

---

## Ship Package Creation

The ship helper is:

```text
make_ship_package.ps1
```

Use it after a successful Nuitka build.

The ship package should include the complete standalone distribution folder and required runtime assets.

Do not include:

```text
.venv/
.venv_packaging/
python_env/
build/
dist/
site-packages/
source test media
local preferences
```


---

## Known Caution Areas

### Do not rewrite playback before v5.0

The current proxy + frame-buffer/MPV model is stable enough for v5.0.

Only make minimal targeted fixes before ship.

### Do not simplify packaging casually

The current packaging script contains manual copy steps that are required for the known-good packaged runtime.

Simplifying it may reintroduce:

- Missing PyAV DLL errors
- Missing `python3.dll`
- `audiolab` resource-reader errors
- Missing `http.cookies`
- NumPy collisions
- Missing `rnnoise.dll`
- Missing SoundFile native data

### Do not commit local/generated artifacts

Keep release zips, build output, virtual environments, local preferences, and test media out of Git.

---

## Recommended Next Build Step

For release validation:

1. Confirm Git working tree is clean
2. Run tests
3. Run `.\build_nuitka.ps1`
4. Confirm executable exists
5. Run packaged app locally
6. Create ship package with `.\make_ship_package.ps1`
7. Test on clean Windows machine
8. Update `SESSION_CONTEXT.txt`
9. Regenerate clean project export

$distRoot = "build_nuitka\launcher.dist"

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $distBin | Out-Null

Copy-Item "bin\windows\ffprobe.exe" $distBin -Force

$SitePackages = python -c "import av, pathlib, inspect; print(pathlib.Path(inspect.getfile(av)).parent.parent)"

Write-Host "[INFO] Site-packages: $SitePackages" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# PyAV manual copy.
# Do NOT let Nuitka compile av.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

$AvRoot = python -c "import av, pathlib, inspect; print(pathlib.Path(inspect.getfile(av)).parent)"
$AvTarget = Join-Path $distRoot "av"

if ($AvRoot -and (Test-Path $AvRoot)) {
    Write-Host "[INFO] Copying PyAV package: $AvRoot -> $AvTarget" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Remove-Item $AvTarget -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Copy-Item $AvRoot $AvTarget -Recurse -Force
}
else {
    Write-Host "[ERROR] Could not locate av package folder." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

$AvLibs = Join-Path $SitePackages "av.libs"
$AvLibsTarget = Join-Path $distRoot "av.libs"

if (Test-Path $AvLibs) {
    Write-Host "[INFO] Copying PyAV native libs: $AvLibs -> $AvLibsTarget" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Remove-Item $AvLibsTarget -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Copy-Item $AvLibs $AvLibsTarget -Recurse -Force

    Write-Host "[INFO] Copying PyAV DLLs into dist root, av folder, and bin folder..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Copy-Item "$AvLibs\*.dll" $distRoot -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Copy-Item "$AvLibs\*.dll" $AvTarget -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Copy-Item "$AvLibs\*.dll" $distBin -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
else {
    Write-Host "[ERROR] av.libs missing at: $AvLibs" -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

$AvDistInfos = Get-ChildItem $SitePackages -Directory -Filter "av-*.dist-info" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

foreach ($Info in $AvDistInfos) {
    $Target = Join-Path $distRoot $Info.Name
    Write-Host "[INFO] Copying PyAV dist-info: $($Info.FullName) -> $Target" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Remove-Item $Target -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Copy-Item $Info.FullName $Target -Recurse -Force
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# python3.dll for PyAV stable ABI extensions.
# av._core.pyd imports python3.dll directly.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

$Python3Dll = python -c "import sys, pathlib; roots=[pathlib.Path(sys.executable).parent, pathlib.Path(sys.base_prefix), pathlib.Path(sys.base_prefix)/'DLLs']; found=[str(p/'python3.dll') for p in roots if (p/'python3.dll').exists()]; print(found[0] if found else '')"

if ($Python3Dll -and (Test-Path $Python3Dll)) {
    Write-Host "[INFO] Copying python3.dll for PyAV: $Python3Dll" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Copy-Item $Python3Dll $distRoot -Force

    Copy-Item $Python3Dll $distBin -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

else {

    exit 1
}

$NumpyLibs = Join-Path $SitePackages "numpy.libs"

    Remove-Item $Target -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

Write-Host "[INFO] Verifying packaged runtime files..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$RequiredPaths = @(
    "$distBin\ffmpeg.exe",
    "$distBin\ffprobe.exe",

    }

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: core/audio_enhance.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Subtitle Studio v5 — Hybrid audio enhancement pipeline.

Primary:
    pyrnnoise RNNoise speech denoise

Fallback:
    FFmpeg arnndn using bundled .rnnn model

Final fallback:
    FFmpeg built-in speech cleanup

Goal:
    Use pyrnnoise as the benchmark-quality path, while keeping packaged app stable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import shutil
import tempfile
import datetime
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

_DLL_DIRECTORY_HANDLES = []
_DLL_SEARCH_PATHS_ADDED = set()


def _add_packaged_dll_search_paths():
    """
    Help Windows find native DLLs in Nuitka standalone builds.

    Important:
        os.add_dll_directory() returns a handle.
        The handle must be kept alive, otherwise the path can be removed.

    Needed for:
        av._core.pyd -> av.libs/*.dll
        pyrnnoise/rnnoise.dll
        soundfile/_soundfile_data/libsndfile_x64.dll
        numpy.libs/*.dll
    """
    try:
        roots = []

        try:
            exe_root = Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent
            roots.extend([
                exe_root,
                exe_root / "av",
                exe_root / "av.libs",
                exe_root / "pyrnnoise",
                exe_root / "pyrnnoise.libs",
                exe_root / "audiolab",
                exe_root / "audiolab.libs",
                exe_root / "numpy.libs",
                exe_root / "_soundfile_data",
                exe_root / "bin" / "windows",
            ])
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            file_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
            roots.extend([
                file_root,
                file_root.parent,
                file_root.parent / "av",
                file_root.parent / "av.libs",
                file_root.parent / "pyrnnoise",
                file_root.parent / "pyrnnoise.libs",
                file_root.parent / "audiolab",
                file_root.parent / "audiolab.libs",
                file_root.parent / "numpy.libs",
                file_root.parent / "_soundfile_data",
                file_root.parent / "bin" / "windows",
            ])
        except Exception:
            pass

        valid_paths = []

        for root in roots:
            try:
                p = Path(root).resolve()
                if not p.is_dir():
                    continue

                s = str(p)

                if s in _DLL_SEARCH_PATHS_ADDED:
                    continue

                _DLL_SEARCH_PATHS_ADDED.add(s)
                valid_paths.append(s)

                if hasattr(os, "add_dll_directory"):
                    try:
                        handle = os.add_dll_directory(s)
                        _DLL_DIRECTORY_HANDLES.append(handle)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            except Exception:
                pass

        # PATH fallback for native loaders that still consult PATH.
        if valid_paths:
            existing = os.environ.get("PATH", "")
            existing_parts = set(existing.split(os.pathsep))
            prepend = [p for p in valid_paths if p not in existing_parts]

            if prepend:
                os.environ["PATH"] = os.pathsep.join(prepend) + os.pathsep + existing

    except Exception:
        pass

def _timestamp_slug() -> str:
    return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")


def build_stage_output_path(prefix: str, ext: str = ".wav") -> str:
    return os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), f"{prefix}_{_timestamp_slug()}{ext}")


def _run_subprocess(cmd: list[str], timeout: int = 900) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=timeout,
            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
        )
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)

    if result.returncode == 0:
        return True, ""

    err = (result.stderr or result.stdout or "").strip()
    if not err:
        err = f"Process failed with return code {result.returncode}"

    return False, err


def _resolve_ffmpeg(
    ffmpeg_path: str | None = None,
    app_dir: str | None = None,
) -> str | None:
    candidates: list[str] = []

    if ffmpeg_path:
        candidates.append(ffmpeg_path)

    roots: list[Path] = []

    if app_dir:
        roots.append(Path(app_dir))

    try:
        roots.append(Path.cwd())
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        roots.append(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        roots.append(Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    for root in roots:
        candidates.extend([
            str(root / "bin" / "windows" / "ffmpeg.exe"),
            str(root / "_internal" / "bin" / "windows" / "ffmpeg.exe"),
            str(root / "bin" / "ffmpeg.exe"),
        ])

    found = shutil.which("ffmpeg")
    if found:
        candidates.append(found)

    seen = set()
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            key = str(candidate).lower()
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)

            if candidate and os.path.isfile(candidate):
                return candidate
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None


def _verify_output(path: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    if not path or not os.path.isfile(path):
        return False, "Output file was not created."

    try:
        if os.path.getsize(path) <= 0:
            return False, "Output file is empty."
    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"Could not verify output file: {e}"

    return True, ""


def _ffmpeg_filter_path(path: str) -> str:
    safe = str(path).replace("\\", "/")
    safe = safe.replace(":", r"\:")
    safe = safe.replace("'", r"\'")
    return safe


def _resolve_arnndn_model(app_dir: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    roots: list[Path] = []

    if app_dir:
        roots.append(Path(app_dir))

    try:
        roots.append(Path.cwd())
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        roots.append(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        roots.append(Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    model_names = [
        "rnnoise-model.rnnn",
        "rnnoise.rnnn",
        "arnndn-model.rnnn",
        "std.rnnn",
    ]

    candidates: list[Path] = []

    for root in roots:
        for name in model_names:
            candidates.extend([
                root / "bin" / "windows" / "models" / name,
                root / "_internal" / "bin" / "windows" / "models" / name,
                root / "models" / name,
            ])

    seen = set()
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            key = str(candidate.resolve()).lower()
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)

            if candidate.is_file():
                return str(candidate)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None


def _ffmpeg_supports_filter(ffmpeg_path: str, filter_name: str) -> bool:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [ffmpeg_path, "-hide_banner", "-filters"],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=20,
            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
        )
        text = (result.stdout or "") + "\n" + (result.stderr or "")
        return filter_name in text
    except Exception:
        return False


def _preconvert_for_pyrnnoise(
    source_audio_path: str,
    ffmpeg_path: str,
) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str]:
    """
    Convert source to predictable RNNoise-friendly audio:
        48 kHz
        mono
        PCM WAV

    RNNoise-style denoisers are generally happiest with 48 kHz mono speech.
    """
    output_path = build_stage_output_path("subtitle_studio_pyrnnoise_input", ".wav")

    cmd = [
        ffmpeg_path,
        "-hide_banner",
        "-y",
        "-i", source_audio_path,
        "-vn",
        "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
        "-ar", "48000",
        "-ac", "1",
        output_path,
    ]

    ok, err = _run_subprocess(cmd, timeout=900)
    if not ok:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise preconvert failed: {err}"

    verified, msg = _verify_output(output_path)
    if not verified:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise preconvert failed: {msg}"

    return True, output_path, "pyrnnoise preconvert complete."


def _make_rnnoise_instance(pyrnnoise, sample_rate: int):
    """
    Try multiple constructor styles because pyrnnoise builds can differ.
    """
    errors = []

    constructors = [
        lambda: pyrnnoise.RNNoise(sample_rate),
        lambda: pyrnnoise.RNNoise(),
    ]

    for ctor in constructors:
        try:
            return ctor(), ""
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(str(e))

    return None, " | ".join(errors) if errors else "Unknown RNNoise constructor error"


def _run_denoiser_method(denoiser, waveform_f32):
    """
    Try common pyrnnoise method names.
    """
    errors = []

    for method_name in [
        "denoise_chunk",
        "process_chunk",
        "denoise",
        "process",
    ]:
        method = getattr(denoiser, method_name, None)
        if not method:
            continue

        try:
            return method(waveform_f32), method_name
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(f"{method_name}: {e}")

    raise RuntimeError("No usable pyrnnoise processing method worked. " + " ; ".join(errors))


def _extract_audio_frame(item):
    import numpy as np

    candidate = None

    if isinstance(item, tuple):
        if len(item) >= 2:
            candidate = item[1]
        elif len(item) == 1:
            candidate = item[0]
    elif isinstance(item, list):
        if len(item) >= 2:
            candidate = item[1]
        elif len(item) == 1:
            candidate = item[0]
    else:
        candidate = item

    if candidate is None:
        raise RuntimeError("RNNoise item did not contain audio.")

    arr = np.asarray(candidate).reshape(-1)

    if arr.size == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("RNNoise frame is empty.")

    if np.issubdtype(arr.dtype, np.integer):
        arr = arr.astype("float32") / 32768.0
    else:
        arr = arr.astype("float32")

    return arr


def _run_pyrnnoise_stage(
    source_audio_path: str,
    ffmpeg_path: str,
) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str]:
    """
    Primary premium denoise stage.
    """
    try:
        _add_packaged_dll_search_paths()

        try:
            import numpy as np
        except Exception as e:
            return False, None, f"numpy unavailable for pyrnnoise path: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

        try:
            import soundfile as sf
        except Exception as e:
            return False, None, f"soundfile unavailable for pyrnnoise path: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

        try:
            import av
        except Exception as e:
            return False, None, f"av unavailable for pyrnnoise path: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

        try:
            import pyrnnoise
        except Exception as e:
            return False, None, f"pyrnnoise import failed after dependencies loaded: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

    except Exception as e:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise dependency setup failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

    pre_ok, pre_path, pre_msg = _preconvert_for_pyrnnoise(source_audio_path, ffmpeg_path)
    if not pre_ok or not pre_path:
        return False, None, pre_msg

    try:
        waveform, sample_rate = sf.read(pre_path, always_2d=False)

        waveform = np.asarray(waveform, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)

        if waveform.size == 0:
            return False, None, "pyrnnoise input audio is empty."

        peak = float(np.max(np.abs(waveform))) if waveform.size else 0.0
        if peak > 1.5:
            waveform = waveform / 32768.0

        denoiser, ctor_err = _make_rnnoise_instance(pyrnnoise, sample_rate)
        if denoiser is None:
            return False, None, f"pyrnnoise failed to initialize: {ctor_err}"

        try:
            rn_result, method_name = _run_denoiser_method(
                denoiser,
                waveform.astype(np.float32),
            )
        except Exception as e:
            return False, None, f"pyrnnoise processing failed: {e}"

        frames = []

        try:
            iterator = iter(rn_result)
            for item in iterator:
                try:
                    frames.append(_extract_audio_frame(item))
                except Exception:
                    continue
        except TypeError:
            try:
                frames.append(_extract_audio_frame(rn_result))
            except Exception as e:
                return False, None, f"pyrnnoise output parse failed: {e}"

        if not frames:
            return False, None, "pyrnnoise produced no audio frames."

        enhanced = np.concatenate(frames).astype(np.float32)

        if enhanced.size == 0:
            return False, None, "pyrnnoise produced empty audio."

        if enhanced.size < waveform.size:
            enhanced = np.pad(enhanced, (0, waveform.size - enhanced.size))
        else:
            enhanced = enhanced[:waveform.size]

        enhanced = np.clip(enhanced, -1.0, 1.0)

        output_path = build_stage_output_path("subtitle_studio_pyrnnoise", ".wav")
        sf.write(output_path, enhanced, 48000, subtype="PCM_16")

        verified, msg = _verify_output(output_path)
        if not verified:
            return False, None, f"pyrnnoise failed: {msg}"

        return True, output_path, f"pyrnnoise stage complete using {method_name}."

    except Exception as e:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise stage failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"


def _run_light_ffmpeg_polish(
    input_audio_path: str,
    ffmpeg_path: str,
) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str]:
    """
    Minimal polish after pyrnnoise.

    Do not overprocess. pyrnnoise is the main enhancement.
    """
    output_path = build_stage_output_path("subtitle_studio_enhanced_pyrnnoise", ".wav")

    filter_chain = ",".join([
        "highpass=f=60",
        "loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5:LRA=9",
        "alimiter=limit=0.95",
    ])

    cmd = [
        ffmpeg_path,
        "-hide_banner",
        "-y",
        "-i", input_audio_path,
        "-af", filter_chain,
        "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
        "-ar", "48000",
        "-ac", "2",
        output_path,
    ]

    ok, err = _run_subprocess(cmd, timeout=900)
    if not ok:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise polish failed: {err}"

    verified, msg = _verify_output(output_path)
    if not verified:
        return False, None, f"pyrnnoise polish failed: {msg}"

    return True, output_path, "pyrnnoise light polish complete."


def _run_ffmpeg_fallback(
    source_audio_path: str,
    ffmpeg_path: str,
    app_dir: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str]:
    """
    Fallback path:
        arnndn std.rnnn if available
        otherwise built-in FFmpeg cleanup
    """
    errors = []

    filter_chains = []

    model = _resolve_arnndn_model(app_dir)
    arnndn_supported = _ffmpeg_supports_filter(ffmpeg_path, "arnndn")

    if model and arnndn_supported:
        model_path = _ffmpeg_filter_path(model)
        filter_chains.append((
            "ffmpeg-arnndn-std",
            ",".join([
                "aresample=48000",
                "aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:channel_layouts=mono",
                f"arnndn=m='{model_path}':mix=0.92",
                "highpass=f=65",
                "loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5:LRA=9",
                "alimiter=limit=0.95",
            ]),
        ))

    filter_chains.append((
        "ffmpeg-built-in",
        ",".join([
            "highpass=f=70",
            "lowpass=f=10000",
            "afftdn=nf=-25:nt=w",
            "acompressor=threshold=-20dB:ratio=2.2:attack=12:release=250:makeup=2",
            "loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5:LRA=9",
            "alimiter=limit=0.95",
        ]),
    ))

    for name, filter_chain in filter_chains:
        output_path = build_stage_output_path(f"subtitle_studio_enhanced_{name}", ".wav")

        cmd = [
            ffmpeg_path,
            "-hide_banner",
            "-y",
            "-i", source_audio_path,
            "-af", filter_chain,
            "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
            "-ar", "48000",
            "-ac", "2",
            output_path,
        ]

        ok, err = _run_subprocess(cmd, timeout=900)
        if not ok:
            errors.append(f"{name}: {err}")
            continue

        verified, msg = _verify_output(output_path)
        if not verified:
            errors.append(f"{name}: {msg}")
            continue

        return True, output_path, f"Fallback enhancement complete using {name}."

    return False, None, "All FFmpeg fallback chains failed: " + " | ".join(errors)


def enhance_audio(
    source_audio_path: str,
    ffmpeg_path: str | None = None,
    app_dir: str | None = None,
) -> dict:
    stages: list[str] = []

    if not source_audio_path:
        return {
            "success": False,
            "output_path": None,
            "message": "No source audio path provided.",
            "stages": stages,
        }

    if not os.path.isfile(source_audio_path):
        return {
            "success": False,
            "output_path": None,
            "message": f"Source audio not found: {source_audio_path}",
            "stages": stages,
        }

    resolved_ffmpeg = _resolve_ffmpeg(ffmpeg_path, app_dir)
    if not resolved_ffmpeg:
        return {
            "success": False,
            "output_path": None,
            "message": "FFmpeg not found for audio enhancement.",
            "stages": stages,
        }

    stages.append(f"Using FFmpeg: {resolved_ffmpeg}")

    # 1. Premium path: pyrnnoise.
    rn_ok, rn_output, rn_msg = _run_pyrnnoise_stage(
        source_audio_path=source_audio_path,
        ffmpeg_path=resolved_ffmpeg,
    )
    stages.append(rn_msg)

    if rn_ok and rn_output:
        polish_ok, final_output, polish_msg = _run_light_ffmpeg_polish(
            input_audio_path=rn_output,
            ffmpeg_path=resolved_ffmpeg,
        )
        stages.append(polish_msg)

        if polish_ok and final_output:
            return {
                "success": True,
                "output_path": final_output,
                "message": "Audio enhancement complete using pyrnnoise.",
                "stages": stages,
            }

        stages.append("pyrnnoise succeeded but polish failed; trying FFmpeg fallback.")

    # 2. Fallback path: arnndn / FFmpeg.
    fallback_ok, fallback_output, fallback_msg = _run_ffmpeg_fallback(
        source_audio_path=source_audio_path,
        ffmpeg_path=resolved_ffmpeg,
        app_dir=app_dir,
    )
    stages.append(fallback_msg)

    if fallback_ok and fallback_output:
        return {
            "success": True,
            "output_path": fallback_output,
            "message": fallback_msg,
            "stages": stages,
        }

    return {
        "success": False,
        "output_path": None,
        "message": "Audio enhancement failed.",
        "stages": stages,
    }

"""
Subtitle Studio v4.0 — Invisible Automation.
Runs silently on load, edit, and export.
No UI — just fixes things in the background.
"""

import os
import re
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional


def auto_fix_on_load(cues, log_fn=None) -> Tuple[list, int]:
    """
    Run silent fixes when subtitles are first loaded.
    Returns: (fixed_cues, total_fixes)
    """
    fixes = 0

    # 1. Reindex
    for i, cue in enumerate(cues):
        if cue.index != i + 1:
            cue.index = i + 1
            fixes += 1

    # 2. Fix overlaps (trim previous cue end)
    for i in range(1, len(cues)):
        prev = cues[i - 1]
        curr = cues[i]
        if curr.start < prev.end:
            gap = 0.001  # 1ms gap
            new_end = curr.start - gap
            if new_end > prev.start:
                prev.end = new_end
                fixes += 1
                if log_fn:
                    log_fn(
                        f"Auto-fix: Cue {prev.index} "
                        f"overlap trimmed")

    # 3. Remove empty cues
    before = len(cues)
    cues = [c for c in cues if c.text and c.text.strip()]
    removed = before - len(cues)
    if removed > 0:
        fixes += removed
        if log_fn:
            log_fn(f"Auto-fix: Removed {removed} empty cue(s)")
        # Reindex after removal
        for i, cue in enumerate(cues):
            cue.index = i + 1

    # 4. Merge very short cues (< 0.3s) with next cue
    merged = 0
    i = 0
    while i < len(cues) - 1:
        curr = cues[i]
        nxt = cues[i + 1]
        duration = curr.end - curr.start
        if duration < 0.3 and duration > 0:
            gap = nxt.start - curr.end
            if gap < 0.5:
                # Merge: extend next cue to start earlier
                nxt.start = curr.start
                nxt.text = curr.text + " " + nxt.text
                cues.pop(i)
                merged += 1
                continue
        i += 1

    if merged > 0:
        fixes += merged
        if log_fn:
            log_fn(
                f"Auto-fix: Merged {merged} "
                f"very short cue(s)")
        for i, cue in enumerate(cues):
            cue.index = i + 1

    # 5. Strip whitespace from all cue text
    for cue in cues:
        stripped = cue.text.strip()
        if stripped != cue.text:
            cue.text = stripped
            fixes += 1

    # 6. Fix double spaces
    for cue in cues:
        cleaned = re.sub(r'  +', ' ', cue.text)
        if cleaned != cue.text:
            cue.text = cleaned
            fixes += 1

    return cues, fixes


def auto_fix_adjacent(cues, edited_index: int) -> int:
    """
    After editing a single cue, check overlap with
    adjacent cues and fix silently.
    Returns number of fixes applied.
    """
    fixes = 0
    idx = edited_index

    if idx < 0 or idx >= len(cues):
        return 0

    curr = cues[idx]

    # Check overlap with previous cue
    if idx > 0:
        prev = cues[idx - 1]
        if curr.start < prev.end:
            new_end = curr.start - 0.001
            if new_end > prev.start:
                prev.end = new_end
                fixes += 1

    # Check overlap with next cue
    if idx < len(cues) - 1:
        nxt = cues[idx + 1]
        if curr.end > nxt.start:
            new_end = nxt.start - 0.001
            if new_end > curr.start:
                curr.end = new_end
                fixes += 1

    return fixes


def auto_fix_before_export(cues, log_fn=None) -> Tuple[list, int]:
    """
    Final cleanup pass before export.
    Returns: (cleaned_cues, total_fixes)
    """
    fixes = 0

    # 1. Reindex
    for i, cue in enumerate(cues):
        if cue.index != i + 1:
            cue.index = i + 1
            fixes += 1

    # 2. Fix any remaining overlaps
    for i in range(1, len(cues)):
        prev = cues[i - 1]
        curr = cues[i]
        if curr.start < prev.end:
            new_end = curr.start - 0.001
            if new_end > prev.start:
                prev.end = new_end
                fixes += 1
                if log_fn:
                    log_fn(
                        f"Export fix: Cue {prev.index} "
                        f"overlap trimmed")

    # 3. Ensure no negative durations
    for cue in cues:
        if cue.end <= cue.start:
            cue.end = cue.start + 0.5
            fixes += 1
            if log_fn:
                log_fn(
                    f"Export fix: Cue {cue.index} "
                    f"negative duration corrected")

    # 4. Final whitespace cleanup
    for cue in cues:
        cleaned = cue.text.strip()
        cleaned = re.sub(r'  +', ' ', cleaned)
        if cleaned != cue.text:
            cue.text = cleaned
            fixes += 1

    return cues, fixes


def find_matching_subtitle(video_path: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Given a video path, look for a subtitle file with
    matching name in the same folder.
    Returns subtitle path or None.
    """
    if not video_path:
        return None

    folder = os.path.dirname(video_path)
    base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(video_path))[0]

    # Try exact match first
    for ext in [".srt", ".vtt"]:
        candidate = os.path.join(folder, base + ext)
        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
            return candidate

    # Try partial match (video name contained in subtitle name)
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(folder):
            if f.lower().endswith((".srt", ".vtt")):
                f_base = os.path.splitext(f)[0].lower()
                if base.lower() in f_base:
                    return os.path.join(folder, f)
    except Exception:
        pass
def auto_split_long_cues(
        cues,
        max_chars_per_line: int = 42,
        max_lines: int = 2,
        max_duration: float = 7.0,
        log_fn=None) -> Tuple[list, int]:
    """
    Intelligently split and line-break long cues.
    
    1. If text fits in 2 lines (≤84 chars) → add line break
       at best natural point (comma, conjunction, clause)
    2. If text is too long for 2 lines → split into
       separate cues at sentence boundaries
    
    Returns: (new_cues, split_count)
    """
    import re
    from core.state import Cue

    new_cues = []
    splits = 0

    # Natural break points ranked by priority
    # Higher priority = better place to break
    _break_patterns = [
        # Sentence endings (split into separate cues)
        (r'[.!?]\s+', 'sentence'),
        # Semicolons and colons
        (r'[;:]\s+', 'clause'),
        # Comma + conjunction (So, And, But, Or, Because)
        (r',\s+(?=So\b|And\b|But\b|Or\b|Because\b|'
         r'Then\b|While\b|When\b|If\b|After\b|Before\b)',
         'conjunction'),
        # Any comma
        (r',\s+', 'comma'),
        # Conjunctions without comma
        (r'\s+(?=and\b|but\b|or\b|so\b|because\b|'
         r'which\b|where\b|when\b|that\b|while\b)',
         'word_conjunction'),
        # Prepositions (of, in, with, for, to, at, from)
        (r'\s+(?=of\b|in\b|with\b|for\b|to\b|'
         r'at\b|from\b|about\b|into\b)',
         'preposition'),
    ]

    def _find_best_break(text: str, max_len: int):
        """Find best position to break text."""
        best_pos = None
        best_priority = -1
        mid = len(text) // 2

        for priority, (pattern, _) in enumerate(
                reversed(_break_patterns)):
            for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):
                pos = match.end()
                # Must leave reasonable text on both sides
                if pos < 8 or pos > len(text) - 8:
                    continue
                # Prefer breaks near the middle
                distance = abs(pos - mid)
                # Score: higher priority + closer to middle
                score = (priority * 1000) - distance
                if best_pos is None or score > best_priority:
                    best_pos = pos
                    best_priority = score

        return best_pos

    def _find_sentence_break(text: str):
        """Find sentence boundary for splitting into
        separate cues."""
        for match in re.finditer(r'[.!?]\s+', text):
            pos = match.end()
            if pos >= 15 and pos <= len(text) - 10:
                return pos

        # Try semicolon/colon + conjunction
        for match in re.finditer(
                r'[;:,]\s+(?=So\b|And\b|But\b|Or\b|'
                r'Because\b|Then\b|Now\b)', text):
            pos = match.start() + 1
            return match.end()

        return None

    def _add_line_break(text: str) -> str:
        """Add a single line break at the best point."""
        if "\n" in text:
            return text
        if len(text) <= max_chars_per_line:
            return text

        pos = _find_best_break(text, max_chars_per_line)
        if pos and pos < len(text):
            line1 = text[:pos].rstrip()
            line2 = text[pos:].lstrip()
            # Only break if both lines fit
            if (len(line1) <= max_chars_per_line + 5
                    and len(line2) <= max_chars_per_line + 5):
                return f"{line1}\n{line2}"

        # Fallback: break at word boundary near middle
        words = text.split()
        if len(words) >= 4:
            mid = len(words) // 2
            line1 = " ".join(words[:mid])
            line2 = " ".join(words[mid:])
            return f"{line1}\n{line2}"

        return text

    for cue in cues:
        text = cue.text.strip()
        duration = cue.end - cue.start
        total_chars = len(text.replace("\n", " "))
        max_total = max_chars_per_line * max_lines

        # Case 1: Short enough for one line
        if total_chars <= max_chars_per_line:
            new_cues.append(Cue(
                0, cue.start, cue.end, text))
            continue

        # Case 2: Fits in 2 lines — add line break
        if (total_chars <= max_total
                and duration <= max_duration):
            broken = _add_line_break(text)
            new_cues.append(Cue(
                0, cue.start, cue.end, broken))
            continue

        # Case 3: Too long — split into separate cues
        # Try sentence boundary first
        sent_break = _find_sentence_break(text)
        if sent_break:
            text1 = text[:sent_break].rstrip()
            text2 = text[sent_break:].lstrip()
        else:
            # Fall back to best natural break
            pos = _find_best_break(text, len(text) // 2)
            if pos:
                text1 = text[:pos].rstrip()
                text2 = text[pos:].lstrip()
            else:
                words = text.split()
                mid = len(words) // 2
                text1 = " ".join(words[:mid])
                text2 = " ".join(words[mid:])

        # Distribute time proportionally
        ratio = len(text1) / max(1, len(text))
        mid_time = cue.start + (duration * ratio)

        # Add line breaks within each part if needed
        text1 = _add_line_break(text1)
        text2 = _add_line_break(text2)

        new_cues.append(Cue(
            0, cue.start, mid_time, text1))
        new_cues.append(Cue(
            0, mid_time, cue.end, text2))
        splits += 1

    # Reindex
    for i, c in enumerate(new_cues):
        c.index = i + 1

    if splits > 0 and log_fn:
        log_fn(f"Auto-split: {splits} long cues divided")

    return new_cues, max(0, splits)

FILE: core/dictionary.py
