"""
Subtitle Studio v4.0 — Video player with audio.
Video: OpenCV sequential read from low-res proxy.
Audio: pygame.mixer with MP3.
Proxy video + frame buffer = smooth playback.
"""

import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import threading
import time as _time
import tkinter as tk

import cv2
import pygame
from PIL import Image, ImageTk

from core.parser import seconds_to_display


class FramePlayer:
    """
    Video preview player with audio playback.
    Creates a low-res proxy video for smooth preview.
    Audio drives position — frames follow via buffer.
    """

    TICK_MS = 33  # ~30 fps display refresh

    def __init__(self, canvas: tk.Canvas, state, ui: dict):
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.state = state
        self.ui = ui

        # Playback state
        self.video_path: str | None = None
        self.duration: float = 0.0
        self.position: float = 0.0
        self.playing: bool = False
        self.loaded: bool = False

        # Loop playback
        self.loop_enabled: bool = False
        self.loop_start: float | None = None
        self.loop_end: float | None = None

        # Audio
        self._audio_path: str | None = None
        self._audio_ready: bool = False
        self._audio_start_pos: float = 0.0
        self._play_start_time: float = 0.0
        self._paused: bool = False

        # Init pygame mixer
        try:
            pygame.mixer.init(frequency=44100, size=-16,
                              channels=2, buffer=4096)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # OpenCV captures
        self._cap: cv2.VideoCapture | None = None
        self._cap_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._video_fps: float = 30.0

        # Proxy video for smooth playback
        self._proxy_path: str | None = None
        self._proxy_cap: cv2.VideoCapture | None = None
        self._proxy_fps: float = 15.0
        self._proxy_ready: bool = False

        # Frame buffer: list of PhotoImage, indexed by
        # proxy frame number
        self._frame_buffer: list = []
        self._buffer_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._buffer_ready: bool = False
        self._buffer_cancel: bool = False

        # Frame display
        self._photo = None
        self._canvas_image = None
        self._last_frame_idx: int = -1

        # Overlay text cache
        self._current_overlay_text = ''

        # Threading
        self._timer_id = None

        # Canvas dimensions
        self.width = 540
        self.height = 300
        self._resize_timer = None

        def _on_canvas_resize(event):
            self.width = event.width
            self.height = event.height
            if self.loaded and not self.playing:
                if self._resize_timer is not None:
                    self.canvas.after_cancel(
                        self._resize_timer)
                self._resize_timer = self.canvas.after(
                    150, lambda: self._show_direct(
                        self.position))

        self.canvas.bind("<Configure>", _on_canvas_resize)

        self._auto_selecting = False

    def load(self, video_path: str, duration: float):
        """Load video: fully reset previous media state, show
        first frame, then start background proxy/audio prep."""
        self._buffer_cancel = True

        if self._timer_id:
            try:
                self.canvas.after_cancel(self._timer_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._timer_id = None

        self.playing = False
        self.loaded = False
        self._audio_ready = False
        self._proxy_ready = False
        self._buffer_ready = False
        self._last_frame_idx = -1
        self._current_overlay_text = ''

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.unload()
        except Exception:
            pass

        with self._buffer_lock:
            self._frame_buffer.clear()

        with self._cap_lock:
            if self._cap is not None:
                self._cap.release()
                self._cap = None

        if self._audio_path and os.path.isfile(self._audio_path):
            try:
                os.remove(self._audio_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self._proxy_path and os.path.isfile(self._proxy_path):
            try:
                os.remove(self._proxy_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._audio_path = None
        self._proxy_path = None

        self.video_path = video_path
        self.duration = duration
        self.position = 0.0
        self._audio_start_pos = 0.0
        self._play_start_time = 0.0
        self._buffer_cancel = False
        self.loaded = True

        self._open_capture()
        self.ui["seek_slider"].configure(to=duration)
        self._update_time_display()

        self.canvas.after(200, lambda: self._show_direct(0.0))

        self.state.log(
            "Preparing video preview "
            "(this runs once per video)..."
        )

        threading.Thread(
            target=self._prepare_all,
            daemon=True
        ).start()

    def _open_capture(self):
        """Open source video with OpenCV."""
        with self._cap_lock:
            if self._cap is not None:
                self._cap.release()
            self._cap = cv2.VideoCapture(self.video_path)
            if self._cap.isOpened():
                self._video_fps = (
                    self._cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
                    or 30.0)
            else:
                self.state.log(
                    "[!!] Failed to open video.")

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # BACKGROUND PREPARATION
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _prepare_all(self):
        """Create proxy, extract audio, buffer frames.
        Runs in background thread."""
        # Audio extraction in parallel thread
        audio_thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._extract_audio, daemon=True)
        audio_thread.start()

        # Create proxy video (480p, 15fps, fast codec)
        self._create_proxy()

        if self._proxy_ready and not self._buffer_cancel:
            # Buffer all proxy frames sequentially
            self._buffer_proxy_frames()

        # Wait for audio
        audio_thread.join()

    def _get_proxy_resolution(self):
        """Get proxy resolution from preview dropdown."""
        preview_res = self.ui.get("_preview_res")
        if preview_res:
            res = preview_res.get("value", "720p")
        else:
            res = "720p"

        res_map = {
            "Auto":  (1280, 720),
            "1080p": (1920, 1080),
            "720p":  (1280, 720),
            "480p":  (854, 480),
            "360p":  (640, 360),
        }
        return res_map.get(res, (1280, 720))

    def _create_proxy(self):
        """Use ffmpeg to create a fast-decode proxy
        video at the selected preview resolution."""
        try:
            temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
            proxy_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                prefix="ss_proxy_",
                suffix=".mp4",
                delete=False,
                dir=temp_dir
            )
            proxy_file.close()
            self._proxy_path = proxy_file.name

            try:
                os.remove(self._proxy_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

            pw, ph = self._get_proxy_resolution()

            # Scale filter preserves aspect ratio
            scale_filter = (
                f"scale={pw}:{ph}:"
                f"force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
                f"scale=trunc(iw/2)*2:trunc(ih/2)*2")

            cmd = [
                self.state.ffmpeg_path,
                "-i", self.video_path,
                "-vf", scale_filter,
                "-r", "15",
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-preset", "ultrafast",
                "-crf", "28",
                "-an",
                "-y",
                self._proxy_path
            ]

            self.state.set_progress(
                5, f"Creating {pw}x{ph} preview proxy...")

            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=180,
                creationflags=getattr(
                    subprocess,
                    'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))

            if (result.returncode == 0
                    and os.path.isfile(self._proxy_path)):
                file_mb = (
                    os.path.getsize(self._proxy_path)
                    / (1024 * 1024))
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Proxy ready "
                    f"({pw}x{ph}, {file_mb:.1f} MB)")
                self.state.set_progress(30, "Proxy ready")
                self._proxy_ready = True
            else:
                err = (
                    result.stderr[-1000:].strip()
                    if result.stderr
                    else "unknown"
                )
                self.state.log(
                    f"[!] Proxy creation failed:\n{err}")

        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!] Proxy error: {e}")

    def _buffer_proxy_frames(self):
        """Read all proxy frames sequentially into
        PIL Image list. Sequential read = no seeking
        = extremely fast."""
        try:
            proxy_cap = cv2.VideoCapture(
                self._proxy_path)
            if not proxy_cap.isOpened():
                self.state.log(
                    "[!] Cannot open proxy for "
                    "buffering.")
                return

            self._proxy_fps = (
                proxy_cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
                or 15.0)
            total = int(proxy_cap.get(
                cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))

            self.state.log(
                f"Buffering {total} preview frames...")
            self.state.set_progress(
                35, f"Buffering {total} frames...")

            frames = []
            count = 0

            while True:
                if self._buffer_cancel:
                    break

                ret, frame = proxy_cap.read()
                if not ret:
                    break

                try:
                    frame_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(
                        frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                    pil_img = Image.fromarray(frame_rgb)
                    frames.append(pil_img)
                    count += 1

                    # Update progress every 50 frames
                    if count % 50 == 0 and total > 0:
                        pct = 35 + int(
                            (count / total) * 25)
                        self.state.set_progress(
                            pct,
                            f"Buffering frames... "
                            f"{count}/{total}")
                except Exception:
                    frames.append(None)

            proxy_cap.release()

            with self._buffer_lock:
                self._frame_buffer = frames

            self._buffer_ready = True
            self.state.set_progress(
                60, "Frames buffered")
            self.state.log(
                f"[OK] Buffered {count} frames. "
                f"Playback ready.")

            try:
                on_preview_ready = self.ui.get("on_preview_ready")
                if on_preview_ready:
                    self.canvas.after(0, on_preview_ready)
            except Exception:
                pass

        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!] Buffer error: {e}")

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # FRAME DISPLAY
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _show_frame_at(self, timestamp):
        """Show frame at timestamp from buffer.
        Falls back to direct OpenCV if buffer not ready."""
        if self._buffer_ready:
            idx = int(timestamp * self._proxy_fps)
            idx = max(0, min(idx,
                             len(self._frame_buffer) - 1))

            # Skip if same frame already showing
            if (idx == self._last_frame_idx
                    and self.playing):
                return

            self._last_frame_idx = idx

            with self._buffer_lock:
                if idx < len(self._frame_buffer):
                    pil_img = self._frame_buffer[idx]
                else:
                    pil_img = None

            if pil_img is not None:
                self._display_pil(pil_img)
                return

        # Fallback
        self._show_direct(timestamp)

    def _show_direct(self, timestamp):
        """Read frame directly from source video.
        Used when paused, seeking, or buffer not ready."""
        with self._cap_lock:
            if (self._cap is None
                    or not self._cap.isOpened()):
                return

            self._cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC,
                          timestamp * 1000.0)
            ret, frame = self._cap.read()

        if not ret or frame is None:
            return

        try:
            frame_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(
                frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

            cw = max(1, self.width)
            ch = max(1, self.height)

            # Apply preview resolution cap
            preview_res = self.ui.get("_preview_res")
            if preview_res:
                res = preview_res.get("value", "480p")
                res_map = {
                    "1080p": (1920, 1080),
                    "720p": (1280, 720),
                    "480p": (854, 480),
                    "360p": (640, 360),
                }
                if res in res_map:
                    max_w, max_h = res_map[res]
                    cw = min(cw, max_w)
                    ch = min(ch, max_h)

            fh, fw = frame_rgb.shape[:2]
            scale = min(cw / fw, ch / fh)
            new_w = max(1, int(fw * scale))
            new_h = max(1, int(fh * scale))

            interp = (cv2.INTER_LINEAR
                      if not self.playing
                      else cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
            resized = cv2.resize(
                frame_rgb, (new_w, new_h),
                interpolation=interp)

            pil_img = Image.fromarray(resized)
            self._display_pil(pil_img)

        except Exception:
            pass

    def _display_pil(self, pil_img):
        """Convert PIL image to PhotoImage and display.
        Skips resize during playback for speed."""
        try:
            cw = self.canvas.winfo_width()
            ch = self.canvas.winfo_height()
            if cw < 10:
                cw = self.width
            if ch < 10:
                ch = self.height

            iw, ih = pil_img.size

            # During playback: skip resize if close enough
            # (within 100px). Avoids expensive PIL resize.
            if self.playing:
                if (abs(cw - iw) > 100
                        or abs(ch - ih) > 100):
                    scale = min(cw / iw, ch / ih)
                    new_w = max(1, int(iw * scale))
                    new_h = max(1, int(ih * scale))
                    pil_img = pil_img.resize(
                        (new_w, new_h), Image.NEAREST)
            else:
                # When paused: always fit to canvas
                scale = min(cw / iw, ch / ih)
                new_w = max(1, int(iw * scale))
                new_h = max(1, int(ih * scale))
                if new_w != iw or new_h != ih:
                    pil_img = pil_img.resize(
                        (new_w, new_h), Image.NEAREST)

            photo = ImageTk.PhotoImage(image=pil_img)
            self._photo = photo

            cx = cw // 2
            cy = ch // 2

            if self._canvas_image:
                self.canvas.coords(
                    self._canvas_image, cx, cy)
                self.canvas.itemconfigure(
                    self._canvas_image, image=photo)
            else:
                self._canvas_image = (
                    self.canvas.create_image(
                        cx, cy, image=photo,
                        anchor="center"))

            overlay_fn = self.ui.get(
                "update_subtitle_overlay")
            if overlay_fn:
                txt = self._current_overlay_text
                if txt:
                    overlay_fn(txt)
                else:
                    self.canvas.tag_raise("overlay_bg")
                    self.canvas.tag_raise("overlay")

        except Exception:
            pass

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # PLAYBACK CONTROLS
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def play(self):
        """Start or resume playback."""
        if not self.loaded:
            return

        if not self._buffer_ready:
            self.state.log("[!] Preview still preparing — please wait.")
            return

        if (self.loop_enabled
                and self.loop_start is not None
                and self.loop_end is not None
                and self.loop_end > self.loop_start):
            if (self.position < self.loop_start
                    or self.position >= self.loop_end):
                self.position = self.loop_start

        if self.playing:
            return

        self.playing = True
        self._last_frame_idx = -1

        # Always start playback from the app timeline position.
        # pygame pause/unpause can resume from a stale audio position after
        # seeks, scrubs, audio swaps, or repeated play/pause operations.
        self._audio_start_pos = self.position
        self._play_start_time = _time.time()
        self._paused = False

        if self._audio_ready and self._audio_path:
            try:
                pygame.mixer.music.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                pygame.mixer.music.play(start=self.position)
            except Exception as e:
                self.state.log(f"[!] Audio play error: {e}")
        else:
            self.state.log("[OK] Playing silent preview.")

        self.canvas.after(50, self._tick)

    def pause(self):
        """Pause playback, keep current position."""
        if not self.playing:
            return

        self._sync_position()
        self.playing = False
        self._paused = True

        if self._timer_id:
            try:
                self.canvas.after_cancel(
                    self._timer_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._timer_id = None

        if self._audio_ready and self._audio_path:
            try:
                pygame.mixer.music.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()

        # High-quality frame at pause point
        self._show_direct(self.position)

    def stop(self):
        """Stop playback and reset to beginning."""
        self.playing = False
        if self._timer_id:
            try:
                self.canvas.after_cancel(
                    self._timer_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._timer_id = None

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass

        self.position = 0.0
        self._audio_start_pos = 0.0
        self._play_start_time = 0.0
        self._last_frame_idx = -1
        self._current_overlay_text = ''

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()
        self._show_frame_at(0.0)

    def seek(self, seconds: float):
        """Jump to a specific time."""
        if not self.loaded:
            return

        was_playing = self.playing

        # Seeking invalidates any paused audio state. The next play must start
        # from self.position, not resume an old pygame pause point.
        self._paused = False
        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if was_playing:
            self.playing = False
            if self._timer_id:
                try:
                    self.canvas.after_cancel(self._timer_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._timer_id = None
            pass

        self.position = max(0.0, min(seconds, self.duration))
        self._audio_start_pos = self.position
        self._play_start_time = _time.time()
        self._paused = False
        self._last_frame_idx = -1

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

        if self._buffer_ready:
            self._show_frame_at(self.position)
        else:
            self._show_direct(self.position)

        if was_playing:
            self.play()

    def get_position(self) -> float:
        return self.position

    def set_loop(self, enabled: bool, start: float | None = None,
                 end: float | None = None):
        self.loop_enabled = bool(enabled)
        self.loop_start = start
        self.loop_end = end

    def clear_loop(self):
        self.loop_enabled = False
        self.loop_start = None
        self.loop_end = None

    def get_frame_duration(self) -> float:
        """Duration of one frame in seconds."""
        if self._video_fps > 0:
            return 1.0 / self._video_fps
        return 1.0 / 30.0

    def step_frames(self, count: int):
        """Step forward/backward by N frames.
        Only when paused."""
        if not self.loaded or self.playing:
            return

        frame_dur = self.get_frame_duration()
        new_pos = self.position + (count * frame_dur)
        new_pos = max(
            0.0, min(new_pos, self.duration))
        self.position = new_pos

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()
        self._show_direct(self.position)

    def reset(self):
        """Reset player for a new video."""
        self._buffer_cancel = True
        self.stop()
        self.loaded = False
        self._audio_ready = False
        self._proxy_ready = False
        self._buffer_ready = False
        self._last_frame_idx = -1
        self._current_overlay_text = ''
        self._photo = None
        self._canvas_image = None

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.unload()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self._audio_path and os.path.isfile(self._audio_path):
            try:
                os.remove(self._audio_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._audio_path = None

        with self._buffer_lock:
            self._frame_buffer.clear()

        with self._cap_lock:
            if self._cap is not None:
                self._cap.release()
                self._cap = None

        # Clean up proxy
        if (self._proxy_path
                and os.path.isfile(self._proxy_path)):
            try:
                os.remove(self._proxy_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        self.canvas.delete("all")
        self.canvas.create_text(
            270, 150,
            text="Video preview will appear here",
            fill="#555555", font=("Arial", 10),
            tags="placeholder")

    def cleanup(self):
        """Clean up all resources."""
        self._buffer_cancel = True

        try:
            pygame.mixer.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            pygame.mixer.quit()
        except Exception:
            pass

        with self._buffer_lock:
            self._frame_buffer.clear()

        with self._cap_lock:
            if self._cap is not None:
                self._cap.release()
                self._cap = None

        for path in [self._audio_path,
                     self._proxy_path]:
            if path and os.path.isfile(path):
                try:
                    os.remove(path)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # AUDIO EXTRACTION
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _extract_audio(self):
        """Extract audio as MP3. Background thread."""
        try:
            temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
            audio_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                prefix="subtitle_studio_audio_",
                suffix=".wav",
                delete=False,
                dir=temp_dir
            )
            audio_file.close()
            self._audio_path = audio_file.name

            try:
                os.remove(self._audio_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

            cmd = [
                self.state.ffmpeg_path,
                "-i", self.video_path,
                "-vn",
                "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
                "-ar", "44100",
                "-ac", "2",
                "-y",
                self._audio_path
            ]

            self.state.log(
                f"Extracting audio...")
            self.state.set_progress(
                65, "Extracting audio...")

            result = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=120,
                creationflags=getattr(
                    subprocess,
                    'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))

            if result.returncode != 0:
                err = (
                    result.stderr.strip()
                    if result.stderr
                    else "unknown"
                )
                self.state.log(
                    f"[!!] Audio failed:\n{err}")
                return

            if os.path.isfile(self._audio_path):
                file_mb = (
                    os.path.getsize(self._audio_path)
                    / (1024 * 1024))
                if file_mb < 0.001:
                    self.state.log(
                        "[!!] Audio file empty.")
                    return

                pygame.mixer.music.load(
                    self._audio_path)
                self._audio_ready = True
                self.state.set_progress(
                    85, "Audio ready")
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Audio ready "
                    f"({file_mb:.1f} MB).")
            else:
                self.state.log(
                    "[!!] Audio file not found.")

        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!!] Audio error: {e}")

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # POSITION SYNC
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _sync_position(self):
        """Advance playback position using the app timeline.

        pygame.mixer.music.get_pos() is relative to the current audio play call
        and can become unreliable after reloads, seeks, pauses, or swapping from
        source audio to enhanced audio. Subtitle timing must stay locked to the
        video timeline, so wall-clock playback time is the master clock.
        """
        if not self.playing:
            return

        elapsed = _time.time() - self._play_start_time
        self.position = self._audio_start_pos + elapsed

        if self.position >= self.duration:
            self.position = self.duration
            self.playing = False
            if self._audio_ready and self._audio_path:
                try:
                    pygame.mixer.music.stop()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # PLAYBACK TICK
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _tick(self):
        """Update UI and frame during playback."""
        if not self.playing:
            return

        self._sync_position()

        if (self.playing
                and self.loop_enabled
                and self.loop_start is not None
                and self.loop_end is not None
                and self.loop_end > self.loop_start
                and self.position >= self.loop_end):
            self.seek(self.loop_start)
            self.canvas.after(10, self.play)
            return

        if not self.playing:
            self._update_time_display()
            self.state.log("Playback reached end.")
            return

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

        # Show buffered frame
        self._show_frame_at(self.position)

        self._timer_id = self.canvas.after(
            self.TICK_MS, self._tick)

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # UI UPDATES
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _update_time_display(self):
        def _short_time(seconds):
            try:
                total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
            except Exception:
                total_seconds = 0
            hours = total_seconds // 3600
            minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
            secs = total_seconds % 60
            return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"

        current = _short_time(self.position)
        total = _short_time(self.duration)
        self.ui["time_label"].configure(text=current)
        if "total_dur_label" in self.ui:
            self.ui["total_dur_label"].configure(text=total)

    def _update_slider(self):
        self.ui["seek_slider"].set(self.position)

    def _highlight_cue(self):
        """Auto-highlight active cue and update
        subtitle overlay."""
        if not self.state.cues:
            return

        listbox = self.ui["sub_listbox"]
        active_label = self.ui["active_cue_label"]

        active_idx = None
        for i, cue in enumerate(self.state.cues):
            if cue.start <= self.position <= cue.end:
                active_idx = i
                break

        overlay_fn = self.ui.get(
            "update_subtitle_overlay")
        if overlay_fn:
            if active_idx is not None:
                self._current_overlay_text = (
                    self.state.cues[active_idx].text)
                overlay_fn(
                    self._current_overlay_text)
            else:
                self._current_overlay_text = ''
                overlay_fn("")

        if active_idx is None:
            return

        self._auto_selecting = True
        try:
            listbox.selection_clear(0, "end")
            listbox.selection_set(active_idx)
            listbox.see(active_idx)

            cue = self.state.cues[active_idx]

            def _short_time(seconds):
                try:
                    total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
                except Exception:
                    total_seconds = 0
                hours = total_seconds // 3600
                minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
                secs = total_seconds % 60
                return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"

            active_label.configure(
                text=(
                    f"[{cue.index}] "
                    f"{_short_time(cue.start)}"
                    f" → "
                    f"{_short_time(cue.end)}"
                    f"\n{cue.text}"))
        finally:
            self._auto_selecting = False


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: core/player_mpv.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Subtitle Studio v5 — MPV/libmpv playback backend.

This backend uses python-mpv + libmpv for smooth A/V-synced preview.
It intentionally mimics the subset of FramePlayer used by tab_edit.py.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path


def diagnose_mpv_environment(app_dir=None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Return whether python-mpv/libmpv appears available, plus a human message."""
    try:
        import os
        from pathlib import Path

        root = Path(app_dir or Path.cwd())
        mpv_dir = root / "bin" / "windows"

        candidates = [
            mpv_dir / "mpv-2.dll",
            mpv_dir / "mpv-1.dll",
            mpv_dir / "libmpv-2.dll",
        ]

        found_dll = None
        for candidate in candidates:
            if candidate.is_file():
                found_dll = candidate
                break

        if mpv_dir.is_dir():
            try:
                os.add_dll_directory(str(mpv_dir))
            except Exception:
                pass

            os.environ["PATH"] = str(mpv_dir) + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", "")

        try:
            import mpv  # noqa: F401
        except Exception as import_error:
            if found_dll:
                return (
                    False,
                    "python-mpv/libmpv could not be loaded even though "
                    f"{found_dll.name} exists. "
                    f"Details: {import_error}",
                )

            return (
                False,
                "python-mpv/libmpv is unavailable. Expected python-mpv package "
                "and one of: bin/windows/mpv-2.dll, mpv-1.dll, libmpv-2.dll. "
                f"Details: {import_error}",
            )

        if found_dll:
            return True, f"python-mpv available; using {found_dll.name}."

        return (
            True,
            "python-mpv imported successfully. libmpv DLL was resolved from system PATH.",
        )

    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"MPV diagnostics failed: {e}"

class MpvPlayer:
    """
    MPV-backed preview player.

    Public interface intentionally mirrors core.player.FramePlayer where possible:
        load(video_path, duration)
        play()
        pause()
        stop()
        seek(seconds)
        get_position()
        get_frame_duration()
        step_frames(count)
        set_loop(enabled, start=None, end=None)
        clear_loop()
        reset()
        destroy()
    """

    TICK_MS = 100

    def __init__(self, canvas, state, ui: dict):
        self.canvas = canvas
        self.state = state
        self.ui = ui

        self.video_path: str | None = None
        self.duration: float = 0.0
        self.position: float = 0.0
        self.playing: bool = False
        self.loaded: bool = False

        # Compatibility flags expected by tab_edit.py.
        self._proxy_ready: bool = False
        self._buffer_ready: bool = False
        self._buffer_cancel: bool = False
        self._audio_ready: bool = False
        self._audio_path: str | None = None
        self._external_audio_path: str | None = None

        # Loop playback.
        self.loop_enabled: bool = False
        self.loop_start: float | None = None
        self.loop_end: float | None = None

        # Approx frame duration. Updated from state.video_info when possible.
        self._video_fps: float = 30.0

        self._mpv = None
        self._mpv_module = None
        self._timer_id = None
        self._command_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._closed = False

        self._current_overlay_text = ""
        self._auto_selecting = False

        self._status_text_id = None
        self._status_resize_bound = False

        self._ensure_mpv()

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # MPV bootstrap
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _ensure_mpv(self):
        """Import python-mpv after making bundled DLLs discoverable."""
        if self._mpv_module is not None:
            return self._mpv_module

        app_dir = Path(getattr(self.state, "app_dir", Path.cwd()))
        mpv_dir = app_dir / "bin" / "windows"

        if mpv_dir.is_dir():
            try:
                os.add_dll_directory(str(mpv_dir))
            except Exception:
                pass

            os.environ["PATH"] = (
                str(mpv_dir)
                + os.pathsep
                + os.environ.get("PATH", "")
            )

        import mpv  # python-mpv

        self._mpv_module = mpv
        return mpv

    def _create_player(self):
        """Create the underlying libmpv instance."""
        if self._mpv is not None:
            return

        mpv = self._ensure_mpv()

        try:
            self.canvas.update_idletasks()
        except Exception:
            pass

        wid = str(self.canvas.winfo_id())

        self._mpv = mpv.MPV(
            wid=wid,
            idle=True,
            keep_open=True,
            osc=False,
            input_default_bindings=False,
            input_vo_keyboard=False,
            vo="gpu-next",
            gpu_api="d3d11",
            hwdec="auto-safe",

            # Subtitle Studio draws active cue text through MPV OSD
            # because MPV owns the embedded video surface.
            osd_align_x="center",
            osd_align_y="bottom",
            osd_margin_y=42,
            osd_font_size=28,
            osd_color="#FFFFFFFF",
            osd_border_color="#CC000000",
            osd_border_size=3,

            log_handler=self._mpv_log,
            loglevel="warn",
        )

        self.state.log("[OK] MPV preview backend started.")

    def _mpv_log(self, level, component, message):
        msg = (message or "").strip()
        if not msg:
            return

        # Avoid noisy audio-device warnings unless they matter later.
        if "Under-run" in msg:
            return

        try:
            self.state.log(f"[MPV:{level}:{component}] {msg}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _run_mpv_async(self, label: str, fn):
        """Run MPV operations off the Tk thread."""
        def worker():
            try:
                with self._command_lock:
                    fn()
            except Exception as e:
                try:
                    self.canvas.after(
                        0,
                        lambda: self.state.log(f"[!] MPV {label} failed: {e}")
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Centered preview status
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _center_canvas_status(self, event=None):
        if not self._status_text_id:
            return

        try:
            self.canvas.update_idletasks()
            cw = max(1, self.canvas.winfo_width())
            ch = max(1, self.canvas.winfo_height())
            self.canvas.coords(self._status_text_id, cw // 2, ch // 2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _show_canvas_status(self, text: str):
        """Show centered status text on the Tk canvas before MPV paints."""
        try:
            self.canvas.delete("placeholder")
            self.canvas.delete("mpv_status")
        except Exception:
            pass

        if not text:
            self._status_text_id = None
            return

        try:
            self.canvas.update_idletasks()
            cw = max(1, self.canvas.winfo_width())
            ch = max(1, self.canvas.winfo_height())

            self._status_text_id = self.canvas.create_text(
                cw // 2,
                ch // 2,
                text=text,
                fill="#BBBBBB",
                font=("Arial", 13, "bold"),
                anchor="center",
                justify="center",
                tags=("mpv_status",),
            )

            if not self._status_resize_bound:
                self.canvas.bind("<Configure>", self._center_canvas_status, add="+")
                self._status_resize_bound = True

        except Exception:
            pass

    def _clear_canvas_status(self):
        try:
            self.canvas.delete("mpv_status")
        except Exception:
            pass
        self._status_text_id = None

    def show_status_text(self, text: str, duration_ms: int = 2500, center: bool = True):
        """Show status through MPV OSD when MPV owns the preview surface."""
        if self._mpv is None:
            self._show_canvas_status(text)
            return

        text = text or ""

        def worker():
            try:
                if center:
                    self._mpv.osd_align_y = "center"
                    self._mpv.osd_margin_y = 0
                else:
                    self._mpv.osd_align_y = "bottom"
                    self._mpv.osd_margin_y = 42

                self._mpv.command("show-text", text, max(1, int(duration_ms)))
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._run_mpv_async("status text", worker)
    
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Load / reset
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def load(self, video_path: str, duration: float = 0.0):
        """Load a video into MPV."""
        self.reset(terminate=False)

        self._closed = False
        self.video_path = video_path
        self.duration = float(duration or 0.0)
        self.position = 0.0
        self.playing = False
        self.loaded = True

        self._proxy_ready = False
        self._buffer_ready = False
        self._audio_ready = False
        self._audio_path = None
        self._external_audio_path = None

        try:
            info = getattr(self.state, "video_info", None) or {}
            fps = float(info.get("fps") or info.get("frame_rate") or 30.0)
            if fps > 0:
                self._video_fps = fps
        except Exception:
            self._video_fps = 30.0

        self._show_canvas_status("Loading video...")
        self._create_player()
        self.show_status_text("Loading video...", 3000, center=True)

        try:
            self.ui["seek_slider"].configure(to=max(1.0, self.duration))
            self.ui["seek_slider"].set(0.0)
        except Exception:
            pass

        self._update_time_display()
        self.state.log("Loading video into MPV preview...")

        def worker():
            self._mpv.pause = True
            self._mpv.volume = 80
            self._mpv.mute = False
            self._mpv.play(video_path)

            # Wait briefly for MPV duration if app duration was missing.
            mpv_duration = self.duration
            for _ in range(30):
                time.sleep(0.1)
                try:
                    mpv_duration = float(self._mpv.duration or mpv_duration or 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                if mpv_duration > 0:
                    break

            def finish():
                self.duration = float(mpv_duration or self.duration or 0.0)
                self.position = 0.0
                self.playing = False

                self._proxy_ready = True
                self._buffer_ready = True
                self._audio_ready = True

                try:
                    self.ui["seek_slider"].configure(to=max(1.0, self.duration))
                    self.ui["seek_slider"].set(0.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass

                self._clear_canvas_status()
                self._update_time_display()
                self._update_slider()
                self._start_tick()

                self.state.log("[OK] MPV playback ready.")

                on_ready = self.ui.get("on_preview_ready")
                if on_ready:
                    on_ready()

            self.canvas.after(0, finish)

        self._run_mpv_async("load", worker)

    def reset(self, terminate: bool = False):
        """Reset playback state."""
        self._cancel_tick()

        self.playing = False
        self.loaded = False
        self.position = 0.0
        self.video_path = None

        self._proxy_ready = False
        self._buffer_ready = False
        self._audio_ready = False
        self._audio_path = None
        self._external_audio_path = None

        try:
            if self._mpv is not None:
                self._mpv.pause = True
                self._mpv.command("stop")
        except Exception:
            pass

        if terminate:
            self.destroy()

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()

    def destroy(self):
        self._closed = True
        self._cancel_tick()

        try:
            if self._mpv is not None:
                self._mpv.terminate()
        except Exception:
            pass

        self._mpv = None

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Playback controls
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def play(self):
        if not self.loaded or self._mpv is None:
            return

        def worker():
            self._mpv.pause = False

        self.playing = True
        self._run_mpv_async("play", worker)
        self._start_tick()

    def pause(self):
        if not self.loaded or self._mpv is None:
            return

        def worker():
            self._mpv.pause = True

        self.playing = False
        self._sync_position_from_mpv()
        self._run_mpv_async("pause", worker)
        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

    def stop(self):
        if not self.loaded or self._mpv is None:
            return

        self.playing = False

        def worker():
            self._mpv.pause = True
            self._mpv.command("seek", 0.0, "absolute", "exact")

        self.position = 0.0
        self._run_mpv_async("stop", worker)
        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

    def seek(self, seconds: float):
        if not self.loaded or self._mpv is None:
            return

        try:
            seconds = max(0.0, min(float(seconds), self.duration or float(seconds)))
        except Exception:
            seconds = 0.0

        self.position = seconds

        def worker():
            self._mpv.command("seek", seconds, "absolute", "exact")

        self._run_mpv_async("seek", worker)
        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

    def get_position(self) -> float:
        self._sync_position_from_mpv()
        return float(self.position or 0.0)

    def get_frame_duration(self) -> float:
        fps = self._video_fps or 30.0
        return 1.0 / fps if fps > 0 else 1.0 / 30.0

    def set_external_audio(self, audio_path: str | None):
        """Use an external WAV/audio file as MPV's selected audio track.

        This lets Subtitle Studio keep video playback in MPV while switching
        between source and enhanced audio later.
        """
        if self._mpv is None or not self.loaded:
            return

        if not audio_path:
            return

        audio_path = os.path.abspath(str(audio_path))
        if not os.path.isfile(audio_path):
            try:
                self.state.log(f"[!] MPV external audio not found: {audio_path}")
            except Exception:
                pass
            return

        self._audio_path = audio_path
        self._audio_ready = True
        self._external_audio_path = audio_path

        try:
            current_pos = self.get_position()
        except Exception:
            current_pos = self.position or 0.0

        was_playing = bool(self.playing)

        def worker():
            # Add and select the external audio track. MPV will switch away
            # from the video's embedded audio when "select" is used.
            self._mpv.command(
                "audio-add",
                audio_path,
                "select",
                "Subtitle Studio Source Audio",
            )

            # Keep timeline stable after audio track switch.
            try:
                self._mpv.command("seek", float(current_pos), "absolute", "exact")
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.pause = not was_playing
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] MPV external audio selected: {os.path.basename(audio_path)}"
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._run_mpv_async("external audio", worker)
    
    def set_volume(self, value: float):
        """Set MPV volume. Accepts 0.0–1.0 or 0–100."""
        if self._mpv is None:
            return

        try:
            volume = float(value)
        except Exception:
            return

        if volume <= 1.0:
            volume *= 100.0

        volume = max(0.0, min(100.0, volume))

        def worker():
            self._mpv.volume = volume

        self._run_mpv_async("set volume", worker)

    def get_volume(self) -> float:
        """Return MPV volume as 0.0–1.0."""
        if self._mpv is None:
            return 0.8

        try:
            return max(0.0, min(1.0, float(self._mpv.volume or 0.0) / 100.0))
        except Exception:
            return 0.8

    def set_muted(self, muted: bool):
        if self._mpv is None:
            return

        def worker():
            self._mpv.mute = bool(muted)

        self._run_mpv_async("mute", worker)

    def get_muted(self) -> bool:
        if self._mpv is None:
            return False

        try:
            return bool(self._mpv.mute)
        except Exception:
            return False
    
    def step_frames(self, count: int):
        if not self.loaded or self._mpv is None:
            return

        self.playing = False

        def worker():
            self._mpv.pause = True

            steps = abs(int(count))
            command = "frame-step" if count >= 0 else "frame-back-step"
            for _ in range(max(1, steps)):
                self._mpv.command(command)

            # Give MPV a moment to update time-pos.
            time.sleep(0.05)
            try:
                pos = float(self._mpv.time_pos or self.position or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                pos = self.position

            self.canvas.after(0, lambda: self._after_step(pos))

        self._run_mpv_async("frame step", worker)

    def _after_step(self, pos: float):
        self.position = max(0.0, float(pos or 0.0))
        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Loop
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def set_loop(self, enabled: bool, start=None, end=None):
        self.loop_enabled = bool(enabled)
        self.loop_start = start
        self.loop_end = end

    def clear_loop(self):
        self.loop_enabled = False
        self.loop_start = None
        self.loop_end = None

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Legacy compatibility no-ops
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _create_proxy(self):
        self._proxy_ready = True

    def _buffer_proxy_frames(self):
        self._buffer_ready = True

    def _extract_audio(self):
        # MPV plays the media audio directly for now.
        self._audio_ready = True

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Tick / UI sync
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _start_tick(self):
        if self._timer_id is None and not self._closed:
            self._timer_id = self.canvas.after(self.TICK_MS, self._tick)

    def _cancel_tick(self):
        if self._timer_id:
            try:
                self.canvas.after_cancel(self._timer_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._timer_id = None

    def _tick(self):
        self._timer_id = None

        if self._closed or not self.loaded:
            return

        self._sync_position_from_mpv()

        if (
            self.playing
            and self.loop_enabled
            and self.loop_start is not None
            and self.loop_end is not None
            and self.loop_end > self.loop_start
            and self.position >= self.loop_end
        ):
            self.seek(self.loop_start)

        self._update_time_display()
        self._update_slider()
        self._highlight_cue()

        self._timer_id = self.canvas.after(self.TICK_MS, self._tick)

    def _sync_position_from_mpv(self):
        if self._mpv is None:
            return

        try:
            pos = self._mpv.time_pos
            if pos is not None:
                self.position = max(0.0, float(pos))
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            dur = self._mpv.duration
            if dur:
                self.duration = max(0.0, float(dur))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _update_time_display(self):
        def _short_time(seconds):
            try:
                total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
            except Exception:
                total_seconds = 0
            hours = total_seconds // 3600
            minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
            secs = total_seconds % 60
            return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"

        try:
            self.ui["time_label"].configure(text=_short_time(self.position))
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            self.ui["total_dur_label"].configure(text=_short_time(self.duration))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _update_slider(self):
        try:
            self.ui["seek_slider"].set(self.position)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _show_mpv_subtitle_text(
        self,
        text: str,
        cue_end: float | None = None,
        alignment: str | None = None,
    ):
        """Show active subtitle cue using MPV OSD.

        Keep this defensive: some MPV builds reject certain OSD properties.
        Subtitle display must never fail just because alignment commands fail.
        """
        if self._mpv is None:
            return

        text = text or ""

        if alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
            alignment = "center"

        align_x = {
            "left": "left",
            "center": "center",
            "right": "right",
        }[alignment]

        if not text:
            duration_ms = 1
        else:
            try:
                remaining = max(0.5, float(cue_end or 0.0) - float(self.position or 0.0))
                duration_ms = int(min(10000, max(800, remaining * 1000)))
            except Exception:
                duration_ms = 2000

        def worker():
            try:
                self._mpv.command("show-text", "", 1)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.command("set", "osd-align-x", align_x)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.command("set", "osd-align-y", "bottom")
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.command("set", "osd-margin-x", "64")
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.command("set", "osd-margin-y", "42")
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                self._mpv.command("show-text", text, duration_ms)
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._run_mpv_async("subtitle overlay", worker)
    
    def _highlight_cue(self):
        if not getattr(self.state, "cues", None):
            if self._current_overlay_text:
                self._current_overlay_text = ""

                overlay_fn = self.ui.get("update_subtitle_overlay")
                if overlay_fn:
                    try:
                        overlay_fn("")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                self._show_mpv_subtitle_text("", None)
            return

        active_idx = None
        for i, cue in enumerate(self.state.cues):
            if cue.start <= self.position <= cue.end:
                active_idx = i
                break

        overlay_fn = self.ui.get("update_subtitle_overlay")

        if active_idx is not None:
            cue = self.state.cues[active_idx]
            text = cue.text
            alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
            if alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
                alignment = "center"
                cue.alignment = "center"

            overlay_key = f"{alignment}:{text}"

            if overlay_key != self._current_overlay_text:
                self._current_overlay_text = overlay_key

                # Tk canvas overlay is kept for compatibility, but MPV owns
                # the preview surface, so MPV OSD is the visible overlay.
                if overlay_fn:
                    try:
                        overlay_fn(text, alignment)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                self._show_mpv_subtitle_text(text, cue.end, alignment)

            try:
                active_label = self.ui["active_cue_label"]
                active_label.configure(
                    text=(
                        f"[{cue.index}] "
                        f"{self._short_time(cue.start)} → "
                        f"{self._short_time(cue.end)}\n"
                        f"{cue.text}"
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

            return

        if self._current_overlay_text:
            self._current_overlay_text = ""

            if overlay_fn:
                try:
                    overlay_fn("")
                except Exception:
                    pass

            self._show_mpv_subtitle_text("", None, "center")

    @staticmethod
    def _short_time(seconds):
        try:
            total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
        except Exception:
            total_seconds = 0
        hours = total_seconds // 3600
        minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
        secs = total_seconds % 60
        return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: core/qc.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Subtitle Studio v4.0 — Quality Check Engine.
Detects issues in subtitle cues and provides auto-fixes.
"""

from typing import List, Tuple, Optional


class Issue:
    """A single QC issue found in a cue."""
    __slots__ = ("cue_index", "type", "severity",
                 "message", "auto_fixable")

    def __init__(self, cue_index: int, issue_type: str,
                 severity: str, message: str,
                 auto_fixable: bool = False):
        self.cue_index = cue_index
        self.type = issue_type
        self.severity = severity
        self.message = message
        self.auto_fixable = auto_fixable

    def __repr__(self):
        return (f"Issue(cue={self.cue_index}, "
                f"{self.type}, {self.severity})")


# ── Thresholds ──
MAX_CPS = 25           # characters per second
MAX_LINE_LENGTH = 42   # characters per line
MAX_LINES = 2          # lines per cue
MIN_DURATION = 0.5     # seconds
MAX_DURATION = 10.0    # seconds
MIN_GAP = 0.04         # seconds between cues (1 frame @25fps)


def run_qc(cues) -> List[Issue]:
    """
    Run all QC checks on a list of Cue objects.
    Returns list of Issue objects.
    """
    issues = []

    for i, cue in enumerate(cues):
        # ── Empty text ──
        if not cue.text or not cue.text.strip():
            issues.append(Issue(
                cue.index, "EMPTY", "critical",
                f"Cue {cue.index}: empty text",
                auto_fixable=True))
            continue

        # ── Negative or zero duration ──
        duration = cue.end - cue.start
        if duration <= 0:
            issues.append(Issue(
                cue.index, "BAD_DURATION", "critical",
                f"Cue {cue.index}: end before start "
                f"({duration:.3f}s)",
                auto_fixable=True))
            continue

        # ── Too short ──
        if duration < MIN_DURATION:
            issues.append(Issue(
                cue.index, "TOO_SHORT", "warning",
                f"Cue {cue.index}: too short "
                f"({duration:.2f}s < {MIN_DURATION}s)",
                auto_fixable=False))

        # ── Too long ──
        if duration > MAX_DURATION:
            issues.append(Issue(
                cue.index, "TOO_LONG", "warning",
                f"Cue {cue.index}: too long "
                f"({duration:.1f}s > {MAX_DURATION}s)",
                auto_fixable=False))

        # ── Characters per second ──
        clean_text = cue.text.replace("\n", " ").strip()
        char_count = len(clean_text)
        if duration > 0:
            cps = char_count / duration
            if cps > MAX_CPS:
                issues.append(Issue(
                    cue.index, "HIGH_CPS", "warning",
                    f"Cue {cue.index}: {cps:.1f} chars/sec "
                    f"(max {MAX_CPS})",
                    auto_fixable=False))

        # ── Line count ──
        lines = cue.text.split("\n")
        if len(lines) > MAX_LINES:
            issues.append(Issue(
                cue.index, "TOO_MANY_LINES", "warning",
                f"Cue {cue.index}: {len(lines)} lines "
                f"(max {MAX_LINES})",
                auto_fixable=False))

        # ── Line length ──
        for line_num, line in enumerate(lines):
            if len(line.strip()) > MAX_LINE_LENGTH:
                issues.append(Issue(
                    cue.index, "LINE_TOO_LONG", "info",
                    f"Cue {cue.index} line {line_num+1}: "
                    f"{len(line.strip())} chars "
                    f"(max {MAX_LINE_LENGTH})",
                    auto_fixable=False))

        # ── Overlap with next cue ──
        if i < len(cues) - 1:
            nxt = cues[i + 1]
            if cue.end > nxt.start:
                overlap = cue.end - nxt.start
                issues.append(Issue(
                    cue.index, "OVERLAP", "critical",
                    f"Cue {cue.index}-{nxt.index}: "
                    f"overlap {overlap:.3f}s",
                    auto_fixable=True))

        # ── Gap too small ──
        if i < len(cues) - 1:
            nxt = cues[i + 1]
            gap = nxt.start - cue.end
            if 0 < gap < MIN_GAP:
                issues.append(Issue(
                    cue.index, "SMALL_GAP", "info",
                    f"Cue {cue.index}-{nxt.index}: "
                    f"gap {gap:.3f}s "
                    f"(min {MIN_GAP}s)",
                    auto_fixable=False))

    return issues


def run_fixes(cues, issues: List[Issue]) -> Tuple[list, list]:
    """
    Auto-fix fixable issues.
    Returns: (fixed_cues, log_messages)
    """
    log = []

    # Work on copies
    from core.state import Cue
    fixed = [Cue(c.index, c.start, c.end, c.text)
             for c in cues]

    # Fix overlaps
    for issue in issues:
        if issue.type == "OVERLAP" and issue.auto_fixable:
            idx = issue.cue_index - 1
            if 0 <= idx < len(fixed) - 1:
                curr = fixed[idx]
                nxt = fixed[idx + 1]
                if curr.end > nxt.start:
                    new_end = nxt.start - 0.001
                    if new_end > curr.start:
                        curr.end = new_end
                        log.append(
                            f"Fixed overlap: cue {curr.index} "
                            f"end trimmed")

    # Fix negative durations
    for issue in issues:
        if issue.type == "BAD_DURATION" and issue.auto_fixable:
            idx = issue.cue_index - 1
            if 0 <= idx < len(fixed):
                cue = fixed[idx]
                if cue.end <= cue.start:
                    cue.end = cue.start + 0.5
                    log.append(
                        f"Fixed duration: cue {cue.index} "
                        f"end set to start + 0.5s")

    # Remove empty cues
    empty_indices = set()
    for issue in issues:
        if issue.type == "EMPTY" and issue.auto_fixable:
            empty_indices.add(issue.cue_index - 1)

    if empty_indices:
        fixed = [c for i, c in enumerate(fixed)
                 if i not in empty_indices]
        log.append(
            f"Removed {len(empty_indices)} empty cue(s)")

    # Reindex
    for i, cue in enumerate(fixed):
        cue.index = i + 1

    return fixed, log


def get_summary(issues: List[Issue]) -> dict:
    """
    Get a summary of QC issues.
    Returns dict with counts by type and severity.
    """
    summary = {
        "total": len(issues),
        "critical": 0,
        "warning": 0,
        "info": 0,
        "auto_fixable": 0,
        "by_type": {},
    }

    for issue in issues:
        # Count by severity
        sev = issue.severity
        if sev in summary:
            summary[sev] += 1

        # Count auto-fixable
        if issue.auto_fixable:
            summary["auto_fixable"] += 1

        # Count by type
        t = issue.type
        summary["by_type"][t] = (
            summary["by_type"].get(t, 0) + 1)

    return summary

FILE: core/state.py
