"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Shared application state.
Single source of truth for all tabs.
"""

import os
import sys
import datetime
import json
import queue
import shutil
import tempfile


class Cue:
    """One subtitle cue with index, start/end times, text, and alignment."""
    __slots__ = ("index", "start", "end", "text", "alignment")

    def __init__(
        self,
        index: int,
        start: float,
        end: float,
        text: str,
        alignment: str = "center",
    ):
        self.index = index
        self.start = start
        self.end = end
        self.text = text
        self.alignment = alignment if alignment in ("left", "center", "right") else "center"

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"Cue({self.index}, {self.start:.3f}-{self.end:.3f}, "
            f"{self.text[:30]!r})"
        )


class Section:
    """A marked IN/OUT section on the timeline."""
    __slots__ = ("start", "end", "label")

    def __init__(self, start: float, end: float, label: str = ""):
        self.start = start
        self.end = end
        self.label = label

    @property
    def duration(self) -> float:
        return self.end - self.start


def _locate_exe(name, app_dir):
    """Find ffmpeg/ffprobe — check platform bin folder,
    app folder, then system PATH."""
    import platform

    system = platform.system()
    ext = ".exe" if system == "Windows" else ""

    platform_dir = {
        "Windows": "windows",
        "Darwin": "macos",
        "Linux": "linux",
    }.get(system, "")

    # 1. Check frozen (PyInstaller) directory
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        exe_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)

        candidate = os.path.join(exe_dir, "bin", platform_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
            return candidate

        candidate = os.path.join(exe_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
            return candidate

    # 2. Check bin/platform/ in project
    if platform_dir:
        candidate = os.path.join(app_dir, "bin", platform_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
            return candidate

        candidate = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "bin", platform_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
            return candidate

    # 3. Check project root (legacy layout)
    candidate = os.path.join(app_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
    if os.path.isfile(candidate):
        return candidate

    candidate = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), f"{name}{ext}")
    if os.path.isfile(candidate):
        return candidate

    # 4. Check system PATH
    found = shutil.which(name)
    if found:
        return found

    # 5. Fallback — return expected path for error message
    if platform_dir:
        return os.path.join(app_dir, "bin", platform_dir, f"{name}{ext}")
    return os.path.join(app_dir, f"{name}{ext}")


class AppState:
    """
    Central state object. Created once in main.py, passed to every tab.

    Attributes:
        video_path:      Loaded video file path (or None)
        sub_path:        Loaded subtitle file path (or None)
        video_info:      Dict from ffprobe
        cues:            List[Cue] — all subtitle cues in memory
        original_cues:   List[Cue] — snapshot before edits
        sections:        List[Section] — marked IN/OUT sections
        language:        "us" or "uk" — user preference from Import
        session_log:     List[str] — all operations (imports, exports, etc.)
        subtitle_log:    List[str] — subtitle text edits only
        msg_queue:       Queue for thread-safe log messages
        ffmpeg_path:     Path to ffmpeg.exe
        ffprobe_path:    Path to ffprobe.exe

        v5 audio workflow:
        source_audio_path:         Extracted original audio from loaded video
        enhanced_audio_path:       Optional enhanced audio created from source
        active_audio_path:         Audio currently used for playback/waveform/editing
        export_use_enhanced_audio: If True, export uses enhanced audio when available
    """

    _progress_fn = None
    _progress_status_fn = None

    def __init__(self, app_dir: str):
        # PyInstaller/Nuitka: exe's folder
        if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
            self.app_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
        else:
            self.app_dir = app_dir

        # Locate ffmpeg/ffprobe
        self.ffmpeg_path = _locate_exe("ffmpeg", self.app_dir)
        self.ffprobe_path = _locate_exe("ffprobe", self.app_dir)

        # File paths
        self.video_path: str | None = None
        self.sub_path: str | None = None

        # Video metadata from ffprobe
        self.video_info: dict = {}

        # Subtitle data
        self.cues: list[Cue] = []
        self.original_cues: list[Cue] = []

        # Marked sections
        self.sections: list[Section] = []

        # Language preference ("us" or "uk")
        self.language: str = "us"

        # Subtitle overlay/export alignment: "left", "center", or "right"
        self.subtitle_alignment: str = "center"

        # Dual changelogs
        self.session_log: list[str] = []
        self.subtitle_log: list[str] = []

        # Legacy compatibility
        self.changelog = self.session_log

        # Undo stack
        self._undo_stack: list[list] = []
        self._max_undo = 30

        # Thread communication
        self.msg_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()

        # Progress ownership.
        # When an owner is active, unrelated background jobs cannot overwrite
        # or clear the global progress bar.
        self._progress_owner: str | None = None

        # v5 audio workflow
        self.source_audio_path: str | None = None
        self.enhanced_audio_path: str | None = None
        self.active_audio_path: str | None = None
        self.export_use_enhanced_audio: bool = False

        # Media lifecycle flag.
        # Set True when a video is loaded/reloaded so the Edit tab rebuilds
        # player/audio/proxy state even if the file path is unchanged.
        self.force_player_reload: bool = False

    def log_session(self, message: str):
        """Log to session changelog (all operations)."""
        self.session_log.append(message)

    def log_subtitle(self, message: str):
        """Log to both session and subtitle changelogs."""
        self.session_log.append(message)
        self.subtitle_log.append(message)

    def save_undo(self):
        """Save cue snapshot for undo."""
        snapshot = [
            Cue(
                c.index,
                c.start,
                c.end,
                c.text,
                getattr(c, "alignment", "center"),
            )
            for c in self.cues
        ]
        self._undo_stack.append(snapshot)
        if len(self._undo_stack) > self._max_undo:
            self._undo_stack.pop(0)

    def undo(self) -> bool:
        """Restore cues from last snapshot."""
        if not self._undo_stack:
            return False
        self.cues = self._undo_stack.pop()
        return True

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # v5 AUDIO HELPERS
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def set_source_audio(self, path: str | None):
        """Set original extracted audio and make it active if appropriate."""
        old_source = self.source_audio_path
        self.source_audio_path = path

        if self.active_audio_path is None or self.active_audio_path == old_source:
            self.active_audio_path = path

    def set_enhanced_audio(self, path: str | None, make_active: bool = True):
        """Set enhanced audio and optionally make it active."""
        self.enhanced_audio_path = path
        if make_active and path:
            self.active_audio_path = path

    def use_source_audio(self):
        """Switch active working audio to original extracted audio."""
        self.active_audio_path = self.source_audio_path

    def use_enhanced_audio(self) -> bool:
        """Switch active working audio to enhanced audio if available."""
        if self.enhanced_audio_path and os.path.isfile(self.enhanced_audio_path):
            self.active_audio_path = self.enhanced_audio_path
            return True
        return False

    def has_enhanced_audio(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if an enhanced audio file exists."""
        return bool(
            self.enhanced_audio_path and os.path.isfile(self.enhanced_audio_path)
        )

    def get_export_audio_path(self) -> str | None:
        """
        Return the audio path that should be used for export.
        Defaults to source audio unless enhanced audio is explicitly selected.
        """
        if self.export_use_enhanced_audio and self.has_enhanced_audio():
            return self.enhanced_audio_path
        return self.source_audio_path

    def clear_audio_state(self) -> None:
        """Reset all audio workflow paths and export preference."""
        self.source_audio_path = None
        self.enhanced_audio_path = None
        self.active_audio_path = None
        self.export_use_enhanced_audio = False

    def validate_audio_paths(self) -> None:
        """Keep source/enhanced/active audio paths safe after project load.

        Project files may reference temporary enhanced audio files that no
        longer exist. If that happens, fall back to the source audio instead of
        leaving the app pointed at a missing file.
        """
        source_exists = bool(
            self.source_audio_path and os.path.isfile(self.source_audio_path)
        )
        enhanced_exists = bool(
            self.enhanced_audio_path and os.path.isfile(self.enhanced_audio_path)
        )
        active_exists = bool(
            self.active_audio_path and os.path.isfile(self.active_audio_path)
        )

        if not source_exists:
            self.source_audio_path = None

        if not enhanced_exists:
            self.enhanced_audio_path = None
            self.export_use_enhanced_audio = False

        if active_exists:
            return

        if self.export_use_enhanced_audio and self.enhanced_audio_path:
            self.active_audio_path = self.enhanced_audio_path
            return

        if self.source_audio_path:
            self.active_audio_path = self.source_audio_path
            self.export_use_enhanced_audio = False
            return

        self.active_audio_path = None
        self.export_use_enhanced_audio = False

    def reset(self):
        """Clear all loaded data."""
        self.video_path = None
        self.sub_path = None
        self.video_info = {}
        self.cues = []
        self.original_cues = []
        self.sections = []
        self.session_log = []
        self.subtitle_log = []
        self.changelog = self.session_log
        self.force_player_reload = False
        self.clear_audio_state()

    def log(self, message: str):
        """Send a message to the UI log via thread-safe queue."""
        self.msg_queue.put(message)

    def get_autosave_path(self) -> str:
        return os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "subtitle_studio_autosave.srt")

    def get_autosave_meta_path(self) -> str:
        return os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "subtitle_studio_autosave.json")

    def autosave(self) -> bool:
        if not self.cues:
            return False
        try:
            from core.parser import write_srt

            write_srt(self.cues, self.get_autosave_path())

            meta = {
                "video_path": self.video_path,
                "sub_path": self.sub_path,
                "language": self.language,
                "cue_count": len(self.cues),
                "timestamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
            }
            with open(self.get_autosave_meta_path(), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(meta, f)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def has_autosave(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.isfile(self.get_autosave_path())

    def load_autosave(self) -> bool:
        try:
            from core.parser import parse_subtitle_file

            path = self.get_autosave_path()
            if not os.path.isfile(path):
                return False

            cues = parse_subtitle_file(path)
            if not cues:
                return False

            self.cues = cues
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    def clear_autosave(self):
        for path in [self.get_autosave_path(), self.get_autosave_meta_path()]:
            try:
                if os.path.isfile(path):
                    os.remove(path)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def save_project(self, path: str) -> bool:
        try:
            project = {
                "version": "5.0",
                "video_path": self.video_path,
                "sub_path": self.sub_path,
                "language": self.language,
                "subtitle_alignment": self.subtitle_alignment,
                "source_audio_path": self.source_audio_path,
                "enhanced_audio_path": self.enhanced_audio_path,
                "active_audio_path": self.active_audio_path,
                "export_use_enhanced_audio": self.export_use_enhanced_audio,
                "cues": [
                    {
                        "index": c.index,
                        "start": c.start,
                        "end": c.end,
                        "text": c.text,
                        "alignment": getattr(c, "alignment", "center"),
                    }
                    for c in self.cues
                ],
                "original_cues": [
                    {
                        "index": c.index,
                        "start": c.start,
                        "end": c.end,
                        "text": c.text,
                        "alignment": getattr(c, "alignment", "center"),
                    }
                    for c in self.original_cues
                ],
                "sections": [
                    {
                        "start": s.start,
                        "end": s.end,
                        "label": s.label,
                    }
                    for s in self.sections
                ],
                "session_log": self.session_log.copy(),
                "subtitle_log": self.subtitle_log.copy(),
                "timestamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
            }

            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(project, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"[ERROR] Save project failed: {e}")
            return False

    def load_project(self, path: str) -> bool:
        try:
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                project = json.load(f)

            self.video_path = project.get("video_path")
            self.sub_path = project.get("sub_path")
            self.language = project.get("language", "us")
            self.subtitle_alignment = project.get("subtitle_alignment", "center")
            if self.subtitle_alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
                self.subtitle_alignment = "center"

            self.source_audio_path = project.get("source_audio_path")
            self.enhanced_audio_path = project.get("enhanced_audio_path")
            self.active_audio_path = project.get("active_audio_path")
            self.export_use_enhanced_audio = project.get(
                "export_use_enhanced_audio", False
            )

            self.validate_audio_paths()

            self.cues = [
                Cue(
                    c["index"],
                    c["start"],
                    c["end"],
                    c["text"],
                    c.get("alignment", "center"),
                )
                for c in project.get("cues", [])
            ]

            self.original_cues = [
                Cue(
                    c["index"],
                    c["start"],
                    c["end"],
                    c["text"],
                    c.get("alignment", "center"),
                )
                for c in project.get("original_cues", [])
            ]

            self.sections = [
                Section(s["start"], s["end"], s.get("label", ""))
                for s in project.get("sections", [])
            ]

            self.session_log = project.get("session_log", [])
            self.subtitle_log = project.get("subtitle_log", [])
            self.changelog = self.session_log

            if self.video_path and os.path.isfile(self.video_path):
                try:
                    from core.ffmpeg import probe_video

                    self.video_info = probe_video(
                        self.ffprobe_path, self.video_path
                    )
                except Exception:
                    self.video_info = {}
            else:
                self.video_info = {}

            return True
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"[ERROR] Load project failed: {e}")
            return False

    @property
    def spell_language(self) -> str:
        return "en_US" if self.language == "us" else "en_GB"

    def get_estimated_sizes(self):
        if not self.video_info:
            return {
                "original": "?",
                "high": "?",
                "medium": "?",
                "low": "?",
            }

        duration = self.video_info.get("duration", 0)
        source_mb = self.video_info.get("size_mb", 0)

        if duration <= 0 or source_mb <= 0:
            return {
                "original": "?",
                "high": "?",
                "medium": "?",
                "low": "?",
            }

        mb_per_second = source_mb / duration

        return {
            "original": f"{source_mb:.0f}",
            "high": f"{source_mb * 0.75:.0f}",
            "medium": f"{source_mb * 0.40:.0f}",
            "low": f"{source_mb * 0.20:.0f}",
            "mb_per_second": mb_per_second,
        }

    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # UNIVERSAL PROGRESS BAR
    # ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def begin_progress_owner(self, owner: str):
        """Reserve the global progress bar for one workflow."""
        self._progress_owner = owner

    def end_progress_owner(self, owner: str):
        """Release the global progress bar if owned by this workflow."""
        if self._progress_owner == owner:
            self._progress_owner = None

    def set_progress(
        self,
        value: float,
        status: str = "",
        owner: str | None = None,
    ):
        """Set progress bar (0-100) and optional status text.

        If a progress owner is active, unrelated background tasks are ignored.
        """
        if self._progress_owner and owner != self._progress_owner:
            return

        try:
            value = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
        except Exception:
            value = 0.0

        if self._progress_fn:
            try:
                self._progress_fn(value)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self._progress_status_fn:
            try:
                self._progress_status_fn(status or "")
            except Exception:
                pass

    def clear_progress(self, owner: str | None = None):
        """Reset progress bar and status to idle."""
        if self._progress_owner and owner != self._progress_owner:
            return
        self.set_progress(0, "", owner=owner)

# ================================================================
# COLOUR ACCESSORS — every part of the app reads these
# ================================================================

def _pal():
    """Return the active palette dict."""
    return _LIGHT if _current_mode == "light" else _DARK

# Each colour is accessed as theme.ACCENT, theme.CARD_BG, etc.
# We use a module-level refresh approach.

ACCENT = _LIGHT["ACCENT"]
ACCENT_HOVER = _LIGHT["ACCENT_HOVER"]
WHITE = _LIGHT["WHITE"]
BLACK = _LIGHT["BLACK"]
DARK_TEXT = _LIGHT["DARK_TEXT"]
TAB_BG = _LIGHT["TAB_BG"]
CARD_BG = _LIGHT["CARD_BG"]
CARD_BORDER = _LIGHT["CARD_BORDER"]
BORDER = _LIGHT["BORDER"]
FIELD_BG = _LIGHT["FIELD_BG"]
SEPARATOR = _LIGHT["SEPARATOR"]
HEADER_BG = _LIGHT["HEADER_BG"]
HEADER_EDGE = _LIGHT["HEADER_EDGE"]
SUCCESS = _LIGHT["SUCCESS"]
ERROR = _LIGHT["ERROR"]
WARNING = _LIGHT["WARNING"]
TREE_SELECT = _LIGHT["TREE_SELECT"]
ROW_ALT = _LIGHT["ROW_ALT"]
ROW_NORMAL = _LIGHT["ROW_NORMAL"]
LOG_BG = _LIGHT["LOG_BG"]
LOG_FG = _LIGHT["LOG_FG"]

def _refresh_module_colours():
    """Push the active palette values into module-level names."""
    g = globals()
    p = _pal()
    for key in p:
        g[key] = p[key]


def get_mode():
    return _current_mode


def is_dark():
    return _current_mode == "dark"

def init_ctk():
    ctk.set_appearance_mode("light")
    ctk.set_default_color_theme("blue")


def apply_global_fonts(root):
    root.option_add("*Font", FONT_BODY)


# ================================================================
# TTK TREEVIEW STYLES — applies both light and dark
# ================================================================

def apply_ttk_styles(root):
    p = _pal()
    style = ttk.Style(root)
    style.theme_use("clam")

    style.configure("Treeview",
                     background=p["TREE_BG"],
                     foreground=p["TREE_FG"],
                     fieldbackground=p["TREE_FIELD"],
                     rowheight=28,
                     font=FONT_BODY)
    style.configure("Treeview.Heading",
                     background=p["TREE_HEADING_BG"],
                     foreground=p["TREE_HEADING_FG"],
                     font=FONT_BODY_BOLD)
    style.map("Treeview",
              background=[("selected", p["TREE_SELECT"])],
              foreground=[("selected", p["ACCENT"])])

    style.configure("spell_error.Treeview",
                     foreground=p["ERROR"])


# ================================================================
# TOGGLE FUNCTION — call from the UI
# ================================================================

_toggle_callbacks = []   # list of  fn()  to call after toggle


def register_theme_callback(fn):
    """Register a function to run after every theme toggle."""
    _toggle_callbacks.append(fn)


def toggle_mode(root):
    """Switch between light ↔ dark and refresh the app."""
    global _current_mode

    # Create overlay to mask the transition
    # Uses the TARGET mode's background color
    new_mode = "dark" if _current_mode == "light" else "light"
    new_pal = _DARK if new_mode == "dark" else _LIGHT
    overlay = tk.Frame(
        root, bg=new_pal["WINDOW_BG"])
    overlay.place(x=0, y=0, relwidth=1, relheight=1)
    overlay.lift()
    root.update_idletasks()

    # Now switch everything behind the overlay
    _current_mode = new_mode
    _refresh_module_colours()

    # Update CTk appearance
    ctk.set_appearance_mode("dark" if is_dark() else "light")

    # Re-apply ttk styles for Treeview
    apply_ttk_styles(root)

    # Update root window background
    p = _pal()
    try:
        root.configure(bg=p["WINDOW_BG"])
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Notify every registered callback
    for fn in _toggle_callbacks:
        try:
            fn()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[theme callback error] {e}")

    # Process all pending updates behind overlay
    root.update_idletasks()
    root.update()

    # Remove overlay — everything is already rendered
    overlay.destroy()

def make_primary_btn(parent, text, icon=None, command=None):
    label = f"{icon}  {text}" if icon else text
    return ctk.CTkButton(
        parent, text=label, command=command,
        font=FONT_BUTTON,
        fg_color=_pal()["ACCENT"],
        hover_color=_pal()["ACCENT_HOVER"],
        text_color=_pal()["BTN_TEXT"],
        corner_radius=RADIUS_SM,
        height=34
    )


def make_secondary_btn(parent, text, icon=None, command=None):
    p = _pal()
    return ctk.CTkButton(
        parent, text=f"{icon}  {text}" if icon else text,
        command=command,
        font=FONT_BUTTON,
        fg_color=p["CARD_BG"],
        hover_color=p["SEPARATOR"],
        text_color=p["DARK_TEXT"],
        border_width=1,
        border_color=p["BORDER"],
        corner_radius=RADIUS_SM,
        height=34
    )


def make_danger_btn(parent, text, icon=None, command=None):
    p = _pal()
    return ctk.CTkButton(
        parent, text=f"{icon}  {text}" if icon else text,
        command=command,
        font=FONT_BUTTON,
        fg_color=p["ERROR"],
        hover_color="#B71C1C",
        text_color=p["BTN_TEXT"],
        corner_radius=RADIUS_SM,
        height=34
    )


def make_section_label(parent, text):
    return ctk.CTkLabel(
        parent, text=text,
        font=FONT_BODY_BOLD,
        text_color=_pal()["SECTION_TEXT"]
    )


def make_card(parent):
    p = _pal()
    return ctk.CTkFrame(
        parent,
        fg_color=p["CARD_BG"],
        border_color=p["CARD_BORDER"],
        border_width=1,
        corner_radius=RADIUS_MD
    )


def disable_btn(btn):
    p = _pal()
    btn.configure(state="disabled",
                  fg_color=p["DISABLED_BG"],
                  text_color=p["DISABLED_FG"])


def enable_btn(btn, primary=True):
    p = _pal()
    if primary:
        btn.configure(state="normal",
                      fg_color=p["ACCENT"],
                      text_color=p["BTN_TEXT"])
    else:
        btn.configure(state="normal",
                      fg_color=p["CARD_BG"],
                      text_color=p["DARK_TEXT"])


def flash_widget(widget, color=None):
    if color is None:
        color = _pal()["SUCCESS"]
    original = widget.cget("fg_color") if hasattr(widget, 'cget') else None
    try:
        widget.configure(fg_color=color)
        widget.after(400, lambda: widget.configure(
            fg_color=original if original else _pal()["CARD_BG"]))
    except Exception:
        pass

"""
Subtitle Studio v4.3 — Transcription Disabled.

AI subtitle generation has been removed from this build to improve
cross-machine reliability and simplify distribution.
"""

from typing import List, Callable, Optional


def check_whisper_available() -> bool:
    """AI transcription is disabled in v4.3."""
    return False


def get_whisper_import_error() -> str:
    """Return user-facing disabled message."""
    return "AI transcription is disabled in this build."


def get_available_models() -> List[str]:
    """No transcription models are available."""
    return []


def transcribe_audio(
        audio_path: str,
        model_name: str = "small",
        language: str = "en",
        log_fn: Optional[Callable] = None,
) -> List[dict]:
    """Transcription is disabled."""
    if log_fn:
        log_fn("[!] AI transcription is disabled in Subtitle Studio v4.3.")
    return []


def segments_to_cues(segments: List[dict]) -> list:
    """Convert raw segment dicts to Cue objects."""
    from core.state import Cue

    cues = []
    for i, seg in enumerate(segments):
        cues.append(Cue(
            index=i + 1,
            start=seg["start"],
            end=seg["end"],
            text=seg["text"],
        ))
    return cues

# core/user_dict.py  — Subtitle Studio v4.0 Session 2
# Persistent user dictionary for spell checking

import os

_USER_DICT_FILE = os.path.join(
    os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
    "user_dictionary.txt"
)

_words = set()
_loaded = False


def _ensure_loaded():
    """Load the word list from disk once."""
    global _loaded
    if _loaded:
        return
    _loaded = True
    if os.path.isfile(_USER_DICT_FILE):
        try:
            with open(_USER_DICT_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for line in f:
                    w = line.strip().lower()
                    if w:
                        _words.add(w)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[user_dict] load error: {e}")


def contains(word):
    """Check if a word is in the user dictionary."""
    _ensure_loaded()
    return word.strip().lower() in _words


def add_word(word):
    """Add a word and save to disk immediately."""
    _ensure_loaded()
    w = word.strip().lower()
    if not w or w in _words:
        return
    _words.add(w)
    try:
        with open(_USER_DICT_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(w + "\n")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[user_dict] save error: {e}")


def get_all():
    """Return a copy of all user dictionary words."""
    _ensure_loaded()
    return set(_words)

"""
Subtitle Studio v3.0 — Custom widgets.
Seek slider, tooltip, and reusable UI components.
"""

import tkinter as tk
from core.theme import (
    ACCENT, ACCENT_HOVER, BORDER, CARD_BG, DARK_TEXT,
    SEPARATOR, TAB_BG, SECTION_TEXT,
    FONT_SMALL
)


class SeekSlider(tk.Canvas):
    """
    Premiere-style seek slider with triangular playhead,
    visual scrubbing support, and draggable IN/OUT markers.

    Usage:
        slider = SeekSlider(parent, from_=0, to=100, command=on_change)
        slider.pack(fill="x")
        slider.set(50.0)
        slider.set_marks(mark_in=5.0, mark_out=10.0)
    """

    TRACK_HEIGHT = 3
    TRACK_PAD = 8
    CANVAS_HEIGHT = 50
    HEAD_WIDTH = 18
    HEAD_HEIGHT = 14
    LINE_WIDTH = 2
    MARK_HIT_RADIUS = 14

    def __init__(self, parent, from_=0.0, to=100.0,
                 command=None, bg=None, **kwargs):
        bg = bg or parent.cget("bg")
        super().__init__(
            parent, height=self.CANVAS_HEIGHT, bg=bg,
            highlightthickness=0, bd=0, cursor="hand2",
            **kwargs)

        self._from = float(from_)
        self._to = float(to)
        self._value = self._from
        self._command = command
        self._dragging = False
        self._pending_drag_value = None
        self._hovering = False
        self._scrub_callback = None
        self._waveform_photo = None
        self._waveform_path = None

        # Visual section markers
        self._mark_in = None
        self._mark_out = None

        # Marker drag callbacks
        self._mark_change_callback = None
        self._dragging_marker = None   # None / "in" / "out"

        # Optional snapping support
        self._snap_step = None

        # Drag tooltip
        self._drag_label_text = None

        self.bind("<Configure>", self._on_configure)
        self.bind("<Button-1>", self._on_click)
        self.bind("<B1-Motion>", self._on_drag)
        self.bind("<ButtonRelease-1>", self._on_release)
        self.bind("<Enter>", self._on_enter)
        self.bind("<Leave>", self._on_leave)
        self.bind("<Motion>", self._on_motion)

    # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────

    def set(self, value):
        self._value = max(self._from,
                          min(self._to, float(value)))
        self._redraw()

    def get(self):
        return self._value

    def set_scrub_callback(self, fn):
        """Set callback for real-time scrubbing during drag.
        fn(value) called on every drag movement."""
        self._scrub_callback = fn

    def set_mark_change_callback(self, fn):
        """Set callback for IN/OUT marker dragging.
        fn(which, value) where which is 'in' or 'out'."""
        self._mark_change_callback = fn

    def set_snap_step(self, step_seconds):
        """Set snapping step for marker dragging, e.g. frame duration."""
        try:
            step = float(step_seconds)
            self._snap_step = step if step > 0 else None
        except Exception:
            self._snap_step = None

    def set_marks(self, mark_in=None, mark_out=None):
        """Set visible IN/OUT markers on the timeline."""
        self._mark_in = mark_in
        self._mark_out = mark_out
        self._redraw()

    def clear_marks(self):
        """Clear visible IN/OUT markers."""
        self._mark_in = None
        self._mark_out = None
        self._dragging_marker = None
        self._drag_label_text = None
        self._redraw()

    def load_waveform(self, png_path):
        """Load a waveform PNG as slider background."""
        try:
            from PIL import Image, ImageTk
            w = self.winfo_width() - (self.TRACK_PAD * 2)
            h = self.CANVAS_HEIGHT
            if w < 20:
                w = 800
            img = Image.open(png_path)
            img = img.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
            self._waveform_photo = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
            self._waveform_path = png_path
            self._redraw()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[waveform] Failed to load: {e}")
            self._waveform_photo = None

    def clear_waveform(self):
        """Remove waveform background."""
        self._waveform_photo = None
        self._waveform_path = None
        self._redraw()

    def configure_range(self, from_=None, to=None):
        if from_ is not None:
            self._from = float(from_)
        if to is not None:
            self._to = float(to)
        self._value = max(self._from,
                          min(self._to, self._value))
        self._redraw()

    def configure(self, **kwargs):
        from_ = kwargs.pop("from_", None)
        to = kwargs.pop("to", None)
        if from_ is not None or to is not None:
            self.configure_range(from_=from_, to=to)
        if kwargs:
            super().configure(**kwargs)

    # ── Geometry helpers ──────────────────────────────────

    def _track_left(self):
        return self.TRACK_PAD

    def _track_right(self):
        return self.winfo_width() - self.TRACK_PAD

    def _track_width(self):
        return max(1, self._track_right() - self._track_left())

    def _value_to_x(self, value):
        rng = self._to - self._from
        if rng <= 0:
            return self._track_left()
        frac = (value - self._from) / rng
        return self._track_left() + frac * self._track_width()

    def _x_to_value(self, x, snap=False):
        rng = self._to - self._from
        if rng <= 0:
            return self._from
        frac = (x - self._track_left()) / self._track_width()
        frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, frac))
        value = self._from + frac * rng

        if snap and self._snap_step:
            value = round(value / self._snap_step) * self._snap_step

        return max(self._from, min(self._to, value))

    def _format_time(self, seconds):
        try:
            total_ms = max(0, int(round(float(seconds) * 1000)))
        except Exception:
            total_ms = 0

        total_seconds = total_ms // 1000
        ms = total_ms % 1000
        h = total_seconds // 3600
        m = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
        s = total_seconds % 60
        return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}.{ms:03d}"

    def _hit_test_marker(self, x, y=None):
        """Return 'in', 'out', or None if click is near a marker/label."""
        hits = []

        def _check(which, value):
            if value is None:
                return
            mx = self._value_to_x(value)

            x_hit = abs(x - mx) <= self.MARK_HIT_RADIUS
            y_hit = True if y is None else (0 <= y <= self.CANVAS_HEIGHT)

            label_hit = False
            if y is not None:
                label_hit = (mx - 18 <= x <= mx + 18 and 0 <= y <= 16)

            if (x_hit and y_hit) or label_hit:
                dist = abs(x - mx)
                hits.append((which, dist))

        _check("in", self._mark_in)
        _check("out", self._mark_out)

        if not hits:
            return None

        hits.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
        return hits[0][0]

    # ── Drawing ───────────────────────────────────────────

    def _draw_mark(self, x, label, color):
        """Draw one IN/OUT marker."""
        h = self.CANVAS_HEIGHT

        self.create_line(
            x, 16, x, h - 4,
            fill=color, width=2)

        self.create_rectangle(
            x - 16, 2, x + 16, 14,
            fill=color, outline="")
        self.create_text(
            x, 8,
            text=label,
            fill="#FFFFFF",
            font=("Arial", 8, "bold"))

    def _draw_drag_label(self, x, text, color):
        self.create_rectangle(
            x - 42, 18, x + 42, 34,
            fill=color, outline="")
        self.create_text(
            x, 26,
            text=text,
            fill="#FFFFFF",
            font=("Consolas", 8, "bold"))

    def _redraw(self):
        self.delete("all")

        w = self.winfo_width()
        h = self.CANVAS_HEIGHT
        if w < 20:
            return

        tl = self._track_left()
        tr = self._track_right()
        th = self.TRACK_HEIGHT
        track_y = h - 6
        px = self._value_to_x(self._value)

        from core import theme

        # Waveform background
        if self._waveform_photo:
            self.create_image(
                tl, 0, image=self._waveform_photo,
                anchor="nw")

        # Track background
        self.create_rectangle(
            tl, track_y - th // 2,
            tr, track_y + th // 2,
            fill=theme.BORDER, outline="", width=0)

        # Soft highlighted selected range
        if self._mark_in is not None and self._mark_out is not None:
            in_x = self._value_to_x(self._mark_in)
            out_x = self._value_to_x(self._mark_out)
            left = min(in_x, out_x)
            right = max(in_x, out_x)

            self.create_rectangle(
                left, 18,
                right, h - 8,
                fill="#E65100",
                outline="",
                stipple="gray50")

        # Track fill to playhead
        if px > tl:
            self.create_rectangle(
                tl, track_y - th // 2,
                px, track_y + th // 2,
                fill=theme.ACCENT, outline="", width=0)

        # IN/OUT markers
        if self._mark_in is not None:
            self._draw_mark(
                self._value_to_x(self._mark_in),
                "IN",
                "#2E7D32")

        if self._mark_out is not None:
            self._draw_mark(
                self._value_to_x(self._mark_out),
                "OUT",
                "#C62828")

        # Drag tooltip label
        if self._dragging_marker == "in" and self._mark_in is not None:
            self._draw_drag_label(
                self._value_to_x(self._mark_in),
                self._format_time(self._mark_in),
                "#2E7D32")
        elif self._dragging_marker == "out" and self._mark_out is not None:
            self._draw_drag_label(
                self._value_to_x(self._mark_out),
                self._format_time(self._mark_out),
                "#C62828")

        # Playhead triangle
        hw = self.HEAD_WIDTH
        hh = self.HEAD_HEIGHT
        if self._hovering or self._dragging:
            hw = 22
            hh = 16

        self.create_polygon(
            px - hw // 2, 0,
            px + hw // 2, 0,
            px, hh,
            fill=theme.ACCENT, outline="")

        # Playhead vertical line
        line_top = hh + 2
        line_bot = h - 8
        lw = self.LINE_WIDTH
        if self._hovering or self._dragging:
            lw = 2
        self.create_line(
            px, line_top, px, line_bot,
            fill=theme.ACCENT, width=lw)

    # ── Events ────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _on_configure(self, event=None):
        if self._waveform_path and self._waveform_photo:
            try:
                from PIL import Image, ImageTk
                w = self.winfo_width() - (self.TRACK_PAD * 2)
                h = self.CANVAS_HEIGHT
                if w > 20:
                    img = Image.open(self._waveform_path)
                    img = img.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
                    self._waveform_photo = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._redraw()

    def _on_click(self, event):
        """Start marker drag or timeline drag.

        Timeline dragging updates the visual playhead immediately, but the
        actual seek command is committed once on mouse release.
        """
        marker = self._hit_test_marker(event.x, event.y)
        if marker:
            self._dragging_marker = marker
            self._dragging = False
            self._pending_drag_value = None
            self.configure(cursor="sb_h_double_arrow")
            self._redraw()
            return

        self._dragging = True
        self._dragging_marker = None
        self._pending_drag_value = self._x_to_value(event.x)
        self._value = self._pending_drag_value
        self._redraw()

        if self._scrub_callback:
            self._scrub_callback(self._pending_drag_value)

    def _on_drag(self, event):
        """Update visual drag position. Commit timeline seek only on release."""
        if self._dragging_marker:
            new_val = self._x_to_value(event.x, snap=True)

            if self._dragging_marker == "in":
                if self._mark_out is not None:
                    new_val = min(new_val, self._mark_out)
                self._mark_in = new_val

            elif self._dragging_marker == "out":
                if self._mark_in is not None:
                    new_val = max(new_val, self._mark_in)
                self._mark_out = new_val

            self._value = new_val
            self._redraw()

            if self._mark_change_callback:
                self._mark_change_callback(
                    self._dragging_marker,
                    new_val)
            return

        if not self._dragging:
            return

        self._pending_drag_value = self._x_to_value(event.x)
        self._value = self._pending_drag_value
        self._redraw()

        if self._scrub_callback:
            self._scrub_callback(self._pending_drag_value)

    def _on_release(self, event):
        """Commit timeline seek once when drag ends."""
        if self._dragging_marker:
            self._dragging_marker = None
            self._drag_label_text = None
            self.configure(cursor="hand2")
            self._redraw()
            return

        if self._dragging:
            final_value = self._pending_drag_value
            if final_value is None:
                final_value = self._x_to_value(event.x)

            self._value = final_value
            self._pending_drag_value = None
            self._dragging = False
            self.configure(cursor="hand2")
            self._redraw()

            if self._command:
                self._command(final_value)
            return

        self._pending_drag_value = None
        self._dragging = False
        self.configure(cursor="hand2")

    def _on_enter(self, event):
        self._hovering = True
        self._redraw()

    def _on_leave(self, event):
        self._hovering = False
        self.configure(cursor="hand2")
        if not self._dragging and not self._dragging_marker:
            self._redraw()

    def _on_motion(self, event):
        if self._dragging_marker:
            self.configure(cursor="sb_h_double_arrow")
            return

        marker = self._hit_test_marker(event.x, event.y)
        if marker:
            self.configure(cursor="sb_h_double_arrow")
        else:
            self.configure(cursor="hand2")

    def _update_from_x(self, x, scrubbing=False):
        new_val = self._x_to_value(x, snap=False)
        self._value = max(self._from,
                          min(self._to, new_val))
        self._redraw()

        if scrubbing and self._scrub_callback:
            self._scrub_callback(self._value)
        elif not scrubbing and self._command:
            self._command(self._value)
            
class Tooltip:
    """
    Lightweight tooltip that appears on hover after a delay.

    Usage:
        Tooltip(widget, "This button does X")
    """

    DELAY_MS = 500
    BG = "#333333"
    FG = "#EEEEEE"

    def __init__(self, widget, text):
        self.widget = widget
        self.text = text
        self._tip_window = None
        self._after_id = None

        widget.bind("<Enter>", self._on_enter, add="+")
        widget.bind("<Leave>", self._on_leave, add="+")
        widget.bind("<Button-1>", self._on_leave, add="+")

    def _on_enter(self, event=None):
        self._cancel()
        self._after_id = self.widget.after(
            self.DELAY_MS, self._show)

    def _on_leave(self, event=None):
        self._cancel()
        self._hide()

    def _cancel(self):
        if self._after_id:
            self.widget.after_cancel(self._after_id)
            self._after_id = None

    def _show(self):
        if self._tip_window:
            return

        x = self.widget.winfo_rootx() + 20
        y = (self.widget.winfo_rooty()
             + self.widget.winfo_height() + 4)

        self._tip_window = tw = tk.Toplevel(self.widget)
        tw.wm_overrideredirect(True)
        tw.wm_geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
        tw.wm_attributes("-topmost", True)

        label = tk.Label(
            tw, text=self.text,
            font=FONT_SMALL, bg=self.BG, fg=self.FG,
            relief="flat", padx=8, pady=4)
        label.pack()

    def _hide(self):
        if self._tip_window:
            self._tip_window.destroy()
            self._tip_window = None

param(
    [string]$ModelUrl = ""
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$ModelDir = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "bin\windows\models"
$ModelPath = Join-Path $ModelDir "rnnoise-model.rnnn"

if (-not $ModelUrl) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Usage:" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "  .\download_arnndn_model.ps1 -ModelUrl `"https://example.com/model.rnnn`""
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Search for a direct/raw .rnnn model URL, then rerun this script."
    exit 1
}

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $ModelDir | Out-Null

Write-Host "[i] Downloading arnndn model..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "    URL: $ModelUrl"
Write-Host "    To : $ModelPath"

Invoke-WebRequest `
    -Uri $ModelUrl `
    -OutFile $ModelPath `
    -UseBasicParsing

if (-not (Test-Path $ModelPath)) {
    throw "Download failed: model file was not created."
}

$size = (Get-Item $ModelPath).Length

if ($size -le 0) {
    throw "Download failed: model file is empty."
}

Write-Host "[OK] Model downloaded." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "     $ModelPath"
Write-Host "     Size: $size bytes"

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[i] Checking bundled FFmpeg arnndn support..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$Ffmpeg = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe"

if (-not (Test-Path $Ffmpeg)) {
    throw "Bundled ffmpeg.exe not found: $Ffmpeg"
}

$filters = & $Ffmpeg -hide_banner -filters 2>&1

if ($filters -match "arnndn") {
    Write-Host "[OK] FFmpeg supports arnndn." -ForegroundColor Green
} else {
    Write-Host "[!!] FFmpeg does not appear to support arnndn." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[OK] arnndn model setup complete." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Model path:"
Write-Host "  bin\windows\models\rnnoise-model.rnnn"


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: download_arnndn_models_from_github.ps1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$ModelDir = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "bin\windows\models"
$FinalModelPath = Join-Path $ModelDir "rnnoise-model.rnnn"

$TempRoot = Join-Path $env:TEMP ("arnndn_models_" + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString("N"))
$ZipPath = Join-Path $TempRoot "arnndn-models.zip"
$ExtractDir = Join-Path $TempRoot "extract"

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $TempRoot | Out-Null
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $ModelDir | Out-Null

$urls = @(
    "https://github.com/richardpl/arnndn-models/archive/refs/heads/master.zip",
    "https://github.com/richardpl/arnndn-models/archive/refs/heads/main.zip"
)

$downloaded = $false

foreach ($url in $urls) {
    try {
        Write-Host "[i] Trying download:" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        Write-Host "    $url"

        Invoke-WebRequest `
            -Uri $url `
            -OutFile $ZipPath `
            -UseBasicParsing

        if ((Test-Path $ZipPath) -and ((Get-Item $ZipPath).Length -gt 0)) {
            $downloaded = $true
            break
        }
    }
    catch {
        Write-Host "[!] Failed URL, trying next if available." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }
}

if (-not $downloaded) {
    throw "Could not download arnndn-models repo zip."
}

Write-Host "[OK] Repo downloaded." -ForegroundColor Green

Expand-Archive -Path $ZipPath -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

$models = Get-ChildItem -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Filter "*.rnnn" | Sort-Object FullName

if (-not $models -or $models.Count -eq 0) {
    throw "No .rnnn model files found in downloaded repo."
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Available .rnnn models:" -ForegroundColor Cyan

for ($i = 0; $i -lt $models.Count; $i++) {
    $sizeKb = [Math]::Round($models[$i].Length / 1KB, 1)
    Write-Host ("[{0}] {1} ({2} KB)" -f $i, $models[$i].Name, $sizeKb)
}

Write-Host ""
$choice = Read-Host "Enter model number to use"

if ($choice -notmatch "^\d+$") {
    throw "Invalid selection."
}

$idx = [int]$choice

if ($idx -lt 0 -or $idx -ge $models.Count) {
    throw "Selection out of range."
}

$selected = $models[$idx]

Copy-Item $selected.FullName $FinalModelPath -Force

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[OK] Selected model copied:" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "    From: $($selected.FullName)"
Write-Host "    To  : $FinalModelPath"

$Ffmpeg = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe"

if (-not (Test-Path $Ffmpeg)) {
    throw "Bundled ffmpeg.exe not found: $Ffmpeg"
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[i] Checking FFmpeg arnndn support..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$filters = & $Ffmpeg -hide_banner -filters 2>&1

if ($filters -match "arnndn") {
    Write-Host "[OK] FFmpeg supports arnndn." -ForegroundColor Green
} else {
    Write-Host "[!!] FFmpeg does not list arnndn." -ForegroundColor Red
    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[OK] arnndn model setup complete." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Final app model path:"
Write-Host "  bin\windows\models\rnnoise-model.rnnn"

Remove-Item $TempRoot -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: export_project_context.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import argparse
import datetime
import os
import platform
from pathlib import Path


DEFAULT_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py",
    ".md",
    ".txt",
    ".json",
    ".toml",
    ".yaml",
    ".yml",
    ".ini",
    ".cfg",
    ".html",
    ".css",
    ".js",
    ".ts",
    ".tsx",
    ".jsx",
    ".ps1",
    ".bat",
    ".cmd",
    ".sh",
}

EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    ".hg",
    ".svn",
    ".venv",
    ".venv_packaging",
    "venv",
    "env",
    "python_env",
    "__pycache__",
    ".pytest_cache",
    ".mypy_cache",
    ".ruff_cache",
    ".idea",
    ".vscode",
    "node_modules",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "build_nuitka",
    "launcher.build",
    "launcher.dist",
    "launcher.onefile-build",
    "release",
    "releases",
    "models",
    "subtitlestudio_v4.1_release",
}

EXCLUDED_FILES = {
    ".env",
    ".env.local",
    ".env.production",
    ".env.development",
    "secrets.json",
    "credentials.json",
    "token.json",
    "subtitle_studio_prefs.json",
}

EXCLUDED_OUTPUT_PREFIXES = {
    "PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT",
}

MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES = 500_000


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Export project source code and docs into manageable text chunks."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--root",
        default=".",
        help="Project root folder to scan. Default: current folder.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-prefix",
        default="PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT",
        help="Output file prefix. Default: PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--extensions",
        default="",
        help="Optional comma-separated extensions, example: .py,.md,.json",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-mb",
        type=float,
        default=5.0,
        help="Maximum size per chunk in MB. Default: 5",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()


def get_extensions(raw_extensions: str) -> set[str]:
    if not raw_extensions.strip():
        return DEFAULT_EXTENSIONS

    extensions = set()

    for item in raw_extensions.split(","):
        extension = item.strip().lower()

        if extension and not extension.startswith("."):
            extension = f".{extension}"

        if extension:
            extensions.add(extension)

    return extensions


def should_skip_dir(path: Path) -> bool:
    return path.name.lower() in EXCLUDED_DIRS


def is_generated_export_file(path: Path, output_prefix: str) -> bool:
    file_name = path.name.upper()
    active_prefix = output_prefix.upper()

    if file_name.startswith(active_prefix):
        return True

    for prefix in EXCLUDED_OUTPUT_PREFIXES:
        if file_name.startswith(prefix.upper()):
            return True

    return False


def should_skip_file(path: Path, extensions: set[str], output_prefix: str) -> bool:
    file_name_lower = path.name.lower()

    if is_generated_export_file(path, output_prefix):
        return True

    if file_name_lower in EXCLUDED_FILES:
        return True

    if file_name_lower.startswith(".env"):
        return True

    if path.suffix.lower() not in extensions:
        return True

    try:
        if path.stat().st_size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES:
            return True
    except OSError:
        return True

    return False


def collect_files(root: Path, extensions: set[str], output_prefix: str) -> list[Path]:
    collected_files = []

    for current_root, dir_names, file_names in os.walk(root):
        current_path = Path(current_root)

        dir_names[:] = [
            dir_name
            for dir_name in dir_names
            if not should_skip_dir(current_path / dir_name)
        ]

        for file_name in file_names:
            file_path = current_path / file_name

            if should_skip_file(file_path, extensions, output_prefix):
                continue

            collected_files.append(file_path)

    return sorted(collected_files, key=lambda item: str(item).lower())


def read_text_file(path: Path) -> str:
    encodings_to_try = ["utf-8", "utf-8-sig", "cp1252"]

    for encoding in encodings_to_try:
        try:
            return path.read_text(encoding=encoding)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
        except OSError as error:
            return f"[ERROR READING FILE: {error}]"

    try:
        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except OSError as error:
        return f"[ERROR READING FILE: {error}]"


def get_relative_path(root: Path, file_path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        relative_path = file_path.relative_to(root)
    except ValueError:
        relative_path = file_path

    return str(relative_path).replace("\\", "/")


def build_directory_tree(root: Path, files: list[Path]) -> str:
    lines = []

    for file_path in files:
        lines.append(get_relative_path(root, file_path))

    return "\n".join(lines)


def build_file_section(root: Path, file_path: Path) -> str:
    display_path = get_relative_path(root, file_path)
    content = read_text_file(file_path)

    return "\n".join(
        [
            "",
            "-" * 80,
            f"FILE: {display_path}",
            "-" * 80,
            content,
            "",
        ]
    )


def build_manifest_text(root: Path, files: list[Path], output_prefix: str, chunk_count: int) -> str:
    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    system_name = platform.system()
    python_version = platform.python_version()

    return "\n".join(
        [
            "PROJECT CODE EXPORT MANIFEST",
            "=" * 80,
            f"Generated: {timestamp}",
            f"Root folder: {root.resolve()}",
            f"Platform: {system_name}",
            f"Python: {python_version}",
            f"Files included: {len(files)}",
            f"Chunk files created: {chunk_count}",
            f"Output prefix: {output_prefix}",
            "",
            "HOW TO USE",
            "=" * 80,
            "Read this manifest first, then open the chunk files in order:",
            f"{output_prefix}_PART_001.txt",
            f"{output_prefix}_PART_002.txt",
            f"{output_prefix}_PART_003.txt",
            "",
            "DIRECTORY / FILE LIST",
            "=" * 80,
            build_directory_tree(root, files),
            "",
        ]
    )


def build_chunk_header(root: Path, part_number: int, output_prefix: str) -> str:
    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

    return "\n".join(
        [
            "PROJECT CODE EXPORT CHUNK",
            "=" * 80,
            f"Generated: {timestamp}",
            f"Root folder: {root.resolve()}",
            f"Output prefix: {output_prefix}",
            f"Part: {part_number:03d}",
            "",
            "FILE CONTENTS",
            "=" * 80,
            "",
        ]
    )


def write_text(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")


def get_text_size_bytes(text: str) -> int:
    return len(text.encode("utf-8"))


def delete_previous_exports(output_folder: Path, output_prefix: str) -> None:
    patterns = [
        f"{output_prefix}_MANIFEST.txt",
        f"{output_prefix}_PART_*.txt",
        f"{output_prefix}.txt",
    ]

    for pattern in patterns:
        for path in output_folder.glob(pattern):
            try:
                path.unlink()
                print(f"Deleted old export: {path}")
            except OSError as error:
                print(f"Warning: could not delete old export {path}: {error}")


def write_chunk_files(
    root: Path,
    files: list[Path],
    output_prefix: str,
    max_chunk_bytes: int,
) -> int:
    output_folder = root
    part_number = 1
    current_sections = [build_chunk_header(root, part_number, output_prefix)]
    current_size = get_text_size_bytes(current_sections[0])

    for file_path in files:
        section = build_file_section(root, file_path)
        section_size = get_text_size_bytes(section)

        if current_size + section_size > max_chunk_bytes and len(current_sections) > 1:
            chunk_path = output_folder / f"{output_prefix}_PART_{part_number:03d}.txt"
            write_text(chunk_path, "\n".join(current_sections))
            print(f"Wrote chunk: {chunk_path}")

            part_number += 1
            current_sections = [build_chunk_header(root, part_number, output_prefix)]
            current_size = get_text_size_bytes(current_sections[0])

        current_sections.append(section)
        current_size += section_size

    if len(current_sections) > 1:
        chunk_path = output_folder / f"{output_prefix}_PART_{part_number:03d}.txt"
        write_text(chunk_path, "\n".join(current_sections))
        print(f"Wrote chunk: {chunk_path}")
        return part_number

    return 0


def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()

    root = Path(args.root).resolve()
    output_prefix = args.output_prefix.strip() or "PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT"
    extensions = get_extensions(args.extensions)
    max_chunk_bytes = int(args.max_mb * 1024 * 1024)

    if not root.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: Root folder does not exist: {root}")
        return

    if not root.is_dir():
        print(f"ERROR: Root path is not a folder: {root}")
        return

    if max_chunk_bytes < 100_000:
        print("ERROR: --max-mb is too small. Use at least 0.1")
        return

    print(f"Scanning project: {root}")
    print(f"Max chunk size: {args.max_mb} MB")

    delete_previous_exports(root, output_prefix)

    files = collect_files(root, extensions, output_prefix)
    print(f"Files found: {len(files)}")

    chunk_count = write_chunk_files(root, files, output_prefix, max_chunk_bytes)

    manifest_text = build_manifest_text(root, files, output_prefix, chunk_count)
    manifest_path = root / f"{output_prefix}_MANIFEST.txt"
    write_text(manifest_path, manifest_text)

    print(f"Wrote manifest: {manifest_path}")
    print("Export complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

if not exist "%PYTHON_EXE%" (

    pause
    exit /b 1
)

    echo [!!] ffprobe.exe not found.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

"%PYTHON_EXE%" "%~dp0main.py"

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo [!!] App exited with an error.
    pause
)

endlocal

FILE: launcher.py

"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Launcher
Ensures working directory is correct and all paths resolve
before launching the main application.
"""

import os
import sys


def get_app_dir():
    """Get the directory where the application lives."""
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))


def setup_environment():
    """Configure paths and environment before app launch."""
    app_dir = get_app_dir()

    os.chdir(app_dir)

    if app_dir not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, app_dir)

    internal_dir = os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal")
    if os.path.isdir(internal_dir) and internal_dir not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, internal_dir)

    os.environ["PATH"] = app_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", "")

    root_bin_dir = os.path.join(app_dir, "bin", "windows")
    if os.path.isdir(root_bin_dir):
        os.environ["PATH"] = root_bin_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

    import platform
    if platform.system() == "Windows":
        bin_dir = os.path.join(internal_dir, "bin", "windows")
    elif platform.system() == "Darwin":
        bin_dir = os.path.join(internal_dir, "bin", "macos")
    else:
        bin_dir = os.path.join(internal_dir, "bin", "linux")

    if os.path.isdir(bin_dir):
        os.environ["PATH"] = bin_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

    return app_dir


def _resource_path(app_dir, *parts):
    """
    Resolve a resource path, checking both the app root
    and the PyInstaller _internal subfolder.
    Returns the first path that exists, or the app_dir version.
    """
    root_path = os.path.join(app_dir, *parts)
    internal_path = os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal", *parts)

    if os.path.isfile(root_path) or os.path.isdir(root_path):
        return root_path
    if os.path.isfile(internal_path) or os.path.isdir(internal_path):
        return internal_path
    return root_path


def check_requirements(app_dir):
    """Check that required files exist and report status."""
    issues = []
    import shutil

    ffmpeg = _resource_path(app_dir, "bin", "windows", "ffmpeg.exe")
    ffprobe = _resource_path(app_dir, "bin", "windows", "ffprobe.exe")
    dictionary = _resource_path(app_dir, "dictionary.csv")

    if not os.path.isfile(ffmpeg) and not shutil.which("ffmpeg"):
        issues.append(
            "ffmpeg.exe not found.\n"
            "Expected at bin\\windows\\ffmpeg.exe or on PATH."
        )

    if not os.path.isfile(ffprobe) and not shutil.which("ffprobe"):
        issues.append(
            "ffprobe.exe not found.\n"
            "Expected at bin\\windows\\ffprobe.exe or on PATH."
        )

    if not os.path.isfile(dictionary):
        issues.append(
            "dictionary.csv not found.\n"
            "US/UK spell conversion will not work."
        )

    internal_bin = os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal", "bin", "windows")
    if os.path.isdir(internal_bin):
        current_path = os.environ.get("PATH", "")
        if internal_bin not in current_path:
            os.environ["PATH"] = internal_bin + os.pathsep + current_path

    user_dict = os.path.join(app_dir, "user_dictionary.txt")
    if not os.path.isfile(user_dict):
        try:
            with open(user_dict, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return issues


def show_error_dialog(message):
    """Show an error dialog without requiring CTk to be loaded."""
    try:
        import tkinter as tk
        from tkinter import messagebox
        root = tk.Tk()
        root.withdraw()
        messagebox.showerror("Subtitle Studio — Startup Error", message)
        root.destroy()
    except Exception:
        print(f"[ERROR] {message}")


def _write_startup_diagnostics(app_dir):
    """Write runtime diagnostics to a text file beside the EXE."""
    try:
        import traceback

        diag_path = os.path.join(app_dir, "startup_diagnostics.txt")
        internal_dir = os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal")

        lines = []
        lines.append("Subtitle Studio Startup Diagnostics")
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append(f"app_dir: {app_dir}")
        lines.append(f"sys.executable: {sys.executable}")
        lines.append(f"sys.frozen: {getattr(sys, 'frozen', False)}")
        lines.append(f"cwd: {os.getcwd()}")
        lines.append(f"_internal exists: {os.path.isdir(internal_dir)}")
        lines.append("")

        lines.append("sys.path:")
        for p in sys.path:
            lines.append(f"  {p}")
        lines.append("")

        checks = [
            os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal", "bin", "windows", "ffmpeg.exe"),
            os.path.join(app_dir, "_internal", "dictionary.csv"),
        ]

        lines.append("file checks:")
        for path in checks:
            lines.append(f"  {path} -> {os.path.exists(path)}")
        lines.append("")

        imports = [
            "customtkinter",
            "mpv",
        ]

        lines.append("import checks:")
        for mod_name in imports:
            try:
                mod = __import__(mod_name)
                mod_file = getattr(mod, "__file__", "(built-in)")
                lines.append(f"  {mod_name}: OK -> {mod_file}")
            except Exception as e:
                lines.append(
                    f"  {mod_name}: FAIL -> {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
                )
                lines.append(traceback.format_exc())

        with open(diag_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("\n".join(lines))
    except Exception:
        pass


def main():
    """Launch Subtitle Studio."""
    app_dir = setup_environment()
    _write_startup_diagnostics(app_dir)

    issues = check_requirements(app_dir)
    if issues:
        warning_text = "Startup warnings:\n\n" + "\n\n".join(issues)
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import messagebox
            root = tk.Tk()
            root.withdraw()
            messagebox.showwarning(
                "Subtitle Studio — Warning", warning_text
            )
            root.destroy()
        except Exception:
            print(f"[WARNING] {warning_text}")

    try:
        from main import SubtitleStudio
        app = SubtitleStudio()
        app.run()
    except ImportError as e:
        show_error_dialog(
            f"Failed to load application module:\n\n{e}\n\n"
            f"Make sure all files are in:\n{app_dir}"
        )
        sys.exit(1)
    except Exception as e:
        show_error_dialog(f"Unexpected error during startup:\n\n{e}")
        sys.exit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Entry point.
3-tab architecture: Edit, Export, Log.
CustomTkinter edition — Session 6.

Focus:
  - Robust startup diagnostics
  - Graceful degradation when modules missing
  - Safe save/open/close flows
  - Lazy tab loading to reduce startup cost
  - Bounded log buffering and managed timers
  - Recent projects and persistent UI preferences
  - Richer keyboard shortcuts and diagnostics
  - Self-test mode for headless validation
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import shutil
import platform
import traceback
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import customtkinter as ctk

from core import theme
from core.theme import (
    init_ctk, apply_global_fonts, apply_ttk_styles,
    toggle_mode, is_dark, register_theme_callback,
    ICON_SUN, ICON_MOON,
)
