# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SAFE MODULE IMPORTS
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
_IMPORT_ERRORS = {}

def _safe_import(module_path, name):
    """Import a module, return None on failure."""
    try:
        mod = __import__(module_path, fromlist=[name])
        return getattr(mod, name)
    except Exception as e:
        _IMPORT_ERRORS[module_path] = str(e)
        print(f"[!] {module_path}.{name} failed: {e}")
        return None

build_edit_tab = _safe_import(
    "tabs.tab_edit", "build_edit_tab")
build_export_tab = _safe_import(
    "tabs.tab_export", "build_export_tab")
build_logs_tab = _safe_import(
    "tabs.tab_logs", "build_logs_tab")

# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# CONSTANTS
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
APP_VERSION = "5.0"
PREFS_FILE = "subtitle_studio_prefs.json"
MAX_RECENT = 8
MAX_LOG_LINES = 500
LOG_POLL_MS = 100
AUTOSAVE_MS = 60_000

TAB_DEFS = [
    ("Edit",    "edit"),
    ("Export",  "export"),
    ("Log",     "logs"),
]


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# PREFERENCES MANAGER
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class Preferences:
    """Persistent user preferences stored as JSON."""

    DEFAULTS = {
        "window_width": theme.WINDOW_WIDTH,
        "window_height": theme.WINDOW_HEIGHT,
        "window_x": None,
        "window_y": None,
        "dark_mode": False,
        "preview_resolution": "720p",
        "last_tab": "edit",
        "recent_projects": [],
        "last_video_dir": "",
        "last_sub_dir": "",
        "last_export_dir": "",
        "font_size": 12,
        "volume": 0.8,
    }

    def __init__(self, app_dir: str):
        self._path = os.path.join(app_dir, PREFS_FILE)
        self._data = dict(self.DEFAULTS)
        self.load()

    def load(self):
        """Load preferences from disk."""
        try:
            if os.path.isfile(self._path):
                with open(self._path, "r",
                           encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    saved = json.load(f)
                for key in self.DEFAULTS:
                    if key in saved:
                        self._data[key] = saved[key]
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[!] Prefs load failed: {e}")

    def save(self):
        """Save preferences to disk."""
        try:
            with open(self._path, "w",
                       encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._data, f, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[!] Prefs save failed: {e}")

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        return self._data.get(
            key, self.DEFAULTS.get(key, default))

    def set(self, key, value):
        self._data[key] = value

    def add_recent(self, path: str):
        """Add a project path to recent list."""
        recent = self._data.get("recent_projects", [])
        # Remove if already in list
        recent = [p for p in recent if p != path]
        # Add to front
        recent.insert(0, path)
        # Trim to max
        self._data["recent_projects"] = (
            recent[:MAX_RECENT])

    def get_recent(self) -> list:
        """Return list of recent project paths that
        still exist."""
        recent = self._data.get("recent_projects", [])
        return [p for p in recent
                if os.path.isfile(p)]


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# STARTUP DIAGNOSTICS
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_diagnostics(app_dir: str) -> list:
    """Run startup checks. Returns list of
    (level, message) tuples."""
    results = []

    # Python version
    ver = sys.version_info
    if ver < (3, 10):
        results.append((
            "WARN",
            f"Python {ver.major}.{ver.minor} — "
            f"3.12+ recommended"))
    else:
        results.append((
            "OK",
            f"Python {ver.major}.{ver.minor}.{ver.micro}"))

    # Platform
    results.append((
        "INFO",
        f"Platform: {platform.system()} "
        f"{platform.machine()}"))

    # Core dependencies
    deps = [
        ("customtkinter", "customtkinter"),
        ("PIL", "Pillow"),
        ("spellchecker", "pyspellchecker"),
        ("numpy", "numpy"),
        ("soundfile", "soundfile"),
        ("docx", "python-docx"),
    ]
    for mod_name, pip_name in deps:
        try:
            __import__(mod_name)
            results.append((
                "OK", f"{pip_name} available"))
        except ImportError:
            results.append((
                "ERROR",
                f"{pip_name} missing — "
                f"pip install {pip_name}"))

    # Optional deps
    opt_deps = [
        ("docx", "python-docx"),
    ]
    for mod_name, pip_name in opt_deps:
        try:
            __import__(mod_name)
            results.append((
                "OK", f"{pip_name} available"))
        except ImportError:
            results.append((
                "INFO",
                f"{pip_name} not installed (optional)"))

    # FFmpeg
    for name in ["ffmpeg", "ffprobe"]:
        found = shutil.which(name)
        if found:
            results.append((
                "OK", f"{name}: {found}"))
        else:
            # Check bin dirs
            for subdir in ["windows", "macos", "linux"]:
                ext = ".exe" if subdir == "windows" else ""
                candidate = os.path.join(
                    app_dir, "bin", subdir,
                    f"{name}{ext}")
                if os.path.isfile(candidate):
                    results.append((
                        "OK",
                        f"{name}: {candidate}"))
                    break
            else:
                results.append((
                    "WARN",
                    f"{name} not found — "
                    f"place in bin/ or PATH"))

    # MPV / libmpv
    try:
        from core.player_mpv import diagnose_mpv_environment

        ok, msg = diagnose_mpv_environment(app_dir)
        if ok:
            results.append((
                "OK",
                f"MPV: {msg}"
            ))
        else:
            results.append((
                "ERROR",
                f"MPV: {msg}"
            ))
    except Exception as e:
        results.append((
            "ERROR",
            f"MPV diagnostic failed: {e}"
        ))
    
    # Module import errors
    for mod_path, err in _IMPORT_ERRORS.items():
        results.append((
            "ERROR",
            f"Module failed: {mod_path} — {err}"))

    return results


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SELF-TEST MODE
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_self_test():
    """Headless validation — run from command line with
    --self-test flag. Returns exit code 0 on success."""
    print("=" * 50)
    print(f"Subtitle Studio v{APP_VERSION} — Self Test")
    print("=" * 50)

    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        app_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    else:
        app_dir = os.path.dirname(
            os.path.abspath(__file__))

    results = run_diagnostics(app_dir)
    errors = 0

    for level, msg in results:
        icon = {"OK": "✓", "INFO": "ℹ",
                "WARN": "⚠", "ERROR": "✗"}.get(
            level, "?")
        print(f"  {icon} [{level}] {msg}")
        if level == "ERROR":
            errors += 1

    # Run unit tests
    print()
    print("Running unit tests...")
    tests_dir = os.path.join(app_dir, "tests")
    test_errors = 0
    test_count = 0

    if os.path.isdir(tests_dir):
        # Add app dir to path so imports work
        if app_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, app_dir)

        import importlib
        import glob

        test_files = sorted(glob.glob(
            os.path.join(tests_dir, "test_*.py")))

        for test_file in test_files:
            mod_name = os.path.splitext(
                os.path.basename(test_file))[0]
            try:
                mod = importlib.import_module(
                    f"tests.{mod_name}")
                if hasattr(mod, "run_all"):
                    mod.run_all()
                    test_count += 1
                else:
                    print(f"  [!] {mod_name}: "
                          f"no run_all() found")
            except AssertionError as e:
                print(f"  ✗ {mod_name} FAILED: {e}")
                test_errors += 1
                test_count += 1
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"  ✗ {mod_name} ERROR: {e}")
                test_errors += 1
                test_count += 1

        if test_count == 0:
            print("  [!] No test modules found")
        else:
            print(f"\n  Ran {test_count} test module(s), "
                  f"{test_errors} failed")

        errors += test_errors
    else:
        print(f"  [!] Tests directory not found: "
              f"{tests_dir}")

    print()
    if errors == 0:
        print("All checks passed. ✓")
    else:
        print(f"{errors} error(s) found. ✗")
    return 1 if errors else 0

# MAIN APPLICATION
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class SubtitleStudio:
    """Main application window."""

    def __init__(self):
        init_ctk()

        # ── App directory ──
        if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
            self._app_dir = os.path.dirname(
                sys.executable)
        else:
            self._app_dir = os.path.dirname(
                os.path.abspath(__file__))

        # ── Preferences ──
        self.prefs = Preferences(self._app_dir)

        # ── Apply saved dark mode ──
        if self.prefs.get("dark_mode"):
            from core.theme import _refresh_module_colours
            import core.theme as _t
            _t._current_mode = "dark"
            _refresh_module_colours()
            ctk.set_appearance_mode("dark")

        self.root = ctk.CTk()
        self.root.title(
            f"Subtitle Studio v{APP_VERSION}")

        # ── Restore window geometry ──
        w = self.prefs.get("window_width")
        h = self.prefs.get("window_height")
        x = self.prefs.get("window_x")
        y = self.prefs.get("window_y")
        if x is not None and y is not None:
            self.root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}")
        else:
            self.root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}")

        self.root.resizable(True, True)
        self.root.minsize(900, 650)
        self.root.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)

        # ── App icon ──
        icon_path = os.path.join(
            self._app_dir, "icon.ico")
        if os.path.isfile(icon_path):
            try:
                self.root.iconbitmap(icon_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        apply_global_fonts(self.root)
        apply_ttk_styles(self.root)

        # ── State ──
        self.state = AppState(self._app_dir)

        # ── Track current tab ──
        self.active_tab: str | None = None
        self._is_fullscreen = False
        self._project_path = None
        self._shutting_down = False
        self._tabs_built: set = set()

        # ── Build UI (order matters) ──
        self._build_menu_bar()
        self._build_header()
        self._refresh_recent_menu()
        self._build_tab_bar()
        self._build_tab_frames()
        self._build_progress()
        self._build_log()

        # ── Start on Edit tab ──
        self.select_tab("edit")

        # ── Start log polling ──
        self._poll_log()

        # ── Report FFmpeg status ──
        self._check_tools()

        # ── Startup diagnostics ──
        self._log_diagnostics()

        # ── Auto-save ──
        self._start_autosave()

        # ── Crash recovery check ──
        self._check_autosave_recovery()

        # ── Keyboard shortcuts ──
        self._bind_shortcuts()

        # ── Register dark-mode refresh callback ──
        register_theme_callback(self._on_theme_changed)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # MENU BAR

    def _build_menu_bar(self):

        self.menu_bar = tk.Menu(self.root)
        self.root.configure(menu=self.menu_bar)

        self._build_file_menu()
        self._build_edit_menu()
        self._build_view_menu()
        self._build_tools_menu()
        self._build_help_menu()

        file_menu = tk.Menu(self.menu_bar, tearoff=0)

        file_menu.add_command(

        tools_menu = tk.Menu(self.menu_bar, tearoff=0)

        tools_menu.add_command(
            label="Enhance Audio",
            command=self._menu_enhance_audio,

        tools_menu.add_command(
            label="Reload Video",
            command=self._menu_reload_video,

        self.menu_bar.add_cascade(label="Tools", menu=tools_menu)

        help_menu = tk.Menu(self.menu_bar, tearoff=0)

        help_menu.add_command(
            label="User Manual",
            command=self._show_user_manual,
        )
        help_menu.add_command(
            label="Keyboard Shortcuts",
            command=self._show_shortcuts,
        )
        help_menu.add_command(
            label="Troubleshooting / Diagnostics",
            command=self._show_diagnostics,
        )

        help_menu.add_separator()

        help_menu.add_command(
            label="About Subtitle Studio",
            command=self._show_about,
        )

        self.menu_bar.add_cascade(label="Help", menu=help_menu)

    def _set_theme_mode(self, mode: str):
        wants_dark = mode == "dark"
        if wants_dark == is_dark():
            return
        self._toggle_theme()

    def _set_preview_resolution(self, value: str):
        self.prefs.set("preview_resolution", value)
        self.prefs.save()

        if self.edit_ui and "_preview_res" in self.edit_ui:
            self.edit_ui["_preview_res"]["value"] = value
            self.state.log(f"Preview resolution set from menu: {value}")
        else:
            self.state.log(f"Preview resolution preference set: {value}")

    def _menu_open_video(self):
        """Open a video directly from the File menu."""
        from tkinter import filedialog

        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Select Video File",
            filetypes=[
                ("Video files", "*.mp4 *.mov *.avi *.mkv *.mxf *.wmv"),
                ("All files", "*.*"),
            ],
        )
        if not path:
            return

        self._load_video_path(path)

    def _menu_open_subtitles(self):
        """Open subtitles directly from the File menu."""
        from tkinter import filedialog

        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Select Subtitle File",
            filetypes=[
                ("Subtitle files", "*.srt *.vtt"),
                ("All files", "*.*"),
            ],
        )
        if not path:
            return

        self._load_subtitle_path(path, auto=False)
        self._activate_edit_after_media_change()

    def _same_path(self, path_a, path_b):
        if not path_a or not path_b:
            return False
        try:
            return os.path.abspath(path_a) == os.path.abspath(path_b)
        except Exception:
            return path_a == path_b

    def _timestamp(self):
        import datetime
        return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

    def _prepare_state_for_video_load(self, new_video_path):
        previous_video_path = self.state.video_path
        same_video = self._same_path(previous_video_path, new_video_path)

        self.state.force_player_reload = True

        # A manual video load starts a clean media session.
        self.state.clear_audio_state()
        self.state.sub_path = None
        self.state.cues = []
        self.state.original_cues = []
        self.state.sections = []

        try:
            self.state._undo_stack.clear()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if not previous_video_path:
            self.state.log("Starting clean media session.")
        elif same_video:
            self.state.log("Reloading current video as clean media session.")
        else:
            self.state.log("New source video selected. Cleared subtitles and sections.")

    def _load_video_path(self, path: str):
        """Load a video path directly, without using the Import tab."""
        from core.ffmpeg import probe_video
        from core.parser import seconds_to_display
        from core.auto import find_matching_subtitle

        self.state.log(f"Loading video: {os.path.basename(path)}")

        try:
            info = probe_video(self.state.ffprobe_path, path)
        except RuntimeError as e:
            self.state.log(f"[ERROR] {e}")
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Video Load Failed",
                str(e),
                parent=self.root,
            )
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(f"[ERROR] Failed to load video: {e}")
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Video Load Failed",
                str(e),
                parent=self.root,
            )
            return False

        self._prepare_state_for_video_load(path)

        self.state.video_path = path
        self.state.video_info = info

        matching_sub = find_matching_subtitle(path)
        if matching_sub:
            self.state.log(
                f"Found subtitle: {os.path.basename(matching_sub)}"
            )
            self._load_subtitle_path(matching_sub, auto=True)

        ts = self._timestamp()
        self.state.log_session(
            f"[{ts}] Loaded video: {os.path.basename(path)}"
        )
        self.state.log(
            f"[OK] Video: {info['width']}x{info['height']}, "
            f"{seconds_to_display(info['duration'])}, "
            f"{info['size_mb']}MB"
        )

        self._activate_edit_after_media_change()
        return True

    def _load_subtitle_path(self, path: str, auto: bool = False):
        """Load a subtitle path directly, without using the Import tab."""
        from core.parser import parse_subtitle_file
        from core.auto import auto_fix_on_load
        from core.state import Cue

        if auto:
            log_prefix = "Auto-loaded subtitle"
        else:
            log_prefix = "Loaded subtitle"
            self.state.log(
                f"Loading subtitle: {os.path.basename(path)}"
            )

        try:
            cues = parse_subtitle_file(path)
        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(f"[ERROR] Failed to parse subtitle: {e}")
            if not auto:
                messagebox.showerror(
                    "Subtitle Load Failed",
                    str(e),
                    parent=self.root,
                )
            return False

        if not cues:
            self.state.log("[ERROR] No subtitle cues found in file.")
            if not auto:
                messagebox.showerror(
                    "Subtitle Load Failed",
                    "No subtitle cues found in file.",
                    parent=self.root,
                )
            return False

        self.state.sub_path = path
        self.state.cues = cues
        self.state.original_cues = [
            Cue(c.index, c.start, c.end, c.text) for c in cues
        ]

        self.state.cues, fix_count = auto_fix_on_load(
            self.state.cues,
            log_fn=lambda m: self.state.log_subtitle(
                f"[{self._timestamp()}] {m}"
            ),
        )

        ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower()
        fmt = "WebVTT" if ext == ".vtt" else "SRT"

        ts = self._timestamp()
        self.state.log_session(
            f"[{ts}] {log_prefix}: {os.path.basename(path)} "
            f"({len(cues)} cues, {fmt})"
        )

        if auto:
            if fix_count > 0:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Auto-detected & loaded "
                    f"{os.path.basename(path)} "
                    f"({fix_count} auto-fixes applied)"
                )
            else:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Auto-detected & loaded "
                    f"{os.path.basename(path)}"
                )
        else:
            if fix_count > 0:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Subtitles: {len(self.state.cues)} cues "
                    f"({fmt}) — {fix_count} auto-fixes applied"
                )
            else:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Subtitles: {len(self.state.cues)} cues "
                    f"({fmt})"
                )

        return True

    def _activate_edit_after_media_change(self):
        """Refresh or activate Edit tab after media/subtitle loading."""
        already_on_edit = self.active_tab == "edit"

        self.select_tab("edit")

        # select_tab() returns early if already on edit,
        # so manually run the activation hook in that case.
        if already_on_edit:
            try:
                fn = self.edit_ui.get("on_tab_activated")
                if fn:
                    fn()
            except Exception as e:
                self.state.log(f"[!!] Edit refresh failed: {e}")
    
    def _menu_find_replace(self):
        """Navigate to Edit tab and focus the Find field."""
        self.select_tab("edit")

        try:
            find_entry = self.edit_ui.get("find_entry")
            if find_entry:
                find_entry.focus_set()
                try:
                    find_entry.select_range(0, "end")
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(f"[!] Could not focus Find / Replace: {e}")

        self.select_tab("edit")
        self.state.log("Reload video requested from Tools menu.")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # HEADER
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_header(self):
        self.header_wrap = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.root, fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.header_wrap.pack(fill="x")

        self.header = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.header_wrap, fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            corner_radius=0,
            height=theme.HEADER_HEIGHT)
        self.header.pack(fill="x")
        self.header.pack_propagate(False)

        self.header_title = ctk.CTkLabel(
            self.header, text="Subtitle Studio",
            font=theme.FONT_HEADER,
            text_color=theme.BLACK,
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header_title.pack(
            side="left", padx=16, pady=10)

        self.header_version = ctk.CTkLabel(
            self.header, text=f"v{APP_VERSION}",
            font=theme.FONT_VERSION,
            text_color="#000000",
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header_version.pack(
            side="left", pady=10)

        # ── Right-side quick control ──
        # Full app actions live in the native menu bar.
        # Header stays clean: branding + theme toggle only.
        self.proj_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.header, fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.proj_frame.pack(
            side="right", padx=(0, 12))

        self.theme_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
            self.proj_frame,
            text=(ICON_MOON if not is_dark()
                  else ICON_SUN),
            font=("Arial", 16), width=36, height=28,
            fg_color="#D4580A",
            text_color=theme.WHITE,
            hover_color="#B84A08",
            corner_radius=4,
            command=self._toggle_theme)
        self.theme_btn.pack(side="left")

        self.header_edge = tk.Frame(
            self.header_wrap,
            bg=theme.HEADER_EDGE, height=2)
        self.header_edge.pack(fill="x")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TAB BAR
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_tab_bar(self):
        self.tab_bar = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.root, fg_color=theme.WHITE,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.tab_bar.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD)

        self.tab_bar_line = tk.Frame(
            self.tab_bar, bg=theme.BORDER, height=1)
        self.tab_bar_line.pack(fill="x")

        self.tab_buttons_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.tab_bar, fg_color=theme.WHITE,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.tab_buttons_frame.pack(fill="x")

        self.tab_buttons: dict = {}
        self.tab_labels: dict = {}

        for label_text, key in TAB_DEFS:
            btn_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
                self.tab_buttons_frame,
                fg_color=theme.WHITE,
                corner_radius=0, cursor="hand2")
            btn_frame.pack(side="left")

            accent_bar = tk.Frame(
                btn_frame, bg=theme.WHITE, height=3)
            accent_bar.pack(fill="x")

            lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
                btn_frame, text=label_text,
                font=theme.FONT_TAB,
                text_color="#999999",
                fg_color=theme.WHITE,
                cursor="hand2")
            lbl.pack(padx=16, pady=8)

            self.tab_buttons[key] = btn_frame
            self.tab_labels[key] = lbl
            btn_frame._accent_bar = accent_bar

            for widget in (btn_frame, lbl, accent_bar):
                widget.bind(
                    "<Button-1>",
                    lambda e, k=key:
                        self.select_tab(k))
                widget.bind(
                    "<Enter>",
                    lambda e, f=btn_frame, l=lbl,
                    k=key: self._tab_hover(
                        f, l, k, True))
                widget.bind(
                    "<Leave>",
                    lambda e, f=btn_frame, l=lbl,
                    k=key: self._tab_hover(
                        f, l, k, False))

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TAB CONTENT FRAMES (LAZY LOADING)
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_tab_frames(self):
        self.tab_container = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.root, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.tab_container.pack(
            fill="both", expand=True,
            padx=theme.PAD)

        self.tab_frames: dict = {}
        self.edit_ui = {}
        self.export_ui = {}
        self.logs_ui = {}

        for _, key in TAB_DEFS:
            frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
                self.tab_container,
                fg_color=theme.TAB_BG,
                corner_radius=0)
            frame.place(relx=0, rely=0,
                        relwidth=1, relheight=1)
            self.tab_frames[key] = frame

            # Placeholder — replaced on first visit
            placeholder = ctk.CTkLabel(
                frame,
                text=f"Loading {key.title()} tab...",
                font=("Arial", 11),
                text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
                fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
            placeholder.place(
                relx=0.5, rely=0.4,
                anchor="center")
            frame._placeholder = placeholder

    def _build_tab_lazy(self, key: str):
        """Build a tab on first visit."""
        if key in self._tabs_built:
            return

        frame = self.tab_frames[key]

        # Remove placeholder
        if hasattr(frame, "_placeholder"):
            try:
                frame._placeholder.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            if key == "edit":
                if build_edit_tab is None:
                    raise ImportError(
                        "Module not loaded")
                self.edit_ui = build_edit_tab(
                    frame, self.state)

            elif key == "export":
                if build_export_tab is None:
                    raise ImportError(
                        "Module not loaded")
                self.export_ui = build_export_tab(
                    frame, self.state,
                    progress_fn=self.set_progress)

            elif key == "logs":
                if build_logs_tab is None:
                    raise ImportError(
                        "Module not loaded")
                self.logs_ui = build_logs_tab(
                    frame, self.state)

            self._tabs_built.add(key)

        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!!] {key.title()} tab failed: {e}")
            self._show_tab_error(key, e)

    def _show_tab_error(self, key, error):
        """Show error message in a failed tab."""
        try:
            err_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
                self.tab_frames[key],
                text=(
                    f"⚠ {key.title()} tab failed "
                    f"to load.\n\n"
                    f"Error: {error}\n\n"
                    f"Other tabs may still work.\n"
                    f"Check log for details."),
                font=("Arial", 12),
                text_color=theme.ERROR,
                fg_color=theme.TAB_BG,
                justify="center")
            err_label.place(
                relx=0.5, rely=0.4,
                anchor="center")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TAB HOVER + SWITCHING
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _tab_hover(self, btn_frame, lbl, key,
                   entering):
        if key == self.active_tab:
            return
        if entering:
            hover_bg = (theme.SEPARATOR if is_dark()
                        else "#F0F0F0")
            btn_frame.configure(fg_color=hover_bg)
            lbl.configure(fg_color=hover_bg)
        else:
            btn_frame.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)
            lbl.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)

    def select_tab(self, key: str):
        if key == self.active_tab:
            return

        # Lazy-build on first visit
        self._build_tab_lazy(key)

        try:
            self.tab_frames[key].tkraise()
        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!!] Tab frame error: {e}")
            return

        try:
            for tab_key in self.tab_buttons:
                btn = self.tab_buttons[tab_key]
                lbl = self.tab_labels[tab_key]
                if tab_key == key:
                    btn._accent_bar.configure(
                        bg=theme.ACCENT)
                    btn.configure(
                        fg_color=theme.WHITE)
                    lbl.configure(
                        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                        fg_color=theme.WHITE)
                else:
                    btn._accent_bar.configure(
                        bg=theme.WHITE)
                    btn.configure(
                        fg_color=theme.WHITE)
                    lbl.configure(
                        text_color="#999999",
                        fg_color=theme.WHITE)
        except Exception:
            pass

        self.active_tab = key
        self.prefs.set("last_tab", key)

        # Tab activation hooks
        hooks = {
            "edit": (self.edit_ui,
                     "on_tab_activated"),
            "export": (self.export_ui,
                       "on_tab_activated"),
            "logs": (self.logs_ui,
                     "on_tab_activated"),
        }
        if key in hooks:
            ui, fn_name = hooks[key]
            try:
                if fn_name in ui:
                    ui[fn_name]()
            except Exception as e:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[!!] {key} activation: {e}")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # PROGRESS BAR
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_progress(self):
        self.progress_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
            self.root, fg_color=theme.WHITE,
            corner_radius=0)
        self.progress_frame.pack(
            fill="x", padx=theme.PAD,
            pady=(theme.PAD_XS, 0))

        self.progress_status = ctk.CTkLabel(
            self.progress_frame, text="",
            font=("Arial", 9),
            text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
            fg_color=theme.WHITE,
            anchor="w", corner_radius=0)
        self.progress_status.pack(
            fill="x", padx=0, pady=(0, 1))

        self.progress_var = tk.DoubleVar(value=0)
        self.progress_bar = ctk.CTkProgressBar(
            self.progress_frame,
            variable=self.progress_var,
            progress_color=theme.ACCENT,
            fg_color=theme.BORDER,
            corner_radius=4,
            height=theme.PROGRESS_HEIGHT)
        self.progress_bar.pack(fill="x")
        self.progress_bar.set(0)

        self.state._progress_fn = (
            self._set_progress_safe)
        self.state._progress_status_fn = (
            self._set_status_safe)

    def _set_progress_safe(self, value: float):
        try:
            self.root.after(
                0, lambda v=value:
                    self.progress_var.set(
                        min(max(v, 0), 100) / 100))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _set_status_safe(self, text: str):
        try:
            self.root.after(
                0, lambda t=text:
                    self.progress_status.configure(
                        text=t))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def set_progress(self, value: float):
        self._set_progress_safe(value)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # LOG BOX (bounded)
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_log(self):
        self.log_box = tk.Text(
            self.root, height=3,
            font=theme.FONT_LOG,
            bg=theme.LOG_BG, fg=theme.LOG_FG,
            relief="flat", padx=6, pady=4,
            state="disabled", wrap="word")
        self.log_box.pack(
            fill="x", padx=theme.PAD,
            pady=(theme.PAD_XS, theme.PAD))
        self._log_line_count = 0

    def _poll_log(self):
        if self._shutting_down:
            return
        try:
            batch = 0
            while (not self.state.msg_queue.empty()
                   and batch < 20):
                msg = (
                    self.state.msg_queue.get_nowait())
                self.log_box.configure(state="normal")
                self.log_box.insert(
                    "end", msg + "\n")
                self._log_line_count += 1
                batch += 1

                # Trim old lines if over limit
                if self._log_line_count > MAX_LOG_LINES:
                    self.log_box.delete(
                        "1.0",
                        f"{self._log_line_count - MAX_LOG_LINES}.0")
                    self._log_line_count = MAX_LOG_LINES

            if batch > 0:
                self.log_box.see("end")
                self.log_box.configure(
                    state="disabled")
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.root.after(LOG_POLL_MS, self._poll_log)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # FFMPEG CHECK
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _check_tools(self):
        for name, path in [
            ("ffmpeg", self.state.ffmpeg_path),
            ("ffprobe", self.state.ffprobe_path),
        ]:
            if os.path.isfile(path):
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] {name}: {path}")
            elif shutil.which(path):
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] {name}: {path} "
                    f"(via PATH)")
            elif shutil.which(name):
                resolved = shutil.which(name)
                if name == "ffmpeg":
                    self.state.ffmpeg_path = resolved
                else:
                    self.state.ffprobe_path = resolved
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] {name}: {resolved} "
                    f"(via PATH)")
            else:
                self.state.log(
                    f"[!!] {name}: not found at "
                    f"'{path}' — place in bin/ "
                    f"or add to PATH")

    def _log_diagnostics(self):
        """Log import errors from startup."""
        for mod, err in _IMPORT_ERRORS.items():
            self.state.log(
                f"[!!] Module {mod}: {err}")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # AUTOSAVE
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _start_autosave(self):
        def _tick():
            if self._shutting_down:
                return
            try:
                if self.state.cues:
                    self.state.autosave()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.root.after(AUTOSAVE_MS, _tick)
        self.root.after(AUTOSAVE_MS, _tick)

    def _check_autosave_recovery(self):
        if not self.state.has_autosave():
            return
        try:
            meta = {}
            meta_path = (
                self.state.get_autosave_meta_path())
            if os.path.isfile(meta_path):
                with open(meta_path, "r") as f:
                    meta = json.load(f)

            timestamp = meta.get(
                "timestamp", "unknown")
            cue_count = meta.get("cue_count", "?")
            source = meta.get("sub_path", "unknown")

            from tkinter import messagebox
            recover = messagebox.askyesno(
                "Recover Autosave",
                f"An autosaved session was found.\n\n"
                f"  Time:  {timestamp}\n"
                f"  Cues:  {cue_count}\n"
                f"  Source: "
                f"{os.path.basename(str(source))}"
                f"\n\n"
                f"Would you like to recover it?")

            if recover:
                if self.state.load_autosave():
                    if meta.get("video_path"):
                        self.state.video_path = (
                            meta["video_path"])
                    if meta.get("sub_path"):
                        self.state.sub_path = (
                            meta["sub_path"])
                    if meta.get("language"):
                        self.state.language = (
                            meta["language"])
                    self.state.log(
                        f"[OK] Recovered "
                        f"{len(self.state.cues)} "
                        f"cues from autosave.")
                    self.state.log_session(
                        f"Recovered autosave from "
                        f"{timestamp}")
                    self.select_tab("edit")
                else:
                    self.state.log(
                        "[!] Failed to load "
                        "autosave.")
            else:
                self.state.clear_autosave()
                self.state.log(
                    "Autosave discarded.")
        except Exception as e:
            self.state.log(
                f"[!] Autosave recovery: {e}")
            self.state.clear_autosave()

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # PROJECT SAVE / OPEN / RECENT
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _save_project(self) -> bool:
        from tkinter import filedialog
        if (not self.state.cues
                and not self.state.video_path):
            self.state.log("[!] Nothing to save.")
            return False

        if self._project_path:
            success = self.state.save_project(
                self._project_path)
            if success:
                name = os.path.basename(
                    self._project_path)
                self.state.log(
                    f"[OK] Project saved: {name}")
                self.state.log_session(
                    f"Project saved: {name}")
                self.prefs.add_recent(
                    self._project_path)
                self.prefs.save()
                self._refresh_recent_menu()
                return True
            else:
                self.state.log("[!] Save failed.")
                return False
        return self._save_project_as()

    def _save_project_as(self) -> bool:
        from tkinter import filedialog
        base = "untitled"
        if self.state.video_path:
            base = os.path.splitext(
                os.path.basename(
                    self.state.video_path))[0]

        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            title="Save Project",
            defaultextension=".ssproj",
            initialfile=f"{base}.ssproj",
            confirmoverwrite=True,
            filetypes=[
                ("Subtitle Studio Project",
                 "*.ssproj"),
                ("All files", "*.*")])
        if not path:
            return False

        success = self.state.save_project(path)
        if success:
            self._project_path = path
            name = os.path.basename(path)
            self.root.title(
                f"Subtitle Studio v{APP_VERSION}"
                f" — {name}")
            self.state.log(
                f"[OK] Project saved: {name}")
            self.state.log_session(
                f"Project saved: {name}")
            self.prefs.add_recent(path)
            self.prefs.save()
            self._refresh_recent_menu()
            return True
        else:
            self.state.log("[!] Save failed.")
            return False

    def _open_project(self):
        from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox
        if self.state.cues:
            save_first = messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                "Open Project",
                "You have unsaved changes.\n\n"
                "• Yes — Save first\n"
                "• No — Discard and open\n"
                "• Cancel — Go back")
            if save_first is None:
                return
            elif save_first:
                if not self._save_project():
                    return

        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Open Project",
            filetypes=[
                ("Subtitle Studio Project",
                 "*.ssproj"),
                ("All files", "*.*")])
        if not path:
            return

        self._load_project_path(path)

    def _on_recent_select(self, name: str):
        """Handle recent project dropdown selection."""
        self.recent_dropdown.set("Recent")
        if name == "(no recent projects)":
            return

        recent = self.prefs.get_recent()
        for path in recent:
            if os.path.basename(path) == name:
                if self.state.cues:
                    from tkinter import messagebox
                    save_first = (
                        messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                            "Open Recent",
                            "Save current work first?"))
                    if save_first is None:
                        return
                    elif save_first:
                        if not self._save_project():
                            return

                self._load_project_path(path)
                return

        self.state.log(
            f"[!] Recent project not found: {name}")

    def _load_project_path(self, path: str):
        """Load a project from a file path."""
        success = self.state.load_project(path)
        if success:
            self._project_path = path
            name = os.path.basename(path)
            self.root.title(
                f"Subtitle Studio v{APP_VERSION}"
                f" — {name}")
            self.state.log(
                f"[OK] Project loaded: {name}")
            self.state.log_session(
                f"Project loaded: {name}")
            self.prefs.add_recent(path)
            self.prefs.save()
            self._refresh_recent_menu()
            self._refresh_after_project_load()
        else:
            self.state.log(
                "[!] Failed to load project.")

    def _refresh_recent_menu(self):
        """Update the recent projects dropdown and native menu."""
        recent = self.prefs.get_recent()
        if recent:
            names = [os.path.basename(p)
                     for p in recent]
        else:
            names = ["(no recent projects)"]

        if hasattr(self, "recent_dropdown"):
            try:
                self.recent_dropdown.configure(values=names)
                self.recent_dropdown.set("Recent")
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            self.recent_menu.delete(0, "end")
            if recent:
                for path in recent:
                    name = os.path.basename(path)
                    self.recent_menu.add_command(
                        label=name,
                        command=lambda p=path: self._load_project_path(p),
                    )
            else:
                self.recent_menu.add_command(
                    label="(no recent projects)",
                    state="disabled",
                )
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _refresh_after_project_load(self):
        if hasattr(self, "edit_ui") and self.edit_ui:
            if "populate_subtitle_list" in self.edit_ui:
                self.edit_ui[
                    "populate_subtitle_list"]()
            if "refresh_sections" in self.edit_ui:
                self.edit_ui["refresh_sections"]()
            if "player" in self.edit_ui:
                player = self.edit_ui["player"]
                if (self.state.video_path
                        and self.state.video_info
                        and not player.loaded):
                    player.load(
                        self.state.video_path,
                        self.state.video_info.get(
                            "duration", 0))
        self.select_tab("edit")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # DARK MODE TOGGLE
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _toggle_theme(self):
        toggle_mode(self.root)
        self.theme_btn.configure(
            text=(ICON_MOON if not is_dark()
                  else ICON_SUN))
        self.prefs.set("dark_mode", is_dark())
        self.prefs.save()

    def _on_theme_changed(self):
        self.root.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)

        self.header_wrap.configure(
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header.configure(
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header_title.configure(
            text_color="#000000",
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header_version.configure(
            text_color="#000000",
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.proj_frame.configure(
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT)
        self.header_edge.configure(
            bg=theme.HEADER_EDGE)

        self.tab_bar.configure(
            fg_color=theme.WHITE)
        self.tab_bar_line.configure(
            bg=theme.BORDER)
        self.tab_buttons_frame.configure(
            fg_color=theme.WHITE)

        for tab_key in self.tab_buttons:
            btn = self.tab_buttons[tab_key]
            lbl = self.tab_labels[tab_key]
            btn.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)
            lbl.configure(fg_color=theme.WHITE)
            if tab_key == self.active_tab:
                btn._accent_bar.configure(
                    bg=theme.ACCENT)
                lbl.configure(
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
            else:
                btn._accent_bar.configure(
                    bg=theme.WHITE)
                lbl.configure(
                    text_color="#999999")

        self.tab_container.configure(
            fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        for frame in self.tab_frames.values():
            frame.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)

        self.progress_frame.configure(
            fg_color=theme.WHITE)
        self.progress_status.configure(
            text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
            fg_color=theme.WHITE)
        self.progress_bar.configure(
            progress_color=theme.ACCENT,
            fg_color=theme.BORDER)

        self.log_box.configure(
            bg=theme.LOG_BG, fg=theme.LOG_FG)

        # Recent dropdown
        try:
            self.recent_dropdown.configure(
                dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
                dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                dropdown_hover_color=(
                    theme.TREE_SELECT))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _show_diagnostics(self):
        popup = ctk.CTkToplevel(self.root)
        popup.title("System Diagnostics")
        popup.geometry("500x450")
        popup.resizable(False, False)
        popup.transient(self.root)
        popup.grab_set()

        try:
            x = self.root.winfo_x() + 150
            y = self.root.winfo_y() + 80
            popup.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
        except Exception:
            pass

        ctk.CTkLabel(
            popup, text="🔧  System Diagnostics",
            font=("Georgia", 14, "bold"),
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT
        ).pack(pady=(16, 12))

        frame = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(
            popup, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            corner_radius=0)
        frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                   padx=16, pady=(0, 12))

        results = run_diagnostics(self._app_dir)

        for level, msg in results:
            color = {
                "OK": theme.SUCCESS,
                "INFO": theme.SECTION_TEXT,
                "WARN": theme.WARNING,
                "ERROR": theme.ERROR,
            }.get(level, theme.DARK_TEXT)

            icon = {
                "OK": "✓", "INFO": "ℹ",
                "WARN": "⚠", "ERROR": "✗",
            }.get(level, "?")

            row = ctk.CTkFrame(
                frame, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
                corner_radius=0)
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=1)

            ctk.CTkLabel(
                row,
                text=f"{icon} [{level}]",
                width=80,
                font=("Consolas", 10),
                text_color=color,
                fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
                anchor="w"
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))

            ctk.CTkLabel(
                row, text=msg,
                font=("Arial", 10),
                text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
                anchor="w"
            ).pack(side="left", fill="x",
                   expand=True)

        # Extra info
        ctk.CTkLabel(
            frame,
            text=(
                f"\nApp dir: {self._app_dir}\n"
                f"Prefs: {self.prefs._path}\n"
                f"Tabs loaded: "
                f"{', '.join(self._tabs_built) or 'none'}"),
            font=("Consolas", 9),
            text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
            fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            anchor="w", justify="left"
        ).pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0))

        ctk.CTkButton(
            popup, text="Close", width=100,
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
            command=popup.destroy
        ).pack(pady=(0, 12))

    # SHORTCUTS POPUP

    def _show_shortcuts(self):
        popup = ctk.CTkToplevel(self.root)
        popup.title("Keyboard Shortcuts")
        popup.geometry("440x620")
        popup.resizable(False, False)
        popup.transient(self.root)
        popup.grab_set()

        try:
            x = self.root.winfo_x() + 200
            y = self.root.winfo_y() + 60
            popup.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
        except Exception:
            pass

        ctk.CTkLabel(
            popup, text="⌨  Keyboard Shortcuts",
            font=("Georgia", 14, "bold"),
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT
        ).pack(pady=(16, 12))

        shortcuts = [
            ("PLAYBACK", None),
            ("Space", "Play / Pause"),
            ("L", "Play forward"),
            ("K", "Pause"),
            ("J", "Rewind 5 seconds"),
            ("← / →", "Step 1 frame (paused)"),
            ("Shift+← / →", "Step 10 frames"),
            ("", ""),
            ("SECTION MARKING", None),
            ("I", "Mark IN point"),
            ("O", "Mark OUT point"),
            ("P", "Add section from IN/OUT"),
            ("C", "Clear IN/OUT marks"),
            ("", ""),
            ("SUBTITLE EDITING", None),
            ("[", "Set cue START to frame"),
            ("]", "Set cue END to frame"),
            ("Double-click", "Edit cell inline"),
            ("Ctrl+Click", "Play from cue"),
            ("Enter", "Save timecode edit"),
            ("Ctrl+Enter", "Save text edit"),
            ("Escape", "Cancel edit"),
            ("Right-click", "Spelling suggestions"),
            ("", ""),
            ("NAVIGATION", None),
            ("Ctrl+1/2/3", "Switch tabs"),
            ("", ""),
            ("GENERAL", None),
            ("Ctrl+N", "New project"),
            ("Ctrl+O", "Open project"),
            ("Ctrl+S", "Save project"),
            ("Ctrl+Z", "Undo"),
            ("Ctrl+1", "Go to Edit"),
            ("Ctrl+2", "Go to Export"),
            ("Ctrl+3", "Go to Log"),
            ("F11", "Toggle fullscreen"),
        ]

        frame = ctk.CTkScrollableFrame(
            popup, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            corner_radius=0)
        frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                   padx=16, pady=(0, 12))

        for key_text, action in shortcuts:
            if action is None:
                ctk.CTkLabel(
                    frame, text=key_text,
                    font=("Arial", 10, "bold"),
                    text_color=theme.ACCENT,
                    fg_color=theme.CARD_BG
                ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(10, 2))

            if key_text == "" and action == "":
                continue

                row, text=key_text, width=130,

                corner_radius=4, anchor="w"
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

            ctk.CTkLabel(
                row, text=action,
                font=("Arial", 10),
                text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
                fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, anchor="w"
            ).pack(side="left", fill="x",
                   expand=True)

        ctk.CTkButton(
            popup, text="Close", width=100,
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
            command=popup.destroy
        ).pack(pady=(0, 12))

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _bind_shortcuts(self):
        r = self.root

        # Playback
        r.bind("<space>", self._on_space)
        r.bind("<F11>", self._on_fullscreen)

        # Undo / Save / Open / New
        r.bind("<Control-z>", self._on_undo)
        r.bind("<Control-Z>", self._on_undo)
        r.bind("<Control-s>",
               lambda e: self._save_project())
        r.bind("<Control-S>",
               lambda e: self._save_project())
        r.bind("<Control-o>",
               lambda e: self._open_project())
        r.bind("<Control-O>",
               lambda e: self._open_project())
        r.bind("<Control-n>",
               lambda e: self._new_project())
        r.bind("<Control-N>",
               lambda e: self._new_project())

        # Tab switching: Ctrl+1/2/3
        tab_keys = [
            ("edit", "1"),
            ("export", "2"),
            ("logs", "3")]
        for tab_key, num in tab_keys:
            r.bind(
                f"<Control-Key-{num}>",
                lambda e, k=tab_key:
                    self.select_tab(k))
            r.bind(
                f"<Control-KeyPress-{num}>",
                lambda e, k=tab_key:
                    self.select_tab(k))

        # J-K-L
        r.bind("<j>", lambda e: self._jkl("j", e))
        r.bind("<J>", lambda e: self._jkl("j", e))
        r.bind("<k>", lambda e: self._jkl("k", e))
        r.bind("<K>", lambda e: self._jkl("k", e))
        r.bind("<l>", lambda e: self._jkl("l", e))
        r.bind("<L>", lambda e: self._jkl("l", e))

    def _is_typing(self, event=None):
        focused = self.root.focus_get()
        if focused is None:
            return False
        if isinstance(focused, (
                tk.Entry, tk.Text,
                ctk.CTkEntry, ctk.CTkTextbox)):
            return True
        wc = str(focused.winfo_class())
        return wc in (
            "Entry", "Text",
            "TEntry", "TCombobox")

    def _on_space(self, event=None):
        if self._is_typing(event):
            return
        if self.active_tab != "edit":
            return
        # Delegate to the tab's play/pause toggle
        toggle_fn = self.edit_ui.get("on_play_pause")
        if toggle_fn:
            toggle_fn()

    def _jkl(self, key, event):
        if self._is_typing(event):
            return
        if self.active_tab != "edit":
            return
        toggle_fn = self.edit_ui.get("on_play_pause")
        player = self.edit_ui.get("player")
        if not player:
            return
        if key == "l" and not player.playing:
            if toggle_fn:
                toggle_fn()
            else:
                player.play()
        elif key == "k" and player.playing:
            if toggle_fn:
                toggle_fn()
            else:
                player.pause()
        elif key == "j":
            player.seek(
                max(player.get_position() - 5.0, 0))

    def _on_fullscreen(self, event=None):
        self._is_fullscreen = not self._is_fullscreen
        self.root.attributes(
            "-fullscreen", self._is_fullscreen)

    def _on_undo(self, event=None):
        if self._is_typing(event):
            return
        if self.state.undo():
            self.state.log("[OK] Undo successful.")
            fn = self.edit_ui.get(
                "populate_subtitle_list")
            if fn:
                fn()
        else:
            self.state.log("[!] Nothing to undo.")

    def _new_project(self):
        """Start a fresh project — full reset."""
        from tkinter import messagebox
        if self.state.cues or self.state.video_path:
            save_first = messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                "New Project",
                "Save current work first?\n\n"
                "• Yes — Save then start new\n"
                "• No — Discard and start new\n"
                "• Cancel — Go back")
            if save_first is None:
                return
            elif save_first:
                if not self._save_project():
                    return

        # Reset all state
        self.state.reset()
        self.state.force_player_reload = False
        self._project_path = None
        self.root.title(
            f"Subtitle Studio v{APP_VERSION}")

        # Reset edit tab if built
        if "edit" in self._tabs_built:
            try:
                if "player" in self.edit_ui:
                    self.edit_ui["player"].reset()
                if "populate_subtitle_list" in (
                        self.edit_ui):
                    self.edit_ui[
                        "populate_subtitle_list"]()
                if "refresh_sections" in self.edit_ui:
                    self.edit_ui[
                        "refresh_sections"]()
                # Clear active cue label
                if "active_cue_label" in self.edit_ui:
                    self.edit_ui[
                        "active_cue_label"].configure(
                        text="No active cue")
                # Clear seek slider / waveform
                if "seek_slider" in self.edit_ui:
                    try:
                        self.edit_ui["seek_slider"].clear_waveform()
                        self.edit_ui["seek_slider"].clear_marks()
                        self.edit_ui["seek_slider"].set(0.0)
                        self.edit_ui["seek_slider"].configure(
                            from_=0,
                            to=100,
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                # Reset time displays
                if "time_label" in self.edit_ui:
                    self.edit_ui[
                        "time_label"].configure(
                        text="00:00:00")
                if "total_dur_label" in self.edit_ui:
                    self.edit_ui[
                        "total_dur_label"].configure(
                        text="00:00:00")
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Reset export tab if built
        if "export" in self._tabs_built:
            try:
                if "reset" in self.export_ui:
                    self.export_ui["reset"]()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Clear progress
        self.state.clear_progress()

        # Go to Edit tab — blank canvas
        already_on_edit = self.active_tab == "edit"
        self.select_tab("edit")

        # select_tab() returns early if already on Edit,
        # so manually run the activation hook.
        if already_on_edit:
            try:
                fn = self.edit_ui.get("on_tab_activated")
                if fn:
                    fn()
            except Exception as e:
                self.state.log(f"[!!] Edit refresh failed: {e}")

        self.state.log(
            "[OK] New project — ready. Use File > Open Video or File > Open Subtitles.")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # RUN + SHUTDOWN
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def run(self):
        self.root.protocol(
            "WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._on_close)
        self.root.mainloop()

    def _on_close(self):
        from tkinter import messagebox

        has_unsaved = bool(
            self.state.cues
            or self.state.sections
            or self.state.subtitle_log
            or self.state.video_path
            or self.state.sub_path)

        if has_unsaved:
            save = messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                "Close Subtitle Studio",
                "You have unsaved changes.\n\n"
                "Yes - Save and close\n"
                "No - Close without saving\n"
                "Cancel - Keep working")
            if save is None:
                return
            if save:
                if not self._save_project():
                    return

        self._shutting_down = True

        # Save window geometry + preferences
        try:
            geo = self.root.geometry()
            # Parse WxH+X+Y
            import re
            m = re.match(
                r"(\d+)x(\d+)\+(-?\d+)\+(-?\d+)",
                geo)
            if m:
                self.prefs.set(
                    "window_width", int(m.group(1)))
                self.prefs.set(
                    "window_height", int(m.group(2)))
                self.prefs.set(
                    "window_x", int(m.group(3)))
                self.prefs.set(
                    "window_y", int(m.group(4)))
            self.prefs.save()
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Clean up
        self.state.clear_autosave()
        try:
            if (hasattr(self, "edit_ui")
                    and "player" in self.edit_ui):
                self.edit_ui["player"].cleanup()
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.root.destroy()


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ENTRY POINT
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Self-test mode
    if "--self-test" in sys.argv:
        code = run_self_test()
        sys.exit(code)

    # Normal launch
    app = SubtitleStudio()
    app.run()

FILE: make_icon.py

"""One-time script: generate Subtitle Studio icon."""
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import os

sizes = [16, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256]
images = []

for s in sizes:
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (s, s), (253, 81, 8, 255))  # PwC orange
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # White "SS" text, centered
    try:
        font_size = int(s * 0.5)
        font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", font_size)
    except Exception:
        font = ImageFont.load_default()

    text = "SS"
    bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), text, font=font)
    tw = bbox[2] - bbox[0]
    th = bbox[3] - bbox[1]
    x = (s - tw) // 2
    y = (s - th) // 2 - bbox[1]
    draw.text((x, y), text, fill=(255, 255, 255, 255), font=font)
    images.append(img)

out = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "icon.ico")
images[0].save(out, format="ICO", sizes=[(s, s) for s in sizes],
               append_images=images[1:])
print(f"Created: {out}")

============================================================
Final working Nuitka packaging strategy
============================================================

The working packaging strategy is:

1. av / PyAV
   - Do not let Nuitka compile av.
   - Use:

        --nofollow-import-to=av

   - Manually copy:

        av\
        av.libs\
        python3.dll

   Reason:
        PyAV native extensions need av.libs and python3.dll available
        beside the packaged app.

2. audiolab
   - Do not include/compile audiolab with Nuitka.
   - Use:

        --nofollow-import-to=audiolab

   - Manually copy:

        audiolab\
        audiolab-*.dist-info

   Reason:
        Compiling audiolab with Nuitka caused:

        TypeError: iter() returned non-iterator of type 'nuitka_resource_reader_files'

3. audiolab dependencies
   - Do not manually copy the dependency closure.
   - Include pure-Python dependencies with Nuitka instead:

        requests
        urllib3
        certifi
        idna
        charset_normalizer
        jinja2
        markupsafe
        smart_open
        humanize
        wrapt
        http
        email
        netrc
        mimetypes
        ssl
        socket

   Reason:
        Manual dependency copying caused missing stdlib issues such as:

        No module named 'http.cookies'

4. numpy
   - Let Nuitka handle numpy.
   - Use:

        --include-package=numpy

   - Only manually copy:

        numpy.libs\

   Do not manually copy the whole site-packages\numpy folder.

5. pyrnnoise
   - Include with Nuitka:

        --include-package=pyrnnoise
        --include-package-data=pyrnnoise

   - Also manually copy pyrnnoise folder and rnnoise.dll for runtime safety.

6. soundfile
   - Include soundfile.
   - Manually copy _soundfile_data.

7. FFmpeg / MPV
   - Bundle under:

        bin\windows\

   Required runtime files:

        ffmpeg.exe

        mpv-2.dll
        d3dcompiler_43.dll

============================================================

Do not re-add:

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: mpv_lib_smoke_test.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import os
import sys
import threading
import time
import tkinter as tk
from pathlib import Path
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MPV_DIR = ROOT / "bin" / "windows"

# Make libmpv DLL discoverable before importing mpv.
if MPV_DIR.is_dir():
    try:
        os.add_dll_directory(str(MPV_DIR))
    except Exception:
        pass
    os.environ["PATH"] = str(MPV_DIR) + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", "")

try:
    import mpv
except Exception as e:
    print("[ERROR] Could not import/load python-mpv/libmpv.")
    print(e)
    print()
    print("Expected one of these DLLs in bin\\windows:")
    print("  mpv-2.dll")
    print("  libmpv-2.dll")
    raise


class MpvLibSmokeTest:
    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = root
        self.root.title("Subtitle Studio libmpv Smoke Test")
        self.root.geometry("1000x650")

        self.player = None
        self.duration = 0.0
        self._slider_dragging = False
        self._closed = False

        self.video_frame = tk.Frame(root, bg="black")
        self.video_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=(10, 5))

        controls = tk.Frame(root)
        controls.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=5)

        tk.Button(controls, text="Load Video", command=self.load_video).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="Play", command=self.play).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="Pause", command=self.pause).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="Stop", command=self.stop).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="-1 frame", command=self.frame_back).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="+1 frame", command=self.frame_fwd).pack(side="left", padx=3)

        self.time_label = tk.Label(controls, text="00:00:00 / 00:00:00", width=24)
        self.time_label.pack(side="right", padx=3)

        self.slider = tk.Scale(
            root,
            from_=0,
            to=100,
            orient="horizontal",
            showvalue=False,
            command=self.on_slider_move,
        )
        self.slider.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))
        self.slider.bind("<ButtonPress-1>", self.on_slider_press)
        self.slider.bind("<ButtonRelease-1>", self.on_slider_release)

        self.status = tk.Label(root, text="Starting libmpv...", anchor="w")
        self.status.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 8))

        self.root.update_idletasks()
        self.root.after(100, self.start_player)
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.close)

    def start_player(self):
        try:
            wid = str(self.video_frame.winfo_id())

            # python-mpv maps Python kwargs to MPV options:
            # input_default_bindings -> --input-default-bindings
            # keep_open -> --keep-open
            self.player = mpv.MPV(
                wid=wid,
                idle=True,
                keep_open=True,
                osc=False,
                input_default_bindings=False,
                input_vo_keyboard=False,
                vo="gpu-next",
                gpu_api="d3d11",
                hwdec="auto-safe",
                log_handler=self._mpv_log,
                loglevel="warn",
            )

            self.status.configure(text="libmpv started.")
            print("[OK] libmpv started")
            self.tick()

        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror("libmpv start failed", str(e))
            self.status.configure(text=f"libmpv start failed: {e}")
            print("[ERROR] libmpv start failed:", e)

    def _mpv_log(self, level, component, message):
        msg = message.strip()
        if msg:
            print(f"[MPV:{level}:{component}] {msg}")

    def _run_async(self, label, fn):
        def worker():
            try:
                fn()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ERROR] {label} failed: {e}")
                self.root.after(
                    0,
                    lambda: self.status.configure(text=f"{label} failed: {e}")
                )

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()

    def load_video(self):
        if not self.player:
            return

        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Choose video",
            filetypes=[
                ("Video files", "*.mp4 *.mov *.mkv *.avi *.m4v *.webm"),
                ("All files", "*.*"),
            ],
        )
        if not path:
            return

        self.status.configure(text=f"Loading: {path}")

        def worker():
            print(f"[MPV] Loading via libmpv: {path}")

            # Start paused. Then load.
            self.player.pause = True
            self.player.play(path)

            # Wait briefly for duration to become available.
            dur = 0.0
            for attempt in range(30):
                time.sleep(0.2)
                try:
                    dur = float(self.player.duration or 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    dur = 0.0

                print(f"[MPV] duration attempt {attempt + 1}: {dur}")
                if dur > 0:
                    break

            self.duration = max(0.0, dur)

            self.root.after(
                0,
                lambda: self.status.configure(
                    text=(
                        f"Loaded. Duration: {self.format_time(self.duration)}"
                        if self.duration > 0
                        else "Loaded, but duration is still 0."
                    )
                )
            )

        self._run_async("Load video", worker)

    def play(self):
        if self.player:
            self._run_async("Play", lambda: setattr(self.player, "pause", False))

    def pause(self):
        if self.player:
            self._run_async("Pause", lambda: setattr(self.player, "pause", True))

    def stop(self):
        if self.player:
            def worker():
                self.player.pause = True
                self.player.command("seek", 0.0, "absolute", "exact")
            self._run_async("Stop", worker)

    def frame_back(self):
        if self.player:
            self._run_async("Frame back", lambda: self.player.command("frame-back-step"))

    def frame_fwd(self):
        if self.player:
            self._run_async("Frame forward", lambda: self.player.command("frame-step"))

    def on_slider_press(self, event):
        self._slider_dragging = True

    def on_slider_move(self, value):
        pass

    def on_slider_release(self, event):
        self._slider_dragging = False
        if not self.player:
            return

        try:
            value = float(self.slider.get())
        except Exception:
            value = 0.0

        self._run_async(
            "Seek",
            lambda v=value: self.player.command("seek", v, "absolute", "exact"),
        )

    def tick(self):
        if self._closed:
            return

        if self.player:
            try:
                pos = float(self.player.time_pos or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                pos = 0.0

            try:
                dur = float(self.player.duration or self.duration or 0.0)
            except Exception:
                dur = self.duration

            if dur > 0:
                self.duration = dur
                self.slider.configure(to=max(1.0, dur))

            if not self._slider_dragging:
                self.slider.set(pos)

            self.time_label.configure(
                text=f"{self.format_time(pos)} / {self.format_time(self.duration)}"
            )

        self.root.after(200, self.tick)

    @staticmethod
    def format_time(seconds):
        try:
            seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
        except Exception:
            seconds = 0

        h = seconds // 3600
        m = (seconds % 3600) // 60
        s = seconds % 60
        return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"

    def close(self):
        self._closed = True

        try:
            if self.player:
                self.player.terminate()
        except Exception:
            pass

        self.root.destroy()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    root = tk.Tk()
    app = MpvLibSmokeTest(root)
    root.mainloop()

# MPV Preview Backend Setup

Subtitle Studio v5 uses `python-mpv` and `libmpv` for smooth preview playback.

## Python dependency

```powershell
python -m pip install python-mpv
```

## Windows DLL

Place `mpv-2.dll` in:

```text
bin/windows/mpv-2.dll
```

The app will also look for:

```text
bin/windows/mpv-1.dll
bin/windows/libmpv-2.dll
```

## Runtime behavior

If MPV is available, the Edit tab uses the MPV preview backend.

If MPV is unavailable, the app logs a diagnostic message and falls back to the legacy preview backend.

## Release packaging

Do not rely on the developer machine PATH. Release builds should bundle the MPV DLL and its required runtime files.
```

Then:

```powershell
git add MPV_SETUP.md
git commit -m "Document MPV preview setup"
```

This is useful before calling v5-finalization “ready.”

FILE: README.md
