﻿# Subtitle Studio

Subtitle Studio is a desktop application for preparing, editing, enhancing, and exporting subtitle-based video projects.

Current target release: **v5.0 ship candidate**  
Current confirmed branch: **v5-finalization**  
Current confirmed commit before documentation update: **edffeebb**

---

## What Subtitle Studio Does

Subtitle Studio helps creators and editors:

- Load a source video
- Generate a smoother preview/proxy
- Import subtitle files
- Review and edit subtitle timing/text
- Mark sections using IN/OUT points
- Enhance source audio using packaged denoise tooling
- Export subtitles, audio, and video outputs
- Burn subtitles into video when needed
- Keep detailed logs for troubleshooting

The intended workflow is:

```text
Load media -> Import subtitles -> Edit/review -> Enhance audio if needed -> Export deliverables
```

---

## Current Product Status

Subtitle Studio v5.0 is in a stable ship-candidate state for Windows internal/beta distribution.

Confirmed working areas:

- Video loading
- Bundled FFmpeg / FFprobe detection
- Proxy generation
- Preview playback through MPV
- Subtitle import and editing
- Section marking workflow
- Audio enhancement using `pyrnnoise`
- Source/enhanced audio state handling
- Video/audio/subtitle export
- Nuitka standalone Windows packaging
- Clean project export / handoff workflow

Primary remaining release step:

- Clean-machine QA on a Windows machine without the development environment installed

---

## Main Workspace

The intended v5 workspace structure is:

```text
Edit | Export | Log
```

### Edit

Used for:

- Loading and previewing media
- Importing subtitles
- Editing subtitle text and timing
- Marking IN/OUT ranges
- Managing section selections
- Playback and review

### Export

Used for:

- Exporting subtitle files
- Exporting audio
- Exporting video
- Burning subtitles into video
- Running export preflight checks

### Log

Used for:

- FFmpeg diagnostics
- Proxy generation messages
- Audio enhancement messages
- Export progress
- Packaging/runtime troubleshooting

The Log tab is intentionally separate so the Edit workspace stays clean.

---

## Important Files

```text
launcher.py                         App launcher / startup entry
main.py                             Main desktop UI application
core/state.py                       Shared application state
core/ffmpeg.py                      FFmpeg/FFprobe helpers
core/audio_enhance.py               Audio enhancement pipeline
core/player_mpv.py                  MPV playback integration
core/parser.py                      Subtitle parsing helpers
core/qc.py                          Subtitle QC helpers
tabs/tab_edit.py                    Edit workspace
tabs/tab_export.py                  Export workspace
tabs/tab_logs.py                    Log workspace
build_nuitka.ps1                    Working Nuitka build script
make_ship_package.ps1               Ship package helper
requirements.txt                    Development requirements
requirements_WORKING_SHIP.txt       Known working ship requirements
BUILD_NOTES.txt                     Build/package notes
SESSION_CONTEXT.txt                 Current project handoff context
export_project_context.py           Clean project export script
```

---

## Development Setup

Recommended Python version:

```text
Python 3.12
```

Install dependencies from the project root:

```powershell
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
```

For packaging parity, preserve:

```text
requirements_WORKING_SHIP.txt
```

Corporate network note:

If package installation fails due to SSL inspection or certificate restrictions, use the approved corporate Python/package index workflow or install from a trusted offline wheel cache.

---


Alternative helper scripts may exist:

```text

launch.bat
```

Use `launcher.py` as the authoritative source entry point.


---

## Building the Windows Standalone App

From the project root:

```powershell
.\build_nuitka.ps1
```

Expected final executable:

```text
build_nuitka\launcher.dist\SubtitleStudio.exe
```

The build script performs:

1. Python compile checks
2. Nuitka standalone build
3. Runtime file copying
4. PyAV manual package copy
5. PyAV native library copy
6. `python3.dll` copy
7. NumPy native library copy
8. SoundFile native data copy
9. `pyrnnoise` runtime copy
10. `audiolab` manual package copy
11. Final runtime verification

Do not simplify `build_nuitka.ps1` without retesting the packaged app.

---

## Current Packaging Strategy

The Windows standalone package uses a working hybrid packaging strategy.

Important runtime decisions:

- `av` / PyAV is not compiled by Nuitka
- PyAV package files are manually copied
- `av.libs` is manually copied
- `python3.dll` is manually copied for `av._core.pyd`
- `audiolab` is manually copied instead of compiled
- `pyrnnoise` is included and manually copied for `rnnoise.dll`
- `numpy` is handled by Nuitka, with only `numpy.libs` manually copied
- SoundFile native data is copied
- FFmpeg and MPV runtime files are bundled under `bin/windows`

---

## Creating a Ship Package

Use:

```powershell
.\make_ship_package.ps1
```

The ship package should include the standalone app and required bundled runtime files.

Do not distribute development virtual environments.

---

```powershell
python export_project_context.py --output-prefix PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT_CLEAN --max-mb 5
```

The handoff package should include:

```text
PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT_CLEAN_MANIFEST.txt
PROJECT_CODE_EXPORT_CLEAN_PART_001.txt
```

Before using an export for development, confirm it does not include:

```text
dist/

site-packages/
```

## Clean-Machine QA Checklist

Before distributing the v5 ship candidate:

1. Copy the ship package to a clean Windows machine
2. Launch `SubtitleStudio.exe`
3. Load a video
4. Confirm preview playback works
5. Import subtitles
6. Confirm subtitle editing works
7. Run audio enhancement
8. Confirm log contains successful `pyrnnoise` processing
9. Export video with burned subtitles
10. Export audio
11. Export subtitle file
12. Play exported video manually
13. Play exported audio manually
14. Open exported subtitle file manually
15. Confirm no missing DLL/runtime errors

---

## Troubleshooting

### App does not launch

Check:

- Windows security quarantine
- Missing bundled DLLs
- `build_nuitka\launcher.dist` contents
- `python3.dll` presence
- MPV DLL presence
- FFmpeg/FFprobe presence

### Audio enhancement fails

Check:

- `pyrnnoise` package copied into dist
- `rnnoise.dll` copied into dist
- `audiolab` package copied into dist
- `av` package copied into dist
- `av.libs` copied into dist
- `python3.dll` copied into dist

### Export fails

Check the Log tab first.

Then verify:

- FFmpeg exists under `bin/windows`
- FFprobe exists under `bin/windows`
- Output folder is writable
- Source media path still exists
- Subtitle data is valid
- Audio mode is source/enhanced as expected

---

## Development Rules

- Keep the app stable before adding features
- Build one feature at a time
- Test after every meaningful change
- Do not rewrite playback before v5.0
- Do not simplify packaging unless the packaged app is retested
- Keep build/runtime notes current
- Regenerate clean project exports before handoff

---

## License / Distribution

Internal/beta distribution status is project-dependent.

Before external release, confirm license compatibility for:

- FFmpeg
- MPV
- PyAV
- pyrnnoise
- SoundFile/libsndfile
- NumPy
- CustomTkinter
- Any bundled models or native DLLs

FILE: run_tests.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Automated Test Suite
Run all tests: python run_tests.py
"""

import sys
import os
import time

# Ensure project root is in path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

# Suppress pygame welcome message
os.environ['PYGAME_HIDE_SUPPORT_PROMPT'] = '1'


def main():
    print("=" * 50)
    print("  SUBTITLE STUDIO v5.0 — TEST SUITE")
    print("=" * 50)

    start = time.time()
    failures = []

    # ── Run each test module ──
    test_modules = [
        ("Parser", "tests.test_parser"),
        ("State", "tests.test_state"),
        ("Auto-Fix", "tests.test_auto"),
        ("Dictionary", "tests.test_dictionary"),
        ("QC Engine", "tests.test_qc"),
    ]

    for name, module_path in test_modules:
        try:
            mod = __import__(module_path, fromlist=["run_all"])
            mod.run_all()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"\n❌ {name} FAILED: {e}")
            failures.append((name, str(e)))

    # ── Summary ──
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    total_modules = len(test_modules)
    passed = total_modules - len(failures)

    print("=" * 50)
    print(f"  RESULTS: {passed}/{total_modules} modules passed")
    print(f"  Time: {elapsed:.2f}s")

    if failures:
        print(f"\n  FAILURES:")
        for name, err in failures:
            print(f"    ❌ {name}: {err}")
        print("=" * 50)
        sys.exit(1)
    else:
        print(f"\n  🎉 ALL TESTS PASSED")
        print("=" * 50)
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: RUNTIME_MANIFEST_SHIP.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Subtitle Studio Runtime Manifest - Ship Candidate
=================================================

Purpose
-------

This file documents the runtime files, folders, and packaging decisions required
for a working Subtitle Studio Windows standalone distribution.

Use this manifest when:

- validating a Nuitka build
- preparing a ship package
- testing on a clean machine
- debugging missing DLL/module errors
- handing the project to another developer/session

Current baseline
----------------

Product:
    Subtitle Studio

Release track:
    v5.0 ship candidate / v6 polish preparation

Branch:
    v5-finalization

Known documentation commits:
    42b42413 Update session context after documentation pass
    d2b77c5b Update build notes for v5 ship candidate
    99963d44 Update product README for v5 ship candidate
    edffeebb Finalize v5 ship candidate packaging and cleanup

Authoritative build script:
    build_nuitka.ps1

Known-good requirements reference:
    requirements_WORKING_SHIP.txt

Expected packaged executable:
    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\SubtitleStudio.exe

============================================================
A. Distribution root
============================================================

Expected distribution root after build:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\

Expected executable:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\SubtitleStudio.exe

This folder is generated build output and should not be committed to Git.

============================================================
B. Required bundled binary tools
============================================================

Expected folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\

Required files:

    ffmpeg.exe
    ffprobe.exe
    mpv.exe
    mpv-2.dll
    d3dcompiler_43.dll

Purpose:

    ffmpeg.exe
        Used for media conversion, audio extraction, proxy generation,
        subtitle burn-in, and export rendering.

    ffprobe.exe
        Used for media probing and metadata extraction.

    mpv.exe / mpv-2.dll
        Used for preview playback/runtime integration.

    d3dcompiler_43.dll
        Required by MPV runtime on some Windows systems.

Optional model folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\models\

Optional model file:

    rnnoise-model.rnnn

============================================================
C. PyAV runtime
============================================================

PyAV is intentionally excluded from Nuitka compilation:

    --nofollow-import-to=av

Required copied package folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\

Required file:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\_core.pyd

Required copied native library folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av.libs\

Required copied dist-info:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av-*.dist-info\

Required Python stable ABI DLL:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\python3.dll

Reason:

    PyAV native extensions require av.libs and python3.dll to be available
    at runtime. Without these files, the packaged app may fail when importing
    or using av._core.pyd.

Additional runtime DLL placement:

    PyAV DLLs are copied into:
        build_nuitka\launcher.dist\
        build_nuitka\launcher.dist\av\
        build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\

This redundancy is intentional for runtime resolution reliability.

============================================================
D. audiolab runtime
============================================================

audiolab is intentionally excluded from Nuitka compilation:

    --nofollow-import-to=audiolab

Required copied package folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\audiolab\

Required copied dist-info:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\audiolab-*.dist-info\

Reason:

    Compiling audiolab with Nuitka caused a packaged runtime failure:

        TypeError: iter() returned non-iterator of type
        'nuitka_resource_reader_files'

Working strategy:

    - Do not compile audiolab with Nuitka
    - Manually copy audiolab
    - Include its pure-Python dependencies through Nuitka

Do not manually copy the full dependency closure from site-packages.

============================================================
E. audiolab dependency coverage
============================================================

The following dependency areas are included through Nuitka:

    requests
    urllib3
    certifi
    idna
    charset_normalizer
    jinja2
    markupsafe
    smart_open
    humanize
    wrapt
    http
    email
    netrc
    mimetypes
    ssl
    socket

Reason:

    Manual dependency copying previously caused missing stdlib/runtime issues,
    including:

        No module named 'http.cookies'

The current hybrid strategy avoids this issue.

============================================================
F. NumPy runtime
============================================================

NumPy is handled by Nuitka:

    --include-package=numpy

Only native libraries are manually copied:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\numpy.libs\

Do not manually copy:

    site-packages\numpy\

Reason:

    Manual full-package copying caused package collision/runtime instability.

============================================================
G. SoundFile runtime
============================================================

SoundFile is included through Nuitka:

    --include-module=soundfile

Native data is copied when available:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\_soundfile_data\

DLLs from _soundfile_data are also copied into the dist root when present.

Purpose:

    Supports packaged audio read/write operations needed by enhancement/export.

============================================================
H. pyrnnoise runtime
============================================================

pyrnnoise is included with Nuitka:

    --include-package=pyrnnoise
    --include-package-data=pyrnnoise

Manual copied package folder:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise\

Required DLL:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise\rnnoise.dll

Required copied dist-info:

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\pyrnnoise-*.dist-info\

Additional DLL placement:

    rnnoise.dll and related DLLs may also be copied into:
        build_nuitka\launcher.dist\
        build_nuitka\launcher.dist\bin\windows\

Purpose:

    Supports premium audio denoise/enhancement path.

Known-good log patterns:

    [OK] pyrnnoise stage complete using denoise_chunk.
    [OK] pyrnnoise light polish complete.
    [OK] Enhanced audio ready: subtitle_studio_enhanced_pyrnnoise_*.wav

============================================================
I. CustomTkinter runtime
============================================================

CustomTkinter is included with package data:

    --include-package=customtkinter
    --include-package-data=customtkinter

Purpose:

    Required for the desktop UI.

============================================================
J. Spellchecker/runtime text dependencies
============================================================

Spellchecker is included with package data:

    --include-package=spellchecker
    --include-package-data=spellchecker

Dictionary data in project root:

    dictionary.csv

Included as data file:

    --include-data-file=dictionary.csv=dictionary.csv

Purpose:

    Supports subtitle text checking/dictionary features.

============================================================
K. Explicit no-follow modules
============================================================

The build intentionally avoids following these modules/packages:

    cv2
    pygame
    whisper
    torch
    moviepy
    av
    audiolab
    matplotlib

Reason:

    These are either unnecessary for the current packaged workflow, too large,
    unstable under packaging, or intentionally handled manually.

    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\audiolab

    source code

    VS Code
    Git

    [ ] No missing DLL error
    [ ] No missing Python module error
    [ ] FFmpeg works
    [ ] FFprobe works
    [ ] MPV playback works
    [ ] Subtitle import works
    [ ] Audio enhancement works
    [ ] Subtitle export works
    [ ] Audio export works
    [ ] Video export works

BURNED SUBTITLE RENDERING HOTFIX
--------------------------------

Commit:

- 49df56f3 Fix burned subtitle quote and background rendering

Files changed:

- tabs/tab_export.py

Confirmed behavior:

- Burned/exported video subtitles now use correct opening and closing curly double quotes.
- Burned/exported video subtitles now use correct opening and closing curly single quotes.
- Apostrophes in contractions and possessives are curly apostrophes.
- Smart typography is export-only and does not mutate editor subtitle text.
- Multi-line burned subtitle backgrounds are now one merged square-corner shape.
- Transparent per-line background overlap has been eliminated.
- T-shaped and inverted-T-shaped backgrounds work depending on line widths.
- Text is vertically centered inside the background shape.
- Padding was visually tuned and approved.
- Left, center, and right subtitle alignment were confirmed working.

Important implementation knowhow:

- Burned subtitle ASS rendering now uses:
  - Layer 0: merged vector background shape
  - Layer 1: positioned subtitle text
- Do not return to ASS per-line boxed backgrounds using BorderStyle=4.
- Do not add rounded corners unless explicitly requested.
- Important functions in tabs/tab_export.py include:
  - _smart_curly_quotes
  - _ass_escape_text
  - _estimate_ass_text_width
  - _line_background_rect
  - _merged_rect_union_points
  - _ass_draw_path
  - _write_current_subs_to_temp

“Hello,” she said.

SESSION_CONTEXT.txt
===================

Project: Subtitle Studio
Target Release: v5.0
Session Date: 2026-05-04

CURRENT STATUS
--------------

Subtitle Studio is in a stable v5.0 development state.

The app currently supports:

- video loading
- bundled ffmpeg/ffprobe detection
- proxy generation
- frame-buffered preview playback
- source audio extraction
- waveform generation
- subtitle import/edit workflow
- section marking with IN/OUT
- audio enhancement using RNNoise + FFmpeg
- source/enhanced audio state groundwork
- export workflow
- automated test suite

Current tabs/product direction:

    Edit | Export | Log

Decision: remove/simplify the Import tab later. Import should become File menu actions.

Current source health:

- Python source compiles cleanly
- automated tests pass
- clean project export now produces 47 files / 1 chunk instead of 7,386 files / 23 chunks
- playback sync works before and after audio enhancement
- shortcuts work well


IMPORTANT PRODUCT DECISIONS MADE
--------------------------------

1. Tabs

Final intended tab structure:

    Edit | Export | Log

No separate Import tab.
No separate QC/Review tab.

QC/Review is not a workspace. It is an automatic safety system.

2. QC

Auto QC should run immediately after subtitle import.

Supported import types include:

    .srt
    .vtt
    other supported subtitle formats

Workflow:

    User imports subtitles
    App parses subtitles
    App immediately runs QC silently
    Log tab records summary
    Serious issues show lightweight warning
    Export preflight QC runs again before export

QC and Auto-Fix should not be prominent Tools menu actions.

Before export:

    If issues are found:
        show modal with:
            Fix Automatically
            Export Anyway
            Cancel

Details should go to the Log tab.

3. Log

Keep Log as a separate tab.

Reason:

- useful for ffmpeg/proxy/audio/export diagnostics
- keeps Edit clean
- helps support/debugging

4. Menus

Planned top menu:

    File | Edit | View | Tools | Help

File menu:

    New Project
    Open Project...
    Save Project
    Save Project As...

    Import Video...
    Import Subtitles...

    Export...

    Recent Projects >

    Exit

Edit menu:

    Undo
    Redo

    Cut
    Copy
    Paste
    Delete

    Select All
    Deselect All

    Add Selection from IN/OUT
    Delete Selected Selection
    Move Selection Up
    Move Selection Down

    Find / Replace...

Undo/Redo may be disabled until a reliable undo stack exists.

View menu:

    Theme >
        Dark
        Light
        System

    Preview Resolution >
        480p
        720p
        1080p

    Show Waveform
    Show Subtitle Overlay

    Go to Edit
    Go to Export
    Go to Log

Dark mode should live in View > Theme and also remain available as a quick top-right toggle.

Tools menu:

    Enhance Audio
    Use Source Audio
    Use Enhanced Audio

    Rebuild Preview
    Reload Video

    Dictionary Manager

Help menu:

    User Manual
    Keyboard Shortcuts
    Troubleshooting
    Release Notes
    About Subtitle Studio

Shortcut menu should live under:

    Help > Keyboard Shortcuts

5. Selection Bin

Selection bin needs:

    [▲] [▼] [Delete]

Behaviour:

- ▲ moves selected section up
- ▼ moves selected section down
- Delete deletes selected section
- visible order should equal export order
- keyboard shortcuts:
    Delete      delete selected section
    Ctrl+Up     move selected section up
    Ctrl+Down   move selected section down

6. Playback

Playback is currently good.

Must still add:

- disable Play while proxy is rebuilding
- disable seek while proxy is rebuilding
- show Preparing/Rebuilding Preview state
- re-enable playback controls only when proxy/buffer are ready

Do not rewrite playback architecture before v5.0.

Current proxy + frame buffer model is good enough and stable.

7. Audio Enhancement

Current behaviour:

- Enhance Audio works
- RNNoise stage completes
- FFmpeg polish stage completes
- enhanced waveform loads
- sync works after enhancement

Decision:

Before enhancement:

    show Enhance Audio button

After enhancement:

    replace/augment with Source / Enhanced toggle

Both should exist:

- Edit UI toggle for fast workflow
- Tools menu commands for discoverability

Required audio choices:

Edit working audio:

    Source
    Enhanced

Export audio:

    Use Working Audio
    Source Audio
    Enhanced Audio
    Mute

Default export audio:

    Use Working Audio

8. Export Tab

Export tab should be simplified.

Final intended layout:

    Export Mode
    [Full Video ▼]

    Subtitles
    [Burn Into Video ▼]

    Audio
    [Use Working Audio ▼]

    Quality
    [Standard MP4 ▼]

    Font Size
    [12]

    Output
    [Choose...]

    [Export]

Export Mode options:

    Full Video
    Selections as Separate Clips
    Remove Selections from Video
    Selections Merged Into One Video
    Subtitles Only
    Audio Only

Subtitles options:

    Burn Into Video
    Sidecar SRT
    No Subtitles

Default subtitle mode:

    Burn Into Video

Audio options:

    Use Working Audio
    Source Audio
    Enhanced Audio
    Mute

Quality options:

    Small MP4
    Standard MP4
    High Quality MP4
    Lossless

Default font size:

    12

Burn-in style should not be exposed.
Brand styling is already set.
Only font size should be user-adjustable for v5.

tests/test_state.py:

- added tests for audio validation fallback
- added tests for missing audio cleanup

10. Edit tab audio status label added

Edit tab now shows active audio state such as:

    Audio: not loaded
    Audio: source active
    Audio: enhancing...
    Audio: enhanced active

11. Full export external audio groundwork added

tabs/tab_export.py:

- helper added to retrieve export audio path
- full export can map external audio input

Current issue:

- export used source extracted WAV, not enhanced WAV
- log showed:
    Using external export audio: subtitle_studio_audio_...
- expected enhanced audio would be:
    subtitle_studio_enhanced_...

Root cause:

- no explicit working/export audio selector yet
- state.export_use_enhanced_audio is likely false or not connected to UI

Do not continue hacking export until audio selector is implemented.

12. Playback sync hardening

core/player.py:

- playback position sync changed to use app wall-clock timeline as master
- pygame.mixer.music.get_pos() no longer controls subtitle/video timeline

Reason:

- pygame audio clock can become unreliable after reloads, seeks, pauses, or swapping source/enhanced audio

Manual result:

- video plays well before enhancement
- shortcuts work well
- sync works well after enhancement

    sub_visible_card
    sub_visible_row
    sub_visible_formats_frame
    sub_visible_selection_lbl
    sub_visible_selection_dropdown
    sub_visible_formats_lbl
    sub_visible_fmt_srt_check
    sub_visible_fmt_vtt_check
    sub_visible_fmt_txt_check
    sub_visible_fmt_docx_check
    sub_visible_fmt_fcpxml_check

After adding visible widgets, update:

    _set_export_controls_enabled()
    _refresh_theme()

with the new visible widget names.

CURRENT LIMITATIONS STILL PRESENT
---------------------------------
The UI is ahead of the backend. These are not fully implemented yet:

1. Embedded Audio = Mute does not yet map to FFmpeg -an.
2. Embedded Audio = Original/Enhanced/Use Working Audio is not yet fully plan-driven.
3. Audio Source = Original/Enhanced/Use Working Audio is not yet fully plan-driven.
4. Multi-format subtitle export is not fully implemented.
   Current compatibility behavior exports only the first checked subtitle format.
5. Subtitle selection scopes are visible but not fully implemented:

    Full Source
    Remove Clip Selections
    Clip Selections as Separate Files
    Merge Clip Selections

6. Destination is still legacy hybrid file/folder behavior.
   Final v5 should use folder-only destination.

NEXT BACKEND PHASES AFTER UI STABILIZES
---------------------------------------
Phase 2: Implement real multi-format subtitle export:

    _export_subtitles_from_plan(plan)

Phase 3: Implement subtitle selection scopes.

Phase 4: Refactor standalone audio export to use plan:

    selection
    source
    format
    bitrate
    destination folder

Phase 5: Refactor video export to use plan:

    selection
    resolution
    compression
    burn_subtitles
    embedded_audio

Including:

    Mute -> FFmpeg -an
    Original Audio
    Enhanced Audio
    Use Working Audio

Phase 6: Switch Destination to folder-only output.

Phase 7: Remove legacy mode/subtitle/sidecar UI and bridge code after plan-based export is stable.

----------------

    [x] Video
    [ ] Audio
    [ ] Subtitles

    [ ] Audio

    [ ] Audio
    [ ] Subtitles

Expected: warning on Export.

@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion

:: ================================================================
:: Subtitle Studio v4.1 — Windows Auto-Launcher
:: No install, no manual download. Everything automatic.
:: Handles Windows Store Python stub gracefully.
:: ================================================================

title Subtitle Studio v4.1 — Setup
color 0F

echo.
echo  ============================================
echo    Subtitle Studio v4.1 Launcher
echo    Windows Auto-Setup + Run
echo  ============================================
echo.

:: ── Get script directory ──
cd /d "%~dp0"
set "APP_DIR=%~dp0"

:: ── Check for existing venv ──
if exist "python_env\Scripts\python.exe" (
    echo [OK] Python environment found.
    goto :check_deps
)

echo [..] Setting up Python environment...
echo     This only happens once. Please wait ~3-5 minutes.
echo.

:: ── Check system Python ──
:: Windows has a fake "python" stub that redirects to
:: Microsoft Store. We must verify it actually works.
set "PYTHON_CMD="

:: Try python3 first
where python3 >nul 2>&1
if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
    python3 -c "import sys; print(sys.version)" >nul 2>&1
    if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
        set "PYTHON_CMD=python3"
    )
)

:: Try python
if not defined PYTHON_CMD (
    where python >nul 2>&1
    if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
        python -c "import sys; print(sys.version)" >nul 2>&1
        if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
            set "PYTHON_CMD=python"
        )
    )
)

:: Try py launcher (official Python installer)
if not defined PYTHON_CMD (
    where py >nul 2>&1
    if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
        py -3 -c "import sys; print(sys.version)" >nul 2>&1
        if !errorlevel! equ 0 (
            set "PYTHON_CMD=py -3"
        )
    )
)

if not defined PYTHON_CMD (
    echo [!!] Python not found on your system.
    echo.
    echo     The Windows Store stub is NOT real Python.
    echo     Please install Python 3.12+ from:
    echo.
    echo     https://www.python.org/downloads/
    echo.
    echo     IMPORTANT - Check these boxes during install:
    echo       [x] Add Python to PATH
    echo       [x] Install for all users
    echo.
    echo     After installing, close and reopen this script.
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ── Verify Python version ──
for /f "tokens=*" %%i in ('!PYTHON_CMD! -c "import sys; print(f'{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}')"') do set PYVER=%%i
echo [OK] Found Python %PYVER% via %PYTHON_CMD%

:: ── Create virtual environment ──
echo [..] Creating virtual environment...
!PYTHON_CMD! -m venv python_env
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [!!] Failed to create virtual environment.
    echo.
    echo     If you see a Microsoft Store error, you need
    echo     to install real Python from python.org
    echo.
    echo     Then disable the Store alias:
    echo     Settings - Apps - Advanced app settings
    echo     - App execution aliases
    echo     Turn OFF python.exe and python3.exe
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
echo [OK] Virtual environment created.

:check_deps
:: ── Use explicit venv Python/pip paths ──
set "VENV_PYTHON=python_env\Scripts\python.exe"
set "VENV_PIP=python_env\Scripts\pip.exe"

:: ── Check if deps are installed ──
%VENV_PYTHON% -c "import customtkinter" >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    goto :install_deps
)
%VENV_PYTHON% -c "import pygame" >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    goto :install_deps
)
%VENV_PYTHON% -c "import cv2" >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    goto :install_deps
)

echo [OK] All dependencies installed.
goto :check_ffmpeg

:install_deps
echo.
echo [..] Installing dependencies...
echo     This may take 2-3 minutes on first run.
echo.

:: ── Core dependencies ──
%VENV_PIP% install --quiet --disable-pip-version-check ^
    customtkinter pygame python-docx opencv-python ^
    Pillow pyspellchecker 2>nul

if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [!] Some core packages failed. Retrying...
    %VENV_PIP% install customtkinter pygame python-docx ^
        opencv-python Pillow pyspellchecker
)

:: ── AI transcription ──
echo [..] Installing AI transcription support...
%VENV_PIP% install --quiet --disable-pip-version-check ^
    openai-whisper 2>nul

if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [!] Whisper install failed. AI generation
    echo     will not be available. You can install
    echo     it later with:
    echo     python_env\Scripts\pip install openai-whisper
)

:: ── Verify installation ──
%VENV_PYTHON% -c "import customtkinter; import pygame; import cv2; import PIL" >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [!!] Core dependency check failed.
    echo     Try running:
    echo     python_env\Scripts\pip install customtkinter pygame opencv-python Pillow
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo [OK] All dependencies installed.

:check_ffmpeg
:: ── Check FFmpeg ──
if exist "bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe" (
    echo [OK] FFmpeg found in bin\windows\
    goto :run_app
)

where ffmpeg >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel% equ 0 (
    echo [OK] FFmpeg found on system PATH.
    goto :run_app
)

echo.
echo [!!] FFmpeg not found!
echo.
echo     Option 1: Place ffmpeg.exe and ffprobe.exe in:
echo               %APP_DIR%bin\windows\
echo.
echo     Option 2: Install FFmpeg and add to system PATH.
echo.
echo     Download from: https://www.gyan.dev/ffmpeg/builds/
echo     Use the "essentials" build.
echo.
echo     The app will start but video features won't work
echo     until FFmpeg is available.
echo.

:run_app
echo.
echo ============================================
echo  Starting Subtitle Studio v4.1...
echo ============================================
echo.

:: ── Run the app using venv Python ──
%VENV_PYTHON% main.py

:: ── If app exits with error ──
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo.
    echo [!!] Application exited with an error.
    echo     Check the output above for details.
    echo.
    pause
)

endlocal

#!/bin/bash
echo ""
echo "╔══════════════════════════════════════════╗"
echo "║       Subtitle Studio v4.0               ║"
echo "║       Professional Subtitle Editor        ║"
echo "╚══════════════════════════════════════════╝"
echo ""

# ── Check Python ──
if ! command -v python3 &> /dev/null; then
    echo "[!] Python not found."
    echo ""
    if command -v brew &> /dev/null; then
        echo "Installing Python via Homebrew..."
        brew install python
    else
        echo "Installing Homebrew first..."
        /bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)"
        brew install python
    fi
    echo ""
fi

echo "[OK] Python found"
python3 --version
echo ""

# ── Check FFmpeg ──
if ! command -v ffmpeg &> /dev/null; then
    echo "[!] FFmpeg not found. Installing..."
    if command -v brew &> /dev/null; then
        brew install ffmpeg
    else
        echo "[ERROR] Install Homebrew first:"
        echo "  /bin/bash -c \"\$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)\""
        exit 1
    fi
    echo ""
fi

echo "[OK] FFmpeg found"
echo ""

# ── Check dependencies ──
python3 -c "import customtkinter" 2>/dev/null
if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "First time setup — installing dependencies..."
    echo "──────────────────────────────────────────"
    pip3 install customtkinter pygame python-docx opencv-python Pillow pyspellchecker openai-whisper
    echo "──────────────────────────────────────────"
    echo ""
    echo "[OK] Dependencies installed"
else
    echo "[OK] Dependencies ready"
fi

# ── Verify AI ──
python3 -c "import whisper" 2>/dev/null
if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "Installing AI transcription..."
    pip3 install openai-whisper
    echo "[OK] AI module installed"
else
    echo "[OK] AI transcription ready"
fi

echo ""
echo "Starting Subtitle Studio..."
echo "══════════════════════════════════════════"
echo ""

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
python3 "$SCRIPT_DIR/main.py"

FILE: setup_runtime.bat

@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion

cd /d "%~dp0"

title Subtitle Studio - Setup Runtime
color 0F

echo.
echo ============================================
echo        Subtitle Studio - Setup Runtime
echo ============================================
echo.

where py >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    where python >nul 2>&1
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo [!!] Python was not found on this machine.
        echo.
        echo Install Python 3.12 or compatible first:
        echo https://www.python.org/downloads/
        echo.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    ) else (
        set PY_CMD=python
    )
) else (
    set PY_CMD=py -3.12
)

echo [..] Using Python command: !PY_CMD!
echo.

if not exist "python_env" (
    echo [..] Creating local virtual environment...
    !PY_CMD! -m venv python_env
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo [!!] Failed to create virtual environment.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
) else (
    echo [OK] Existing python_env found.
)

set VENV_PY=%~dp0python_env\Scripts\python.exe
set VENV_PIP=%~dp0python_env\Scripts\pip.exe

if not exist "!VENV_PY!" (
    echo [!!] Virtual environment Python not found.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo [..] Upgrading pip...
"!VENV_PY!" -m pip install --upgrade pip setuptools wheel

echo.
echo [..] Installing app dependencies...
"!VENV_PIP!" install ^
    customtkinter ^
    pygame ^
    python-docx ^
    opencv-python ^
    Pillow ^
    pyspellchecker ^
    openai-whisper

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo [!!] Dependency install failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo [..] Verifying critical imports...
"!VENV_PY!" -c "import whisper; print('whisper OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] whisper import failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

"!VENV_PY!" -c "import torch; print('torch OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] torch import failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

"!VENV_PY!" -c "import tiktoken; print('tiktoken OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] tiktoken import failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

"!VENV_PY!" -c "import regex; print('regex OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] regex import failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

"!VENV_PY!" -c "import numpy; print('numpy OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] numpy import failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo [..] Checking bundled model...
if exist "models\whisper\small.pt" (
    echo [OK] Found models\whisper\small.pt
) else (
    echo [!!] Missing models\whisper\small.pt
    echo      Put your whisper model here:
    echo      models\whisper\small.pt
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo [..] Verifying model load...
"!VENV_PY!" -c "import whisper; whisper.load_model(r'%~dp0models\whisper\small.pt'); print('model load OK')"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [!!] Whisper model failed to load.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo ============================================
echo [OK] Runtime setup complete.
echo.
echo You can now launch the app with:
echo     launch.bat
echo ============================================
echo.
pause
endlocal

Purpose
-------

This checklist verifies that the app still works after documentation updates,
small polish changes, packaging changes, or release preparation.

Use this checklist before calling a build or commit "ship ready".

----------------

    v5-finalization

    42b42413 Update session context after documentation pass
    d2b77c5b Update build notes for v5 ship candidate

    python main.py

Packaged app path:
    build_nuitka\launcher.dist\SubtitleStudio.exe

Important rule
--------------

    1. what action was performed

documented and intentionally accepted.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tabs/tab_edit.py

"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Tab 1: Edit.
Clean rewrite focused on stability, simpler structure, and UX clarity.
Left: video preview, transport, seek, section marking.
Right: subtitle bin, inline editing, spell suggestions, find/replace.

Built-in AI transcription is removed.
v5 groundwork includes source/enhanced/active audio state support.
"""

import os
import re
import datetime
import threading
import platform
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import customtkinter as ctk

from core import theme
from core.theme import (
    make_primary_btn, make_secondary_btn, make_danger_btn,
    make_section_label, make_card,
    disable_btn, enable_btn,
    register_theme_callback,
)
from core.state import AppState, Cue, Section
from core.parser import seconds_to_display, time_to_seconds

try:
    from core.player_mpv import MpvPlayer, diagnose_mpv_environment
except Exception:
    MpvPlayer = None
    diagnose_mpv_environment = None
from core.widgets import SeekSlider, Tooltip
from core.user_dict import contains as user_dict_contains
from core.user_dict import add_word as user_dict_add


def build_edit_tab(parent, state: AppState):
    """Build the Edit tab. Returns ui dict."""
    ui = {}

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Small helpers
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _timestamp() -> str:
        return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")

    def _display_time_short(seconds: float | int | None) -> str:
        """Visible UI time only: HH:MM:SS."""
        if seconds is None:
            return "--:--:--"
        try:
            total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
        except Exception:
            return "00:00:00"
        hours = total_seconds // 3600
        minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
        secs = total_seconds % 60
        return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"

    def _is_text_focus() -> bool:
        focused = parent.winfo_toplevel().focus_get()
        if isinstance(focused, (tk.Entry, tk.Text)):
            return True
        try:
            if isinstance(focused, (ctk.CTkEntry, ctk.CTkTextbox)):
                return True
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False

    def _log_cue_time_change(cue: Cue, field_name: str, old_value: float, new_value: float):
        ts = _timestamp()
        state.log_subtitle(
            f"[{ts}] Cue {cue.index}: {field_name} "
            f"{seconds_to_display(old_value)} → "
            f"{seconds_to_display(new_value)}"
        )
        state.log(
            f"Cue {cue.index} {field_name.upper()} → "
            f"{seconds_to_display(new_value)}"
        )

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Spell checker
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _spellchecker = {"checker": None}

    def _init_spellcheck():
        try:
            from spellchecker import SpellChecker
            _spellchecker["checker"] = SpellChecker(language="en")
        except ImportError:
            _spellchecker["checker"] = None
        except Exception:
            _spellchecker["checker"] = None

    def _get_misspelled(text: str) -> set:
        checker = _spellchecker["checker"]
        if checker is None:
            return set()
        words = re.findall(r"[a-zA-Z']+", text)
        if not words:
            return set()
        misspelled = checker.unknown(words)
        return {w for w in misspelled if not user_dict_contains(w)}

    def _get_suggestions(word: str, max_count: int = 5) -> list:
        checker = _spellchecker["checker"]
        if checker is None:
            return []
        try:
            candidates = checker.candidates(word)
            correction = checker.correction(word)
        except Exception:
            return []

        result = []
        if correction and correction != word:
            result.append(correction)

        for cand in sorted(candidates):
            if cand not in result and cand != word:
                result.append(cand)
            if len(result) >= max_count:
                break
        return result

    def _apply_spellcheck_tags(text_widget):
        text_widget.tag_remove("spell_error", "1.0", "end")
        content = text_widget.get("1.0", "end-1c")
        if not content.strip():
            return
        misspelled = _get_misspelled(content)
        if not misspelled:
            return

        for match in re.finditer(r"[a-zA-Z']+", content):
            word = match.group()
            if word.lower() in misspelled or word in misspelled:
                line_num = content[:match.start()].count("\n") + 1
                line_start = content.rfind("\n", 0, match.start()) + 1
                col = match.start() - line_start
                start_pos = f"{line_num}.{col}"
                end_pos = f"{line_num}.{col + len(word)}"
                text_widget.tag_add("spell_error", start_pos, end_pos)

    _init_spellcheck()

    main_frame.add(right, minsize=320, stretch="always")

    _initial_sash_done = {"value": False}

    def _set_initial_sash(event=None):
        if _initial_sash_done["value"]:
            return

        def _place_sash():
            if _initial_sash_done["value"]:
                return

            try:
                total_width = main_frame.winfo_width()

                # Wait until the PanedWindow has a real usable width.
                if total_width < 700:
                    return

                # Give the left editing/video controls priority on startup.
                sash_x = int(total_width * 0.62)

                # Keep both panes usable.
                sash_x = max(520, sash_x)
                sash_x = min(sash_x, total_width - 320)

                main_frame.sash_place(0, sash_x, 0)
                _initial_sash_done["value"] = True

                try:
                    main_frame.unbind("<Map>")
                except Exception:
                    pass
                try:
                    main_frame.unbind("<Configure>")
                except Exception:
                    pass

            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            parent.after(120, _place_sash)
        except Exception:
            _place_sash()

    main_frame.bind("<Map>", _set_initial_sash)
    main_frame.bind("<Configure>", _set_initial_sash)

    def _show_centered_canvas_message(text, tag="placeholder"):
        try:
            video_canvas.delete(tag)
            video_canvas.update_idletasks()
            cw = max(1, video_canvas.winfo_width())
            ch = max(1, video_canvas.winfo_height())
            video_canvas.create_text(
                cw // 2,
                ch // 2,
                text=text,
                fill="#555555",
                font=("Arial", 10),
                anchor="center",
                justify="center",
                tags=tag,
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _recenter_canvas_messages(event=None):
        try:
            cw = max(1, video_canvas.winfo_width())
            ch = max(1, video_canvas.winfo_height())
            for tag in ("placeholder", "loading_message"):
                ids = video_canvas.find_withtag(tag)
                for item_id in ids:
                    video_canvas.coords(item_id, cw // 2, ch // 2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    video_canvas.bind("<Configure>", _recenter_canvas_messages, add="+")
    _show_centered_canvas_message("Video preview will appear here")
    ui["video_canvas"] = video_canvas

    _overlay = {"bg_id": None, "text_id": None, "current_text": ""}

    def update_subtitle_overlay(text: str, alignment: str | None = None):
        if alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
            alignment = "center"

        overlay_key = f"{alignment}:{text}"
        if overlay_key == _overlay["current_text"]:
            return
        _overlay["current_text"] = overlay_key

        if _overlay["bg_id"]:
            video_canvas.delete(_overlay["bg_id"])
            _overlay["bg_id"] = None
        if _overlay["text_id"]:
            video_canvas.delete(_overlay["text_id"])
            _overlay["text_id"] = None

        if not text:
            return

        try:
            video_canvas.update_idletasks()
            cw = video_canvas.winfo_width()
            ch = video_canvas.winfo_height()
            if cw < 50 or ch < 50:
                return

            safe_margin = max(20, int(cw * 0.06))
            text_width = max(50, cw - (safe_margin * 2))
            scaled_font = max(9, min(16, int(ch / 30)))

            if alignment == "left":
                x_pos = safe_margin
                anchor = "sw"
                justify = "left"
            elif alignment == "right":
                x_pos = cw - safe_margin
                anchor = "se"
                justify = "right"
            else:
                x_pos = cw // 2
                anchor = "s"
                justify = "center"

            _overlay["text_id"] = video_canvas.create_text(
                x_pos,
                ch - 20,
                text=text,
                fill=theme.OVERLAY_FG,
                font=("Arial", scaled_font),
                anchor=anchor,
                justify=justify,
                width=text_width,
                tags="overlay",
            )

            bbox = video_canvas.bbox(_overlay["text_id"])
            if bbox:
                pad = 12
                _overlay["bg_id"] = video_canvas.create_rectangle(
                    0,
                    bbox[1] - pad,
                    cw,
                    min(bbox[3] + pad, ch),
                    fill=theme.OVERLAY_BG,
                    outline="",
                    tags="overlay_bg",
                )
                video_canvas.tag_raise("overlay", "overlay_bg")
        except Exception:
            pass

    ui["update_subtitle_overlay"] = update_subtitle_overlay

    if current_alignment not in subtitle_align_reverse:
        current_alignment = "center"
        state.subtitle_alignment = "center"

    subtitle_align_var = tk.StringVar(
        value=subtitle_align_reverse[current_alignment]
    )

    def _on_subtitle_alignment_change(label):
        selected_alignment = subtitle_align_labels.get(label, "center")

        try:
            selected = tree.selection()
            if not selected:
                return

            _, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(selected[0])
            if cue is None:
                return

            old_alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
            if old_alignment == selected_alignment:
                return

            state.save_undo()
            cue.alignment = selected_alignment
            state.log(f"Cue {cue.index} alignment set to {selected_alignment}.")

            _overlay["current_text"] = ""
            update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text, cue.alignment)

            try:
                player._current_overlay_text = ""
                if hasattr(player, "_show_mpv_subtitle_text"):
                    player._show_mpv_subtitle_text(
                        cue.text,
                        cue.end,
                        cue.alignment,
                    )
                else:
                    player._highlight_cue()
            except Exception:
                pass

        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[ERROR] Failed to update cue alignment: {e}")

    subtitle_align_dropdown = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(
        lang_row,
        variable=subtitle_align_var,
        values=["Left", "Center", "Right"],
        command=_on_subtitle_alignment_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        dropdown_hover_color=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        width=90,
    )
    subtitle_align_dropdown.pack(side="left", padx=(theme.PAD_SM, 0))
    Tooltip(
        subtitle_align_dropdown,
        "Align preview subtitles to the left, center, or right safety margin."
    )

    tree_card = tk.Frame(right, bg=theme.CARD_BORDER)
    tree_card.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=theme.PAD_SM, pady=(0, 0))

    tree = ttk.Treeview(
        tree_card,
        style="Sub.Treeview",
        columns=("num", "start", "end", "text"),
        show="headings",
        selectmode="browse",
    )
    tree.heading("num", text="#", anchor="w")
    tree.heading("start", text="Start", anchor="w")
    tree.heading("end", text="End", anchor="w")
    tree.heading("text", text="Text", anchor="w")

    tree.column("num", width=40, minwidth=30, stretch=False)
    tree.column("start", width=120, minwidth=110, stretch=False)
    tree.column("end", width=120, minwidth=110, stretch=False)
    tree.column("text", width=400, minwidth=100, stretch=True)

    tree.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)

    tree_scroll_y = ttk.Scrollbar(tree_card, command=tree.yview, style="Slim.Vertical.TScrollbar")
    tree_scroll_y.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    tree.configure(yscrollcommand=tree_scroll_y.set)
    ui["tree"] = tree

    hints_strip = ctk.CTkLabel(
        right,
        text=(
            "Space: play/pause  •  Dbl-click: edit  •  "
            "Ctrl+Click: play from cue  •  "
            "[ ] set sub cue start/end  •  I/O/P Sections  •  "
            "Right-click: spelling"
        ),
        font=("Arial", 8),
        text_color="#999999",
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG,
        anchor="w",
    )
    hints_strip.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_SM, pady=(2, 2))

    active_cue_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
        right,
        text="",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        wraplength=500,
        justify="left",
        anchor="w",
        corner_radius=theme.RADIUS_SM,
    )
    active_cue_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(theme.PAD_XS, 0))
    ui["active_cue_label"] = active_cue_label

    bottom_strip = tk.Frame(right, bg=theme.TAB_BG)
    bottom_strip.pack(fill="x", pady=(theme.PAD_XS, 0))

    btn_split_cue = make_secondary_btn(bottom_strip, "Split", icon=theme.ICON_CUT)
    btn_split_cue.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 2))

    btn_add_cue = make_primary_btn(bottom_strip, "New", icon=theme.ICON_ADD)
    btn_add_cue.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 2))

    btn_delete_cue = make_danger_btn(bottom_strip, "Delete", icon=theme.ICON_DELETE)
    btn_delete_cue.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 2))
    ui["btn_delete_cue"] = btn_delete_cue

    strip_sep = tk.Frame(bottom_strip, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, width=1, height=24)
    strip_sep.pack(side="left", padx=(theme.PAD_XS, theme.PAD_XS))

    find_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(
        bottom_strip,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        border_color=theme.BORDER,
        placeholder_text="Find",
    )
    find_entry.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 2))

    replace_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(
        bottom_strip,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        border_color=theme.BORDER,
        placeholder_text="Replace",
    )
    replace_entry.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 2))

    btn_find_replace = make_primary_btn(bottom_strip, "Go")
    btn_find_replace.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 0))

    Tooltip(btn_split_cue, "Split selected cue at midpoint")
    Tooltip(btn_add_cue, "Insert new cue after selection")
    Tooltip(btn_delete_cue, "Delete selected cue")
    Tooltip(find_entry, "Text to search for")
    Tooltip(replace_entry, "Replacement text")
    Tooltip(btn_find_replace, "Replace all occurrences")

    ui["find_entry"] = find_entry
    ui["replace_entry"] = replace_entry
    ui["btn_find_replace"] = btn_find_replace

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Tree tags and row helpers
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _iid_to_idx = {}

    def _related_subtitle_bg():
        """Soft one-way highlight for cues related to selected clip section."""
        try:
            # Light/dark-friendly fallback.
            if getattr(theme, "DARK_MODE", False):
                return "#4A3F1A"
        except Exception:
            pass
        return "#FFF3C4"

    def _apply_tree_tags():
        tree.tag_configure("even", background=theme.ROW_NORMAL)
        tree.tag_configure("odd", background=theme.ROW_ALT)
        tree.tag_configure(
            "even_err",
            background=theme.ROW_NORMAL,
            foreground=theme.ERROR,
        )
        tree.tag_configure(
            "odd_err",
            background=theme.ROW_ALT,
            foreground=theme.ERROR,
        )

        # One-way Selection Bin → Subtitle Bin visual relation.
        # These tags do not alter data and do not affect Selection Bin.
        related_bg = _related_subtitle_bg()
        tree.tag_configure(
            "even_related",
            background=related_bg,
            foreground=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        )
        tree.tag_configure(
            "odd_related",
            background=related_bg,
            foreground=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        )
        tree.tag_configure(
            "even_err_related",
            background=related_bg,
            foreground=theme.ERROR,
        )
        tree.tag_configure(
            "odd_err_related",
            background=related_bg,
            foreground=theme.ERROR,
        )

    _apply_tree_tags()

    def _format_tree_text(cue_text: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        text_display = cue_text.replace("\n", " ↵ ")
        misspelled = _get_misspelled(cue_text)
        if misspelled:
            words_marked = text_display
            for word in misspelled:
                pattern = re.compile(r"\b" + re.escape(word) + r"\b", re.IGNORECASE)
                words_marked = pattern.sub(f"•{word}", words_marked)
            return words_marked, True
        return text_display, False

    def _row_tag_for(index: int, has_error: bool, related=False) -> str:
        even = index % 2 == 0

        if related and has_error:
            return "even_err_related" if even else "odd_err_related"

        if related:
            return "even_related" if even else "odd_related"

        if has_error:
            return "even_err" if even else "odd_err"

        return "even" if even else "odd"

    def _refresh_tree_row(row_id: str, cue_idx: int):
        if cue_idx < 0 or cue_idx >= len(state.cues):
            return
        cue = state.cues[cue_idx]
        text_display, has_error = _format_tree_text(cue.text)
        related = row_id in _selection_related_subtitle_iids
        tree.item(
            row_id,
            values=(
                cue.index,
                _display_time_short(cue.start),
                _display_time_short(cue.end),
                text_display,
            ),
            tags=(_row_tag_for(cue_idx, has_error, related=related),),
        )

    def populate_subtitle_list():
        _iid_to_idx.clear()
        _selection_related_subtitle_iids.clear()
        tree.delete(*tree.get_children())
        if _spellchecker["checker"] is None:
            _init_spellcheck()

        for i, cue in enumerate(state.cues):
            text_display, has_error = _format_tree_text(cue.text)
            iid = tree.insert(
                "",
                "end",
                values=(
                    cue.index,
                    _display_time_short(cue.start),
                    _display_time_short(cue.end),
                    text_display,
                ),
                tags=(_row_tag_for(i, has_error),),
            )
            _iid_to_idx[iid] = i
        
        # If a clip section is still selected after repopulating,
        # restore the one-way visual relation.
        try:
            _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
        except Exception:
            pass

    ui["populate_subtitle_list"] = populate_subtitle_list

    def _get_cue_from_iid(iid: str):
        idx = _iid_to_idx.get(iid)
        if idx is not None and 0 <= idx < len(state.cues):
            return idx, state.cues[idx]
        return None, None

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # One-way Selection Bin → Subtitle Bin visual linking
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _selection_related_subtitle_iids = set()

    def _ranges_overlap(a_start, a_end, b_start, b_end):
        """Return True when two timeline ranges overlap."""
        try:
            return float(a_start) < float(b_end) and float(a_end) > float(b_start)
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _cue_has_error(cue):
        """Return whether cue text currently has spellcheck markings."""
        try:
            return bool(_get_misspelled(cue.text))
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _set_subtitle_row_related(row_id, related):
        """Apply or remove one-way related highlight on a subtitle row."""
        cue_idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        has_error = _cue_has_error(cue)
        tree.item(
            row_id,
            tags=(_row_tag_for(cue_idx, has_error, related=related),),
        )

    def _clear_selection_related_subtitle_highlights():
        """Clear subtitle rows highlighted by Selection Bin relation."""
        if not _selection_related_subtitle_iids:
            return

        for row_id in list(_selection_related_subtitle_iids):
            try:
                _set_subtitle_row_related(row_id, False)
            except Exception:
                pass

        _selection_related_subtitle_iids.clear()

    def _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections():
        """Highlight subtitle cues overlapping selected clip section(s).

        This is intentionally one-way:
        Selection Bin click → Subtitle Bin visual highlight.

        Subtitle Bin clicks do not affect Selection Bin.
        No data is changed, filtered, deleted, or synchronized.
        """
        _clear_selection_related_subtitle_highlights()

        selected = section_listbox.curselection()
        if not selected:
            return

        selected_sections = []
        for raw_idx in selected:
            try:
                idx = int(raw_idx)
                if 0 <= idx < len(state.sections):
                    selected_sections.append(state.sections[idx])
            except Exception:
                pass

        if not selected_sections:
            return

        for row_id in tree.get_children():
            cue_idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
            if cue is None:
                continue

            overlaps = False
            for section in selected_sections:
                if _ranges_overlap(
                    cue.start,
                    cue.end,
                    section.start,
                    section.end,
                ):
                    overlaps = True
                    break

            if overlaps:
                _set_subtitle_row_related(row_id, True)
                _selection_related_subtitle_iids.add(row_id)

    def _on_section_selection_change(event=None):
        """Selection Bin selection changed; update subtitle visual relation."""
        _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
    
    def _set_active_cue_text(cue: Cue | None):
        if cue is None:
            active_cue_label.configure(text="")
            return
        active_cue_label.configure(
            text=(
                f"[{cue.index}] "
                f"{_display_time_short(cue.start)} → "
                f"{_display_time_short(cue.end)}  |  "
                f"{cue.text}"
            )
        )

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Inline editing
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _edit_widget = {"widget": None, "save_fn": None}

    def _destroy_edit_widget():
        if _edit_widget["widget"]:
            try:
                _edit_widget["widget"].destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            _edit_widget["widget"] = None
            _edit_widget["save_fn"] = None

    def on_double_click(event):
        _destroy_edit_widget()

        row_id = tree.identify_row(event.y)
        column = tree.identify_column(event.x)
        if not row_id or not column:
            return

        col_idx = int(column.replace("#", "")) - 1
        col_names = ("num", "start", "end", "text")
        if col_idx < 0 or col_idx >= len(col_names):
            return

        col_name = col_names[col_idx]
        if col_name == "num":
            return

        cue_idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        if col_name == "start":
            current_value = seconds_to_display(cue.start)
        elif col_name == "end":
            current_value = seconds_to_display(cue.end)
        else:
            current_value = cue.text

        bbox = tree.bbox(row_id, column)
        if not bbox:
            return
        x, y, w, h = bbox

        if col_name == "text":
            edit_w = tk.Text(
                tree,
                font=theme.FONT_TIMECODE,
                wrap="word",
                bg=theme.CARD_BG,
                fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                insertbackground=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                relief="solid",
                borderwidth=2,
                height=3,
                highlightbackground=theme.ACCENT,
                highlightthickness=1,
                undo=True,
            )
            edit_w.insert("1.0", current_value)
            edit_w.place(x=x, y=y, width=max(w, 300), height=max(h, 72))
            edit_w.focus_set()
            edit_w.tag_add("sel", "1.0", "end")
            edit_w.tag_configure(
                "spell_error",
                underline=True,
                foreground=theme.ERROR,
                underlinefg=theme.ERROR,
            )
            edit_w.after(100, lambda: _apply_spellcheck_tags(edit_w))

            _spell_timer = {"id": None}

            def _on_text_change(event=None):
                if _spell_timer["id"]:
                    try:
                        edit_w.after_cancel(_spell_timer["id"])
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                _spell_timer["id"] = edit_w.after(300, lambda: _apply_spellcheck_tags(edit_w))

            def _on_text_right_click(event):
                try:
                    index = edit_w.index(f"@{event.x},{event.y}")
                    line_start = edit_w.index(f"{index} linestart")
                    line_end = edit_w.index(f"{index} lineend")
                    line_text = edit_w.get(line_start, line_end)

                    col = int(index.split(".")[1])
                    for match in re.finditer(r"[a-zA-Z']+", line_text):
                        if match.start() <= col <= match.end():
                            word = match.group()
                            misspelled = _get_misspelled(word)
                            if not misspelled:
                                return

                            word_start = f"{index.split('.')[0]}.{match.start()}"
                            word_end = f"{index.split('.')[0]}.{match.end()}"
                            suggestions = _get_suggestions(word)
                            if not suggestions:
                                return

                            menu = tk.Menu(
                                edit_w,
                                tearoff=0,
                                bg=theme.CARD_BG,
                                fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                                activebackground=theme.ACCENT,
                                activeforeground=theme.BTN_TEXT,
                                font=theme.FONT_BODY,
                            )

                            for sug in suggestions:
                                menu.add_command(
                                    label=sug,
                                    command=lambda s=sug, ws=word_start, we=word_end: (
                                        edit_w.delete(ws, we),
                                        edit_w.insert(ws, s),
                                        _apply_spellcheck_tags(edit_w),
                                    ),
                                )

                            menu.add_separator()
                            menu.add_command(
                                label=f'Add "{word}" to dictionary',
                                command=lambda w=word: (
                                    user_dict_add(w),
                                    state.log(f"Added to dictionary: {w}"),
                                    _apply_spellcheck_tags(edit_w),
                                ),
                            )
                            menu.add_command(label="Ignore", command=menu.destroy)
                            menu.tk_popup(event.x_root, event.y_root)
                            break
                except Exception:
                    pass

            edit_w.bind("<KeyRelease>", _on_text_change)
            if platform.system() == "Darwin":
                edit_w.bind("<Button-2>", _on_text_right_click)
            else:
                edit_w.bind("<Button-3>", _on_text_right_click)

        else:
            edit_w = tk.Entry(
                tree,
                font=theme.FONT_TIMECODE,
                bg=theme.CARD_BG,
                fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                relief="solid",
                borderwidth=2,
                highlightbackground=theme.ACCENT,
                highlightthickness=1,
            )
            edit_w.insert(0, current_value)
            edit_w.place(x=x, y=y, width=w, height=h)
            edit_w.focus_set()
            edit_w.select_range(0, "end")

        _edit_widget["widget"] = edit_w
        _save_done = {"value": False}

        def save_edit(event=None):
            if _save_done["value"]:
                return
            _save_done["value"] = True

            if col_name == "text":
                new_value = edit_w.get("1.0", "end").strip()
            else:
                new_value = edit_w.get().strip()

            _destroy_edit_widget()
            if not new_value:
                return

            idx = _iid_to_idx.get(row_id)
            if idx is None or idx >= len(state.cues):
                return

            cue = state.cues[idx]
            changed = False
            state.save_undo()

            if col_name == "start":
                old = cue.start
                cue.start = time_to_seconds(new_value)
                if old != cue.start:
                    _log_cue_time_change(cue, "start", old, cue.start)
                    changed = True

            elif col_name == "end":
                old = cue.end
                cue.end = time_to_seconds(new_value)
                if old != cue.end:
                    _log_cue_time_change(cue, "end", old, cue.end)
                    changed = True

            elif col_name == "text":
                old = cue.text
                cue.text = new_value
                if old != new_value:
                    ts = _timestamp()
                    state.log_subtitle(f"[{ts}] Cue {cue.index}: text edited")
                    state.log(f"Cue {cue.index} text updated.")
                    changed = True

            if cue.end <= cue.start:
                state.log(f"[!] Cue {cue.index}: end must be after start.")

            if changed:
                _refresh_tree_row(row_id, idx)
                _set_active_cue_text(cue)
                update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text)

                if col_name in ("start", "end"):
                    try:
                        _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        def cancel_edit(event=None):
            _destroy_edit_widget()

        _edit_widget["save_fn"] = save_edit

        if col_name == "text":
            edit_w.bind("<Control-Return>", save_edit)
            edit_w.bind("<Escape>", cancel_edit)
        else:
            edit_w.bind("<Return>", save_edit)
            edit_w.bind("<Escape>", cancel_edit)

    tree.bind("<Double-1>", on_double_click)

    def on_tree_click(event):
        if _edit_widget["widget"]:
            widget_under = event.widget.winfo_containing(event.x_root, event.y_root)
            if widget_under != _edit_widget["widget"] and _edit_widget["save_fn"]:
                _edit_widget["save_fn"]()

    tree.bind("<Button-1>", on_tree_click)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Tree selection and playback cue selection
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _user_selected_cue = {"value": False}
    _auto_selecting = {"value": False}

    def on_tree_select(event):
        if _auto_selecting["value"]:
            return
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            return
        row_id = sel[0]
        _, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return
        cue_alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
        if cue_alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
            cue_alignment = "center"
            cue.alignment = "center"

        subtitle_align_var.set(subtitle_align_reverse[cue_alignment])
        _set_active_cue_text(cue)
        update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text, cue_alignment)
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = True

    tree.bind("<<TreeviewSelect>>", on_tree_select)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Cue operations
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def on_split_cue():
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            state.log("[!] No cue selected to split.")
            return

        row_id = sel[0]
        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        text = cue.text
        lines = text.split("\n")
        if len(lines) >= 2:
            mid = len(lines) // 2
            text1 = "\n".join(lines[:mid])
            text2 = "\n".join(lines[mid:])
        else:
            words = text.split(" ")
            if len(words) < 2:
                state.log("[!] Cue too short to split.")
                return
            mid = len(words) // 2
            text1 = " ".join(words[:mid])
            text2 = " ".join(words[mid:])

        state.save_undo()
        mid_time = (cue.start + cue.end) / 2.0
        old_end = cue.end
        cue.text = text1
        cue.end = mid_time

        new_cue = Cue(
            cue.index + 1,
            mid_time,
            old_end,
            text2,
            getattr(cue, "alignment", "center"),
        )
        state.cues.insert(idx + 1, new_cue)

        for i, c in enumerate(state.cues):
            c.index = i + 1

        state.log_subtitle(f"[{_timestamp()}] Split cue {cue.index} into two cues")
        populate_subtitle_list()

        children = tree.get_children()
        if idx < len(children):
            tree.selection_set(children[idx])
            tree.see(children[idx])
            _set_active_cue_text(state.cues[idx])
            update_subtitle_overlay(
                state.cues[idx].text,
                getattr(state.cues[idx], "alignment", "center"),
            )

        state.log(f"Cue split into {cue.index} and {cue.index + 1}.")

    def on_add_cue():
        sel = tree.selection()
        if sel:
            row_id = sel[0]
            idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
            if cue is None:
                idx = len(state.cues) - 1
                cue = state.cues[-1] if state.cues else None
        else:
            idx = len(state.cues) - 1
            cue = state.cues[-1] if state.cues else None

        if cue:
            new_start = cue.end
            new_end = cue.end + 3.0
        else:
            new_start = 0.0
            new_end = 3.0

        state.save_undo()
        new_cue = Cue(idx + 2, new_start, new_end, "(new cue)")
        state.cues.insert(idx + 1, new_cue)

        for i, c in enumerate(state.cues):
            c.index = i + 1

        state.log_subtitle(f"[{_timestamp()}] Added new cue at {seconds_to_display(new_start)}")
        populate_subtitle_list()

        children = tree.get_children()
        if idx + 1 < len(children):
            tree.selection_set(children[idx + 1])
            tree.see(children[idx + 1])
            _set_active_cue_text(state.cues[idx + 1])
            update_subtitle_overlay(
                state.cues[idx + 1].text,
                getattr(state.cues[idx + 1], "alignment", "center"),
            )

        state.log(f"New cue at {seconds_to_display(new_start)}. Double-click to edit.")

    def on_delete_cue():
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            state.log("[!] No cue selected.")
            return

        row_id = sel[0]
        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete Cue", f'Delete cue {cue.index}?\n\n"{cue.text[:60]}"'):
            return

        state.save_undo()
        state.log_subtitle(f"[{_timestamp()}] Deleted cue {cue.index}: '{cue.text[:40]}'")
        state.cues.pop(idx)

        for i, c in enumerate(state.cues):
            c.index = i + 1

        populate_subtitle_list()

        children = tree.get_children()
