        if children:
            select_idx = min(idx, len(children) - 1)
            tree.selection_set(children[select_idx])
            tree.see(children[select_idx])
            _set_active_cue_text(state.cues[select_idx])
            update_subtitle_overlay(
                state.cues[select_idx].text,
                getattr(state.cues[select_idx], "alignment", "center"),
            )
        else:
            active_cue_label.configure(text="")
            update_subtitle_overlay("")

        state.log(f"Cue deleted. {len(state.cues)} remaining.")

    btn_split_cue.configure(command=on_split_cue)
    btn_add_cue.configure(command=on_add_cue)
    btn_delete_cue.configure(command=on_delete_cue)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Find/replace
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def on_find_replace():
        find_str = find_entry.get().strip()
        replace_str = replace_entry.get().strip()
        if not find_str:
            state.log("[!] Enter text to find.")
            return

        state.save_undo()
        count = 0
        for cue in state.cues:
            if find_str in cue.text:
                cue.text = cue.text.replace(find_str, replace_str)
                count += 1

        if count > 0:
            state.log_subtitle(
                f"[{_timestamp()}] Find/Replace: '{find_str}' → '{replace_str}' ({count} cues)"
            )
            populate_subtitle_list()
            sel = tree.selection()
            if sel:
                row_id = sel[0]
                _, cue_sel = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
                if cue_sel:
                    _set_active_cue_text(cue_sel)
                    update_subtitle_overlay(
                        cue_sel.text,
                        getattr(cue_sel, "alignment", "center"),
                    )
            state.log(f"Replaced in {count} cue(s).")
        else:
            state.log(f"'{find_str}' not found.")

    btn_find_replace.configure(command=on_find_replace)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Section marking
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    mark_in_time = {"value": None}
    mark_out_time = {"value": None}
    ui["mark_in_time"] = mark_in_time
    ui["mark_out_time"] = mark_out_time

    def _sync_loop_to_player():
        if _loop_state["enabled"]:
            player.set_loop(
                True,
                start=mark_in_time["value"],
                end=mark_out_time["value"],
            )
        else:
            player.clear_loop()

    def _update_loop_button():
        if _loop_state["enabled"]:
            btn_loop.configure(
                fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
                text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
                hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            )
        else:
            btn_loop.configure(
                fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
                text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR,
            )

    def _toggle_loop():
        _loop_state["enabled"] = not _loop_state["enabled"]
        _sync_loop_to_player()
        _update_loop_button()

        if _loop_state["enabled"]:
            state.log("Loop playback enabled.")
        else:
            state.log("Loop playback disabled.")

    def _update_in_out_label():
        in_out_label.configure(
            text=f"IN: {_display_time_short(mark_in_time['value'])}   OUT: {_display_time_short(mark_out_time['value'])}"
        )
        seek_slider.set_marks(
            mark_in=mark_in_time["value"],
            mark_out=mark_out_time["value"]
        )
        _sync_loop_to_player()

    def _on_mark_drag(which, value):
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        if which == "in":
            mark_in_time["value"] = value
        elif which == "out":
            mark_out_time["value"] = value

        _update_in_out_label()

        if not player.playing:
            try:
                player.seek(float(value))
            except Exception:
                pass
            _update_frame_info()

    def _renumber_sections():
        for idx, section in enumerate(state.sections, start=1):
            if not section.label or re.match(r"^Section \d+$", section.label):
                section.label = f"Section {idx}"

    def _refresh_section_list(selected_index=None):
        section_listbox.delete(0, "end")
        for section in state.sections:
            cue_count = sum(
                1 for c in state.cues
                if c.start >= section.start and c.end <= section.end
            )
            section_listbox.insert(
                "end",
                f" {section.label}  |  "
                f"{_display_time_short(section.start)} → "
                f"{_display_time_short(section.end)}  |  "
                f"{section.duration:.1f}s  |  "
                f"{cue_count} cues"
            )

        if selected_index is not None and state.sections:
            safe_index = max(0, min(int(selected_index), len(state.sections) - 1))
            section_listbox.selection_clear(0, "end")
            section_listbox.selection_set(safe_index)
            section_listbox.activate(safe_index)
            section_listbox.see(safe_index)
        
        try:
            _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _get_selected_section_index():
        sel = section_listbox.curselection()
        if not sel:
            return None

        idx = int(sel[0])
        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(state.sections):
            return None

        return idx

    def _clear_in_out_marks():
        had_marks = (
            mark_in_time["value"] is not None or
            mark_out_time["value"] is not None or
            _loop_state["enabled"]
        )

        mark_in_time["value"] = None
        mark_out_time["value"] = None
        _loop_state["enabled"] = False
        _update_in_out_label()
        _update_loop_button()

        if had_marks:
            state.log("IN/OUT marks cleared.")
        else:
            state.log("No IN/OUT marks to clear.")

    ui["refresh_sections"] = _refresh_section_list

    def on_add_section():
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        if mark_in_time["value"] is None or mark_out_time["value"] is None:
            state.log("[!] Set both IN and OUT marks first.")
            return

        start = mark_in_time["value"]
        end = mark_out_time["value"]
        if end <= start:
            state.log("[!] OUT must be after IN.")
            return

        section = Section(start, end, f"Section {len(state.sections) + 1}")
        state.sections.append(section)
        _refresh_section_list(len(state.sections) - 1)

        mark_in_time["value"] = None
        mark_out_time["value"] = None
        _update_in_out_label()

        state.log_session(
            f"[{_timestamp()}] Added {section.label}: "
            f"{seconds_to_display(start)} → {seconds_to_display(end)}"
        )
        state.log(f"Added {section.label}")

    def on_delete_section():
        idx = _get_selected_section_index()
        if idx is None:
            return

        removed = state.sections.pop(idx)
        _renumber_sections()

        next_index = min(idx, len(state.sections) - 1) if state.sections else None
        _refresh_section_list(next_index)

        state.log_session(
            f"[{_timestamp()}] Deleted {removed.label}: "
            f"{seconds_to_display(removed.start)} → {seconds_to_display(removed.end)}"
        )
        state.log(f"Deleted {removed.label}")

    def on_move_section_up():
        idx = _get_selected_section_index()
        if idx is None or idx <= 0:
            return

        state.sections[idx - 1], state.sections[idx] = (
            state.sections[idx],
            state.sections[idx - 1],
        )
        _renumber_sections()
        _refresh_section_list(idx - 1)
        state.log(f"Moved {state.sections[idx - 1].label} up.")

    def on_move_section_down():
        idx = _get_selected_section_index()
        if idx is None or idx >= len(state.sections) - 1:
            return

        state.sections[idx + 1], state.sections[idx] = (
            state.sections[idx],
            state.sections[idx + 1],
        )
        _renumber_sections()
        _refresh_section_list(idx + 1)
        state.log(f"Moved {state.sections[idx + 1].label} down.")

    btn_add_section.configure(command=on_add_section)
    btn_clear_marks.configure(command=_clear_in_out_marks)
    btn_move_section_up.configure(command=on_move_section_up)
    btn_move_section_down.configure(command=on_move_section_down)
    btn_delete_section.configure(command=on_delete_section)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Tree/list adapter for player highlight compatibility
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    class TreeListboxAdapter:
        def __init__(self, treeview):
            self.tree = treeview

        def selection_clear(self, start, end):
            for item in self.tree.selection():
                self.tree.selection_remove(item)

        def selection_set(self, index):
            children = self.tree.get_children()
            if 0 <= index < len(children):
                self.tree.selection_set(children[index])

        def see(self, index):
            children = self.tree.get_children()
            if 0 <= index < len(children):
                self.tree.see(children[index])

        def curselection(self):
            sel = self.tree.selection()
            if not sel:
                return ()
            children = self.tree.get_children()
            for i, child in enumerate(children):
                if child == sel[0]:
                    return (i,)
            return ()

    ui["sub_listbox"] = TreeListboxAdapter(tree)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Player wiring
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # MPV/libmpv preview backend is required in the v5 packaged app.
    # The legacy OpenCV FramePlayer fallback has been removed to avoid
    # shipping opencv-python as a runtime dependency.
    if MpvPlayer is None:
        msg = (
            "MPV preview backend is unavailable.\n\n"
            "Expected python-mpv and bundled libmpv, for example:\n"
            "bin/windows/mpv-2.dll, mpv-1.dll, or libmpv-2.dll."
        )
        state.log("[ERROR] MPV preview backend could not be imported.")
        state.log(
            "[ERROR] Expected python-mpv and bundled libmpv. "
            "Legacy OpenCV fallback is no longer included."
        )
        raise RuntimeError(msg)

    try:
        if diagnose_mpv_environment is not None:
            ok, msg = diagnose_mpv_environment(
                getattr(state, "app_dir", None)
            )
            if ok:
                state.log(f"[OK] MPV diagnostics: {msg}")
            else:
                state.log(f"[!] MPV diagnostics: {msg}")

        player = MpvPlayer(video_canvas, state, ui)
        state.log("[OK] Using MPV preview backend.")

    except Exception as mpv_error:
        state.log(f"[ERROR] MPV preview unavailable: {mpv_error}")
        state.log(
            "[ERROR] Expected python-mpv and bundled libmpv. "
            "Legacy OpenCV fallback is no longer included."
        )
        raise RuntimeError(
            "MPV preview backend failed to initialize. "
            "Check python-mpv and bundled libmpv files."
        ) from mpv_error

    ui["player"] = player

    _play_state = {"playing": False}
    _transport_guard = {"busy": False}
    _enhance_state = {"busy": False}
    _audio_switch_state = {"busy": False}
    _scrub_state = {"paused_for_scrub": False}
    _mpv_waveform_state = {
        "busy": False,
        "ready": False,
        "video_path": None,
        "last_wait_log": 0.0,
    }

    def _release_transport_guard():
        _transport_guard["busy"] = False

    def _lock_media_controls(message="Loading media..."):
        """Prevent playback/editing while MPV media and waveform prepare."""
        _transport_guard["busy"] = True
        _play_state["playing"] = False
        _update_play_btn_icon()
        _set_transport_controls_enabled(False)

        try:
            if hasattr(player, "show_status_text"):
                player.show_status_text(message, 3000, center=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _unlock_media_controls():
        """Unlock controls after video + waveform preparation completes."""
        _transport_guard["busy"] = False
        _set_transport_controls_enabled(True)
        _play_state["playing"] = False
        _update_play_btn_icon()
        _update_enhance_audio_button()
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

    def _restore_shortcut_focus():
        try:
            video_canvas.after_idle(video_canvas.focus_set)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _update_play_btn_icon():
        if _play_state["playing"]:
            btn_play_pause.configure(text=f"{theme.ICON_PAUSE} Pause")
        else:
            btn_play_pause.configure(text=f"{theme.ICON_PLAY} Play")

    def _get_audio_status_text():
        if _enhance_state["busy"]:
            return "Audio: enhancing..."

        if _audio_switch_state["busy"]:
            return "Audio: switching..."
        
        if state.active_audio_path and state.enhanced_audio_path:
            if os.path.abspath(state.active_audio_path) == os.path.abspath(state.enhanced_audio_path):
                return "Audio: enhanced active"

        if state.active_audio_path and state.source_audio_path:
            if os.path.abspath(state.active_audio_path) == os.path.abspath(state.source_audio_path):
                return "Audio: source active"

        if state.source_audio_path and os.path.isfile(state.source_audio_path):
            return "Audio: source ready"

        return "Audio: not loaded"

    def _update_audio_status_label():
        try:
            audio_status_label.configure(text=_get_audio_status_text())
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _update_working_audio_control():
        """Show Enhance button before enhancement; show Source/Enhanced dropdown after."""
        has_enhanced = bool(state.has_enhanced_audio())

        try:
            if has_enhanced:
                if btn_enhance_audio.winfo_ismapped():
                    btn_enhance_audio.pack_forget()

                if not working_audio_menu.winfo_ismapped():
                    working_audio_menu.pack(
                        side="right",
                        padx=(12, 0),
                        before=audio_status_label,
                    )

                if state.active_audio_path and state.enhanced_audio_path:
                    if os.path.abspath(state.active_audio_path) == os.path.abspath(state.enhanced_audio_path):
                        working_audio_var.set("Enhanced")
                    else:
                        working_audio_var.set("Original")
                else:
                    working_audio_var.set("Original")

                working_audio_menu.configure(
                    state="disabled" if _audio_switch_state["busy"] or _enhance_state["busy"] else "normal"
                )

            else:
                if working_audio_menu.winfo_ismapped():
                    working_audio_menu.pack_forget()

                if not btn_enhance_audio.winfo_ismapped():
                    btn_enhance_audio.pack(
                        side="right",
                        padx=(12, 0),
                        before=audio_status_label,
                    )

        except Exception:
            pass
    
    def _update_enhance_audio_button():
        ready = (
            not _enhance_state["busy"]
            and not _audio_switch_state["busy"]
            and bool(state.source_audio_path)
            and os.path.isfile(state.source_audio_path)
            and player.loaded
            and player._proxy_ready
            and player._buffer_ready
        )

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            ready = ready and bool(_mpv_waveform_state.get("ready"))

        if ready:
            enable_btn(btn_enhance_audio)
        else:
            disable_btn(btn_enhance_audio)

        _update_audio_status_label()
        _update_working_audio_control()

    def _update_frame_info():
        if player.loaded:
            fd = player.get_frame_duration()
            if fd > 0:
                seek_slider.set_snap_step(fd)
                if not player.playing:
                    frame_num = int(player.position / fd)
                    fps = round(1.0 / fd)
                    frame_info.configure(text=f"Frame {frame_num} @ {fps}fps")
                else:
                    frame_info.configure(text="")
            else:
                frame_info.configure(text="")
        else:
            frame_info.configure(text="")

    def _init_player():
        if state.video_path and state.video_info and not player.loaded:
            _set_transport_controls_enabled(False)
            player.load(state.video_path, state.video_info.get("duration", 0))
            seek_slider.set_snap_step(player.get_frame_duration())

    def _unlock_time_display(event=None):
        if _seek_editing["active"]:
            return "break"
        _seek_editing["active"] = True
        current_text = seconds_to_display(player.get_position() if player.loaded else 0.0)
        seek_entry.place(in_=time_label, x=0, y=0, relwidth=1.0, relheight=1.0)
        seek_entry.configure(state="normal")
        seek_entry.delete(0, "end")
        seek_entry.insert(0, current_text)
        seek_entry.focus_set()
        seek_entry.select_range(0, "end")
        seek_entry.lift()
        return "break"

    # Current playback time is display-only under the MPV workflow.
    # Use the dedicated Seek field for manual timecode seeking.
    # time_label.bind("<Button-1>", _unlock_time_display)

    def _lock_time_display(event=None):
        _seek_editing["active"] = False
        try:
            seek_entry.selection_clear()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            seek_entry.icursor("end")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            seek_entry.place_forget()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            seek_entry.configure(state="disabled")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            root = parent.winfo_toplevel()
            root.focus_set()
            root.after_idle(video_canvas.focus_set)
        except Exception:
            try:
                video_canvas.focus_set()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _seek_to_value(raw_value: str):
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        val = raw_value.strip()
        val = re.sub(r"[^0-9:\.]", "", val)
        val = re.sub(r":{2,}", ":", val)
        if not val:
            return

        _close_vol_popup()
        _init_player()

        try:
            seconds = time_to_seconds(val)
            player.seek(seconds)
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
            _update_frame_info()
            state.log(f"Seek: {val}")
        except Exception:
            state.log(f"[!] Invalid timecode: {val}")

    def _on_seek_enter(event=None):
        val = seek_entry.get().strip()
        _lock_time_display()
        if val:
            _seek_to_value(val)

    def _deactivate_seek_go_entry(event=None):
        _seek_go_editing["active"] = False

        try:
            seek_go_entry.selection_clear()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            seek_go_entry.icursor("end")
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            seek_go_entry.configure(state="disabled")
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            root = parent.winfo_toplevel()
            root.focus_set()
            root.after_idle(video_canvas.focus_set)
        except Exception:
            try:
                video_canvas.focus_set()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _on_seek_go(event=None):
        _seek_to_value(seek_go_entry.get())
        _deactivate_seek_go_entry()

    seek_entry.bind("<Return>", _on_seek_enter)
    seek_entry.bind("<Escape>", _lock_time_display)
    seek_entry.bind("<FocusOut>", _lock_time_display)
    seek_go_entry.bind("<Return>", _on_seek_go)
    seek_go_entry.bind("<Escape>", _deactivate_seek_go_entry)
    btn_seek_go.configure(command=_on_seek_go)

    def _prime_seek_go_entry(event=None):
        _seek_go_editing["active"] = True

        try:
            seek_go_entry.configure(state="normal")
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            current_text = time_label.cget("text").strip()
            seek_go_entry.delete(0, "end")
            seek_go_entry.insert(0, current_text if current_text else "00:00:00")
            seek_go_entry.focus_set()
            seek_go_entry.select_range(0, "end")
        except Exception:
            pass

        return "break"

    # Manual seek is intentional: double-click to edit.
    seek_go_entry.bind("<Double-Button-1>", _prime_seek_go_entry)
    seek_go_entry.bind("<FocusOut>", _deactivate_seek_go_entry)

    def _on_preview_res_change(value):
        _preview_res["value"] = value
        state.log(f"Preview resolution: {value}")

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            state.log("MPV preview handles playback resolution automatically.")
            return

        if player.loaded and player._proxy_ready:
            state.log("Rebuilding preview at new resolution...")
            _set_transport_controls_enabled(False)
            player._buffer_ready = False
            player._proxy_ready = False
            player._buffer_cancel = True

            def _rebuild():
                player._buffer_cancel = False
                state.set_progress(5, f"Rebuilding {value} preview...")
                player._create_proxy()
                if player._proxy_ready:
                    player._buffer_proxy_frames()

                if player._proxy_ready and player._buffer_ready:
                    video_canvas.after(0, _on_preview_ready)
                else:
                    video_canvas.after(0, lambda: _set_transport_controls_enabled(False))

            threading.Thread(target=_rebuild, daemon=True).start()

    preview_dropdown.configure(command=_on_preview_res_change)

    def _ensure_mpv_waveform():
        """Extract source audio and generate waveform for MPV backend.

        MPV handles playback A/V sync directly, but Subtitle Studio still needs
        a waveform image for the seek slider and source audio path for later
        enhancement workflow.
        """
        if player.__class__.__name__ != "MpvPlayer":
            return

        video_path = getattr(player, "video_path", None) or state.video_path
        if not video_path or not os.path.isfile(video_path):
            return

        if _mpv_waveform_state["busy"]:
            _lock_media_controls("Preparing waveform...")
            return

        if (
            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"]
            and _mpv_waveform_state["video_path"] == video_path
        ):
            _unlock_media_controls()
            return

        _mpv_waveform_state["busy"] = True
        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = False
        _mpv_waveform_state["video_path"] = video_path
        _lock_media_controls("Preparing waveform...")

        def worker():
            try:
                import subprocess
                import tempfile

                temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
                waveform_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, "subtitle_studio_waveform.png")

                # Preflight: if the video has no audio stream, this is not an
                # error. MPV can still play silent video, but waveform/enhance
                # audio should remain unavailable.
                try:
                    probe_cmd = [
                        state.ffprobe_path,
                        "-v", "error",
                        "-select_streams", "a:0",
                        "-show_entries", "stream=index",
                        "-of", "csv=p=0",
                        video_path,
                    ]

                    probe_result = subprocess.run(
                        probe_cmd,
                        capture_output=True,
                        text=True,
                        timeout=30,
                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
                    )

                    has_audio = (
                        probe_result.returncode == 0
                        and bool((probe_result.stdout or "").strip())
                    )

                    if not has_audio:
                        state.clear_audio_state()
                        player._audio_path = None
                        player._audio_ready = False

                        def _unlock_no_audio():
                            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                            _unlock_media_controls()
                            _update_enhance_audio_button()

                        state.log("[!] No audio track found. Waveform unavailable; silent preview.")
                        video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_no_audio)
                        return

                except Exception as probe_error:
                    # If probing fails, continue to extraction so existing
                    # ffmpeg behavior can handle/report the file.
                    state.log(f"[!] Audio probe failed; attempting extraction anyway. ({probe_error})")

                audio_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                    prefix="subtitle_studio_audio_",
                    suffix=".wav",
                    delete=False,
                    dir=temp_dir,
                )
                audio_file.close()
                audio_path = audio_file.name

                try:
                    os.remove(audio_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass

                cmd = [
                    state.ffmpeg_path,
                    "-i", video_path,
                    "-map", "0:a:0?",
                    "-vn",
                    "-sn",
                    "-dn",
                    "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
                    "-ar", "44100",
                    "-ac", "2",
                    "-y",
                    audio_path,
                ]

                state.log("Extracting audio for waveform...")
                state.set_progress(82, "Extracting audio...")

                result = subprocess.run(
                    cmd,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=120,
                    creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
                )

                if result.returncode != 0 or not os.path.isfile(audio_path):
                    err = result.stderr[-500:].strip() if result.stderr else "unknown"

                    no_audio_error = (
                        "Output file does not contain any stream" in err
                        or "matches no streams" in err
                        or "Stream map" in err
                    )

                    if no_audio_error:
                        state.clear_audio_state()
                        player._audio_path = None
                        player._audio_ready = False
                        state.log("[!] No audio track found. Waveform unavailable; silent preview.")
                    else:
                        state.log(f"[!] Audio extraction failed for waveform: {err}")

                    def _unlock_after_waveform_failure():
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                        _unlock_media_controls()
                        _update_enhance_audio_button()

                    video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_after_waveform_failure)
                    return

                file_mb = os.path.getsize(audio_path) / (1024 * 1024)
                if file_mb <= 0.001:
                    state.log("[!] No usable audio track found for waveform.")
                    def _unlock_after_waveform_failure():
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                        _unlock_media_controls()

                    video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_after_waveform_failure)
                    return

                state.set_source_audio(audio_path)
                player._audio_path = state.active_audio_path or audio_path
                player._audio_ready = True

                if hasattr(player, "set_external_audio"):
                    player.set_external_audio(player._audio_path)

                video_canvas.after(0, _update_enhance_audio_button)
                state.log(f"[OK] Source audio ready for waveform ({file_mb:.1f} MB).")
                state.log("Generating waveform...")
                state.set_progress(88, "Generating waveform...")

                try:
                    if os.path.isfile(waveform_path):
                        try:
                            os.remove(waveform_path)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

                    wf_cmd = [
                        state.ffmpeg_path,
                        "-i", audio_path,
                        "-filter_complex",
                        (
                            "aformat=channel_layouts=mono,"
                            "dynaudnorm=f=150:g=15,"
                            "showwavespic=s=2400x160:"
                            "colors=#E65100|#FF8A40"
                        ),
                        "-frames:v", "1",
                        "-y",
                        waveform_path,
                    ]

                    wf_result = subprocess.run(
                        wf_cmd,
                        capture_output=True,
                        text=True,
                        timeout=60,
                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
                    )

                    if wf_result.returncode == 0 and os.path.isfile(waveform_path):
                        def _load_waveform_done(p=waveform_path):
                            seek_slider.load_waveform(p)
                            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                            _unlock_media_controls()

                            try:
                                if hasattr(player, "show_status_text"):
                                    player.show_status_text("", 1, center=True)
                            except Exception:
                                pass

                        video_canvas.after(0, _load_waveform_done)
                        state.set_progress(95, "Waveform loaded")
                        state.log("[OK] Waveform loaded.")
                    else:
                        err = wf_result.stderr[-300:].strip() if wf_result.stderr else "unknown"
                        state.log(f"[!] Waveform failed: {err}")

                        def _unlock_after_waveform_failure():
                            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                            _unlock_media_controls()

                        video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_after_waveform_failure)

                except Exception as wf_e:
                    state.log(f"[!] Waveform error: {wf_e}")

                    def _unlock_after_waveform_failure():
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                        _unlock_media_controls()

                    video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_after_waveform_failure)

            except Exception as e:
                state.log(f"[!] MPV waveform setup failed: {e}")

                def _unlock_after_waveform_failure():
                    _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                    _unlock_media_controls()

                video_canvas.after(0, _unlock_after_waveform_failure)

            finally:
                _mpv_waveform_state["busy"] = False
                video_canvas.after(2000, state.clear_progress)

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()
    
    def _generate_waveform_from_audio(audio_path, progress_label="Generating waveform...", done_label="[OK] Waveform loaded."):
        """Generate seek-slider waveform from an existing audio file."""
        if not audio_path or not os.path.isfile(audio_path):
            state.log("[!] Cannot generate waveform: audio file missing.")
            return False

        try:
            import subprocess
            import tempfile

            waveform_path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "subtitle_studio_waveform.png")

            if os.path.isfile(waveform_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(waveform_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            state.log(progress_label)
            state.set_progress(88, progress_label)

            wf_cmd = [
                state.ffmpeg_path,
                "-i", audio_path,
                "-filter_complex",
                (
                    "aformat=channel_layouts=mono,"
                    "dynaudnorm=f=150:g=15,"
                    "showwavespic=s=2400x160:"
                    "colors=#E65100|#FF8A40"
                ),
                "-frames:v", "1",
                "-y",
                waveform_path,
            ]

            wf_result = subprocess.run(
                wf_cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=60,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
            )

            if wf_result.returncode == 0 and os.path.isfile(waveform_path):
                video_canvas.after(0, lambda p=waveform_path: seek_slider.load_waveform(p))
                state.set_progress(95, "Waveform loaded")
                state.log(done_label)
                return True

            err = wf_result.stderr[-300:].strip() if wf_result.stderr else "unknown"
            state.log(f"[!] Waveform failed: {err}")
            return False

        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Waveform error: {e}")
            return False
    

    def _switch_working_audio(choice):
        """Switch preview/waveform between source and enhanced audio."""
        if _enhance_state["busy"] or _audio_switch_state["busy"]:
            return

        normalized = (choice or "").strip().lower()

        if normalized.startswith("enhanced"):
            if not state.has_enhanced_audio():
                state.log("[!] Enhanced audio is not available yet.")
                working_audio_var.set("Original")
                return

            target_label = "Enhanced"
            target_path = state.enhanced_audio_path
            use_fn = state.use_enhanced_audio

        else:
            if not state.source_audio_path or not os.path.isfile(state.source_audio_path):
                state.log("[!] Original/source audio is not available.")
                if state.has_enhanced_audio():
                    working_audio_var.set("Enhanced")
                return

            target_label = "Original"
            target_path = state.source_audio_path
            use_fn = state.use_source_audio

        if not target_path or not os.path.isfile(target_path):
            state.log(f"[!] {target_label} file is missing.")
            return

        try:
            if (
                state.active_audio_path
                and os.path.abspath(state.active_audio_path) == os.path.abspath(target_path)
            ):
                working_audio_var.set(target_label)
                _update_audio_status_label()
                _update_working_audio_control()
                return
        except Exception:
            pass

        was_playing = bool(player.playing)

        if was_playing:
            player.pause()
            _play_state["playing"] = False
            _update_play_btn_icon()

        _audio_switch_state["busy"] = True

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = False

        _set_transport_controls_enabled(False)
        _update_audio_status_label()
        _update_working_audio_control()
        state.set_progress(20, f"Switching to {target_label.lower()}...")
        state.log(f"Switching working audio to {target_label.lower()}...")

        def worker():
            try:
                use_fn()
                player._audio_path = state.active_audio_path or target_path
                player._audio_ready = True

                if hasattr(player, "set_external_audio"):
                    player.set_external_audio(player._audio_path)
                else:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "MPV external audio support is unavailable."
                    )

                _generate_waveform_from_audio(
                    player._audio_path,
                    progress_label=f"Generating waveform from {target_label.lower()}...",
                    done_label=f"[OK] {target_label} waveform loaded.",
                )

                def finish():
                    if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True

                    _audio_switch_state["busy"] = False
                    working_audio_var.set(target_label)
                    _unlock_media_controls()
                    _update_audio_status_label()
                    _update_working_audio_control()

                    if was_playing:
                        player.play()
                        _play_state["playing"] = True
                    else:
                        _play_state["playing"] = False

                    _update_play_btn_icon()
                    state.set_progress(100, f"{target_label} active")
                    video_canvas.after(2000, state.clear_progress)
                    state.log(f"[OK] Working audio: {target_label.lower()}")

                video_canvas.after(0, finish)

            except Exception as e:
                def fail():
                    if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True

                    _audio_switch_state["busy"] = False
                    _unlock_media_controls()
                    _update_audio_status_label()
                    _update_working_audio_control()
                    state.log(f"[!] Working audio switch failed: {e}")
                    video_canvas.after(2000, state.clear_progress)

                video_canvas.after(0, fail)

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()
    
    def _set_transport_controls_enabled(enabled):
        state_value = "normal" if enabled else "disabled"

        if enabled:
            if player.loaded:
                enable_btn(btn_play_pause)
            enable_btn(btn_stop)
            _update_enhance_audio_button()
        else:
            disable_btn(btn_play_pause)
            disable_btn(btn_stop)
            disable_btn(btn_enhance_audio)

        for button in (
            btn_frame_back,
            btn_frame_fwd,
            btn_mark_in,
            btn_mark_out,
            btn_clear_marks,
            btn_add_section,
            btn_sub_in,
            btn_sub_out,
            btn_seek_go,
            vol_btn,
            btn_loop,
            working_audio_menu,
        ):
            try:
                button.configure(state=state_value)
            except Exception:
                pass

        for widget in (
            preview_dropdown,
        ):
            try:
                widget.configure(state=state_value)
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            if not _seek_go_editing["active"]:
                seek_go_entry.configure(state="disabled")
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            seek_slider.configure(cursor="hand2" if enabled else "watch")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _preview_is_ready_for_user_actions():
        if _enhance_state["busy"] or _audio_switch_state["busy"]:
            return False

        if not (
            player.loaded
            and bool(getattr(player, "_proxy_ready", False))
            and bool(getattr(player, "_buffer_ready", False))
        ):
            return False

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            return bool(_mpv_waveform_state["ready"])

        return True

    def _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position():
        try:
            seek_slider.set(player.get_position() if player.loaded else 0.0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _block_if_media_busy():
        if _enhance_state["busy"]:
            state.log("[!] Please wait. Audio enhancement is still in progress.")
            _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position()
            return True

        if _audio_switch_state["busy"]:
            state.log("[!] Please wait. Working audio is switching.")
            _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position()
            return True
        
        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            if not player.loaded or not getattr(player, "_proxy_ready", False):
                state.log("[!] Video still loading — please wait.")
                _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position()
                return True

            if not _mpv_waveform_state["ready"]:
                try:
                    import time
                    now = time.time()
                    if now - _mpv_waveform_state.get("last_wait_log", 0.0) > 2.0:
                        state.log("[!] Waveform still preparing — please wait.")
                        _mpv_waveform_state["last_wait_log"] = now
                except Exception:
                    state.log("[!] Waveform still preparing — please wait.")

                _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position()
                return True

        elif not _preview_is_ready_for_user_actions():
            state.log("[!] Preview still preparing — please wait.")
            _reset_seek_slider_to_player_position()
            return True

        return False
    
    def _on_preview_ready():
        _play_state["playing"] = False
        _update_play_btn_icon()
        _update_enhance_audio_button()

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            # Do not unlock yet. MPV video is ready, but the app workflow
            # requires waveform/source-audio prep before user interaction.
            _ensure_mpv_waveform()
            return

        _set_transport_controls_enabled(True)
        state.set_progress(100, "Ready")
        video_canvas.after(2000, state.clear_progress)

    ui["on_preview_ready"] = _on_preview_ready

    def on_enhance_audio():
        state.log("Enhance Audio pressed.")

        if not state.source_audio_path or not os.path.isfile(state.source_audio_path):
            state.log("[!] No source audio available to enhance.")
            disable_btn(btn_enhance_audio)
            return

        if _enhance_state["busy"]:
            state.log("[!] Audio enhancement already in progress.")
            return

        if not _preview_is_ready_for_user_actions():
            state.log("[!] Preview is still preparing. Please wait before enhancing audio.")
            _update_enhance_audio_button()
            return

        if state.has_enhanced_audio():
            choice = messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                "Enhance Audio",
                "Enhanced audio already exists for this session.\n\n"
                "Yes = Re-run enhancement\n"
                "No = Reuse existing enhanced audio\n"
                "Cancel = Do nothing"
            )

            if choice is None:
                return

            if choice is False:
                def _reuse_existing_enhanced():
                    try:
                        state.use_enhanced_audio()
                        player._audio_path = state.active_audio_path
                        player._audio_ready = bool(player._audio_path)

                        if hasattr(player, "set_external_audio"):
                            player.set_external_audio(player._audio_path)
                        else:
                            raise RuntimeError(
                                "MPV external audio support is unavailable."
                            )

                        player._audio_ready = True

                        state.log("[OK] Reusing existing enhanced audio.")

                        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = False
                            video_canvas.after(0, lambda: _set_transport_controls_enabled(False))

                        _generate_waveform_from_audio(
                            player._audio_path,
                            progress_label="Generating waveform from enhanced audio...",
                            done_label="[OK] Enhanced waveform loaded.",
                        )

                        def _finish_reuse():
                            if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                                _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True
                                _unlock_media_controls()

                            working_audio_var.set("Enhanced")
                            _update_enhance_audio_button()
                            _update_working_audio_control()

                        video_canvas.after(0, _finish_reuse)

                    except Exception as reuse_error:
                        state.log(f"[!] Could not reuse enhanced audio: {reuse_error}")

                threading.Thread(target=_reuse_existing_enhanced, daemon=True).start()
                return

        if player.playing:
            player.stop()
            _play_state["playing"] = False
            _update_play_btn_icon()
            update_subtitle_overlay("")
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
            state.log("Playback stopped for audio enhancement.")

        _enhance_state["busy"] = True
        _set_transport_controls_enabled(False)
        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = False
        btn_enhance_audio.configure(text="Enhancing...")
        state.set_progress(10, "Enhancing audio...")

        def _enhance_worker():
            try:
                from core.audio_enhance import enhance_audio

                state.log("Enhancing audio...")

                result = enhance_audio(
                    state.source_audio_path,
                    ffmpeg_path=state.ffmpeg_path,
                    app_dir=state.app_dir,
                )

                if not result.get("success"):
                    state.log(f"[!] {result.get('message', 'Audio enhancement failed.')}")
                    return

                for stage_msg in result.get("stages", []):
                    state.log(f"[OK] {stage_msg}")

                output_path = result.get("output_path")
                if not output_path or not os.path.isfile(output_path):
                    state.log("[!] Enhancement completed but no output file was produced.")
                    return

                state.set_enhanced_audio(output_path, make_active=True)

                player._audio_path = state.active_audio_path or output_path
                player._audio_ready = False

                if hasattr(player, "set_external_audio"):
                    player.set_external_audio(player._audio_path)
                else:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "MPV external audio support is unavailable."
                    )

                player._audio_ready = True

                state.log(f"[OK] Enhanced audio ready: {os.path.basename(output_path)}")
                _generate_waveform_from_audio(
                    player._audio_path,
                    progress_label="Generating waveform from enhanced audio...",
                    done_label="[OK] Enhanced waveform loaded.",
                )

            except Exception as e:
                state.log(f"[!] Enhance Audio failed: {e}")

            finally:
                def _finish():
                    try:
                        if player.loaded:
                            player.stop()
                            player.seek(0.0)
                            seek_slider.set(0.0)
                            update_subtitle_overlay("")
                            _update_frame_info()
                            state.log("Playhead reset to start after audio enhancement.")
                    except Exception as reset_error:
                        state.log(f"[!] Could not reset playhead after enhancement: {reset_error}")

                    if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                        _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = True

                    _enhance_state["busy"] = False
                    btn_enhance_audio.configure(text=f"{theme.ICON_ADD} Enhance Audio")

                    if state.has_enhanced_audio():
                        working_audio_var.set("Enhanced")

                    _set_transport_controls_enabled(True)
                    _update_audio_status_label()
                    _update_working_audio_control()
                    state.set_progress(100, "Enhancement complete")
                    video_canvas.after(2000, state.clear_progress)

                video_canvas.after(0, _finish)

        threading.Thread(target=_enhance_worker, daemon=True).start()

    btn_enhance_audio.configure(command=on_enhance_audio)
    working_audio_menu.configure(command=_switch_working_audio)

    def on_play_pause():
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        if getattr(seek_slider, "_dragging", False):
            return

        if getattr(seek_slider, "_dragging_marker", None):
            return

        if _transport_guard["busy"]:
            return

        _transport_guard["busy"] = True
        video_canvas.after(150, _release_transport_guard)

        if player.playing:
            player.pause()
            _play_state["playing"] = False
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
            _update_play_btn_icon()
            _update_frame_info()
            _restore_shortcut_focus()
            return

        _init_player()

        # Play/Pause must always use the current playhead position.
        # Playing from a cue is handled explicitly by Ctrl+Click only.
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False

        player.play()
        _play_state["playing"] = True
        _update_play_btn_icon()
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

    def on_stop():
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

        if _enhance_state["busy"] or _audio_switch_state["busy"]:
            return
        
        if _transport_guard["busy"]:
            return

        _transport_guard["busy"] = True
        video_canvas.after(150, _release_transport_guard)

        player.stop()
        _play_state["playing"] = False
        _update_play_btn_icon()
        update_subtitle_overlay("")
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        for item in tree.selection():
            tree.selection_remove(item)
        _update_frame_info()
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

    btn_play_pause.configure(command=on_play_pause)
    btn_stop.configure(command=on_stop)
    ui["on_play_pause"] = on_play_pause

    def on_slider_scrub(value):
        """During drag.

        MPV can live-seek while playing without losing A/V sync.
        Legacy player still pauses during scrub to avoid drift.
        """
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        _close_vol_popup()

        try:
            value = float(value)
        except Exception:
            return

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            player.seek(value)
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
            _update_frame_info()
            return

        if player.playing:
            player.pause()
            _play_state["playing"] = False
            _update_play_btn_icon()
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False

            if not _scrub_state["paused_for_scrub"]:
                state.log("Playback paused for timeline scrub. Release and press Space to resume.")

            _scrub_state["paused_for_scrub"] = True

    def on_slider_commit(value):
        """On mouse release/click: commit seek."""
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        _close_vol_popup()

        try:
            value = float(value)
        except Exception:
            return

        if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
            player.seek(value)
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
            _scrub_state["paused_for_scrub"] = False
            _update_frame_info()
            _restore_shortcut_focus()
            return

        if player.playing:
            player.pause()
            _play_state["playing"] = False
            _update_play_btn_icon()
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False

        player.seek(value)
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        _scrub_state["paused_for_scrub"] = False
        _update_frame_info()
        _restore_shortcut_focus()

    seek_slider._command = on_slider_commit
    seek_slider.set_scrub_callback(on_slider_scrub)
    seek_slider.set_mark_change_callback(_on_mark_drag)

    def on_ctrl_click(event):
        row_id = tree.identify_row(event.y)
        if not row_id:
            return

        if _block_if_media_busy() or _transport_guard["busy"]:
            return "break"

        _, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue:
            _init_player()

            if player.playing:
                player.pause()
                _play_state["playing"] = False
                _update_play_btn_icon()

            player.seek(cue.start)
            _user_selected_cue["value"] = False

            player.play()
            _play_state["playing"] = True
            _update_play_btn_icon()
            _restore_shortcut_focus()

            state.log(f"Playing from cue {cue.index}")
            return "break"

    tree.bind("<Control-Button-1>", on_ctrl_click)

    def _get_current_volume():
        try:
            if hasattr(player, "get_volume"):
                return player.get_volume()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return 0.8

    def _set_current_volume(value):
        try:
            value = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(value)))

            if hasattr(player, "set_volume"):
                player.set_volume(value)

        except Exception:
            pass

    def _get_current_muted():
        try:
            if hasattr(player, "get_muted"):
                return player.get_muted()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False

    def _set_current_muted(muted):
        try:
            if hasattr(player, "set_muted"):
                player.set_muted(bool(muted))
            elif muted:
                _set_current_volume(0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _set_vol_icon(value=None, muted=None):
        try:
            if value is None:
                value = _get_current_volume()
            if muted is None:
                muted = _get_current_muted()

            value = float(value or 0.0)

            if muted or value <= 0:
                vol_btn.configure(text="🔇")
            elif value < 0.4:
                vol_btn.configure(text="🔉")
            else:
                vol_btn.configure(text="🔊")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _toggle_mute_from_icon():
        """Left-click speaker icon: mute/unmute."""
        _close_vol_popup()

        current = _get_current_volume()
        muted = _get_current_muted()

        if not muted and current > 0:
            _vol_popup["prev_vol"] = current
            _set_current_muted(True)
            _set_vol_icon(0, True)
        else:
            prev = _vol_popup.get("prev_vol", 0.8)
            if not prev or prev <= 0:
                prev = 0.8

            _set_current_volume(prev)
            _set_current_muted(False)
            _set_vol_icon(prev, False)

        _restore_shortcut_focus()

    def _open_volume_popup_event(event=None):
        """Right-click / Shift-click speaker icon: open volume popup."""
        _toggle_vol_popup()
        return "break"
    
    def _toggle_vol_popup():
        if _vol_popup["window"] is not None:
            _close_vol_popup()
            return

        x = vol_btn.winfo_rootx()
        y = vol_btn.winfo_rooty()

        popup = tk.Toplevel(vol_btn)
        popup.wm_overrideredirect(True)
        popup.wm_attributes("-topmost", True)
        popup.configure(bg=theme.CARD_BG)

        popup_w = 50
        popup_h = 180
        popup.wm_geometry(
            f"{popup_w}x{popup_h}+{x - (popup_w // 2) + 18}+{y - popup_h - 5}"
        )

        border = tk.Frame(popup, bg=theme.CARD_BORDER, padx=1, pady=1)
        border.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        inner = tk.Frame(border, bg=theme.CARD_BG)
        inner.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=1, pady=1)

        vol_pct_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
            inner,
            text="80%",
            font=theme.FONT_BODY_BOLD,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            width=40,
        )
        vol_pct_label.pack(pady=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_XS))

        vol_popup_slider = ctk.CTkSlider(
            inner,
            from_=0,
            to=1,
            orientation="vertical",
            width=18,
            height=110,
            fg_color=theme.BORDER,
            progress_color=theme.ACCENT,
            button_color=theme.ACCENT,
            button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        )
        vol_popup_slider.pack(pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

        current_vol = _get_current_volume()

        vol_popup_slider.set(current_vol)
        vol_pct_label.configure(text=f"{int(current_vol * 100)}%")

        def _on_popup_vol_change(value):
            value = float(value)
            pct = int(value * 100)
            vol_pct_label.configure(text=f"{pct}%")

            _set_current_volume(value)

            if value > 0:
                _set_current_muted(False)

            _set_vol_icon(value, value <= 0)

        def _toggle_mute():
            _toggle_mute_from_icon()

            current = _get_current_volume()
            muted = _get_current_muted()

            if muted:
                vol_popup_slider.set(0)
                vol_pct_label.configure(text="0%")
            else:
                vol_popup_slider.set(current)
                vol_pct_label.configure(text=f"{int(current * 100)}%")

        mute_btn = ctk.CTkButton(
            inner,
            text="Mute",
            width=40,
            height=24,
            font=theme.FONT_SMALL,
            fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR,
            corner_radius=4,
            command=_toggle_mute,
        )
        mute_btn.pack(pady=(0, theme.PAD_XS))

        vol_popup_slider.configure(command=_on_popup_vol_change)
        _vol_popup["window"] = popup
        _vol_popup["auto_close"] = None

        def _reset_auto_close():
            if _vol_popup["auto_close"]:
                try:
                    popup.after_cancel(_vol_popup["auto_close"])
                except Exception:
                    pass
            _vol_popup["auto_close"] = popup.after(3000, _close_vol_popup)

        def _on_window_configure(event):
            if event.widget == parent.winfo_toplevel():
                _close_vol_popup()

        def _on_global_click(event):
            if _vol_popup["window"] is None:
                return
            try:
                clicked_widget = event.widget
                popup_widget = _vol_popup["window"]

                current = clicked_widget
                while current is not None:
                    if current == popup_widget or current == vol_btn:
                        _reset_auto_close()
                        return
                    current = current.master
            except Exception:
                pass
            _close_vol_popup()

        vol_popup_slider.bind("<B1-Motion>", lambda e: _reset_auto_close(), add="+")
        vol_popup_slider.bind("<Button-1>", lambda e: _reset_auto_close(), add="+")
        mute_btn.bind("<Button-1>", lambda e: _reset_auto_close(), add="+")
        _reset_auto_close()
        _vol_popup["config_id"] = parent.winfo_toplevel().bind(
            "<Configure>", _on_window_configure, add="+"
        )
        _vol_popup["click_id"] = parent.winfo_toplevel().bind(
            "<Button-1>", _on_global_click, add="+"
        )

    def _close_vol_popup():
        if _vol_popup["window"] is not None:
            if _vol_popup.get("auto_close"):
                try:
                    _vol_popup["window"].after_cancel(_vol_popup["auto_close"])
                except Exception:
                    pass

            try:
                click_id = _vol_popup.get("click_id")
                if click_id:
                    parent.winfo_toplevel().unbind("<Button-1>", click_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                config_id = _vol_popup.get("config_id")
                if config_id:
                    parent.winfo_toplevel().unbind("<Configure>", config_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                _vol_popup["window"].destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass

            _vol_popup["window"] = None
            _vol_popup["auto_close"] = None
            _vol_popup["click_id"] = None
            _vol_popup["config_id"] = None

    # Left-click = mute/unmute.
    # Right-click or Shift-click = open volume popup.
    vol_btn.configure(command=_toggle_mute_from_icon)
    vol_btn.bind("<Button-3>", _open_volume_popup_event)
    vol_btn.bind("<Shift-Button-1>", _open_volume_popup_event)
    btn_loop.configure(command=_toggle_loop)
    _update_loop_button()

    _orig_step = player.step_frames
    _shift_held = {"value": False}
    _space_held = {"value": False}
    root_win = parent.winfo_toplevel()

    root_win.bind("<Shift_L>", lambda e: _shift_held.update({"value": True}), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<Shift_R>", lambda e: _shift_held.update({"value": True}), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<KeyRelease-Shift_L>", lambda e: _shift_held.update({"value": False}), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<KeyRelease-Shift_R>", lambda e: _shift_held.update({"value": False}), add="+")

    def _patched_step(count):
        _orig_step(count)
        _update_frame_info()

    player.step_frames = _patched_step

    def _on_btn_frame_back():
        if _enhance_state["busy"]:
            return
        if not player.loaded or player.playing:
            return
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        player.step_frames(-5 if _shift_held["value"] else -1)

    def _on_btn_frame_fwd():
        if _enhance_state["busy"]:
            return
        if not player.loaded or player.playing:
            return
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        player.step_frames(5 if _shift_held["value"] else 1)

    btn_frame_back.configure(command=_on_btn_frame_back)
    btn_frame_fwd.configure(command=_on_btn_frame_fwd)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Sub cue boundaries
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def on_mark_in_player():
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        _init_player()
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        t = player.get_position()

        if mark_out_time["value"] is not None and t >= mark_out_time["value"]:
            mark_out_time["value"] = None
            state.log("Previous OUT cleared because new IN is at or after it.")

        mark_in_time["value"] = t
        _update_in_out_label()
        state.log(f"Mark IN: {seconds_to_display(t)}")

    def on_mark_out_player():
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        _init_player()
        _user_selected_cue["value"] = False
        t = player.get_position()

        if mark_in_time["value"] is not None and t <= mark_in_time["value"]:
            mark_in_time["value"] = None
            state.log("Previous IN cleared because new OUT is at or before it.")

        mark_out_time["value"] = t
        _update_in_out_label()
        state.log(f"Mark OUT: {seconds_to_display(t)}")

    def on_sub_in():
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        if not player.loaded:
            return
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            state.log("[!] Select a cue first.")
            return

        row_id = sel[0]
        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        state.save_undo()
        old = cue.start
        cue.start = player.get_position()
        if old != cue.start:
            _log_cue_time_change(cue, "start", old, cue.start)
            _refresh_tree_row(row_id, idx)
            _set_active_cue_text(cue)
            update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text, getattr(cue, "alignment", "center"))

            try:
                _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def on_sub_out():
        if _block_if_media_busy():
            return

        if not player.loaded:
            return
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            state.log("[!] Select a cue first.")
            return

        row_id = sel[0]
        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        state.save_undo()
        old = cue.end
        cue.end = player.get_position()
        if old != cue.end:
            _log_cue_time_change(cue, "end", old, cue.end)
            _refresh_tree_row(row_id, idx)
            _set_active_cue_text(cue)

            try:
                _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
            except Exception:
                pass

    btn_mark_in.configure(command=on_mark_in_player)
    btn_mark_out.configure(command=on_mark_out_player)
    btn_sub_in.configure(command=on_sub_in)
    btn_sub_out.configure(command=on_sub_out)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Right-click spelling menu
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _replace_word_in_cue(cue_idx, row_id, old_word, new_word):
        if cue_idx >= len(state.cues):
            return
        state.save_undo()
        cue = state.cues[cue_idx]
        pattern = re.compile(r"\b" + re.escape(old_word) + r"\b", re.IGNORECASE)
        cue.text = pattern.sub(new_word, cue.text, count=1)

        ts = _timestamp()
        state.log_subtitle(f"[{ts}] Cue {cue.index}: '{old_word}' → '{new_word}'")
        state.log(f"Cue {cue.index}: '{old_word}' → '{new_word}'")

        _refresh_tree_row(row_id, cue_idx)
        _set_active_cue_text(cue)
        update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text, getattr(cue, "alignment", "center"))

    def _add_word_and_refresh(word, row_id, cue_idx):
        user_dict_add(word)
        state.log(f'Added to dictionary: "{word}"')
        if cue_idx < len(state.cues):
            _refresh_tree_row(row_id, cue_idx)

    def _on_tree_right_click(event):
        row_id = tree.identify_row(event.y)
        if not row_id:
            return
        col = tree.identify_column(event.x)
        if col != "#4":
            return

        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        tree.selection_set(row_id)
        misspelled = _get_misspelled(cue.text)
        if not misspelled:
            return

        menu = tk.Menu(
            tree,
            tearoff=0,
            bg=theme.CARD_BG,
            fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            activebackground=theme.ACCENT,
            activeforeground=theme.BTN_TEXT,
            font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        )

        for word in sorted(misspelled):
            suggestions = _get_suggestions(word)
            word_menu = tk.Menu(
                menu,
                tearoff=0,
                bg=theme.CARD_BG,
                fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                activebackground=theme.ACCENT,
                activeforeground=theme.BTN_TEXT,
                font=theme.FONT_BODY,
            )

            if suggestions:
                for sug in suggestions:
                    word_menu.add_command(
                        label=sug,
                        command=lambda s=sug, w=word, r=row_id, i=idx: _replace_word_in_cue(i, r, w, s),
                    )
            else:
                word_menu.add_command(label="(no suggestions)", state="disabled")

            word_menu.add_separator()
            word_menu.add_command(
                label=f'Add "{word}" to dictionary',
                command=lambda w=word, r=row_id, i=idx: _add_word_and_refresh(w, r, i),
            )
            menu.add_cascade(label=f"• {word}", menu=word_menu)

        menu.tk_popup(event.x_root, event.y_root)

    if platform.system() == "Darwin":
        tree.bind("<Button-2>", _on_tree_right_click)
        tree.bind("<Control-Button-1>", lambda e: [on_ctrl_click(e), None][-1])
    else:
        tree.bind("<Button-3>", _on_tree_right_click)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Language conversion
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _convert_cues(new_lang, dictionary, keep_edits=False):
        state.save_undo()

        if not keep_edits:
            state.cues = [Cue(c.index, c.start, c.end, c.text) for c in state.original_cues]

        direction = "uk_to_us" if new_lang == "us" else "us_to_uk"
        count = 0

        for cue in state.cues:
            new_text, changes = dictionary.convert_text(cue.text, direction)
            if changes > 0:
                cue.text = new_text
                count += changes

        state.language = new_lang
        dir_label = "UK → US" if new_lang == "us" else "US → UK"
        ts = _timestamp()

        state.log_session(
            f"[{ts}] Language changed to {'US' if new_lang == 'us' else 'UK'} English ({count} conversions)"
        )
        state.log_subtitle(f"[{ts}] {dir_label}: {count} spelling conversions")
        lang_status.configure(text=f"{theme.ICON_CHECK} {count} converted")
        _init_spellcheck()
        populate_subtitle_list()
        state.log(f"[OK] Converted {count} spellings to {'US' if new_lang == 'us' else 'UK'} English.")

    def _on_language_switch():
        new_lang = lang_var.get()
        if new_lang == state.language:
            return

        dictionary = _dictionary["dict"]
        if dictionary is None or not dictionary.loaded:
            state.log("[!] Dictionary not loaded.")
            return

        has_edits = len(state.subtitle_log) > 0
        if has_edits:
            choice = messagebox.askyesnocancel(
                "Language Change",
                "You've made manual subtitle edits.\n\n"
                "• Yes — Keep your edits and convert\n"
                "• No — Load original subtitles and convert\n"
                "• Cancel — Stay with current language",
                icon="question",
            )
            if choice is None:
                lang_var.set(state.language)
                return
            if choice:
                _convert_cues(new_lang, dictionary, keep_edits=True)
            else:
                _convert_cues(new_lang, dictionary, keep_edits=False)
        else:
            _convert_cues(new_lang, dictionary, keep_edits=False)

    lang_var.trace_add("write", lambda *args: _on_language_switch())

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Keyboard shortcuts
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    root_win = parent.winfo_toplevel()

    def _on_frame_step(event):
        if _is_text_focus() or _edit_widget["widget"]:
            return
        if not player.loaded or player.playing:
            return

        if event.keysym == "Left":
            player.step_frames(-10 if (event.state & 0x1) else -1)
        elif event.keysym == "Right":
            player.step_frames(10 if (event.state & 0x1) else 1)

    def _on_set_cue_boundary(event):
        if _is_text_focus() or _edit_widget["widget"]:
            return
        if not player.loaded:
            return

        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            state.log("[!] Select a cue first.")
            return

        row_id = sel[0]
        idx, cue = _get_cue_from_iid(row_id)
        if cue is None:
            return

        current_pos = player.get_position()
        fd = player.get_frame_duration()
        fps = round(1.0 / fd) if fd > 0 else 30

        state.save_undo()

        if event.char == "[" or event.keysym == "bracketleft":
            old = cue.start
            cue.start = current_pos
            state.log_subtitle(
                f"[{_timestamp()}] Cue {cue.index}: start "
                f"{seconds_to_display(old)} → {seconds_to_display(cue.start)} "
                f"(frame @ {fps}fps)"
            )
            state.log(f"Cue {cue.index} START → {seconds_to_display(cue.start)}")

        elif event.char == "]" or event.keysym == "bracketright":
            old = cue.end
            cue.end = current_pos
            state.log_subtitle(
                f"[{_timestamp()}] Cue {cue.index}: end "
                f"{seconds_to_display(old)} → {seconds_to_display(cue.end)} "
                f"(frame @ {fps}fps)"
            )
            state.log(f"Cue {cue.index} END → {seconds_to_display(cue.end)}")

        if cue.end <= cue.start:
            state.log(f"[!] Cue {cue.index}: end must be after start.")

        _refresh_tree_row(row_id, idx)
        _set_active_cue_text(cue)
        update_subtitle_overlay(cue.text, getattr(cue, "alignment", "center"))

    def _on_shortcut_key(event, action_fn):
        if (
            _is_text_focus()
            or _edit_widget["widget"]
            or _seek_editing["active"]
            or _seek_go_editing["active"]
        ):
            return
        action_fn()

    def _on_section_shortcut(event, action_fn):
        if (
            _is_text_focus()
            or _edit_widget["widget"]
            or _seek_editing["active"]
            or _seek_go_editing["active"]
        ):
            return

        if not section_listbox.curselection():
            return

        action_fn()
        return "break"

    def _on_space_press(event):
        if (
            _is_text_focus()
            or _edit_widget["widget"]
            or _seek_editing["active"]
            or _seek_go_editing["active"]
        ):
            return "break"

        if getattr(seek_slider, "_dragging", False):
            return "break"

        if getattr(seek_slider, "_dragging_marker", None):
            return "break"

        if _space_held["value"]:
            return "break"

        _space_held["value"] = True
        btn_play_pause.invoke()
        return "break"

    def _on_space_release(event):
        _space_held["value"] = False
        return "break"

    root_win.bind("<Left>", _on_frame_step, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<Right>", _on_frame_step, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<bracketleft>", _on_set_cue_boundary, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<bracketright>", _on_set_cue_boundary, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<space>", _on_space_press, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<KeyRelease-space>", _on_space_release, add="+")
    root_win.bind("<i>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_mark_in_player), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<I>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_mark_in_player), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<o>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_mark_out_player), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<O>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_mark_out_player), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<p>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_add_section), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<P>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, on_add_section), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<c>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, _clear_in_out_marks), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<C>", lambda e: _on_shortcut_key(e, _clear_in_out_marks), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<Delete>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_delete_section), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<Control-Up>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_up), add="+")
    root_win.bind("<Control-Down>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_down), add="+")

    section_listbox.bind("<<ListboxSelect>>", _on_section_selection_change)
    section_listbox.bind("<Delete>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_delete_section))
    section_listbox.bind("<Control-Up>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_up))
    section_listbox.bind("<Control-Down>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_down))
    section_listbox.bind("<Control-KeyPress-Up>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_up))
    section_listbox.bind("<Control-KeyPress-Down>", lambda e: _on_section_shortcut(e, on_move_section_down))
