    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Theme refresh
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _refresh_theme():
        main_frame.configure(bg=theme.BORDER)
        left.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        right.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        left_pane.configure(bg=theme.SEPARATOR)
        video_area.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        sections_area.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        video_canvas.configure(bg=theme.CANVAS_BG)
        timecode_row.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        time_label.configure(text_color=theme.ACCENT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        total_dur_label.configure(text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        frame_info.configure(text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        preview_dropdown.configure(
            fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            button_color=theme.ACCENT,
            button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            dropdown_hover_color=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        )
        transport_1.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        transport_2.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        sep_bar.configure(bg=theme.SEPARATOR)
        vol_btn.configure(fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG, text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR)
        btn_frame_back.configure(fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG, text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR)
        btn_frame_fwd.configure(fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG, text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR)
        seek_go_entry.configure(
            fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            border_color=theme.BORDER,
        )
        btn_seek_go.configure(
            fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR,
        )
        btn_enhance_audio.configure(
            fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR,
        )
        working_audio_menu.configure(
            fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            button_color=theme.ACCENT,
            button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            dropdown_hover_color=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        )
        if _loop_state["enabled"]:
            btn_loop.configure(
                fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
                text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
                hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            )
        else:
            btn_loop.configure(
                fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
                text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
                hover_color=theme.SEPARATOR,
            )
        sections_header.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        in_out_label.configure(text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        section_card.configure(fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, border_color=theme.CARD_BORDER)
        section_inner.configure(bg=theme.CARD_BG)
        section_listbox.configure(
            bg=theme.CARD_BG,
            fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
            selectbackground=theme.TREE_SELECT,
            selectforeground=theme.BLACK,
        )
        section_btn_frame.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        subs_header_row.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        subs_header.configure(text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        lang_row.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        lang_label.configure(text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        lang_status.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        rb_us.configure(text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, fg_color=theme.ACCENT, hover_color=theme.ACCENT)
        rb_uk.configure(text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, fg_color=theme.ACCENT, hover_color=theme.ACCENT)
        tree_card.configure(bg=theme.CARD_BORDER)
        _apply_tree_tags()
        try:
            _highlight_subtitles_for_selected_sections()
        except Exception:
            pass
        hints_strip.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        active_cue_label.configure(text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG)
        bottom_strip.configure(bg=theme.TAB_BG)
        strip_sep.configure(bg=theme.SEPARATOR)

    register_theme_callback(_refresh_theme)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Tab activation
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _try_init_player():
        reload_requested = bool(getattr(state, "force_player_reload", False))

        def _reset_mpv_waveform_state():
            _mpv_waveform_state["busy"] = False
            _mpv_waveform_state["ready"] = False
            _mpv_waveform_state["video_path"] = None
            _mpv_waveform_state["last_wait_log"] = 0.0

        def _start_player_load(message="Loading video..."):
            _reset_mpv_waveform_state()
            _lock_media_controls(message)
            seek_slider.clear_waveform()
            player.load(state.video_path, state.video_info.get("duration", 0))
            state.force_player_reload = False

        if not state.video_path or not state.video_info:
            return

        if not player.loaded:
            state.log("Initializing video player...")
            _start_player_load("Loading video...")
            return

        if reload_requested:
            state.log("Reloading video player...")
            player.reset()
            _start_player_load("Reloading video...")
            return

        if player.video_path != state.video_path:
            state.log("New video detected, reloading...")
            player.reset()
            _start_player_load("Loading video...")
            return

        if player.loaded:
            if player.__class__.__name__ == "MpvPlayer":
                if _mpv_waveform_state["ready"]:
                    _unlock_media_controls()
                else:
                    _lock_media_controls("Preparing waveform...")
            else:
                enable_btn(btn_play_pause)

        _update_enhance_audio_button()
        video_canvas.after(0, _update_working_audio_control)

    def on_tab_activated():
        populate_subtitle_list()
        _refresh_section_list()
        _clear_in_out_marks()
        lang_var.set(state.language)
        _update_enhance_audio_button()

        if state.video_path and state.video_info:
            _try_init_player()
            video_canvas.after(300, _try_init_player)
            video_canvas.after(1000, _try_init_player)
        else:
            disable_btn(btn_play_pause)
            disable_btn(btn_enhance_audio)

            try:
                seek_slider.clear_waveform()
                seek_slider.clear_marks()
                seek_slider.set(0.0)
                seek_slider.configure(from_=0, to=100)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                player.reset()
            except Exception:
                pass

            video_canvas.delete("all")
            _show_centered_canvas_message(
                "No video loaded\nSubtitle editing and export available"
            )

    ui["on_tab_activated"] = on_tab_activated

    return ui

FILE: tabs/tab_export.py

"""
Subtitle Studio — Tab 2: Export.

Current v5 hybrid export UI:
- Left column: clip selections.
- Right column: Export Outputs, Video, Subtitles, Audio, Output.
- Video/Subtitles/Audio cards enable only when their output type is selected.
- Subtitles support multi-format export and selection scopes.
- Video export still uses the temporary legacy bridge.
- Audio export is partially plan-aware but still reuses the legacy export function structure.
"""

import os
import datetime
import threading
import tempfile
import time
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox
import customtkinter as ctk

from core import theme
from core.theme import (
    make_primary_btn, make_secondary_btn, make_danger_btn,
    make_section_label, make_card,
    flash_widget, disable_btn, enable_btn,
    register_theme_callback,
)
from core.state import AppState, Section
from core.parser import seconds_to_display
from core.ffmpeg import run_ffmpeg
from core.widgets import Tooltip
from core.auto import auto_fix_before_export


def build_export_tab(parent, state: AppState, progress_fn=None):
    """Build the Export tab. Returns ui dict."""

    ui = {}

    def _display_time_short(seconds: float | int | None) -> str:
        """Visible UI time only: HH:MM:SS."""
        if seconds is None:
            return "--:--:--"
        try:
            total_seconds = max(0, int(float(seconds)))
        except Exception:
            return "00:00:00"
        hours = total_seconds // 3600
        minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
        secs = total_seconds % 60
        return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}"

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SCROLLABLE CONTAINER
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    outer = ctk.CTkFrame(parent, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    outer.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    canvas = tk.Canvas(
        outer, bg=theme.TAB_BG, highlightthickness=0)
    scrollbar = ctk.CTkScrollbar(outer, command=canvas.yview)
    scroll_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
        canvas, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)

    scroll_frame.bind(
        "<Configure>",
        lambda e: canvas.configure(scrollregion=canvas.bbox("all")))

    canvas_window = canvas.create_window(
        (0, 0), window=scroll_frame, anchor="nw")

    def _on_canvas_configure(event):
        requested_height = scroll_frame.winfo_reqheight()
        canvas.itemconfigure(
            canvas_window,
            width=event.width,
            height=max(event.height, requested_height))

    canvas.bind("<Configure>", _on_canvas_configure)
    canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    scrollbar.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=scrollbar.set)

    def _on_mousewheel(event):
        canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * (event.delta / 120)), "units")

    canvas.bind(
        "<Enter>",
        lambda e: canvas.bind_all("<MouseWheel>", _on_mousewheel))
    canvas.bind(
        "<Leave>",
        lambda e: canvas.unbind_all("<MouseWheel>"))

    # ── Title ──
    title_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
        scroll_frame, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    title_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_LG,
                     pady=(theme.PAD_LG, 0))

    title_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        title_frame, text="Export",
        font=("Georgia", 14, "bold"),
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    title_lbl.pack(anchor="w")

    desc_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        title_frame,
        text="Export video, audio, or subtitles with your edits applied.",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    desc_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TWO-COLUMN LAYOUT
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    columns = ctk.CTkFrame(
        scroll_frame, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    columns.pack(
        fill="both",
        expand=True,
        padx=theme.PAD_LG,
        pady=(0, theme.PAD_MD))

    # ── Left Column: Sections ──
    left_col = ctk.CTkFrame(
        columns, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG,
        corner_radius=0, width=220)
    left_col.pack(side="left", fill="both", padx=(0, theme.PAD_MD))
    left_col.pack_propagate(False)

    # ── Right Column ──
    right_col = ctk.CTkFrame(
        columns, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    right_col.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    right_col.pack_propagate(True)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # LEFT: MARKED SECTIONS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    sections_lbl = make_section_label(
        left_col, f"{theme.ICON_CUT}  Clip Selections")
    sections_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    sections_card = make_card(left_col)
    sections_card.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    sections_card.pack_propagate(False)

    sections_listbox = tk.Listbox(
        sections_card, font=theme.FONT_TIMECODE,
        bg=theme.CARD_BG, fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        relief="flat",
        selectbackground=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        selectforeground=theme.BLACK,
        activestyle="none",
        highlightthickness=0, bd=0)
    sections_listbox.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=2, pady=2)

    sections_summary = ctk.CTkLabel(
        left_col, text="No clip selections yet",
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL, text_color="#999999",
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, anchor="w")
    sections_summary.pack(anchor="w", pady=(theme.PAD_XS, 0))

    def _refresh_sections():
        sections_listbox.delete(0, "end")
        if not state.sections:
            sections_summary.configure(
                text="No clip selections yet",
                text_color="#999999")
            return

        total_dur = 0
        for i, s in enumerate(state.sections):
            dur = s.duration
            total_dur += dur
            sections_listbox.insert(
                "end",
                f" {s.label}\n"
                f" {_display_time_short(s.start)} → "
                f"{_display_time_short(s.end)}\n"
                f" {dur:.1f}s")

        sections_summary.configure(
            text=(f"{len(state.sections)} clip selections  •  "
                  f"{total_dur:.1f}s total"),
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)

    Tooltip(sections_listbox,
            "Clip selections from the Edit tab. Switch to Edit to modify.")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # RIGHT: EXPORT MODE
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    mode_section_lbl = make_section_label(right_col, "Export Mode")
    mode_section_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    mode_card = make_card(right_col)
    mode_card.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    mode_var = tk.StringVar(value="full")

    mode_tooltips = {
        "full": "Export the full video with selected subtitle and audio options.",
        "delete": "Remove clip selections from the video and stitch the remaining parts together.",
        "split": "Export each clip selection as a separate video file.",
        "merge": "Merge all clip selections into one video file.",
        "subtitles_only": "Export subtitle delivery files only. No video will be created.",
        "audio_only": "Export a standalone audio file only. No video will be created.",
    }

    mode_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        mode_card, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    mode_row.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_SM, pady=theme.PAD_XS)

    mode_label_to_value = {
        "Full Video": "full",
        "Remove Selected Sections": "delete",
        "Selected Sections as Separate Clips": "split",
        "Merge Selected Sections": "merge",
        "Subtitles Only": "subtitles_only",
        "Audio Only": "audio_only",
    }
    mode_value_to_label = {
        value: label for label, value in mode_label_to_value.items()
    }

    mode_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Full Video")

    def _on_mode_dropdown_change(label):
        mode_var.set(mode_label_to_value.get(label, "full"))

    mode_dropdown = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(
        mode_row,
        variable=mode_display_var,
        values=list(mode_label_to_value.keys()),
        command=_on_mode_dropdown_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        dropdown_hover_color=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        width=330)
    mode_dropdown.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
    Tooltip(mode_dropdown, "Choose how this export should be created.")

    mode_info = ctk.CTkLabel(
        mode_card, text=mode_tooltips["full"],
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL, text_color="#777777",
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, anchor="w")
    mode_info.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_SM,
                   pady=(0, theme.PAD_XS))

    mode_divider = tk.Frame(
        right_col, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, height=1)
    mode_divider.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    # Hide legacy single-mode selector. It remains in memory temporarily
    # because the old export engine still uses mode_var during the v5 migration.
    try:
        mode_section_lbl.pack_forget()
        mode_card.pack_forget()
        mode_divider.pack_forget()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # RIGHT: EXPORT OUTPUTS — v5 model
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    outputs_section_lbl = make_section_label(
        right_col, "Export Outputs")
    outputs_section_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    outputs_card = make_card(right_col)
    outputs_card.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    outputs_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        outputs_card, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    outputs_row.pack(
        fill="x",
        padx=theme.PAD_SM,
        pady=(theme.PAD_XS, theme.PAD_XS),
    )

    export_video_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
    export_audio_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    export_subtitles_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)

    selection_label_to_value = {
        "Full Source": "full",
        "Remove Clip Selections": "delete",
        "Clip Selections as Separate Files": "split",
        "Merge Clip Selections": "merge",
    }
    selection_value_to_label = {
        value: label for label, value in selection_label_to_value.items()
    }

    video_selection_var = tk.StringVar(value="full")
    video_selection_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Full Source")

    audio_selection_var = tk.StringVar(value="full")
    audio_selection_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Full Source")

    subtitle_selection_var = tk.StringVar(value="full")
    subtitle_selection_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Full Source")

    video_burn_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Yes")
    video_embedded_audio_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Use Working Audio")
    video_embedded_audio_var = tk.StringVar(value="working")

    audio_source_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Use Working Audio")
    audio_source_var = tk.StringVar(value="working")

    subtitle_fmt_srt_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
    subtitle_fmt_vtt_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    subtitle_fmt_txt_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    subtitle_fmt_docx_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    subtitle_fmt_fcpxml_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)

    audio_source_label_to_value = {
        "Use Working Audio": "working",
        "Original Audio": "source",
        "Enhanced Audio": "enhanced",
    }

    video_embedded_audio_label_to_value = {
        "Use Working Audio": "working",
        "Original Audio": "source",
        "Enhanced Audio": "enhanced",
        "Mute": "mute",
    }

    def _on_v5_plan_change(*args):
        """Update v5 compatibility state, card enabled states, and summary."""
        try:
            _sync_v5_to_legacy_export_state()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            _update_output_summary()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _on_video_selection_change(label):
        video_selection_var.set(
            selection_label_to_value.get(label, "full"))
        _on_v5_plan_change()

    def _on_video_burn_change(label):
        video_burn_display_var.set(label)
        _on_v5_plan_change()
    
    def _on_audio_selection_change(label):
        audio_selection_var.set(
            selection_label_to_value.get(label, "full"))
        _on_v5_plan_change()

    def _on_subtitle_selection_change(label):
        subtitle_selection_var.set(
            selection_label_to_value.get(label, "full"))
        _on_v5_plan_change()

    def _on_video_embedded_audio_change(label):
        video_embedded_audio_var.set(
            video_embedded_audio_label_to_value.get(label, "working"))
        _on_v5_plan_change()

    def _on_audio_source_change(label):
        audio_source_var.set(
            audio_source_label_to_value.get(label, "working"))
        _on_v5_plan_change()
        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass

    export_video_check = ctk.CTkCheckBox(
        outputs_row,
        text="Video",
        variable=export_video_var,
        command=_on_v5_plan_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
        hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        border_color="#999999",
    )
    export_video_check.pack(side="left", padx=(0, theme.PAD_LG))

    export_audio_check = ctk.CTkCheckBox(
        outputs_row,
        text="Audio",
        variable=export_audio_var,
        command=_on_v5_plan_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
        hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        border_color="#999999",
    )
    export_audio_check.pack(side="left", padx=(0, theme.PAD_LG))

    export_subtitles_check = ctk.CTkCheckBox(
        outputs_row,
        text="Subtitles",
        variable=export_subtitles_var,
        command=_on_v5_plan_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
        hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        border_color="#999999",
    )
    export_subtitles_check.pack(side="left", padx=(0, theme.PAD_LG))

    outputs_hint = ctk.CTkLabel(
        outputs_card,
        text="Select one or more output types. Each output has its own settings below.",
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL,
        text_color="#777777",
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        anchor="w",
    )
    outputs_hint.pack(
        fill="x",
        padx=theme.PAD_SM,
        pady=(0, theme.PAD_XS),
    )

    outputs_divider = tk.Frame(
        right_col, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, height=1)
    outputs_divider.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))
    
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # RIGHT: SUBTITLE OPTIONS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    sub_section_lbl = make_section_label(
        right_col, f"{theme.ICON_SUB}  Subtitle Options")
    sub_section_lbl.pack(
        anchor="w", pady=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_XS))

    sub_card = make_card(right_col)
    sub_card.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    burn_sub_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
    sidecar_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
    subonly_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)

    subtitle_output_to_flags = {
        "Burn subtitles into video": (True, False, False),
        "Export sidecar file": (False, True, False),
        "Burn + sidecar file": (True, True, False),
        "No subtitles": (False, False, False),
    }
    subtitle_flags_to_output = {
        (True, False, False): "Burn subtitles into video",
        (False, True, False): "Export sidecar file",
        (True, True, False): "Burn + sidecar file",
        (False, False, False): "No subtitles",
        (False, False, True): "No subtitles",
    }

    subtitle_output_var = tk.StringVar(
        value="Burn subtitles into video")

    _syncing_export_ui = {"value": False}

    def _on_subtitle_output_change(label):
        if _syncing_export_ui["value"]:
            return

        try:
            if _exporting.get("active"):
                _sync_subtitle_output_dropdown()
                return
        except Exception:
            pass

        burn, sidecar, subonly = subtitle_output_to_flags.get(
            label, (True, False, False))

        _syncing_export_ui["value"] = True
        try:
            burn_sub_var.set(burn)
            sidecar_var.set(sidecar)
            subonly_var.set(subonly)
        finally:
            _syncing_export_ui["value"] = False

        _on_sub_options_change()

    def _sync_subtitle_output_dropdown():
        flags = (
            burn_sub_var.get(),
            sidecar_var.get(),
            subonly_var.get(),
        )
        label = subtitle_flags_to_output.get(
            flags, "Burn subtitles into video")

        if subtitle_output_var.get() == label:
            return

        was_syncing = _syncing_export_ui["value"]
        _syncing_export_ui["value"] = True
        try:
            subtitle_output_var.set(label)
        finally:
            _syncing_export_ui["value"] = was_syncing

    sub_options_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        sub_card, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    sub_options_row.pack(
        fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_SM,
        pady=(theme.PAD_XS, theme.PAD_XS))

    subtitle_output_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        sub_options_row, text="Subtitle output:",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG)
    subtitle_output_lbl.pack(side="left")

    subtitle_output_dropdown = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(
        sub_options_row,
        variable=subtitle_output_var,
        values=list(subtitle_output_to_flags.keys()),
        command=_on_subtitle_output_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,

    font_var = tk.IntVar(value=40)
    font_slider = ctk.CTkSlider(
        sub_options_row, from_=8, to=72, number_of_steps=64,
        width=120, height=16,
        fg_color=theme.BORDER,
        progress_color=theme.ACCENT,

    font_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
        sub_options_row, text="40 @1080p",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY_BOLD,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        width=70)
    font_label.pack(side="left", padx=(theme.PAD_XS, theme.PAD_LG))

    def _get_export_height() -> int:
        """Return final export height for font scaling."""
        if res_var.get() == "1080":
            return 1080
        if res_var.get() == "720":
            return 720

        try:
            return int(state.video_info.get("height", 1080) or 1080)
        except Exception:
            return 1080

    def _scaled_export_font_size() -> int:
        """Scale the 1080p font size to the final export resolution."""
        base_size = int(font_var.get())
        export_height = _get_export_height()
        return max(10, int(round(base_size * export_height / 1080)))

    def _update_font_label():
        base_size = int(font_var.get())
        scaled_size = _scaled_export_font_size()
        export_height = _get_export_height()

        if export_height == 1080:
            label_text = f"{base_size} @1080p"
        else:
            label_text = f"{scaled_size} @~{export_height}p"

        try:
            font_label.configure(text=label_text)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            video_font_label.configure(text=label_text)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _on_font_change(value):
        v = int(float(value))
        font_var.set(v)
        _update_font_label()

    font_slider.configure(command=_on_font_change)
    Tooltip(font_slider, "Adjust burned subtitle font size")

        font=theme.FONT_BODY,

    comp_var = tk.StringVar(value="original")
    comp_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Original Quality")

    _comp_label_to_value = {}
    _comp_value_to_label = {}

    def _format_estimated_mb(value):
        try:
            return f"{float(value):.0f} MB"
        except Exception:
            return "estimating..."

    def _build_compression_label_pairs(est):
        return [
            ("lossless", "Lossless — largest file"),
            (
                "original",
                f"Original Quality — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(est.get('original', 0))}",
            ),
            (
                "high",
                f"High Quality — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(est.get('high', 0))}",
            ),
            (
                "medium",
                f"Balanced — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(est.get('medium', 0))}",
            ),
            (
                "low",
                f"Small File — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(est.get('low', 0))}",
            ),
            ("custom", "Custom Target Size"),
        ]

    def _set_compression_dropdown_labels(label_pairs):
        _comp_label_to_value.clear()
        _comp_value_to_label.clear()

        labels = []
        for value, label in label_pairs:
            labels.append(label)
            _comp_label_to_value[label] = value
            _comp_value_to_label[value] = label

        comp_dropdown.configure(values=labels)
        current_value = comp_var.get()
        comp_display_var.set(
            _comp_value_to_label.get(
                current_value,
                _comp_value_to_label.get("original", labels[0]),
            )
        )

    def _on_compression_change(label):
        comp_var.set(_comp_label_to_value.get(label, "original"))
        try:
            _sync_custom_target_state()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            _update_output_summary()
        except Exception:
            pass

    comp_dropdown = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(

        variable=comp_display_var,
        values=["Original Quality"],
        command=_on_compression_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,

    audio_fmt_var = tk.StringVar(value="mp3")
    audio_fmt_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="MP3")

    audio_format_map = {
        "MP3": "mp3",
        "AAC": "aac",
        "WAV": "wav",
    }

    audio_fmt_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        audio_row, text="Format:",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG)
    audio_fmt_lbl.pack(side="left")

    def _on_audio_format_change(label):
        audio_fmt_var.set(audio_format_map.get(label, "mp3"))
        if label == "WAV":
            audio_br_display_var.set("Uncompressed PCM")
            audio_br_dropdown.configure(state="disabled")
        else:
            if audio_br_display_var.get() == "Uncompressed PCM":
                audio_br_display_var.set("192 kbps")
                audio_br_var.set("192k")
            audio_br_dropdown.configure(state="normal")

        if mode_var.get() == "audio_only":
            current_path = output_path_var.get().strip()
            if current_path:
                ext = {
                    "mp3": ".mp3",
                    "aac": ".m4a",
                    "wav": ".wav",
                }.get(audio_fmt_var.get(), ".mp3")

                root, _old_ext = os.path.splitext(current_path)
                new_path = f"{root}{ext}"
                output_path_var.set(new_path)
                output_label.configure(
                    text=(f"🎧 {os.path.basename(new_path)}\n"
                          f"    {os.path.dirname(new_path)}"),
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            _update_output_summary()
        except Exception:
            pass
    audio_fmt_dropdown = ctk.CTkOptionMenu(
        audio_row,
        variable=audio_fmt_display_var,
        values=list(audio_format_map.keys()),
        command=_on_audio_format_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,

    audio_scope_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(

        font=theme.FONT_BODY,

    audio_scope_var = tk.StringVar(value="full")
    audio_scope_display_var = tk.StringVar(value="Full Audio")

    audio_scope_label_to_value = {
        "Full Audio": "full",
        "Remove Selected Sections": "delete",
        "Selected Sections as Separate Files": "split",
        "Merge Selected Sections": "merge",
    }
    audio_scope_value_to_label = {
        value: label for label, value in audio_scope_label_to_value.items()
    }

    def _on_audio_scope_change(label):
        audio_scope_var.set(
            audio_scope_label_to_value.get(label, "full")
        )

        # If user switches between file/folder style output, force them to
        # choose a fresh destination so we do not export to a stale path.
        if mode_var.get() == "audio_only":
            output_path_var.set("")

                text="Choose an audio output location.",
                text_color="#999999",
            )

        audio_row,

        command=_on_audio_scope_change,
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        fg_color=theme.FIELD_BG,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        button_color=theme.ACCENT,
        button_hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
        dropdown_fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        dropdown_text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        dropdown_hover_color=theme.TREE_SELECT,
        width=210)

    # NOTE:
    # This bridge is temporary. Subtitles are now mostly plan-based,
    # Video still routes through the legacy export engine.
    # Audio is partially plan-aware but still reuses the legacy export function.
    # Keep legacy vars alive until those backends are migrated.
    
    def _sync_v5_to_legacy_export_state():
        """Bridge v5 output checkboxes into the legacy export engine.

        This is temporary during the v5 migration.
        """
        video_enabled = export_video_var.get()
        audio_enabled = export_audio_var.get()
        subtitles_enabled = export_subtitles_var.get()

        # Allow temporarily no outputs selected so users can switch cleanly
        # from Video-only to Audio-only or Subtitles-only.
        # Final validation happens when Export is pressed.
        if not video_enabled and not audio_enabled and not subtitles_enabled:
            mode_var.set("full")
            audio_var.set(False)
            burn_sub_var.set(False)
            sidecar_var.set(False)
            subonly_var.set(False)
            return

        # Map v5 selection controls into the temporary legacy engine.
        #
        # If there is no video output but audio is selected, run the legacy
        # audio-only path. Subtitles can still be exported after audio.
        if audio_enabled and not video_enabled:
            mode_var.set("audio_only")
            audio_scope_var.set(audio_selection_var.get())
            audio_scope_display_var.set(
                audio_scope_value_to_label.get(
                    audio_selection_var.get(), "Full Audio")
            )

        # Pure subtitles-only export.
        elif subtitles_enabled and not video_enabled and not audio_enabled:
            mode_var.set("subtitles_only")

        # Any video output uses the video selection scope.
        else:
            mode_var.set(video_selection_var.get())

        # Legacy flags.
        audio_var.set(bool(audio_enabled))

        if video_enabled:
            burn_sub_var.set(video_burn_display_var.get() == "Yes")
        else:
            burn_sub_var.set(False)

        # Sidecar is removed in the v5 model.
        sidecar_var.set(False)

        # Subtitles output is now controlled by the Subtitles checkbox.
        # Legacy subonly_var should only mean "pure subtitles-only".
        # In v5, subtitles can also be a companion output alongside video/audio.
        subonly_var.set(
            bool(subtitles_enabled and not video_enabled and not audio_enabled)
        )

        # Legacy subtitle-only exporter currently supports one selected format.
        # Until the new multi-format subtitle exporter is wired, use the first
        # checked format as the compatibility format.
        if subtitle_fmt_srt_var.get():
            subonly_format_var.set("SRT")
        elif subtitle_fmt_vtt_var.get():
            subonly_format_var.set("VTT")
        elif subtitle_fmt_txt_var.get():
            subonly_format_var.set("TXT")
        elif subtitle_fmt_docx_var.get():
            subonly_format_var.set("DOCX")
        elif subtitle_fmt_fcpxml_var.get():
            subonly_format_var.set("Final Cut Pro XML")
        else:
            subtitle_fmt_srt_var.set(True)
            subonly_format_var.set("SRT")

    def _build_export_plan():
        """Build v5 export plan from UI state."""
        subtitle_formats = []
        if subtitle_fmt_srt_var.get():
            subtitle_formats.append("srt")
        if subtitle_fmt_vtt_var.get():
            subtitle_formats.append("vtt")
        if subtitle_fmt_txt_var.get():
            subtitle_formats.append("txt")
        if subtitle_fmt_docx_var.get():
            subtitle_formats.append("docx")
        if subtitle_fmt_fcpxml_var.get():
            subtitle_formats.append("fcpxml")

        return {
            "video": {
                "enabled": bool(export_video_var.get()),
                "selection": video_selection_var.get(),
                "resolution": res_var.get(),
                "compression": comp_var.get(),
                "burn_subtitles": video_burn_display_var.get() == "Yes",
                "embedded_audio": video_embedded_audio_var.get(),
            },
            "audio": {
                "enabled": bool(export_audio_var.get()),
                "selection": audio_selection_var.get(),
                "source": audio_source_var.get(),
                "format": audio_fmt_var.get(),
                "bitrate": audio_br_var.get(),
            },
            "subtitles": {
                "enabled": bool(export_subtitles_var.get()),
                "selection": subtitle_selection_var.get(),
                "formats": subtitle_formats,
            },
            "destination": {
                "path": output_path_var.get().strip()
                if "output_path_var" in locals() else "",
            },
        }
    
    Tooltip(
        audio_scope_dropdown,
        "Choose whether Audio Only exports full audio, trimmed audio, separate audio clips, or merged audio selections.")

    audio_divider = tk.Frame(
        va_frame, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, height=1)
    audio_divider.pack(fill="x", pady=(theme.PAD_SM, 0))

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # RIGHT: OUTPUT + EXPORT
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    output_sep = tk.Frame(
        scroll_frame, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, height=1)
    output_sep.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_LG,
                    pady=(theme.PAD_MD, theme.PAD_SM))

    output_section_lbl = make_section_label(
        scroll_frame, f"{theme.ICON_FOLDER}  Output")
    output_section_lbl.pack(
        anchor="w", padx=theme.PAD_LG,
        pady=(0, theme.PAD_XS))

    output_card = make_card(scroll_frame)
    output_card.pack(
        fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_LG,
        pady=(0, theme.PAD_LG))
    output_card.configure(height=150)
    output_card.pack_propagate(False)

    output_path_var = tk.StringVar(value="")

    output_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        output_card, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    output_row.pack(
        fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_MD,
        pady=(theme.PAD_MD, theme.PAD_SM))

    output_text_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
        output_row, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    output_text_frame.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

    output_title = ctk.CTkLabel(
        output_text_frame,
        text="Export destination",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY_BOLD,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        anchor="w")
    output_title.pack(anchor="w")

    output_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
        output_text_frame,
        text="No output location selected",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color="#999999",
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        anchor="w",
        justify="left")
    output_label.pack(anchor="w", fill="x", pady=(2, 0))

    def _update_output_summary():
        """Show a lightweight preview of the v5 export plan."""
        try:
            plan = _build_export_plan()
        except Exception:
            return

        lines = []

        if plan["video"]["enabled"]:
            sel = selection_value_to_label.get(
                plan["video"]["selection"], "Full Source")
            burn = "burned subtitles" if plan["video"]["burn_subtitles"] else "no burned subtitles"
            audio_label = video_embedded_audio_display_var.get()
            lines.append(
                f"• Video: {sel}, {burn}, audio: {audio_label}"
            )

        if plan["audio"]["enabled"]:
            sel = selection_value_to_label.get(
                plan["audio"]["selection"], "Full Source")
            lines.append(
                f"• Audio: {sel}, {plan['audio']['format'].upper()} "
                f"{plan['audio']['bitrate']}, source: {audio_source_display_var.get()}"
            )

        if plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]:
            sel = selection_value_to_label.get(
                plan["subtitles"]["selection"], "Full Source")
            formats = ", ".join(fmt.upper() for fmt in plan["subtitles"]["formats"])
            if not formats:
                formats = "SRT"
            lines.append(f"• Subtitles: {sel}, {formats}")

        if not lines:
            lines.append("• No outputs selected")

        path = output_path_var.get().strip()
        if path:
            header = f"Destination: {path}"
        else:
            header = "Choose an output location."

        output_label.configure(
            text=header + "\n" + "\n".join(lines),
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT if path else "#999999",
        )
    
    def _browse_output():
        mode = mode_var.get()

        if (subonly_var.get()
                and not burn_sub_var.get()
                and not sidecar_var.get()):
            path = filedialog.askdirectory(
                title="Select Output Folder for Subtitles")
            if path:
                output_path_var.set(path)
                output_label.configure(
                    text=f"📁 {path}",
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
            _update_output_summary()
            return

        if mode == "split":
            path = filedialog.askdirectory(
                title="Select Output Folder for Clips")
            if path:
                output_path_var.set(path)
                output_label.configure(
                    text=f"📁 {path}",
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
            _update_output_summary()

        elif mode == "audio_only":
            base = os.path.splitext(
                os.path.basename(
                    state.video_path or "audio"))[0]

            fmt = audio_fmt_var.get()
            ext = {"mp3": ".mp3", "aac": ".m4a", "wav": ".wav"}.get(
                fmt, ".mp3"
            )

            scope = audio_scope_var.get()

            if scope == "split":
                path = filedialog.askdirectory(
                    title="Select Output Folder for Audio Clips")
                if path:
                    output_path_var.set(path)
                    output_label.configure(
                        text=f"📁 {path}",
                        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
                    _update_output_summary()
                return

            filetypes = {
                "mp3": [("MP3 Audio", "*.mp3"), ("All files", "*.*")],
                "aac": [("AAC Audio", "*.m4a"), ("All files", "*.*")],
                "wav": [("WAV Audio", "*.wav"), ("All files", "*.*")],
            }.get(fmt, [("Audio files", "*.*")])

            suffixes = {
                "full": "",
                "delete": "_trimmed",
                "merge": "_merged",
            }
            default_name = f"{base}{suffixes.get(scope, '')}{ext}"

            path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
                title="Save Audio As",
                defaultextension=ext,
                initialfile=default_name,
                confirmoverwrite=True,
                filetypes=filetypes,
            )

            if path:
                root, current_ext = os.path.splitext(path)
                if current_ext.lower() != ext:
                    path = f"{root}{ext}"

                output_path_var.set(path)
                output_label.configure(
                    text=(f"🎧 {os.path.basename(path)}\n"
                          f"    {os.path.dirname(path)}"),
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
            _update_output_summary()    

        else:
            base = os.path.splitext(
                os.path.basename(
                    state.video_path or "output"))[0]
            suffixes = {
                "full": "_export", "delete": "_trimmed",
                "merge": "_merged"}
            default_name = (
                f"{base}{suffixes.get(mode, '_export')}.mp4")

            path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
                title="Save Export As",
                defaultextension=".mp4",
                initialfile=default_name,
                confirmoverwrite=True,
                filetypes=[("MP4 Video", "*.mp4"),
                           ("MKV Video", "*.mkv"),
                           ("AVI Video", "*.avi"),
                           ("All files", "*.*")])
            if path:
                output_path_var.set(path)
                output_label.configure(
                    text=(f"📄 {os.path.basename(path)}\n"
                          f"    {os.path.dirname(path)}"),
                    text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
            _update_output_summary()    

    btn_browse = make_secondary_btn(
        output_row, "Save As…", icon=theme.ICON_FOLDER)
    btn_browse.pack(side="right", padx=(theme.PAD_MD, 0))
    btn_browse.configure(command=_browse_output)
    Tooltip(btn_browse, "Choose where to save exported files")

    output_divider = tk.Frame(
        output_card, bg=theme.SEPARATOR, height=1)
    output_divider.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_MD,
                        pady=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_SM))

    export_bottom = ctk.CTkFrame(
        output_card, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    export_bottom.pack(
        fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_MD,
        pady=(0, theme.PAD_MD))

    export_status_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
        export_bottom, fg_color=theme.CARD_BG, corner_radius=0)
    export_status_frame.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

    export_status = ctk.CTkLabel(
        export_status_frame,
        text="Ready to export",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY_BOLD,
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        anchor="w")
    export_status.pack(anchor="w")

    eta_label = ctk.CTkLabel(
        export_status_frame,
        text="Choose an output location, then export.",
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL,
        text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
        anchor="w")
    eta_label.pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 0))

    btn_export = make_primary_btn(
        export_bottom, "EXPORT", icon=theme.ICON_EXPORT)
    btn_export.configure(
        font=("Arial", 12, "bold"), width=180, height=40)
    btn_export.pack(side="right", padx=(theme.PAD_MD, 0))

    Tooltip(btn_export, "Start export with current settings")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SMART GREYING LOGIC
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # Legacy greying lists.
    #
    # v5 owns the visible Video/Subtitles/Audio cards through
    # _sync_v5_card_states(). Do not let the old mode-based greying
    # touch visible v5 widgets or they will fight and flicker.
    _video_widgets = [
        rb_orig,
        rb_1080,
    ]

    _audio_widgets = [
        audio_check,
    ]

    _burn_widgets = [font_size_lbl, font_slider, font_label]
    _sidecar_widgets = [sidecar_fmt_lbl, sidecar_dropdown]
    _subonly_widgets = [subonly_lbl, subonly_format_dropdown]

    def _sync_mode_specific_widgets():
        """Show/hide mode-specific controls."""
        mode = mode_var.get()
        audio_only = mode == "audio_only"
        subtitles_only = mode == "subtitles_only"

        try:
            subtitle_output_dropdown.pack_forget()
            subtitle_output_static.pack_forget()
            subtitle_output_subonly_static.pack_forget()

            if audio_only:
                subtitle_output_static.pack(
                    side="left",
                    padx=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_LG),
                )
            elif subtitles_only:
                subtitle_output_subonly_static.pack(
                    side="left",
                    padx=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_LG),
                )
            else:
                subtitle_output_dropdown.pack(
                    side="left",
                    padx=(theme.PAD_SM, theme.PAD_LG),
                )
        except Exception:
            pass

        # v5 has its own visible Audio Selection dropdown.
        # Keep the legacy audio scope widgets hidden.
        try:
            audio_scope_lbl.pack_forget()
            audio_scope_dropdown.pack_forget()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _set_widgets_state(widgets, enabled):
        for w in widgets:
            try:
                if enabled:
                    w.configure(state="normal")
                else:
                    w.configure(state="disabled")
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _set_label_group_enabled(labels, enabled):
        """Grey labels when their card/output is disabled."""
        color = theme.SECTION_TEXT if enabled else "#999999"
        for lbl in labels:
            try:
                lbl.configure(text_color=color)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _set_card_enabled(card, enabled):
        """Subtle visual hint for enabled/disabled cards."""
        try:
            card.configure(
                border_color=theme.CARD_BORDER if enabled else "#CFCFCF"
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _sync_v5_card_states():
        """Enable each v5 option card only when its output is selected."""
        video_enabled = bool(export_video_var.get())
        subtitles_enabled = bool(export_subtitles_var.get())
        audio_enabled = bool(export_audio_var.get())

        # ── Video card ──
        video_controls = [
            video_selection_dropdown,
            video_embedded_audio_dropdown,
            video_burn_dropdown,
            res_dropdown,
            comp_dropdown,
        ]

        _set_widgets_state(video_controls, video_enabled)

        burn_enabled = (
            video_enabled
            and video_burn_display_var.get() == "Yes"
        )
        _set_widgets_state([video_font_slider], burn_enabled)

        custom_active = (
            video_enabled
            and comp_var.get() == "custom"
        )
        _set_widgets_state([custom_size_entry], custom_active)

        _set_label_group_enabled(
            [
                video_selection_lbl,
                video_embedded_audio_lbl,
                video_burn_lbl,
                video_font_size_lbl,
                res_title,
                comp_title,
                custom_target_lbl,
                custom_mb_lbl,
            ],
            video_enabled,
        )

        try:
            video_font_label.configure(
                text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT if burn_enabled else "#999999"
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        if video_enabled and not custom_active:
            try:
                custom_target_lbl.configure(text_color="#999999")
                custom_mb_lbl.configure(text_color="#999999")
            except Exception:
                pass

        _set_card_enabled(video_card, video_enabled)

        # ── Subtitles card ──
        subtitle_controls = [
            sub_visible_selection_dropdown,
            sub_visible_fmt_srt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_vtt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_txt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_docx_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_fcpxml_check,
        ]

        _set_widgets_state(subtitle_controls, subtitles_enabled)
        _set_label_group_enabled(
            [
                sub_visible_selection_lbl,
                sub_visible_formats_lbl,
            ],
            subtitles_enabled,
        )
        _set_card_enabled(sub_visible_card, subtitles_enabled)

        # ── Audio card ──
        audio_controls = [
            audio_selection_dropdown,
            audio_source_dropdown,
            audio_fmt_dropdown,
        ]

        _set_widgets_state(audio_controls, audio_enabled)

        # WAV is uncompressed, so bitrate stays disabled for WAV even when
        # the Audio card itself is enabled.
        audio_bitrate_enabled = (
            audio_enabled
            and audio_fmt_var.get() != "wav"
        )
        _set_widgets_state([audio_br_dropdown], audio_bitrate_enabled)

        _set_label_group_enabled(
            [
                audio_selection_lbl,
                audio_source_lbl,
                audio_fmt_lbl,
                audio_br_lbl,
            ],
            audio_enabled,
        )

        if audio_enabled and not audio_bitrate_enabled:
            try:
                audio_br_lbl.configure(text_color="#999999")
            except Exception:
                pass

        _set_card_enabled(audio_card, audio_enabled)
    
    def _sync_custom_target_state():
        """Enable target MB input only for Custom Target Size compression."""
        video_mode_active = bool(export_video_var.get())
        custom_active = video_mode_active and comp_var.get() == "custom"

        try:
            custom_size_entry.configure(
                state="normal" if custom_active else "disabled"
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        label_color = theme.SECTION_TEXT if custom_active else "#999999"
        entry_border = theme.ACCENT if custom_active else theme.BORDER

        try:
            custom_target_lbl.configure(text_color=label_color)
            custom_mb_lbl.configure(text_color=label_color)
            custom_size_entry.configure(border_color=entry_border)
        except Exception:
            pass

        _sync_mode_specific_widgets()

    def _on_sub_options_change(*args):
        if _syncing_export_ui["value"]:
            return

        _sync_subtitle_output_dropdown()

        burn = burn_sub_var.get()
        sidecar = sidecar_var.get()
        subonly = subonly_var.get()

        _set_widgets_state(_burn_widgets, burn)
        _set_widgets_state(_sidecar_widgets, sidecar)
        _set_widgets_state(_subonly_widgets, subonly)

        mode = mode_var.get()

        video_needed = (
            mode not in ("subtitles_only", "audio_only")
            and (burn or sidecar or not subonly)
        )
        audio_needed = mode != "subtitles_only"

        _set_widgets_state(_video_widgets, video_needed)
        _set_widgets_state(_audio_widgets, audio_needed)

        if mode == "audio_only":
            try:
                audio_check.configure(state="disabled")
            except Exception:
                pass

        _sync_custom_target_state()

    burn_sub_var.trace_add("write", _on_sub_options_change)
    sidecar_var.trace_add("write", _on_sub_options_change)
    subonly_var.trace_add("write", _on_sub_options_change)
    comp_var.trace_add("write", lambda *args: _sync_custom_target_state())
    _sync_custom_target_state()

    def _on_mode_change(*args):
        mode = mode_var.get()
        current_label = mode_value_to_label.get(mode, "Full Video")
        if mode_display_var.get() != current_label:
            mode_display_var.set(current_label)

        _syncing_export_ui["value"] = True
        try:
            if mode == "subtitles_only":
                burn_sub_var.set(False)
                sidecar_var.set(False)
                subonly_var.set(True)
                audio_var.set(False)
            elif mode == "audio_only":
                burn_sub_var.set(False)
                sidecar_var.set(False)
                subonly_var.set(False)
                audio_var.set(True)
            elif subonly_var.get():
                burn_sub_var.set(True)
                sidecar_var.set(False)
                subonly_var.set(False)
        finally:
            _syncing_export_ui["value"] = False

        mode_info.configure(
            text=mode_tooltips.get(mode, ""),
            text_color="#777777")
        if (mode in ("delete", "split", "merge")
                and not state.sections):
            mode_info.configure(
                text=(f"{theme.ICON_WARN}  No clip selections found. "
                      f"Create clip selections in the Edit tab first."),
                text_color=theme.WARNING)

        _sync_subtitle_output_dropdown()
        _on_sub_options_change()
        _sync_mode_specific_widgets()
        _update_size_labels_for_mode()

        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _update_size_labels_for_mode():
        """Recalculate compression estimates based on export mode."""
        est = state.get_estimated_sizes()
        mb_per_sec = est.get("mb_per_second", 0)
        mode = mode_var.get()

        if not mb_per_sec or mb_per_sec <= 0:
            _set_compression_dropdown_labels(
                _build_compression_label_pairs(est))
            return

        source_duration = state.video_info.get("duration", 0)

        if mode == "full":
            effective_duration = source_duration
        elif mode in ("split", "merge"):
            if state.sections:
                effective_duration = sum(
                    s.end - s.start for s in state.sections)
            else:
                effective_duration = source_duration
        elif mode == "delete":
            if state.sections:
                section_time = sum(
                    s.end - s.start for s in state.sections)
                effective_duration = max(
                    0, source_duration - section_time)
            else:
                effective_duration = source_duration
        else:
            effective_duration = source_duration

        base_mb = mb_per_sec * effective_duration

        mode_estimates = {
            "original": base_mb,
            "high": base_mb * 0.75,
            "medium": base_mb * 0.40,
            "low": base_mb * 0.20,
        }

        label_pairs = [
            ("lossless", "Lossless — largest file"),
            (
                "original",
                f"Original Quality — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(mode_estimates['original'])}",
            ),
            (
                "high",
                f"High Quality — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(mode_estimates['high'])}",
            ),
            (
                "medium",
                f"Balanced — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(mode_estimates['medium'])}",
            ),
            (
                "low",
                f"Small File — about "
                f"{_format_estimated_mb(mode_estimates['low'])}",
            ),
            ("custom", "Custom Target Size"),
        ]

        _set_compression_dropdown_labels(label_pairs)
    mode_var.trace_add("write", _on_mode_change)
    _on_sub_options_change()

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # CRF / BITRATE HELPERS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _get_crf():
        comp = comp_var.get()
        return {"lossless": 0, "original": 18, "high": 20,
                "medium": 26, "low": 32}.get(comp, 23)

    def _get_target_bitrate():
        try:
            target_mb = float(custom_size_entry.get().strip())
        except (ValueError, AttributeError):
            return None
        duration = state.video_info.get("duration", 0)
        if duration <= 0 or target_mb <= 0:
            return None
        target_kbps = int((target_mb * 8 * 1024) / duration)
        audio_kbps = 128
        video_kbps = max(100, target_kbps - audio_kbps)
        return f"{video_kbps}k"

    def _get_resolution_filter():
        if res_var.get() == "1080":
            return ("scale=1920:1080:"
                    "force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
                    "pad=1920:1080:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2")
        if res_var.get() == "720":
            return ("scale=1280:720:"
                    "force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
                    "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2")
        return None

    def _get_force_style():
        size = _scaled_export_font_size()
        h = _get_export_height()
        margin = max(20, int(h * 0.10))
        # ASS alpha: 00=opaque, FF=transparent
        # BackColour &H33000000 = 80% opaque black box
        # OutlineColour &HFF000000 = fully transparent (invisible)
        return (
            f"FontName=Arial,FontSize={size},"
            f"Bold=0,"
            f"PrimaryColour=&H00FFFFFF,"
            f"SecondaryColour=&HFF000000,"
            f"OutlineColour=&HFF000000,"
            f"BackColour=&H33000000,"
            f"BorderStyle=4,"
            f"Outline=8,Shadow=0,"
            f"Alignment=2,"
            f"MarginV={margin},"
            f"MarginL=20,MarginR=20")  

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # EXPORT LOGIC
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    _export_progress_scope = {
        "base": 0.0,
        "weight": 100.0,
        "label": "Exporting",
    }

    def _set_export_progress_scope(base, weight, label="Exporting"):
        """Map one FFmpeg command's 0-100 progress into total export progress."""
        _export_progress_scope["base"] = float(base)
        _export_progress_scope["weight"] = float(weight)
        _export_progress_scope["label"] = label or "Exporting"

    def _export_progress(percent, eta_str=""):
        """Update total export progress using the active progress scope."""
        try:
            raw = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(percent)))
        except Exception:
            raw = 0.0

        base = _export_progress_scope["base"]
        weight = _export_progress_scope["weight"]
        absolute = max(0.0, min(100.0, base + (raw * weight / 100.0)))

        label = _export_progress_scope["label"]
        status = f"{label}... {eta_str}" if eta_str else f"{label}..."

        parent.after(
            0,
            lambda p=absolute, e=eta_str: _update_eta(p, e),
        )
        state.set_progress(absolute, status, owner="export")

    def _on_ffmpeg_progress(percent, eta_str):
        """Called from background thread by run_ffmpeg."""
        _export_progress(percent, eta_str)

    def _update_eta(percent, eta_str):
        """Update ETA label and progress bar on main thread."""
        if eta_str:
            eta_label.configure(text=f"{percent:.0f}%  —  {eta_str}")
        else:
            eta_label.configure(text=f"{percent:.0f}%")

        try:
            if progress_fn:
                progress_fn(percent)
        except Exception:
            pass
    _exporting = {
        "active": False,
        "cancel_requested": False,
        "cancel_event": threading.Event(),
        "process_holder": {"process": None},
    }
    _temp_files = []

    def _run_export_ffmpeg(args, duration=None):
        """Run FFmpeg for export with shared cancel support."""
        if _exporting["cancel_requested"]:
            state.log("Export cancelled before FFmpeg step started.")
            return False

        return run_ffmpeg(
            state.ffmpeg_path,
            args,
            log_fn=state.log,
            progress_fn=_on_ffmpeg_progress,
            duration=duration,
            cancel_event=_exporting["cancel_event"],
            process_holder=_exporting["process_holder"],
        )

    def _is_export_cancelled():
        return bool(
            _exporting["cancel_requested"]
            or _exporting["cancel_event"].is_set()
        )
    
    def _ass_time(seconds: float) -> str:
        """Return ASS timestamp as H:MM:SS.cs."""
        try:
            total_cs = max(0, int(round(float(seconds) * 100)))
        except Exception:
            total_cs = 0

        cs = total_cs % 100
        total_seconds = total_cs // 100
        secs = total_seconds % 60
        mins = (total_seconds // 60) % 60
        hours = total_seconds // 3600
        return f"{hours}:{mins:02d}:{secs:02d}.{cs:02d}"

    def _smart_curly_quotes(text: str) -> str:
        """Convert straight quotes to contextual curly quotes.

        Export-only helper. This does not mutate editor subtitle text.
        """
        import html
        import re

        s = html.unescape(str(text or ""))

        # Normalize common quote-like glyphs before contextual conversion.
        s = (
            s.replace("\u201c", '"')
             .replace("\u201d", '"')
             .replace("\u201e", '"')
             .replace("\u201f", '"')
             .replace("\u2033", '"')
             .replace("\u2018", "'")
             .replace("\u2019", "'")
             .replace("\u201a", "'")
             .replace("\u201b", "'")
             .replace("\u2032", "'")
             .replace("\u02bc", "'")
             .replace("\u00b4", "'")
             .replace("`", "'")
             .replace("´", "'")
        )

        # Repair common transcript/VTT artifacts where apostrophes became dashes.
        contraction_patterns = [
            (r"\bI[-–—](m|d|ll|ve)\b", "I’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\bYou[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "You’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\byou[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "you’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\bWe[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "We’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\bwe[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "we’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\bThey[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "They’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\bthey[-–—](re|d|ll|ve)\b", "they’" + r"\1"),
            (r"\b(he|she|it|that|there|here|what|who|where|when|why|how)[-–—](s|d|ll)\b", r"\1" + "’" + r"\2"),
            (r"\b(He|She|It|That|There|Here|What|Who|Where|When|Why|How)[-–—](s|d|ll)\b", r"\1" + "’" + r"\2"),
            (r"\b(can|don|doesn|didn|isn|aren|wasn|weren|haven|hasn|hadn|won|wouldn|couldn|shouldn|mustn)[-–—]t\b", r"\1" + "’t"),
            (r"\b(Can|Don|Doesn|Didn|Isn|Aren|Wasn|Weren|Haven|Hasn|Hadn|Won|Wouldn|Couldn|Shouldn|Mustn)[-–—]t\b", r"\1" + "’t"),
            (r"\bLet[-–—]s\b", "Let’s"),
            (r"\blet[-–—]s\b", "let’s"),
        ]

        for pattern, repl in contraction_patterns:
            s = re.sub(pattern, repl, s)

        result = []
        double_open = True
        single_open = True

        opening_context = set("([{<—–-")
        closing_context = set(")]}>.,!?;:")

        def _prev_nonspace(chars):
            for ch in reversed(chars):
                if not ch.isspace():
                    return ch
            return ""

        for i, ch in enumerate(s):
            prev_ch = s[i - 1] if i > 0 else ""
            next_ch = s[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(s) else ""

            if ch == '"':
                if double_open:
                    result.append("“")
                else:
                    result.append("”")
                double_open = not double_open
                continue

            if ch == "'":
                # Apostrophe inside words/numbers: don't, John's, rock'n'roll.
                if prev_ch.isalnum() and next_ch.isalnum():
                    result.append("’")
                    continue

                prev_real = _prev_nonspace(result)

                # Opening single quote at start or after opening punctuation.
                if (
                    not prev_real
                    or prev_real in opening_context
                    or prev_real in {"“", "‘"}
                ):
                    result.append("‘")
                    single_open = False
                    continue

                # Closing quote before punctuation/end, or when a quote is open.
                if (
                    single_open is False
                    or not next_ch
                    or next_ch.isspace()
                    or next_ch in closing_context
                ):
                    result.append("’")
                    single_open = True
                    continue

                # Fallback alternates like a normal quote pair.
                if single_open:
                    result.append("‘")
                else:
                    result.append("’")
                single_open = not single_open
                continue

            result.append(ch)

        return "".join(result)

    def _ass_escape_text(text: str) -> str:
        """Normalize smart typography and escape one ASS subtitle line."""
        safe = _smart_curly_quotes(text)
        safe = safe.replace("\\", r"\\")
        safe = safe.replace("{", r"\{").replace("}", r"\}")
        safe = safe.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
        return safe

    def _estimate_ass_text_width(text: str, font_size: int) -> int:
        """Measure/estimate rendered ASS text width in script pixels.

        On Windows, use GDI text measurement for Arial so custom background
        boxes follow the real rendered text much more closely. Fall back to a
        conservative heuristic if measurement is unavailable.
        """
        display_text = _smart_curly_quotes(text)

        if not display_text:
            return 1

        # Preferred path on Windows: measure Arial with GDI.
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import ctypes

                class SIZE(ctypes.Structure):
                    _fields_ = [
                        ("cx", ctypes.c_long),
                        ("cy", ctypes.c_long),
                    ]

                user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
                gdi32 = ctypes.windll.gdi32

                hdc = user32.GetDC(None)
                if hdc:
                    hfont = gdi32.CreateFontW(
                        -int(font_size),  # character height in pixels
                        0,                # width
                        0,                # escapement
                        0,                # orientation
                        400,              # normal weight
                        0,                # italic
                        0,                # underline
                        0,                # strikeout
                        1,                # DEFAULT_CHARSET
                        0,                # OUT_DEFAULT_PRECIS
                        0,                # CLIP_DEFAULT_PRECIS
                        4,                # ANTIALIASED_QUALITY
                        0,                # DEFAULT_PITCH
                        "Arial",
                    )

                    old_font = None
                    if hfont:
                        old_font = gdi32.SelectObject(hdc, hfont)

                    size = SIZE()
                    ok = gdi32.GetTextExtentPoint32W(
                        hdc,
                        display_text,
                        len(display_text),
                        ctypes.byref(size),
                    )

                    if old_font:
                        gdi32.SelectObject(hdc, old_font)
                    if hfont:
                        gdi32.DeleteObject(hfont)
                    user32.ReleaseDC(None, hdc)

                    if ok and size.cx > 0:
                        # Calibrate GDI measurement to libass/Arial rendering.
                        # Keep a tiny safety allowance to avoid clipping.
                        return max(1, int(round((size.cx * 0.94) + 1)))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Cross-platform fallback. Keep this slightly conservative.
        width = 0.0
        for ch in display_text:
            if ch.isspace():
                width += font_size * 0.24
            elif ch in "ilI.,'’‘|!":
                width += font_size * 0.20
            elif ch in "mwMW@#%&":
                width += font_size * 0.72
            elif ch in "“”\"":
                width += font_size * 0.27
            elif ch in "[](){}":
                width += font_size * 0.31
            elif ch in "-–—":
                width += font_size * 0.35
            else:
                width += font_size * 0.47

        return max(1, int(round(width + 1)))

    def _clamp_rect(rect, width: int, height: int, margin_x: int, margin_v: int):
        """Clamp a subtitle background rectangle inside the safe video area."""
        x1, y1, x2, y2 = rect
        safe_left = margin_x
        safe_right = width - margin_x
        safe_top = margin_v
        safe_bottom = height - margin_v

        rect_w = max(1, x2 - x1)
        rect_h = max(1, y2 - y1)

        if rect_w > (safe_right - safe_left):
            x1 = safe_left
            x2 = safe_right
        else:
            if x1 < safe_left:
                x2 += safe_left - x1
                x1 = safe_left
            if x2 > safe_right:
                x1 -= x2 - safe_right
                x2 = safe_right

        if rect_h > (safe_bottom - safe_top):
            y1 = safe_top
            y2 = safe_bottom
        else:
            if y1 < safe_top:
                y2 += safe_top - y1
                y1 = safe_top
            if y2 > safe_bottom:
                y1 -= y2 - safe_bottom
                y2 = safe_bottom

        return (
            int(round(x1)),
            int(round(y1)),
            int(round(x2)),
            int(round(y2)),
        )

    def _line_background_rect(
            line_text: str,
            alignment: str,
            x_pos: int,
            y_pos: int,
            font_size: int,
            width: int,
            height: int,
            margin_x: int,
            margin_v: int,
            pad_x: int,
            pad_y: int,
            line_h: int):
        """Return padded rectangle for one subtitle visual line."""
        text_w = _estimate_ass_text_width(line_text, font_size)

        if alignment == "left":
            x1 = x_pos - pad_x
            x2 = x_pos + text_w + pad_x
        elif alignment == "right":
            x1 = x_pos - text_w - pad_x
            x2 = x_pos + pad_x
        else:
            x1 = x_pos - (text_w / 2) - pad_x
            x2 = x_pos + (text_w / 2) + pad_x

        # Text uses middle anchors. Put the box evenly around the line center.
        y1 = y_pos - (line_h / 2) - pad_y
        y2 = y_pos + (line_h / 2) + pad_y

        return _clamp_rect((x1, y1, x2, y2), width, height, margin_x, margin_v)

    def _merged_rect_union_points(rects):
        """Return one polygon outline for the union of axis-aligned rectangles.

        Uses coordinate-compressed cells so partially overlapping/touching line
        boxes become one clean stepped shape instead of multiple translucent
        overlapping boxes.
        """
        if not rects:
            return []

        if len(rects) == 1:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = rects[0]
            return [(x1, y1), (x2, y1), (x2, y2), (x1, y2)]

        xs = sorted(set([x for r in rects for x in (r[0], r[2])]))
        ys = sorted(set([y for r in rects for y in (r[1], r[3])]))

        if len(xs) < 2 or len(ys) < 2:
            return []

        filled = set()

        for ix in range(len(xs) - 1):
            cx = (xs[ix] + xs[ix + 1]) / 2
            for iy in range(len(ys) - 1):
                cy = (ys[iy] + ys[iy + 1]) / 2
                for x1, y1, x2, y2 in rects:
                    if x1 <= cx <= x2 and y1 <= cy <= y2:
                        filled.add((ix, iy))
                        break

        if not filled:
            return []

        edges = []

        for ix, iy in filled:
            x1, x2 = xs[ix], xs[ix + 1]
            y1, y2 = ys[iy], ys[iy + 1]

            # Add clockwise boundary edges where adjacent cell is empty.
            if (ix, iy - 1) not in filled:
                edges.append(((x1, y1), (x2, y1)))
            if (ix + 1, iy) not in filled:
                edges.append(((x2, y1), (x2, y2)))
            if (ix, iy + 1) not in filled:
                edges.append(((x2, y2), (x1, y2)))
            if (ix - 1, iy) not in filled:
                edges.append(((x1, y2), (x1, y1)))

        adjacency = {}
        for start, end in edges:
            adjacency.setdefault(start, []).append(end)

        if not adjacency:
            return []

        start = min(adjacency.keys(), key=lambda p: (p[1], p[0]))
        points = [start]
        current = start
        guard = 0
        max_steps = len(edges) + 5

        while guard < max_steps:
            guard += 1
            next_points = adjacency.get(current)
            if not next_points:
                break

            nxt = next_points.pop(0)
            if not next_points:
                adjacency.pop(current, None)

            if nxt == start:
                break
