            points.append(nxt)
            current = nxt

        if len(points) < 3:
            # Safe fallback: one bounding rectangle.
            x1 = min(r[0] for r in rects)
            y1 = min(r[1] for r in rects)
            x2 = max(r[2] for r in rects)
            y2 = max(r[3] for r in rects)
            return [(x1, y1), (x2, y1), (x2, y2), (x1, y2)]

        return points

    def _ass_draw_path(points) -> str:
        """Return ASS vector drawing path for polygon points."""
        if not points:
            return ""

        first_x, first_y = points[0]
        parts = [f"m {int(first_x)} {int(first_y)}"]

        for x, y in points[1:]:
            parts.append(f"l {int(x)} {int(y)}")

        parts.append(f"l {int(first_x)} {int(first_y)}")
        return " ".join(parts)
    
    def _write_current_subs_to_temp(cues=None, temp_name="ss_burn_subs.ass"):
        source_cues = list(cues) if cues is not None else list(state.cues)

        if not source_cues:
            return None

        try:
            temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
            temp_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, temp_name)

            width = int(state.video_info.get("width", 1920) or 1920)
            height = int(state.video_info.get("height", 1080) or 1080)
            font_size = _scaled_export_font_size()

            # Text safe area: keep subtitles inside the inner 80% frame.
            # For 1920x1080 this gives 192 px left/right and 108 px bottom/top.
            margin_v = max(20, int(height * 0.10))
            margin_x = max(40, int(width * 0.10))

            def _ass_line_prefix(alignment: str, x_pos: int, y_pos: int) -> str:
                """Return ASS override tags for deterministic per-line placement.

                Use middle anchors so text is vertically centered inside the
                custom background shape.
                """
                if alignment == "left":
                    return rf"{{\an4\pos({x_pos},{y_pos})\q1}}"
                if alignment == "right":
                    return rf"{{\an6\pos({x_pos},{y_pos})\q1}}"
                return rf"{{\an5\pos({x_pos},{y_pos})\q2}}"

            lines = [
                "[Script Info]",
                "ScriptType: v4.00+",
                f"PlayResX: {width}",
                f"PlayResY: {height}",
                "ScaledBorderAndShadow: yes",
                "",
                "[V4+ Styles]",
                (
                    "Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, "
                    "SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, "
                    "Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, "
                    "BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, "
                    "MarginV, Encoding"
                ),
                (
                    "Style: Default,Arial,"
                    f"{font_size},"
                    "&H00FFFFFF,&HFF000000,&HFF000000,&HFF000000,"
                    "0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,0,0,2,"
                    f"{margin_x},{margin_x},{margin_v},1"
                ),
                (
                    "Style: Box,Arial,"
                    f"{font_size},"
                    "&H33000000,&HFF000000,&HFF000000,&HFF000000,"
                    "0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,0,0,7,"
                    f"0,0,0,1"
                ),
                "",
                "[Events]",
                (
                    "Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, "
                    "MarginV, Effect, Text"
                ),
            ]

            line_h = max(14, int(round(font_size * 0.95)))

            # Absolute brand padding in ASS script pixels.
            # Keep this fixed for all cues/lines so padding is visually uniform.
            box_pad_x = max(5, int(round(height * 0.0048)))
            box_pad_y = max(6, int(round(height * 0.0055)))

            line_box_h = line_h + (2 * box_pad_y)

            # Use touching line boxes so the merged shape is clean and the
            # text remains vertically centered in each line's background area.
            line_gap = line_box_h

            for cue in source_cues:
                alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
                if alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
                    alignment = "center"

                cue_lines = (
                    str(cue.text or "")
                    .replace("\r\n", "\n")
                    .replace("\r", "\n")
                    .split("\n")
                )
                cue_lines = [line.strip() for line in cue_lines if line.strip()]
                if not cue_lines:
                    continue

                if alignment == "left":
                    x_pos = margin_x + box_pad_x
                elif alignment == "right":
                    x_pos = width - margin_x - box_pad_x
                else:
                    x_pos = width // 2

                line_positions = []
                background_rects = []

                for line_index, line_text in enumerate(cue_lines):
                    line_from_bottom = len(cue_lines) - 1 - line_index
                    y_pos = int(
                        height
                        - margin_v
                        - (line_box_h / 2)
                        - (line_from_bottom * line_gap)
                    )
                    line_positions.append((line_text, int(x_pos), int(y_pos)))

                    background_rects.append(
                        _line_background_rect(
                            line_text=line_text,
                            alignment=alignment,
                            x_pos=int(x_pos),
                            y_pos=int(y_pos),
                            font_size=font_size,
                            width=width,
                            height=height,
                            margin_x=margin_x,
                            margin_v=margin_v,
                            pad_x=box_pad_x,
                            pad_y=box_pad_y,
                            line_h=line_h,
                        )
                    )

                # Draw one merged background shape for all visual lines.
                # This avoids visible dark overlap from stacked transparent boxes.
                shape_points = _merged_rect_union_points(background_rects)
                shape_path = _ass_draw_path(shape_points)

                if shape_path:
                    lines.append(
                        "Dialogue: 0,"
                        f"{_ass_time(cue.start)},"
                        f"{_ass_time(cue.end)},"
                        "Box,,0,0,0,,"
                        rf"{{\an7\pos(0,0)\p1}}{shape_path}{{\p0}}"
                    )

                # Write each visual subtitle line as its own text event and
                # position it explicitly. This preserves line order while the
                # background is handled by the single merged shape above.
                for line_text, line_x, line_y in line_positions:
                    text = (
                        _ass_line_prefix(alignment, int(line_x), int(line_y))
                        + _ass_escape_text(line_text)
                    )

                    lines.append(
                        "Dialogue: 1,"
                        f"{_ass_time(cue.start)},"
                        f"{_ass_time(cue.end)},"
                        "Default,,0,0,0,,"
                        f"{text}"
                    )

            with open(temp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

            _temp_files.append(temp_path)
            return temp_path

        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Failed to write temp ASS subtitles: {e}")
            return None

    def _cleanup_temp():
        for path in _temp_files:
            try:
                if os.path.isfile(path):
                    os.remove(path)
            except Exception:
                pass
        _temp_files.clear()

    def _build_vf(sub_mode, sub_path_override=None):
        vf = []
        if sub_mode == "burn":
            if sub_path_override:
                sp = sub_path_override
            else:
                sp = _write_current_subs_to_temp()
            if sp:
                is_ass = sp.lower().endswith(".ass")
                sp = sp.replace("\\", "/").replace(":", "\\:")

                if is_ass:
                    vf.append(f"subtitles='{sp}'")
                else:
                    style = _get_force_style()
                    vf.append(
                        f"subtitles='{sp}':force_style='{style}'")
        res = _get_resolution_filter()
        if res:
            vf.append(res)
        return vf

    def _build_codec_args(include_audio=True):
        args = []
        comp = comp_var.get()

        if comp == "lossless":
            args.extend([
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-crf", "0",
                "-preset", "ultrafast",
            ])
            if include_audio:
                args.extend(["-c:a", "flac"])

        elif comp == "custom":
            br = _get_target_bitrate()
            if br:
                args.extend(["-b:v", br])
            else:
                args.extend(["-crf", "23"])

            args.extend([
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-preset", "medium",
            ])

            if include_audio:
                args.extend(["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k"])

        else:
            args.extend([
                "-crf", str(_get_crf()),
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-preset", "medium",
            ])

            if include_audio:
                args.extend(["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k"])

        return args

    def _video_audio_path_exists(path):
        try:
            return bool(path and os.path.isfile(path))
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _resolve_video_embedded_audio():
        """Resolve v5 Video card Embedded Audio setting.

        Returns:
            (mode, external_audio_path, muted)

        mode:
            working/source/enhanced/mute
        external_audio_path:
            file path to use as second FFmpeg input, or None
        muted:
            True when video should be exported with no audio
        """
        mode = "working"
        try:
            mode = video_embedded_audio_var.get() or "working"
        except Exception:
            mode = "working"

        if mode == "mute":
            state.log("Video embedded audio: Mute")
            return mode, None, True

        if mode == "source":
            state.log("Video embedded audio: Original Audio")
            return mode, None, False

        source_audio = getattr(state, "source_audio_path", None)
        enhanced_audio = getattr(state, "enhanced_audio_path", None)
        active_audio = getattr(state, "active_audio_path", None)

        export_audio = None
        try:
            export_audio = state.get_export_audio_path()
        except Exception:
            export_audio = None

        if mode == "enhanced":
            candidates = [
                enhanced_audio,
                active_audio,
                export_audio,
                source_audio,
            ]
            requested_label = "Enhanced Audio"
        else:
            candidates = [
                active_audio,
                export_audio,
                source_audio,
            ]
            requested_label = "Use Working Audio"

        for path in candidates:
            if _video_audio_path_exists(path):
                state.log(
                    f"Video embedded audio: {requested_label} "
                    f"({os.path.basename(path)})"
                )
                return mode, path, False

        state.log(
            f"Video embedded audio: {requested_label} unavailable; "
            "using original video audio."
        )
        return mode, None, False

    def _append_media_maps(args, external_audio_path, muted=False):
        """Map original video plus optional external audio into output."""
        if muted:
            args.extend(["-map", "0:v:0", "-an"])
            return

        if external_audio_path:
            args.extend(["-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0"])
            args.extend(["-shortest"])
            return

        args.extend(["-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "0:a?"])

    # ── Export: Full ──
    def _export_full(out_path, sub_mode):
        _audio_mode, external_audio_path, video_muted = (
            _resolve_video_embedded_audio()
        )

        args = ["-i", state.video_path]
        if external_audio_path:
            args.extend(["-i", external_audio_path])

        vf = _build_vf(sub_mode)
        if vf:
            args.extend(["-vf", ",".join(vf)])

        _append_media_maps(args, external_audio_path, video_muted)
        args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
        args.extend(["-y", out_path])

        state.log("Exporting full video...")
        _set_export_progress_scope(2, 98, "Exporting video")
        success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
            args,
            duration=state.video_info.get("duration", 0)
                if state.video_info else 0)
        if success:
            _log_file_size(out_path)
            if sub_mode == "sidecar":
                _export_sidecar(out_path)

    # ── Export: Split ──
    def _export_split(out_dir, sub_mode):
        _audio_mode, external_audio_path, video_muted = (
            _resolve_video_embedded_audio()
        )

        total = len(state.sections)
        for i, section in enumerate(state.sections):
            clip_path = os.path.join(
                out_dir, f"clip_{i+1:02d}.mp4")
            args = ["-i", state.video_path]
            if external_audio_path:
                args.extend(["-i", external_audio_path])

            args.extend([
                "-ss", str(section.start),
                "-to", str(section.end),
            ])
            vf = _build_vf(sub_mode)
            if vf:
                args.extend(["-vf", ",".join(vf)])
            _append_media_maps(args, external_audio_path, video_muted)
            args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
            args.extend(["-y", clip_path])

            state.log(f"Exporting clip {i+1}/{total}...")
            _set_export_progress_scope(
                (i / total) * 100.0,
                100.0 / total,
                f"Exporting clip {i+1}/{total}",
            )
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=(section.end - section.start))
            if not success:
                return

            if sub_mode == "sidecar":
                _export_sidecar(clip_path)

        _export_progress(100, "Complete")

    # ── Export: Merge ──
    def _export_merge(out_path, sub_mode):
        _audio_mode, external_audio_path, video_muted = (
            _resolve_video_embedded_audio()
        )

        temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
        concat_list = os.path.join(temp_dir, "ss_concat.txt")
        clips = []

        total = len(state.sections)
        for i, section in enumerate(state.sections):
            clip_path = os.path.join(
                temp_dir, f"ss_seg_{i}.mp4")
            clips.append(clip_path)
            args = ["-i", state.video_path]
            if external_audio_path:
                args.extend(["-i", external_audio_path])

            args.extend([
                "-ss", str(section.start),
                "-to", str(section.end),
            ])

            _append_media_maps(args, external_audio_path, video_muted)
            args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
            args.extend(["-y", clip_path])
            state.log(
                f"Cutting segment {i+1}/{total}...")
            _set_export_progress_scope(
                (i / total) * 70.0,
                70.0 / total,
                f"Cutting segment {i+1}/{total}",
            )
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=(section.end - section.start))
            if not success:
                return

        with open(concat_list, "w") as f:
            for clip in clips:
                f.write(f"file '{clip}'\n")

        state.log("Merging segments...")
        _set_export_progress_scope(70, 30, "Merging segments")
        _export_progress(0, "Starting merge...")

        args = ["-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",
                "-i", concat_list]
        temp_sub = None
        if sub_mode == "burn":
            temp_sub = _write_temp_subs(state.sections)
        vf = _build_vf(sub_mode, sub_path_override=temp_sub)
        if vf:
            args.extend(["-vf", ",".join(vf)])

        if video_muted:
            args.extend(["-an"])

        args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
        args.extend(["-y", out_path])

        success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
            args,
            duration=sum(s.end - s.start
                         for s in state.sections))

        for clip in clips:
            try:
                os.remove(clip)
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            os.remove(concat_list)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if success:
            _log_file_size(out_path)
            if sub_mode == "sidecar":
                _export_sidecar(out_path)

    # ── Export: Delete Selected ──
    def _export_delete(out_path, sub_mode):
        _audio_mode, external_audio_path, video_muted = (
            _resolve_video_embedded_audio()
        )

        duration = state.video_info.get("duration", 0)
        if duration <= 0:
            state.log("[!] Cannot determine video duration.")
            return

        sorted_sections = sorted(
            state.sections, key=lambda s: s.start)
        gaps = []
        prev_end = 0.0
        for section in sorted_sections:
            if section.start > prev_end:
                gaps.append((prev_end, section.start))
            prev_end = max(prev_end, section.end)
        if prev_end < duration:
            gaps.append((prev_end, duration))

        if not gaps:
            state.log(
                "[!] Nothing left after deleting sections.")
            return

        temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
        concat_list = os.path.join(temp_dir, "ss_concat.txt")
        clips = []

        total = len(gaps)
        for i, (start, end) in enumerate(gaps):
            clip_path = os.path.join(
                temp_dir, f"ss_keep_{i}.mp4")
            clips.append(clip_path)
            args = ["-i", state.video_path]
            if external_audio_path:
                args.extend(["-i", external_audio_path])

            args.extend([
                "-ss", str(start),
                "-to", str(end),
            ])

            _append_media_maps(args, external_audio_path, video_muted)
            args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
            args.extend(["-y", clip_path])
            state.log(
                f"Keeping {seconds_to_display(start)} → "
                f"{seconds_to_display(end)}...")
            _set_export_progress_scope(
                (i / total) * 70.0,
                70.0 / total,
                f"Keeping segment {i+1}/{total}",
            )
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=(end - start))
            if not success:
                return

        with open(concat_list, "w") as f:
            for clip in clips:
                f.write(f"file '{clip}'\n")

        state.log("Stitching remaining segments...")
        _set_export_progress_scope(70, 30, "Stitching remaining segments")
        _export_progress(0, "Starting stitch...")

        args = ["-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",
                "-i", concat_list]
        temp_sub = None
        if sub_mode == "burn":
            from core.state import Section as _Section
            keep_sections = [
                _Section(s, e) for s, e in gaps]
            temp_sub = _write_temp_subs(keep_sections)
        vf = _build_vf(sub_mode, sub_path_override=temp_sub)
        if vf:
            args.extend(["-vf", ",".join(vf)])

        if video_muted:
            args.extend(["-an"])

        args.extend(_build_codec_args(include_audio=not video_muted))
        args.extend(["-y", out_path])

        success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
            args,
            duration=sum(e - s for s, e in gaps))

        for clip in clips:
            try:
                os.remove(clip)
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            os.remove(concat_list)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if success:
            _log_file_size(out_path)
            if sub_mode == "sidecar":
                _export_sidecar(out_path)

    # ── Write Temp Subs with Adjusted Times ──
    def _write_temp_subs(sections, offset_mode="concat"):
        if not state.cues or not sections:
            return None

        adjusted_cues = []
        cumulative_offset = 0.0
        cue_index = 1

        for section in sections:
            sec_start = section.start
            sec_end = section.end

            for cue in state.cues:
                if cue.end <= sec_start or cue.start >= sec_end:
                    continue
                new_start = (max(cue.start, sec_start)
                             - sec_start + cumulative_offset)
                new_end = (min(cue.end, sec_end)
                           - sec_start + cumulative_offset)
                if new_end > new_start:
                    from core.state import Cue

                    alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
                    if alignment not in ("left", "center", "right"):
                        alignment = "center"

                    try:
                        adjusted_cues.append(
                            Cue(
                                cue_index,
                                new_start,
                                new_end,
                                cue.text,
                                alignment,
                            )
                        )
                    except TypeError:
                        adjusted_cues.append(
                            Cue(
                                cue_index,
                                new_start,
                                new_end,
                                cue.text,
                            )
                        )

                    cue_index += 1

            cumulative_offset += (sec_end - sec_start)

        if not adjusted_cues:
            return None

        try:
            return _write_current_subs_to_temp(
                adjusted_cues,
                "ss_adjusted_burn_subs.ass",
            )
        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Failed to write adjusted ASS subtitles: {e}")
            return None

    # ── Sidecar ──
    def _export_sidecar(video_path):
        fmt = sidecar_fmt_var.get().lower()
        base = os.path.splitext(video_path)[0]
        ext = ".srt" if fmt == "srt" else ".vtt"
        sub_path = f"{base}{ext}"

        try:
            from core.parser import write_srt, write_vtt
            if fmt == "srt":
                write_srt(state.cues, sub_path)
            else:
                write_vtt(state.cues, sub_path)
            state.log(
                f"[OK] Sidecar: {os.path.basename(sub_path)}")
        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Sidecar export failed: {e}")

    def _get_subtitle_export_base_name():
        """Return safe base filename for subtitle exports."""
        source_name = (
            state.sub_path
            or state.video_path
            or "subtitles"
        )
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(source_name))[0]
        return base or "subtitles"

    def _selected_subtitle_formats_from_plan(plan=None):
        """Return normalized selected subtitle formats from v5 plan/UI."""
        formats = []

        try:
            if plan and plan.get("subtitles", {}).get("formats"):
                formats = list(plan["subtitles"]["formats"])
        except Exception:
            formats = []

        if not formats:
            if subtitle_fmt_srt_var.get():
                formats.append("srt")
            if subtitle_fmt_vtt_var.get():
                formats.append("vtt")
            if subtitle_fmt_txt_var.get():
                formats.append("txt")
            if subtitle_fmt_docx_var.get():
                formats.append("docx")
            if subtitle_fmt_fcpxml_var.get():
                formats.append("fcpxml")

        # Safety fallback.
        if not formats:
            formats = ["srt"]

        normalized = []
        seen = set()
        aliases = {
            "srt": "srt",
            "vtt": "vtt",
            "txt": "txt",
            "docx": "docx",
            "fcpxml": "fcpxml",
            "final cut pro xml": "fcpxml",
            "final_cut_pro_xml": "fcpxml",
        }

        for fmt in formats:
            key = str(fmt or "").strip().lower()
            key = aliases.get(key, key)
            if key in ("srt", "vtt", "txt", "docx", "fcpxml"):
                if key not in seen:
                    normalized.append(key)
                    seen.add(key)

        return normalized or ["srt"]

    def _get_subtitle_source_duration():
        """Best-effort source duration for subtitle timeline operations."""
        try:
            duration = float(state.video_info.get("duration", 0) or 0)
            if duration > 0:
                return duration
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            if state.cues:
                return max(float(c.end) for c in state.cues)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return 0.0

    def _cue_alignment(cue):
        """Return cue alignment, defaulting to center."""
        try:
            alignment = getattr(cue, "alignment", "center")
            if alignment in ("left", "center", "right"):
                return alignment
        except Exception:
            pass
        return "center"

    def _make_export_cue(index, start, end, text, alignment="center"):
        """Create a Cue while preserving alignment when supported."""
        from core.state import Cue

        try:
            return Cue(index, start, end, text, alignment)
        except TypeError:
            return Cue(index, start, end, text)

    def _sorted_valid_sections():
        """Return valid clip selections sorted by start time."""
        sections = []
        try:
            for section in state.sections:
                start = float(section.start)
                end = float(section.end)
                if end > start:
                    sections.append((start, end))
        except Exception:
            pass

        sections.sort(key=lambda item: item[0])
        return sections

    def _append_cue_overlap(adjusted, cue, interval_start, interval_end,
                            timeline_offset, index_holder):
        """Append clipped cue overlap for one interval.

        interval_start / interval_end are source timeline bounds.
        timeline_offset is where interval_start maps in output timeline.
        """
        try:
            cue_start = float(cue.start)
            cue_end = float(cue.end)
        except Exception:
            return

        overlap_start = max(cue_start, interval_start)
        overlap_end = min(cue_end, interval_end)

        if overlap_end <= overlap_start:
            return

        new_start = timeline_offset + (overlap_start - interval_start)
        new_end = timeline_offset + (overlap_end - interval_start)

        if new_end <= new_start:
            return

        adjusted.append(
            _make_export_cue(
                index_holder["value"],
                max(0.0, new_start),
                max(0.001, new_end),
                getattr(cue, "text", ""),
                _cue_alignment(cue),
            )
        )
        index_holder["value"] += 1

    def _subtitle_cues_full_source():
        """Return full-source cues reindexed for export."""
        adjusted = []
        index_holder = {"value": 1}

        for cue in state.cues:
            try:
                start = max(0.0, float(cue.start))
                end = max(start + 0.001, float(cue.end))
            except Exception:
                continue

            adjusted.append(
                _make_export_cue(
                    index_holder["value"],
                    start,
                    end,
                    getattr(cue, "text", ""),
                    _cue_alignment(cue),
                )
            )
            index_holder["value"] += 1

        return adjusted

    def _subtitle_cues_delete_sections():
        """Remove selected ranges and stitch remaining subtitle timeline."""
        duration = _get_subtitle_source_duration()
        sections = _sorted_valid_sections()

        if duration <= 0:
            return _subtitle_cues_full_source()

        if not sections:
            return _subtitle_cues_full_source()

        # Build kept gaps.
        gaps = []
        prev_end = 0.0

        for start, end in sections:
            start = max(0.0, min(start, duration))
            end = max(0.0, min(end, duration))

            if start > prev_end:
                gaps.append((prev_end, start))

            prev_end = max(prev_end, end)

        if prev_end < duration:
            gaps.append((prev_end, duration))

        adjusted = []
        index_holder = {"value": 1}
        output_offset = 0.0

        for gap_start, gap_end in gaps:
            if gap_end <= gap_start:
                continue

            for cue in state.cues:
                _append_cue_overlap(
                    adjusted,
                    cue,
                    gap_start,
                    gap_end,
                    output_offset,
                    index_holder,
                )

            output_offset += (gap_end - gap_start)

        return adjusted

    def _subtitle_cues_merge_sections():
        """Keep selected ranges and stitch them into one subtitle timeline."""
        sections = _sorted_valid_sections()

        if not sections:
            return []

        adjusted = []
        index_holder = {"value": 1}
        output_offset = 0.0

        for section_start, section_end in sections:
            if section_end <= section_start:
                continue

            for cue in state.cues:
                _append_cue_overlap(
                    adjusted,
                    cue,
                    section_start,
                    section_end,
                    output_offset,
                    index_holder,
                )

            output_offset += (section_end - section_start)

        return adjusted

    def _subtitle_cue_groups_for_split_sections():
        """Return one subtitle cue group per selected section."""
        sections = _sorted_valid_sections()
        groups = []

        for idx, (section_start, section_end) in enumerate(sections, start=1):
            adjusted = []
            index_holder = {"value": 1}

            for cue in state.cues:
                _append_cue_overlap(
                    adjusted,
                    cue,
                    section_start,
                    section_end,
                    0.0,
                    index_holder,
                )

            groups.append((f"clip_{idx:02d}", adjusted))

        return groups

    def _subtitle_export_groups_for_selection(selection):
        """Return list of (suffix, cues) for subtitle export selection scope."""
        selection = selection or "full"

        if selection == "full":
            return [("", _subtitle_cues_full_source())]

        if selection == "delete":
            return [("_trimmed", _subtitle_cues_delete_sections())]

        if selection == "merge":
            return [("_merged", _subtitle_cues_merge_sections())]

        if selection == "split":
            return _subtitle_cue_groups_for_split_sections()

        return [("", _subtitle_cues_full_source())]
    
    def _write_one_subtitle_format(cues, out_dir, base, fmt):
        """Write one subtitle format. Returns True on success."""
        try:
            from core.parser import write_srt, write_vtt

            if fmt == "srt":
                path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.srt")
                write_srt(cues, path)
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.srt")
                return True

            if fmt == "vtt":
                path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.vtt")
                write_vtt(cues, path)
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.vtt")
                return True

            if fmt == "txt":
                path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.txt")
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    for cue in cues:
                        f.write(f"{cue.text}\n\n")
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.txt")
                return True

            if fmt == "docx":
                path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.docx")
                try:
                    from docx import Document
                except ImportError:
                    state.log(
                        "[!] python-docx not installed — DOCX skipped")
                    return False

                doc = Document()
                table = doc.add_table(
                    rows=1, cols=4, style="Table Grid")
                hdr = table.rows[0].cells
                hdr[0].text = "#"
                hdr[1].text = "Start"
                hdr[2].text = "End"
                hdr[3].text = "Text"

                for cue in cues:
                    row = table.add_row().cells
                    row[0].text = str(cue.index)
                    row[1].text = seconds_to_display(cue.start)
                    row[2].text = seconds_to_display(cue.end)
                    row[3].text = cue.text

                doc.save(path)
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.docx")
                return True

            if fmt == "fcpxml":
                import html

                path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.fcpxml")
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n')
                    f.write('<!DOCTYPE fcpxml>\n')
                    f.write('<fcpxml version="1.10">\n')
                    f.write('  <resources>\n')
                    f.write('    <format id="r1" name="FFVideoFormat1080p25"/>\n')
                    f.write('  </resources>\n')
                    f.write('  <library>\n')
                    f.write('    <event name="Subtitle Studio">\n')
                    f.write('      <project name="Subtitles">\n')
                    f.write('        <sequence format="r1" duration="3600s">\n')
                    f.write('          <spine>\n')

                    for cue in cues:
                        text = html.escape(cue.text or "", quote=True)
                        start = f"{max(cue.start, 0):.3f}s"
                        duration = f"{max(cue.end - cue.start, 0.001):.3f}s"

                        f.write(
                            f'            <title name="Subtitle {cue.index}" '
                            f'offset="{start}" duration="{duration}" '
                            f'start="0s">\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            f'              <text><text-style ref="ts{cue.index}">'
                            f'{text}</text-style></text>\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            f'              <text-style-def id="ts{cue.index}">\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            '                <text-style font="Helvetica" '
                            'fontSize="48" fontColor="1 1 1 1" '
                            'alignment="center"/>\n'
                        )
                        f.write('              </text-style-def>\n')
                        f.write('            </title>\n')

                    f.write('          </spine>\n')
                    f.write('        </sequence>\n')
                    f.write('      </project>\n')
                    f.write('    </event>\n')
                    f.write('  </library>\n')
                    f.write('</fcpxml>\n')

                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.fcpxml")
                return True

            state.log(f"[!] Unknown subtitle format skipped: {fmt}")
            return False

        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Subtitle {fmt.upper()} export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def _export_subtitles_from_plan(out_dir, plan=None):
        """Export selected subtitle formats using the v5 subtitle scope."""
        if not state.cues:
            state.log("[!] No subtitles/cues to export.")
            return False

        if not os.path.isdir(out_dir):
            out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_dir)

        if not out_dir:
            out_dir = os.getcwd()

        try:
            os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        except Exception as e:
            state.log(f"[!] Could not create subtitle output folder: {e}")
            return False

        selection = "full"
        try:
            if plan:
                selection = plan.get("subtitles", {}).get("selection", "full")
            else:
                selection = subtitle_selection_var.get()
        except Exception:
            selection = "full"

        selection_label = selection_value_to_label.get(
            selection, "Full Source")

        if selection in ("delete", "split", "merge") and not state.sections:
            state.log(
                "[!] Subtitle selection export requires clip selections.")
            return False

        formats = _selected_subtitle_formats_from_plan(plan)
        base = _get_subtitle_export_base_name()
        groups = _subtitle_export_groups_for_selection(selection)

        if not groups:
            state.log("[!] No subtitle export groups were created.")
            return False

        state.log(f"Subtitle selection: {selection_label}")
        state.log(
            "Subtitle formats selected: "
            + ", ".join(fmt.upper() for fmt in formats)
        )

        success_count = 0
        total_count = len(groups) * len(formats)

        for suffix, cues in groups:
            if _is_export_cancelled():
                return False

            group_base = base
            if suffix:
                if suffix.startswith("clip_"):
                    group_base = f"{base}_{suffix}"
                else:
                    group_base = f"{base}{suffix}"

            if not cues:
                state.log(
                    f"[i] No subtitle cues for {group_base}; "
                    "writing empty subtitle file(s)."
                )

            for fmt in formats:
                if _is_export_cancelled():
                    return False

                if _write_one_subtitle_format(
                        cues, out_dir, group_base, fmt):
                    success_count += 1

        if progress_fn:
            progress_fn(100)

        if success_count:
            state.log(
                f"[OK] Subtitle export complete: "
                f"{success_count}/{total_count} file(s)."
            )
            return success_count == total_count

        state.log("[!] Subtitle export failed.")
        return False
    
    # ── Subtitle Only ──
    def _export_subtitle_only(out_dir):
        from core.parser import write_srt, write_vtt

        base = os.path.splitext(
            os.path.basename(
                state.sub_path or "subtitles"))[0]
        selected_format = subonly_format_var.get()

        if selected_format == "SRT":
            path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.srt")
            write_srt(state.cues, path)
            state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.srt")

        elif selected_format == "VTT":
            path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.vtt")
            write_vtt(state.cues, path)
            state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.vtt")

        elif selected_format == "TXT":
            path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.txt")
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for cue in state.cues:
                    f.write(f"{cue.text}\n\n")
            state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.txt")

        elif selected_format == "DOCX":
            path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.docx")
            try:
                from docx import Document
                doc = Document()
                table = doc.add_table(
                    rows=1, cols=4, style="Table Grid")
                hdr = table.rows[0].cells
                hdr[0].text = "#"
                hdr[1].text = "Start"
                hdr[2].text = "End"
                hdr[3].text = "Text"
                for cue in state.cues:
                    row = table.add_row().cells
                    row[0].text = str(cue.index)
                    row[1].text = seconds_to_display(cue.start)
                    row[2].text = seconds_to_display(cue.end)
                    row[3].text = cue.text
                doc.save(path)
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.docx")
            except ImportError:
                state.log(
                    "[!] python-docx not installed — DOCX skipped")

        elif selected_format == "Final Cut Pro XML":
            path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{base}.fcpxml")
            try:
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n')
                    f.write('<!DOCTYPE fcpxml>\n')
                    f.write('<fcpxml version="1.10">\n')
                    f.write('  <resources>\n')
                    f.write('    <format id="r1" name="FFVideoFormat1080p25"/>\n')
                    f.write('  </resources>\n')
                    f.write('  <library>\n')
                    f.write('    <event name="Subtitle Studio">\n')
                    f.write('      <project name="Subtitles">\n')
                    f.write('        <sequence format="r1" duration="3600s">\n')
                    f.write('          <spine>\n')
                    for cue in state.cues:
                        text = (
                            cue.text.replace("&", "&amp;")
                            .replace("<", "&lt;")
                            .replace(">", "&gt;")
                            .replace('"', "&quot;")
                        )
                        start = f"{max(cue.start, 0):.3f}s"
                        duration = f"{max(cue.end - cue.start, 0.001):.3f}s"
                        f.write(
                            f'            <title name="Subtitle {cue.index}" '
                            f'offset="{start}" duration="{duration}" '
                            f'start="0s">\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            f'              <text><text-style ref="ts{cue.index}">'
                            f'{text}</text-style></text>\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            f'              <text-style-def id="ts{cue.index}">\n'
                        )
                        f.write(
                            '                <text-style font="Helvetica" '
                            'fontSize="48" fontColor="1 1 1 1" '
                            'alignment="center"/>\n'
                        )
                        f.write('              </text-style-def>\n')
                        f.write('            </title>\n')
                    f.write('          </spine>\n')
                    f.write('        </sequence>\n')
                    f.write('      </project>\n')
                    f.write('    </event>\n')
                    f.write('  </library>\n')
                    f.write('</fcpxml>\n')
                state.log(f"[OK] Exported: {base}.fcpxml")
            except Exception as e:
                state.log(f"[!] Final Cut Pro XML export failed: {e}")

        else:
            state.log(f"[!] Unknown subtitle format: {selected_format}")

        if progress_fn:
            progress_fn(100)

    # ── Audio Export ──
    def _path_exists(path):
        """Return True if path exists and is a file."""
        try:
            return bool(path and os.path.isfile(path))
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _audio_source_label(source_mode):
        """Human label for v5 audio source values."""
        return {
            "working": "Use Working Audio",
            "source": "Original Audio",
            "enhanced": "Enhanced Audio",
        }.get(source_mode, "Use Working Audio")

    def _resolve_v5_audio_source(plan=None):
        """Resolve the actual input path for standalone/companion audio export.

        The Audio card owns this choice:
        - working: active working audio if available, then export audio, then source audio
        - source: original extracted/source audio if available, otherwise source video
        - enhanced: enhanced audio if available, otherwise fallback to working/source
        """
        source_mode = "working"

        try:
            if plan:
                source_mode = plan.get("audio", {}).get("source", "working")
            else:
                source_mode = audio_source_var.get()
        except Exception:
            source_mode = "working"

        source_mode = source_mode or "working"

        source_audio = getattr(state, "source_audio_path", None)
        enhanced_audio = getattr(state, "enhanced_audio_path", None)
        active_audio = getattr(state, "active_audio_path", None)

        export_audio = None
        try:
            export_audio = state.get_export_audio_path()
        except Exception:
            export_audio = None

        candidates = []

        if source_mode == "enhanced":
            candidates = [
                enhanced_audio,
                active_audio,
                export_audio,
                source_audio,
                state.video_path,
            ]

        elif source_mode == "source":
            candidates = [
                source_audio,
                state.video_path,
            ]

        else:
            # working
            candidates = [
                active_audio,
                export_audio,
                source_audio,
                state.video_path,
            ]

        for path in candidates:
            if _path_exists(path):
                return path, source_mode

        return None, source_mode
    
    def _export_audio(
            video_out_path,
            mode_override=None,
            exact_output=False,
            plan=None):
        fmt = audio_fmt_var.get()
        br = audio_br_var.get()
        ext = {"mp3": ".mp3", "aac": ".m4a",
               "wav": ".wav"}.get(fmt, ".mp3")
        codec = {"mp3": "libmp3lame", "aac": "aac",
                 "wav": "pcm_s16le"}.get(fmt, "libmp3lame")

        audio_input_path, audio_source_mode = _resolve_v5_audio_source(plan)

        if not audio_input_path:
            state.log("[!] No usable audio source found for export.")
            return False

        state.log(
            f"Audio source: {_audio_source_label(audio_source_mode)} "
            f"({os.path.basename(audio_input_path)})"
        )

        source_base = os.path.splitext(
            os.path.basename(audio_input_path or state.video_path or "audio")
        )[0]
        base = os.path.splitext(video_out_path)[0]
        mode = mode_override or mode_var.get()
        if mode == "audio_only":
            mode = "full"

        # ── Full audio / companion audio ──
        if mode == "full" or not state.sections:
            if exact_output:
                if os.path.isdir(video_out_path):
                    audio_path = os.path.join(
                        video_out_path, f"{source_base}{ext}")
                else:
                    root, current_ext = os.path.splitext(video_out_path)
                    audio_path = (
                        video_out_path
                        if current_ext.lower() == ext
                        else f"{root}{ext}"
                    )
            else:
                audio_path = f"{base}_audio{ext}"

            args = ["-i", audio_input_path, "-vn",
                    "-acodec", codec]
            if fmt != "wav":
                args.extend(["-ab", br])
            args.extend(["-y", audio_path])

            duration = (state.video_info.get("duration", 0)
                        if state.video_info else 0)
            state.log(
                f"Exporting audio ({fmt.upper()}, {br})...")
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=duration)
            if success:
                _log_file_size(audio_path)
                state.log(
                    f"[OK] Audio: "
                    f"{os.path.basename(audio_path)}")
                state.log(f"[OK] Audio saved to: {audio_path}")
            return bool(success)

        # ── Split mode: one audio file per section ──
        if mode == "split":
            out_dir = video_out_path
            if not os.path.isdir(out_dir):
                out_dir = os.path.dirname(video_out_path)
            total = len(state.sections)
            for i, section in enumerate(state.sections):
                audio_path = os.path.join(
                    out_dir,
                    f"clip_{i+1:02d}_audio{ext}")
                args = [
                    "-i", audio_input_path,
                    "-ss", str(section.start),
                    "-to", str(section.end),
                    "-vn", "-acodec", codec]
                if fmt != "wav":
                    args.extend(["-ab", br])
                args.extend(["-y", audio_path])

                state.log(
                    f"Exporting audio {i+1}/{total} "
                    f"({fmt.upper()}, {br})...")
                success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                    args,
                    duration=(section.end - section.start))
                if not success:
                    return False
                _log_file_size(audio_path)
            state.log(
                f"[OK] {total} audio clips exported.")
            return True

        # ── Merge mode: extract each section, concat ──
        if mode == "merge":
            temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
            temp_clips = []
            total = len(state.sections)

            for i, section in enumerate(state.sections):
                clip = os.path.join(
                    temp_dir, f"ss_audio_seg_{i}{ext}")
                temp_clips.append(clip)
                args = [
                    "-i", audio_input_path,
                    "-ss", str(section.start),
                    "-to", str(section.end),
                    "-vn", "-acodec", codec]
                if fmt != "wav":
                    args.extend(["-ab", br])
                args.extend(["-y", clip])

                state.log(
                    f"Extracting audio segment "
                    f"{i+1}/{total}...")
                success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                    args,
                    duration=(section.end - section.start))
                if not success:
                    return False

            # Concat
            concat_list = os.path.join(
                temp_dir, "ss_audio_concat.txt")
            with open(concat_list, "w") as f:
                for clip in temp_clips:
                    f.write(f"file '{clip}'\n")

            if exact_output:
                root, current_ext = os.path.splitext(video_out_path)
                audio_path = (
                    video_out_path
                    if current_ext.lower() == ext
                    else f"{root}{ext}"
                )
            else:
                audio_path = f"{base}_audio{ext}"
            args = ["-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",
                    "-i", concat_list,
                    "-c", "copy", "-y", audio_path]

            state.log("Merging audio segments...")
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=sum(s.end - s.start
                             for s in state.sections))

            # Cleanup temp
            for clip in temp_clips:
                try:
                    os.remove(clip)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            try:
                os.remove(concat_list)
            except Exception:
                pass

            if success:
                _log_file_size(audio_path)
                state.log(
                    f"[OK] Audio: "
                    f"{os.path.basename(audio_path)}")
                state.log(f"[OK] Audio saved to: {audio_path}")
            return bool(success)

        # ── Delete mode: extract gaps, concat ──
        if mode == "delete":
            duration = (state.video_info.get("duration", 0)
                        if state.video_info else 0)
            sorted_sections = sorted(
                state.sections, key=lambda s: s.start)
            gaps = []
            prev_end = 0.0
            for section in sorted_sections:
                if section.start > prev_end:
                    gaps.append((prev_end, section.start))
                prev_end = max(prev_end, section.end)
            if prev_end < duration:
                gaps.append((prev_end, duration))

            if not gaps:
                state.log(
                    "[!] No audio remaining after delete.")
                return False

            temp_dir = tempfile.gettempdir()
            temp_clips = []
            total = len(gaps)

            for i, (start, end) in enumerate(gaps):
                clip = os.path.join(
                    temp_dir, f"ss_audio_keep_{i}{ext}")
                temp_clips.append(clip)
                args = [
                    "-i", audio_input_path,
                    "-ss", str(start),
                    "-to", str(end),
                    "-vn", "-acodec", codec]
                if fmt != "wav":
                    args.extend(["-ab", br])
                args.extend(["-y", clip])

                state.log(
                    f"Keeping audio "
                    f"{seconds_to_display(start)} → "
                    f"{seconds_to_display(end)}...")
                success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                    args,
                    duration=(end - start))
                if not success:
                    return False

            # Concat
            concat_list = os.path.join(
                temp_dir, "ss_audio_concat.txt")
            with open(concat_list, "w") as f:
                for clip in temp_clips:
                    f.write(f"file '{clip}'\n")

            if exact_output:
                root, current_ext = os.path.splitext(video_out_path)
                audio_path = (
                    video_out_path
                    if current_ext.lower() == ext
                    else f"{root}{ext}"
                )
            else:
                audio_path = f"{base}_audio{ext}"

            args = ["-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",
                    "-i", concat_list,
                    "-c", "copy", "-y", audio_path]

            state.log("Stitching remaining audio...")
            success = _run_export_ffmpeg(
                args,
                duration=sum(e - s for s, e in gaps))

            # Cleanup temp
            for clip in temp_clips:
                try:
                    os.remove(clip)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            try:
                os.remove(concat_list)
            except Exception:
                pass

            if success:
                _log_file_size(audio_path)
                state.log(
                    f"[OK] Audio: "
                    f"{os.path.basename(audio_path)}")
                state.log(f"[OK] Audio saved to: {audio_path}")
            return bool(success)

    def _log_file_size(path):
        try:
            mb = os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024)
            state.log(f"    File size: {mb:.1f} MB")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _set_export_button_running():
        btn_export.configure(
            text=f"{theme.ICON_WARN} Cancel Export",
            fg_color=theme.ERROR,
            hover_color="#9E1F1F",
            text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
            state="normal",
            command=_cancel_export,
        )

    def _set_export_button_idle():
        btn_export.configure(
            text=f"{theme.ICON_EXPORT} EXPORT",
            fg_color=theme.ACCENT,
            hover_color=theme.ACCENT_HOVER,
            text_color=theme.BTN_TEXT,
            state="normal",
            command=_do_export,
        )

    def _cancel_export():
        if not _exporting["active"]:
            return

        if _exporting["cancel_requested"]:
            return

        _exporting["cancel_requested"] = True
        _exporting["cancel_event"].set()
        export_status.configure(
            text=f"{theme.ICON_WARN}  Cancelling export...",
            text_color=theme.ERROR,
        )
        eta_label.configure(text="Stopping FFmpeg...")
        state.set_progress(0, "Cancelling export...", owner="export")
        state.log("Cancel Export requested.")

        proc = _exporting["process_holder"].get("process")
        if proc is not None:
            try:
                proc.terminate()
            except Exception:
                pass
    
    def _set_export_controls_enabled(enabled):
        """Disable export settings while export is running.

        The Export button itself stays enabled so it can act as Cancel Export.
        """
        state_value = "normal" if enabled else "disabled"

        controls = [
            mode_dropdown,
            subtitle_output_dropdown,
            font_slider,
            sidecar_dropdown,
            subonly_format_dropdown,

            export_video_check,
            export_audio_check,
            export_subtitles_check,

            video_selection_dropdown,
            video_burn_dropdown,
            video_embedded_audio_dropdown,
            video_font_slider,
            res_dropdown,

            sub_visible_selection_dropdown,
            sub_visible_fmt_srt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_vtt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_txt_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_docx_check,
            sub_visible_fmt_fcpxml_check,

            rb_orig,
            rb_1080,
            comp_dropdown,
            custom_size_entry,

            audio_check,
            audio_selection_dropdown,
            audio_source_dropdown,
            audio_fmt_dropdown,
            audio_br_dropdown,
            audio_scope_dropdown,

            btn_browse,
            sections_listbox,
        ]

        for widget in controls:
            try:
                widget.configure(state=state_value)
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            if enabled:
                mode_dropdown.configure(
                    values=list(mode_label_to_value.keys()),
                    command=_on_mode_dropdown_change,
                )
                subtitle_output_dropdown.configure(
                    values=list(subtitle_output_to_flags.keys()),
                    command=_on_subtitle_output_change,
                )
                _sync_mode_specific_widgets()
                comp_dropdown.configure(command=_on_compression_change)
                audio_fmt_dropdown.configure(command=_on_audio_format_change)
                audio_br_dropdown.configure(command=_on_audio_bitrate_change)
                audio_scope_dropdown.configure(command=_on_audio_scope_change)
            else:
                # CTkOptionMenu can still visually open on some systems even
                # when disabled, so restrict values to the current selection.
                mode_dropdown.configure(
                    values=[mode_display_var.get()],
                    command=lambda *_: None,
                )
                subtitle_output_dropdown.configure(
                    values=[subtitle_output_var.get()],
                    command=lambda *_: None,
                )
                _sync_mode_specific_widgets()
                comp_dropdown.configure(command=lambda *_: None)
                audio_fmt_dropdown.configure(command=lambda *_: None)
                audio_br_dropdown.configure(command=lambda *_: None)
                audio_scope_dropdown.configure(command=lambda *_: None)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if enabled:
            try:
                _on_sub_options_change()
                _sync_custom_target_state()
                _sync_v5_card_states()
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            try:
                custom_target_lbl.configure(text_color="#999999")
                custom_mb_lbl.configure(text_color="#999999")
                custom_size_entry.configure(
                    state="disabled",
                    border_color=theme.BORDER,
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
    
    # ── Main export handler ──
    def _do_export():
        if _exporting["active"]:
            state.log("[!] Export already in progress.")
            return

        try:
            _sync_v5_to_legacy_export_state()
            plan = _build_export_plan()
        except Exception:
            plan = None

        if plan:
            no_outputs = (
                not plan["video"]["enabled"]
                and not plan["audio"]["enabled"]
                and not plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]
            )
            if no_outputs:
                messagebox.showwarning(
                    "No Outputs Selected",
                    "Select at least one output type:\n\n"
                    "Video, Audio, or Subtitles."
                )
                return

        if (
            plan
            and plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]
            and not plan["video"]["enabled"]
            and not plan["audio"]["enabled"]
        ):
            # Pure subtitles-only can run without a video, as long as cues exist.
            pass
        elif not state.video_path:
            state.log("[!] No video loaded.")
            return

        out_path = output_path_var.get().strip()
        if not out_path:
            state.log("[!] Select an output location first.")
            return

        mode = mode_var.get()

        if plan:
            state.log("Export plan:")
            state.log(f"  Video: {plan['video']}")
            state.log(f"  Audio: {plan['audio']}")
            state.log(f"  Subtitles: {plan['subtitles']}")

        if mode == "audio_only":
            burn_sub_var.set(False)
            sidecar_var.set(False)
            subonly_var.set(False)
            audio_var.set(True)
        elif mode == "subtitles_only":
            burn_sub_var.set(False)
            sidecar_var.set(False)
            subonly_var.set(True)
            audio_var.set(False)

        do_burn = burn_sub_var.get()
        do_sidecar = sidecar_var.get()
        do_subonly = subonly_var.get()

        if plan:
            section_required = []

            if (
                plan["video"]["enabled"]
                and plan["video"]["selection"] in ("delete", "split", "merge")
            ):
                section_required.append("Video")

            if (
                plan["audio"]["enabled"]
                and plan["audio"]["selection"] in ("delete", "split", "merge")
            ):
                section_required.append("Audio")

            if (
                plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]
                and plan["subtitles"]["selection"] in ("delete", "split", "merge")
            ):
                section_required.append("Subtitles")

            if section_required and not state.sections:
                messagebox.showwarning(
                    "No Clip Selections",
                    "This export requires clip selections:\n\n"
                    + ", ".join(section_required)
                    + "\n\nGo to the Edit tab and mark clip selections first."
                )
                return
        
        if (mode in ("delete", "split", "merge")
                and not state.sections
                and (do_burn or do_sidecar)):
            messagebox.showwarning(
                "No Sections",
                "This mode requires marked sections.\n\n"
                "Go to Edit tab and mark sections first.")
            return

        if (mode == "audio_only"
                and audio_scope_var.get() in ("delete", "split", "merge")
                and not state.sections):
            messagebox.showwarning(
                "No Clip Selections",
                "This Audio Only option requires clip selections.\n\n"
                "Go to Edit tab and mark sections first.")
            return

        _exporting["active"] = True
        _exporting["cancel_requested"] = False
        _exporting["cancel_event"].clear()
        _exporting["process_holder"]["process"] = None
        _set_export_controls_enabled(False)
        _set_export_button_running()
        state.begin_progress_owner("export")
        _set_export_progress_scope(0, 100, "Preparing export")
        _export_progress(0, "Preparing...")
        export_status.configure(
            text="Exporting...", text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT)
        state.log("Export started. Cancel Export is available.")

        ts = _timestamp()
        state.log_session(
            f"[{ts}] Export started: mode={mode}")

        def _export_thread():
            try:
                state.set_progress(2, "Preparing export...", owner="export")

                # Silent pre-export cleanup
                from core.state import Cue
                export_cues, fix_count = auto_fix_before_export(
                    [
                        Cue(
                            c.index,
                            c.start,
                            c.end,
                            c.text,
                            getattr(c, "alignment", "center"),
                        )
                        for c in state.cues
                    ],
                    log_fn=lambda m: state.log(m))
                if fix_count > 0:
                    state.cues = export_cues
                    state.log(
                        f"Pre-export: {fix_count} auto-fixes "
                        f"applied silently")

                if mode == "subtitles_only":
                    sub_out = out_path
                    if not os.path.isdir(sub_out):
                        sub_out = os.path.dirname(out_path)

                    state.log(f"Subtitles Only export selected: {sub_out}")
                    success = _export_subtitles_from_plan(sub_out, plan)

                    parent.after(
                        0, lambda s=bool(success): _export_done(
                            s and not _is_export_cancelled()
                        )
                    )
                    return

                if mode == "audio_only":
                    audio_scope = audio_scope_var.get()
                    audio_scope_label = audio_scope_value_to_label.get(
                        audio_scope, "Full Audio")

                    state.log(
                        f"Audio Only export selected: "
                        f"{audio_scope_label} → {out_path}")

                    success = _export_audio(
                        out_path,
                        mode_override=audio_scope,
                        exact_output=True,
                        plan=plan,
                    )

                    if (
                        success
                        and plan
                        and plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]
                        and not _is_export_cancelled()
                    ):
                        sub_out = out_path
                        if not os.path.isdir(sub_out):
                            sub_out = os.path.dirname(out_path)

                        state.log(
                            f"Companion subtitles export selected: {sub_out}")
                        sub_success = _export_subtitles_from_plan(
                            sub_out, plan)
                        success = bool(success and sub_success)

                    parent.after(
                        0, lambda s=bool(success): _export_done(
                            s and not _is_export_cancelled()
                        )
                    )
                    return

                if do_burn or do_sidecar:
                    sub_mode = "burn" if do_burn else "sidecar"
                    if mode == "full":
                        _export_full(out_path, sub_mode)
                    elif mode == "split":
                        _export_split(out_path, sub_mode)
                    elif mode == "merge":
                        _export_merge(out_path, sub_mode)
                    elif mode == "delete":
                        _export_delete(out_path, sub_mode)

                    if do_burn and do_sidecar:
                        _export_sidecar(out_path)
                    if audio_var.get():
                        _export_audio(
                            out_path,
                            mode_override=plan["audio"]["selection"] if plan else None,
                            plan=plan,
                        )

                elif not do_subonly:
                    if mode == "audio_only":
                        _export_audio(
                            out_path,
                            mode_override=plan["audio"]["selection"] if plan else None,
                            plan=plan,
                        )
                    else:
                        if mode == "full":
                            _export_full(out_path, "none")
                        elif mode == "split":
                            _export_split(out_path, "none")
                        elif mode == "merge":
                            _export_merge(out_path, "none")
                        elif mode == "delete":
                            _export_delete(out_path, "none")
                        if audio_var.get():
                            _export_audio(
                                out_path,
                                mode_override=plan["audio"]["selection"] if plan else None,
                                plan=plan,
                            )

                companion_subtitles = bool(
                    plan
                    and plan["subtitles"]["enabled"]
                    and mode != "subtitles_only"
                    and mode != "audio_only"
                )

                if do_subonly or companion_subtitles:
                    sub_out = out_path
                    if not os.path.isdir(sub_out):
                        sub_out = os.path.dirname(out_path)

                    state.log(f"Subtitle file export selected: {sub_out}")
                    _export_subtitles_from_plan(sub_out, plan)

                parent.after(
                    0, lambda: _export_done(not _is_export_cancelled()))
            except Exception as e:
                state.log(f"[ERROR] Export failed: {e}")
                parent.after(
                    0, lambda: _export_done(False))

        threading.Thread(
            target=_export_thread, daemon=True).start()

    def _export_done(success):
        was_cancelled = _is_export_cancelled()

        _cleanup_temp()
        _exporting["active"] = False
        _exporting["process_holder"]["process"] = None
        eta_label.configure(text="")
        _set_export_controls_enabled(True)
        _set_export_button_idle()

        def _release_export_progress():
            state.clear_progress(owner="export")
            state.end_progress_owner("export")

        if was_cancelled:
            export_status.configure(
                text=f"{theme.ICON_WARN}  Export cancelled.",
                text_color=theme.ERROR)
            state.log("[!] Export cancelled.")
            state.set_progress(0, "Export cancelled", owner="export")
            parent.after(3000, _release_export_progress)
            return

        if success:
            state.clear_autosave()
            export_status.configure(
                text=f"{theme.ICON_CHECK}  Export complete!",
                text_color=theme.SUCCESS)
            state.log("[OK] Export complete.")
            state.set_progress(100, "Export complete", owner="export")
            parent.after(3000, _release_export_progress)
        else:
            export_status.configure(
                text=f"{theme.ICON_WARN}  Export failed.",
                text_color=theme.ERROR)
            state.set_progress(0, "Export failed", owner="export")
            parent.after(3000, _release_export_progress)

    btn_export.configure(command=_do_export)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TAB ACTIVATION
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def on_tab_activated():
        _refresh_sections()
        try:
            _sync_v5_to_legacy_export_state()
        except Exception:
            pass
        _on_mode_change()
        _on_sub_options_change()
        try:
            _sync_v5_card_states()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            _update_output_summary()
        except Exception:
            pass

    ui["on_tab_activated"] = on_tab_activated

    return ui


def _timestamp() -> str:
    return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")

FILE: tabs/tab_logs.py
