"""
Subtitle Studio v5.0 — Tab 3: Logs.
Dual pane: Session log (left) and Subtitle changes (right).
"""

import os
import datetime
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog
import customtkinter as ctk

from core import theme
from core.theme import (
    make_primary_btn, make_secondary_btn, make_card,
    make_section_label, register_theme_callback,
)
from core.state import AppState
from core.widgets import Tooltip


def build_logs_tab(parent, state: AppState):
    """Build the Logs tab. Returns ui dict."""

    ui = {}

    # ── Title ──
    title_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(
        parent, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    title_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=theme.PAD_LG, pady=(theme.PAD_LG, 0))

    title_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        title_frame, text="Logs",
        font=("Georgia", 14, "bold"),
        text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    title_lbl.pack(anchor="w")

    subtitle_lbl = ctk.CTkLabel(
        title_frame,
        text="Session activity and subtitle edit history.",
        font=theme.FONT_BODY,
        text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT,
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    subtitle_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_MD))

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # DUAL PANE
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    panes = ctk.CTkFrame(
        parent, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    panes.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
               padx=theme.PAD_LG, pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    # ── Left: Session Log ──
    left = ctk.CTkFrame(
        panes, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    left.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True,
              padx=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    session_section_lbl = make_section_label(left, "Session Log")
    session_section_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    session_card = make_card(left)
    session_card.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    session_text = tk.Text(
        session_card, font=theme.FONT_LOG,
        bg=theme.CARD_BG, fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        relief="flat", padx=8, pady=6,
        state="disabled", wrap="word",
        highlightthickness=0, bd=0)
    session_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=2, pady=2)

    session_scroll = ctk.CTkScrollbar(
        session_card, command=session_text.yview)
    session_scroll.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    session_text.configure(yscrollcommand=session_scroll.set)

    # Export session log button
    session_btn_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        left, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    session_btn_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(theme.PAD_SM, 0))

    btn_export_session = make_secondary_btn(
        session_btn_row, "Export Session Log",
        icon=theme.ICON_EXPORT)
    btn_export_session.pack(side="left")
    Tooltip(btn_export_session,
            "Save session log as a text file")

    session_count = ctk.CTkLabel(
        session_btn_row, text="0 entries",
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL,
        text_color="#999999",
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    session_count.pack(side="right")

    # ── Right: Subtitle Changes ──
    right = ctk.CTkFrame(
        panes, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    right.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True,
               padx=(theme.PAD_SM, 0))

    changes_section_lbl = make_section_label(
        right, "Subtitle Changes")
    changes_section_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, theme.PAD_SM))

    subtitle_card = make_card(right)
    subtitle_card.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    subtitle_text = tk.Text(
        subtitle_card, font=theme.FONT_LOG,
        bg=theme.CARD_BG, fg=theme.DARK_TEXT,
        relief="flat", padx=8, pady=6,
        state="disabled", wrap="word",
        highlightthickness=0, bd=0)
    subtitle_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=2, pady=2)

    subtitle_scroll = ctk.CTkScrollbar(
        subtitle_card, command=subtitle_text.yview)
    subtitle_scroll.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    subtitle_text.configure(yscrollcommand=subtitle_scroll.set)

    # Export subtitle log button
    subtitle_btn_row = ctk.CTkFrame(
        right, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG, corner_radius=0)
    subtitle_btn_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(theme.PAD_SM, 0))

    btn_export_subtitle = make_secondary_btn(
        subtitle_btn_row, "Export Subtitle Log",
        icon=theme.ICON_EXPORT)
    btn_export_subtitle.pack(side="left")
    Tooltip(btn_export_subtitle,
            "Save subtitle change log as a text file")

    subtitle_count = ctk.CTkLabel(
        subtitle_btn_row, text="0 entries",
        font=theme.FONT_SMALL,
        text_color="#999999",
        fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
    subtitle_count.pack(side="right")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # POPULATE LOGS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _populate_logs():
        """Refresh both log panels from state."""
        # Session log
        session_text.configure(state="normal")
        session_text.delete("1.0", "end")
        if state.session_log:
            session_text.insert(
                "end", "\n".join(state.session_log))
        else:
            session_text.insert(
                "end",
                "No session activity yet.\n\n"
                "Actions like loading files, changing language,\n"
                "and exporting are recorded here.")
        session_text.configure(state="disabled")
        session_text.see("end")
        session_count.configure(
            text=f"{len(state.session_log)} entries")

        # Subtitle log
        subtitle_text.configure(state="normal")
        subtitle_text.delete("1.0", "end")
        if state.subtitle_log:
            subtitle_text.insert(
                "end", "\n".join(state.subtitle_log))
        else:
            subtitle_text.insert(
                "end",
                "No subtitle changes yet.\n\n"
                "Text edits, splits, deletions, and\n"
                "language conversions are recorded here.")
        subtitle_text.configure(state="disabled")
        subtitle_text.see("end")
        subtitle_count.configure(
            text=f"{len(state.subtitle_log)} entries")

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # EXPORT HANDLERS
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _export_session_log():
        if not state.session_log:
            state.log("[!] Session log is empty.")
            return

        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            title="Save Session Log",
            defaultextension=".txt",
            initialfile="session_log.txt",
            filetypes=[("Text files", "*.txt"),
                       ("All files", "*.*")])
        if not path:
            return

        try:
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("Subtitle Studio — Session Log\n")
                f.write(f"Exported: {_timestamp()}\n")
                f.write("=" * 50 + "\n\n")
                for entry in state.session_log:
                    f.write(f"{entry}\n")
            state.log(
                f"[OK] Session log saved: "
                f"{os.path.basename(path)}")
        except Exception as e:
            state.log(
                f"[ERROR] Failed to save session log: {e}")

    def _export_subtitle_log():
        if not state.subtitle_log:
            state.log("[!] Subtitle log is empty.")
            return

        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            title="Save Subtitle Log",
            defaultextension=".txt",
            initialfile="subtitle_changes.txt",
            filetypes=[("Text files", "*.txt"),
                       ("All files", "*.*")])
        if not path:
            return

        try:
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("Subtitle Studio — Subtitle Changes\n")
                f.write(f"Exported: {_timestamp()}\n")
                if state.sub_path:
                    f.write(
                        f"Source: "
                        f"{os.path.basename(state.sub_path)}\n")
                f.write("=" * 50 + "\n\n")
                for entry in state.subtitle_log:
                    f.write(f"{entry}\n")
            state.log(
                f"[OK] Subtitle log saved: "
                f"{os.path.basename(path)}")
        except Exception as e:
            state.log(
                f"[ERROR] Failed to save subtitle log: {e}")

    btn_export_session.configure(command=_export_session_log)
    btn_export_subtitle.configure(command=_export_subtitle_log)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # DARK MODE REFRESH
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _refresh_theme():
        """Re-colour all manually-styled widgets."""
        # Title area
        title_frame.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        title_lbl.configure(
            text_color=theme.DARK_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        subtitle_lbl.configure(
            text_color=theme.SECTION_TEXT, fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)

        # Pane frames
        panes.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        left.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        right.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)

        # Cards
        session_card.configure(
            fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            border_color=theme.CARD_BORDER)
        subtitle_card.configure(
            fg_color=theme.CARD_BG,
            border_color=theme.CARD_BORDER)

        # tk.Text widgets (manual colours)
        session_text.configure(
            bg=theme.CARD_BG, fg=theme.DARK_TEXT)
        subtitle_text.configure(
            bg=theme.CARD_BG, fg=theme.DARK_TEXT)

        # Button rows
        session_btn_row.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        subtitle_btn_row.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)

        # Counts
        session_count.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)
        subtitle_count.configure(fg_color=theme.TAB_BG)

    register_theme_callback(_refresh_theme)

    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # TAB ACTIVATION
    # ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def on_tab_activated():
        _populate_logs()

    ui["on_tab_activated"] = on_tab_activated

    return ui


def _timestamp() -> str:
    return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

FILE: test_all_arnndn_models.ps1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)]
    [string]$InputAudio
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$Ffmpeg = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe"

if (-not (Test-Path $Ffmpeg)) {
    throw "ffmpeg.exe not found: $Ffmpeg"
}

if (-not (Test-Path $InputAudio)) {
    throw "Input audio not found: $InputAudio"
}

$TempRoot = Join-Path $env:TEMP ("arnndn_models_test_" + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString("N"))
$ZipPath = Join-Path $TempRoot "arnndn-models.zip"
$ExtractDir = Join-Path $TempRoot "extract"
$OutputDir = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "arnndn_model_tests"

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $TempRoot | Out-Null
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $OutputDir | Out-Null

$url = "https://github.com/richardpl/arnndn-models/archive/refs/heads/master.zip"

Write-Host "[i] Downloading arnndn models..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $ZipPath -UseBasicParsing

Expand-Archive -Path $ZipPath -DestinationPath $ExtractDir -Force

$models = Get-ChildItem -Path $ExtractDir -Recurse -Filter "*.rnnn" | Sort-Object Name

if (-not $models -or $models.Count -eq 0) {
    throw "No .rnnn models found."
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[i] Testing models..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

foreach ($model in $models) {
    $base = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($model.Name)
    $out = Join-Path $OutputDir ("test_" + $base + ".wav")

    Write-Host "    Testing $($model.Name) -> $out"

    $filter = "aresample=48000,arnndn=m='$($model.FullName.Replace('\','/').Replace(':','\:'))':mix=1,highpass=f=65,lowpass=f=11000,acompressor=threshold=-21dB:ratio=2.0:attack=10:release=220:makeup=1.5,loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5:LRA=11,alimiter=limit=0.95"

    & $Ffmpeg `
        -hide_banner `
        -y `
        -i $InputAudio `
        -af $filter `
        -acodec pcm_s16le `
        -ar 48000 `
        -ac 2 `
        $out
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[OK] Model tests complete." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Listen to files in:"
Write-Host "  arnndn_model_tests"

Remove-Item $TempRoot -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

"""Tests for core/auto.py — Invisible automation."""

from core.state import Cue
from core.auto import (
    auto_fix_on_load,
    auto_fix_adjacent,
    auto_fix_before_export,
    find_matching_subtitle,
)


def test_fix_overlaps():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 5.0, "First"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 8.0, "Second"),  # overlaps
        Cue(3, 9.0, 12.0, "Third"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert fixed[0].end < fixed[1].start, (
        "Overlap should be resolved")
    assert count > 0
    print("  ✓ fix overlapping cues")


def test_remove_empty_cues():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "Good"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 6.0, ""),
        Cue(3, 7.0, 9.0, "  "),
        Cue(4, 10.0, 12.0, "Also good"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert len(fixed) == 2
    assert fixed[0].text == "Good"
    assert fixed[1].text == "Also good"
    print("  ✓ remove empty cues")


def test_reindex():
    cues = [
        Cue(5, 0.0, 3.0, "A"),
        Cue(10, 4.0, 6.0, "B"),
        Cue(15, 7.0, 9.0, "C"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert fixed[0].index == 1
    assert fixed[1].index == 2
    assert fixed[2].index == 3
    print("  ✓ reindex cues sequentially")


def test_strip_whitespace():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "  Hello  "),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 6.0, "  World  "),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert fixed[0].text == "Hello"
    assert fixed[1].text == "World"
    print("  ✓ strip whitespace")


def test_fix_double_spaces():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "Hello  world"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 6.0, "No   extra   spaces"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert "  " not in fixed[0].text
    assert "  " not in fixed[1].text
    print("  ✓ fix double spaces")


def test_merge_very_short():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 0.2, "Hi"),     # < 0.3s
        Cue(2, 0.3, 3.0, "there"),   # gap < 0.5s
        Cue(3, 4.0, 7.0, "Normal"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert len(fixed) == 2
    assert "Hi" in fixed[0].text
    assert "there" in fixed[0].text
    print("  ✓ merge very short cues")


def test_fix_adjacent_overlap():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 5.0, "A"),
        Cue(2, 3.0, 8.0, "B"),  # edited to overlap
        Cue(3, 9.0, 12.0, "C"),
    ]
    fixes = auto_fix_adjacent(cues, 1)
    assert fixes > 0
    assert cues[0].end < cues[1].start
    print("  ✓ fix adjacent overlap after edit")


def test_before_export_negative_duration():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 5.0, 3.0, "Bad duration"),
        Cue(2, 7.0, 10.0, "Normal"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_before_export(cues)
    assert fixed[0].end > fixed[0].start
    assert count > 0
    print("  ✓ fix negative duration before export")


def test_before_export_reindex():
    cues = [
        Cue(3, 0.0, 3.0, "A"),
        Cue(7, 4.0, 6.0, "B"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_before_export(cues)
    assert fixed[0].index == 1
    assert fixed[1].index == 2
    print("  ✓ reindex before export")


def test_no_fixes_needed():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "Perfect"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 7.0, "Subtitles"),
        Cue(3, 8.0, 11.0, "Here"),
    ]
    fixed, count = auto_fix_on_load(cues)
    assert len(fixed) == 3
    assert count == 0
    print("  ✓ no fixes needed on clean cues")


def test_find_matching_subtitle_none():
    result = find_matching_subtitle(None)
    assert result is None
    print("  ✓ find_matching_subtitle(None) returns None")


def run_all():
    print("\n📋 Auto-Fix Tests")
    print("─" * 40)
    test_fix_overlaps()
    test_remove_empty_cues()
    test_reindex()
    test_strip_whitespace()
    test_fix_double_spaces()
    test_merge_very_short()
    test_fix_adjacent_overlap()
    test_before_export_negative_duration()
    test_before_export_reindex()
    test_no_fixes_needed()
    test_find_matching_subtitle_none()
    test_auto_split_long()
    print("─" * 40)
    print("✅ All auto-fix tests passed\n")


def test_auto_split_long():
    from core.auto import auto_split_long_cues
    # Test sentence split
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 10.0,
            "And it's just a very simple way of asking "
            "questions. So in less than five minutes, "
            "you can get really valuable feedback from "
            "your client about how the meeting went."),
    ]
    result, count = auto_split_long_cues(cues)
    assert len(result) >= 2
    assert count > 0
    print("  ✓ auto-split long cues at sentence")


def test_line_break():
    from core.auto import auto_split_long_cues
    # Test line break within cue
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 4.0,
            "So if I was coming out of a sales meeting, "
            "a presentation with a client perhaps,"),
    ]
    result, count = auto_split_long_cues(cues)
    assert len(result) == 1  # no split, just line break
    assert "\n" in result[0].text
    print("  ✓ auto line-break within cue")
    run_all()

"""Tests for core/dictionary.py — US/UK conversion."""

import os
from core.dictionary import Dictionary


def _get_dict():
    """Load the dictionary from project root."""
    csv_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "dictionary.csv")
    if not os.path.isfile(csv_path):
        print("  ⚠ dictionary.csv not found — skipping")
        return None
    return Dictionary(csv_path)


def test_dictionary_loads():
    d = _get_dict()
    if d is None:
        return
    assert d.loaded is True
    count = len(d.us_to_uk) if hasattr(d, 'us_to_uk') else 0
    assert count > 0
    print(f"  ✓ dictionary loaded ({count} pairs)")


def test_us_to_uk():
    d = _get_dict()
    if d is None:
        return
    text, count = d.convert_text("color center", "us_to_uk")
    assert "colour" in text
    assert "centre" in text
    assert count >= 2
    print("  ✓ US → UK conversion")


def test_uk_to_us():
    d = _get_dict()
    if d is None:
        return
    text, count = d.convert_text("colour centre", "uk_to_us")
    assert "color" in text
    assert "center" in text
    assert count >= 2
    print("  ✓ UK → US conversion")


def test_no_changes():
    d = _get_dict()
    if d is None:
        return
    text, count = d.convert_text("hello world", "us_to_uk")
    assert text == "hello world"
    assert count == 0
    print("  ✓ no changes when no convertible words")


def test_case_preservation():
    d = _get_dict()
    if d is None:
        return
    text, count = d.convert_text("Color", "us_to_uk")
    # Should convert but result depends on implementation
    assert count >= 1
    print("  ✓ conversion handles capitalization")


def run_all():
    print("\n📋 Dictionary Tests")
    print("─" * 40)
    test_dictionary_loads()
    test_us_to_uk()
    test_uk_to_us()
    test_no_changes()
    test_case_preservation()
    print("─" * 40)
    print("✅ All dictionary tests passed\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all()

"""Tests for core/parser.py — SRT/VTT parsing and writing."""

import os
import tempfile
from core.parser import (
    parse_subtitle_file, write_srt, write_vtt,
    seconds_to_display, time_to_seconds
)


def test_seconds_to_display():
    assert seconds_to_display(0) == "00:00:00.000"
    assert seconds_to_display(1.5) == "00:00:01.500"
    assert seconds_to_display(61.123) == "00:01:01.123"
    assert seconds_to_display(3661.0) == "01:01:01.000"
    print("  ✓ seconds_to_display")


def test_time_to_seconds():
    assert time_to_seconds("00:00:01.500") == 1.5
    assert time_to_seconds("00:01:01.123") == 61.123
    assert time_to_seconds("01:00:00.000") == 3600.0
    assert time_to_seconds("00:00:00.000") == 0.0
    print("  ✓ time_to_seconds")


def test_roundtrip_time():
    """Converting to display and back should be stable."""
    for val in [0, 0.5, 1.0, 10.123, 61.456, 3661.789]:
        display = seconds_to_display(val)
        back = time_to_seconds(display)
        assert abs(back - val) < 0.002, (
            f"Roundtrip failed: {val} → {display} → {back}")
    print("  ✓ roundtrip time conversion")


def test_write_and_parse_srt():
    """Write SRT, read it back, verify contents."""
    from core.state import Cue
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 1.0, 4.0, "Hello world"),
        Cue(2, 5.0, 8.5, "Second line"),
        Cue(3, 10.0, 12.0, "Line with\nnewline"),
    ]

    path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(),
                        "test_output.srt")
    try:
        write_srt(cues, path)
        assert os.path.isfile(path)

        parsed = parse_subtitle_file(path)
        assert len(parsed) == 3
        assert parsed[0].text == "Hello world"
        assert parsed[1].text == "Second line"
        assert parsed[2].text == "Line with\nnewline"
        assert abs(parsed[0].start - 1.0) < 0.01
        assert abs(parsed[1].end - 8.5) < 0.01
        print("  ✓ write and parse SRT")
    finally:
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            os.remove(path)


def test_write_and_parse_vtt():
    """Write VTT, read it back, verify contents."""
    from core.state import Cue
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "First cue"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 7.0, "Second cue"),
    ]

    path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(),
                        "test_output.vtt")
    try:
        write_vtt(cues, path)
        assert os.path.isfile(path)

        parsed = parse_subtitle_file(path)
        assert len(parsed) == 2
        assert parsed[0].text == "First cue"
        assert abs(parsed[1].start - 4.0) < 0.01
        print("  ✓ write and parse VTT")
    finally:
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            os.remove(path)


def test_empty_file():
    """Parsing an empty file should return empty list."""
    path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(),
                        "test_empty.srt")
    try:
        with open(path, "w") as f:
            f.write("")
        parsed = parse_subtitle_file(path)
        assert parsed == [] or len(parsed) == 0
        print("  ✓ empty file returns empty list")
    finally:
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            os.remove(path)


def run_all():
    print("\n📋 Parser Tests")
    print("─" * 40)
    test_seconds_to_display()
    test_time_to_seconds()
    test_roundtrip_time()
    test_write_and_parse_srt()
    test_write_and_parse_vtt()
    test_empty_file()
    print("─" * 40)
    print("✅ All parser tests passed\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all()

"""Tests for core/qc.py — Quality check engine."""

from core.state import Cue
from core.qc import run_qc, run_fixes, get_summary


def _get_type(issue):
    """Get issue type — works with dict or object."""
    if isinstance(issue, dict):
        return issue.get("type", "")
    return getattr(issue, "type", "")


def _get_severity(issue):
    """Get issue severity — works with dict or object."""
    if isinstance(issue, dict):
        return issue.get("severity", "")
    return getattr(issue, "severity", "")


def test_detect_overlap():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 5.0, "First"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 8.0, "Second"),
    ]
    issues = run_qc(cues)
    overlap_issues = [i for i in issues
                      if _get_type(i) == "OVERLAP"]
    assert len(overlap_issues) > 0
    print("  ✓ detect overlap")


def test_detect_empty():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, ""),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 7.0, "Good"),
    ]
    issues = run_qc(cues)
    empty_issues = [i for i in issues
                    if _get_type(i) == "EMPTY"]
    assert len(empty_issues) > 0
    print("  ✓ detect empty cue")


def test_clean_cues_no_issues():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 3.0, "Hello"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 7.0, "World"),
    ]
    issues = run_qc(cues)
    critical = [i for i in issues
                if _get_severity(i) == "critical"]
    assert len(critical) == 0
    print("  ✓ clean cues produce no critical issues")


def test_get_summary():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 5.0, "First"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 8.0, "Second"),
        Cue(3, 9.0, 12.0, ""),
    ]
    issues = run_qc(cues)
    summary = get_summary(issues)
    assert isinstance(summary, dict)
    total = summary.get("total", 0)
    assert total > 0
    print(f"  ✓ summary: {total} issues found")


def test_auto_fix():
    cues = [
        Cue(1, 0.0, 5.0, "First"),
        Cue(2, 4.0, 8.0, "Second"),
    ]
    issues = run_qc(cues)
    fixed_cues, log = run_fixes(cues, issues)
    if len(fixed_cues) >= 2:
        assert fixed_cues[0].end <= fixed_cues[1].start
    print("  ✓ auto-fix resolves overlap")


def run_all():
    print("\n📋 QC Engine Tests")
    print("─" * 40)
    test_detect_overlap()
    test_detect_empty()
    test_clean_cues_no_issues()
    test_get_summary()
    test_auto_fix()
    print("─" * 40)
    print("✅ All QC tests passed\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all()

"""Tests for core/state.py — AppState, Cue, Section."""

import os
import tempfile
import json
from core.state import AppState, Cue, Section


def test_cue_creation():
    cue = Cue(1, 1.5, 4.0, "Hello")
    assert cue.index == 1
    assert cue.start == 1.5
    assert cue.end == 4.0
    assert cue.text == "Hello"
    print("  ✓ Cue creation")


def test_section_duration():
    s = Section(10.0, 25.0, "Test Section")
    assert s.duration == 15.0
    assert s.label == "Test Section"
    print("  ✓ Section duration")


def test_undo():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.cues = [
        Cue(1, 0, 3, "Original")]

    state.save_undo()
    state.cues[0].text = "Modified"
    assert state.cues[0].text == "Modified"

    result = state.undo()
    assert result is True
    assert state.cues[0].text == "Original"
    print("  ✓ undo restores previous state")


def test_undo_empty():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    result = state.undo()
    assert result is False
    print("  ✓ undo on empty stack returns False")


def test_undo_max_stack():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state._max_undo = 5
    state.cues = [Cue(1, 0, 1, "base")]

    for i in range(10):
        state.save_undo()
        state.cues = [Cue(1, 0, 1, f"edit_{i}")]

    assert len(state._undo_stack) == 5
    print("  ✓ undo stack respects max limit")


def test_dual_changelog():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.log_session("session only")
    state.log_subtitle("both logs")

    assert len(state.session_log) == 2
    assert len(state.subtitle_log) == 1
    assert "session only" in state.session_log
    assert "both logs" in state.subtitle_log
    print("  ✓ dual changelog separation")


def test_save_load_project():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.cues = [
        Cue(1, 1.0, 4.0, "Test cue one"),
        Cue(2, 5.0, 8.0, "Test cue two"),
    ]
    state.original_cues = [
        Cue(1, 1.0, 4.0, "Test cue one"),
        Cue(2, 5.0, 8.0, "Test cue two"),
    ]
    state.sections = [
        Section(1.0, 8.0, "Section 1")]
    state.language = "uk"
    state.log_session("test session log")
    state.log_subtitle("test subtitle log")

    path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(),
                        "test_project.ssproj")
    try:
        result = state.save_project(path)
        assert result is True
        assert os.path.isfile(path)

        # Load into fresh state
        state2 = AppState(os.getcwd())
        result2 = state2.load_project(path)
        assert result2 is True
        assert len(state2.cues) == 2
        assert state2.cues[0].text == "Test cue one"
        assert state2.language == "uk"
        assert len(state2.sections) == 1
        assert state2.sections[0].label == "Section 1"
        assert len(state2.session_log) == 2
        assert len(state2.subtitle_log) == 1
        print("  ✓ save and load project")
    finally:
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            os.remove(path)


def test_reset():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.cues = [Cue(1, 0, 1, "test")]
    state.sections = [Section(0, 1, "s1")]
    state.language = "uk"

    state.reset()
    assert state.cues == []
    assert state.sections == []
    assert state.video_path is None
    print("  ✓ reset clears all state")


def test_estimated_sizes():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.video_info = {
        "duration": 120.0,
        "size_mb": 60.0}

    est = state.get_estimated_sizes()
    assert est["original"] == "60"
    assert float(est["high"]) < 60
    assert float(est["medium"]) < float(est["high"])
    assert float(est["low"]) < float(est["medium"])
    print("  ✓ estimated sizes calculate correctly")


def test_validate_audio_paths_falls_back_to_source():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())

    fd, source_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".wav")
    os.close(fd)

    try:
        state.source_audio_path = source_path
        state.enhanced_audio_path = os.path.join(
            tempfile.gettempdir(), "missing_enhanced_audio.wav"
        )
        state.active_audio_path = state.enhanced_audio_path
        state.export_use_enhanced_audio = True

        state.validate_audio_paths()

        assert state.source_audio_path == source_path
        assert state.enhanced_audio_path is None
        assert state.active_audio_path == source_path
        assert state.export_use_enhanced_audio is False
        print("  ✓ audio validation falls back to source audio")
    finally:
        if os.path.isfile(source_path):
            os.remove(source_path)


def test_validate_audio_paths_clears_missing_audio():
    state = AppState(os.getcwd())
    state.source_audio_path = os.path.join(
        tempfile.gettempdir(), "missing_source_audio.wav"
    )
    state.enhanced_audio_path = os.path.join(
        tempfile.gettempdir(), "missing_enhanced_audio.wav"
    )
    state.active_audio_path = state.enhanced_audio_path
    state.export_use_enhanced_audio = True

    state.validate_audio_paths()

    assert state.source_audio_path is None
    assert state.enhanced_audio_path is None
    assert state.active_audio_path is None
    assert state.export_use_enhanced_audio is False
    print("  ✓ audio validation clears missing audio paths")


def run_all():
    print("\n📋 State Tests")
    print("─" * 40)
    test_cue_creation()
    test_section_duration()
    test_undo()
    test_undo_empty()
    test_undo_max_stack()
    test_dual_changelog()
    test_save_load_project()
    test_reset()
    test_estimated_sizes()
    test_validate_audio_paths_falls_back_to_source()
    test_validate_audio_paths_clears_missing_audio()
    print("─" * 40)
    print("✅ All state tests passed\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all()

# Subtitle Studio v6 Roadmap

Current branch: `v5-finalization`  
Current baseline commit before roadmap creation: `9e26dd52`  
Current product state: v5.0.1 replacement hotfix package / v6 polish preparation

Latest important pre-v6 commits:

```text
49df56f3 Fix burned subtitle quote and background rendering
981556d9 Isolate launcher in ship package
```

---

## v6 Goal

Subtitle Studio v6 should make the existing working app feel more reliable, precise, and professional.

The v6 direction is:

```text
Precision first.
Preview trust second.
Workflow polish third.
Packaging last.
```

This is not a rewrite.  
This is not a broad packaging refactor.  
This is not a rebuild of already-working features.

---

## v5.0.1 Hotfix Work Completed Before v6

Before v6 development resumed, two release-critical fixes were completed and pulled into a v5.0.1 replacement package.

### Completed commits

```text
49df56f3 Fix burned subtitle quote and background rendering
981556d9 Isolate launcher in ship package
```

---

### Completed export rendering hotfix

Burned subtitle export now supports:

- Correct opening and closing curly double quotes
- Correct opening and closing curly single quotes
- Curly apostrophes for contractions and possessives
- Export-only smart typography, without mutating editor subtitle text
- Multi-line subtitle background boxes rendered as one merged square-corner shape
- No visible dark overlap from stacked transparent subtitle boxes
- T-shaped and inverted-T-shaped merged boxes depending on line width
- Left, center, and right subtitle alignment preserved
- Text vertically centered inside the subtitle background shape
- Uniform absolute padding tuned visually

This work was completed in:

```text
tabs/tab_export.py
```

Do not redo this work in v6 unless further export-rendering polish is explicitly requested.

---

### Completed launcher isolation

The planned v6 clean launcher/release-folder work was pulled forward into v5.0.1.

The ship package now uses:

```text
SubtitleStudio_v5.0.1_ship_<timestamp>\
    Start Subtitle Studio.bat
    README_FIRST.txt
    app\
        SubtitleStudio.exe
        runtime files/folders
    _release_docs\
        release documentation
```

Important packaging rule preserved:

```text
Do not change Nuitka build layout.
Keep build_nuitka.ps1 authoritative.
Only make_ship_package.ps1 creates the isolated user-facing ship folder.
```

The momentary command prompt flash from the `.bat` launcher is accepted for v5.0.1 because it is still a major UX improvement over exposing the runtime file sea.

Future polish option:

```text
Replace BAT launcher with silent VBS or tiny compiled launcher.
```

Do not do this unless specifically requested.

---

### Packaged-app smoke evidence

The isolated package was tested successfully with runtime paths resolving under:

```text
release\...\app\bin\windows\ffmpeg.exe
release\...\app\bin\windows\ffprobe.exe
```

Confirmed from packaged app:

- MPV diagnostics OK
- MPV preview backend OK
- Video load OK
- Source audio extraction OK
- Waveform generation OK
- Premium pyrnnoise audio enhancement OK
- FFmpeg light polish OK
- Enhanced waveform reload OK
- IN/OUT marking OK
- Section creation OK

v6 should not redo these completed items unless further polish is requested.

---

## Product Themes

### 1. Core Edit Trust

Users must trust what they see while editing.

Includes:

- Subtitle preview should not vanish unexpectedly
- Subtitle preview should stay inside the actual video area
- Subtitle preview should be toggleable on/off
- Subtitle timing should be frame-aware
- Subtitle bin editing should remain smooth near the bottom of the list

---

### 2. Professional Editor Workflow

The Edit tab should feel closer to a real editing tool.

Includes:

- Frame-aware timecode editing
- Cleaner transport controls
- Existing Seek control renamed/repositioned as GO
- Existing frame-step/skip controls placed more clearly
- Existing Loop IN/OUT control placed more clearly
- Editable IN/OUT and section timecodes
- Play selected section
- Ghost-mark selected section on timeline
- Clearer playhead/waveform colours

---

### 3. Cleaner Workspace

The Edit tab should be less crowded and less technical.

Includes:

- Remove Resolution control from Edit tab
- Remove redundant Subtitle Font Size control from Edit tab
- Hide Log from default workspace tabs
- Open Log from menu when needed

---

### 4. Safer Operations

Long-running or cancellable operations should leave the app in a trustworthy state.

Includes:

- Cancel audio enhancement mid-process
- Fix US/UK conversion radio state after cancellation

---

### 5. Better Deliverables

Export outputs should match user expectations.

Includes:

- Replace DOCX-as-subtitle export with formatted Word transcript export

---

### 6. Cleaner Release Experience

Most of this work was completed early in v5.0.1.

Completed:

- Clean ship folder with obvious launcher
- Nuitka runtime folder kept intact under an `app` folder
- `Start Subtitle Studio.bat` added at package root
- `README_FIRST.txt` added at package root

Remaining optional future polish:

- Replace BAT launcher with silent VBS or tiny compiled launcher if specifically requested

---

## Confirmed Already Working / Not New v6 Work

These items were verified and should not be treated as new features:

- Add section
- Delete section
- Move section up
- Move section down
- Export order matches visible section order
- Keyboard shortcuts behave correctly
- Export works
- Existing Seek-to-time behavior exists
- Existing frame step / frame skip behavior exists
  - Shift currently skips 5 frames
- Existing Loop IN/OUT behavior exists
- Active app tabs are currently `Edit | Export | Log`
- Legacy Import/Review/Convert tabs were removed
- Legacy build scripts were retired
- Current authoritative build script is `build_nuitka.ps1`
- Burned subtitle smart curly quotes completed in v5.0.1
- Burned subtitle merged square-corner background shapes completed in v5.0.1
- Burned subtitle left/center/right alignment preserved after merged-shape fix
- Launcher isolation into `Start Subtitle Studio.bat` + `app\` folder completed in v5.0.1
- `make_ship_package.ps1` now creates the clean user-facing release folder

---

## Export Rendering Knowhow Preserved from v5.0.1

The burned subtitle renderer now uses custom ASS generation.

Important implementation model:

```text
Layer 0: merged vector background shape
Layer 1: positioned subtitle text
```

Do not return to ASS per-line boxed backgrounds using `BorderStyle=4`, because that caused visible dark overlap when transparent boxes touched or overlapped.

Current desired model:

```text
One subtitle cue
 -> split into visual lines
 -> measure/estimate each line width
 -> create padded rectangle per line
 -> merge rectangles into one square-corner vector shape
 -> draw background on lower ASS layer
 -> draw text lines above it
```

Brand shape behavior:

```text
Top line wider    -> T-shaped merged box
Bottom line wider -> inverted-T-shaped merged box
Equal widths      -> unified rectangle
```

Do not add rounded corners unless explicitly requested.

Quote handling:

```text
"Hello"  -> “Hello”
'Hello'  -> ‘Hello’
don't    -> don’t
John's   -> John’s
```

Smart quote conversion should remain export-only unless a future product decision explicitly says editor/source subtitle text should also be changed.

Known important functions in `tabs/tab_export.py`:

```text
_smart_curly_quotes
_ass_escape_text
_estimate_ass_text_width
_line_background_rect
_merged_rect_union_points
_ass_draw_path
_write_current_subs_to_temp
```

Risk note:

```text
Burned subtitle ASS rendering is visual-output-sensitive.
Any future changes must be tested with left, center, and right alignment,
single-line cues, two-line cues, quotes, apostrophes, and enhanced-audio video export.
```

## Roadmap Packages

---

## Package A — Frame / Timecode Foundation

### A1. Frame-aware timecode editing based on video FPS

Original proposal: Change 15

#### User value

Very high.

Users should be able to work in frame-aware timecodes after importing a video.

#### Desired behavior

On video import/load:

```text
Detect video FPS
Store FPS centrally
Use FPS for frame-aware editing, snapping, stepping, and validation
```

Recommended internal model:

```text
Keep seconds internally.
Add frame-aware conversion at UI boundaries.
```

Supported input should include:

```text
HH:MM:SS.mmm
HH:MM:SS,mmm
HH:MM:SS:FF when FPS is known
```

Recommended display for frame-aware edit controls:

```text
HH:MM:SS:FF
```

#### Important rule

Do not automatically snap imported subtitle timings in v6.

Imported subtitle timings should remain unchanged unless the user edits them.

#### Likely files

```text
core/timecode.py
core/ffmpeg.py
core/state.py
core/parser.py
tabs/tab_edit.py
tests/
```

#### Risk

Medium-high.

Timing is core product behavior.

#### Test plan

- Load video and confirm FPS is detected/logged
- Confirm frame-aware helper tests pass
- Confirm imported subtitle timings are preserved
- Confirm edited times snap to frame boundaries
- Confirm SRT/VTT export still writes valid millisecond timestamps
- Confirm existing tests pass

---

## Package B — Subtitle Preview Reliability

### B1. Fix subtitle preview disappearing during edit

Original proposal: Change 2

#### User value

Very high.

The subtitle preview must be reliable during editing.

#### Desired behavior

If Subtitle Preview is enabled and playhead is inside a cue range:

```text
Subtitle should be visible.
Edited text should appear after edit is saved.
Preview should not go blank unless no cue is active.
```

#### Likely files

```text
tabs/tab_edit.py
core/player_mpv.py
core/state.py
```

#### Risk

Medium.

Preview behavior intersects playback, cue selection, edit commit, and refresh timers.

#### Test plan

- Edit subtitle text while paused inside cue
- Confirm preview updates after save
- Select different cues
- Seek across cue boundaries
- Play through multiple cues
- Confirm preview only clears when outside cue timing

---

### B2. Keep subtitle preview inside actual video area

Original proposal: Change 17

#### User value

High.

Preview subtitles should not align to the edge of the preview window or black bars.

#### Desired behavior

Subtitle overlay should be positioned relative to the displayed video rectangle, not the outer preview widget.

Use safe margins:

```text
Horizontal margin: max(20 px, 5% of displayed video width)
Vertical margin: max(16 px, 5% of displayed video height)
```

#### Important rule

This affects preview only.  
It must not change burned subtitle export.

#### Likely files

```text
tabs/tab_edit.py
core/player_mpv.py
core/widgets.py
```

#### Risk

Medium.

Must handle resizing, aspect ratios, vertical video, long text, and alignment options.

#### Test plan

- Test left/center/right subtitle alignment
- Resize app window
- Test 16:9, 4:3, and vertical video if available
- Confirm export remains unchanged

---

Original proposal: Change 7

#### User value

High.

Users should be able to hide/show subtitle overlay during editing.

Add a preference-backed toggle:


Recommended menu placement:

Default:

It must not affect:

- QC
- section export

```text

```

Low-medium.

## Recommended Implementation Order

This is the recommended v6 development order after v5.0.1 completed work.

```text
1. Frame-aware timecode foundation
2. Fix subtitle preview disappearing during edit
3. Keep subtitle preview inside video area
4. Add Subtitle Preview On/Off toggle
5. Reactive Find bar
6. Smooth Subtitle Bin scrolling/editing near bottom
7. Remove Resolution control from Edit tab
8. Remove Subtitle Font Size option from Edit tab
9. Reorganize existing transport/action controls
10. Editable current IN/OUT timecodes
11. Editable Sections Bin IN/OUT timecodes
12. Ghost-mark selected section on timeline
13. Play selected section
14. Make playhead and waveform visually distinct
15. Fix US/UK radio state after cancel
16. Cancel audio enhancement mid-process
17. Replace DOCX subtitle export with Word transcript export
18. Hide Log from default tabs and open from menu
19. Clean user-facing launcher/release folder — completed early in v5.0.1
```

---

## Development Rules for v6

1. No broad rewrites.
2. One small change at a time.
3. Prefer one commit per stable unit.
4. Run compile checks after each touched Python file.
5. Run `python run_tests.py` after each meaningful feature.
6. Do not touch `build_nuitka.ps1` unless packaging QA exposes an issue.
7. Do not change export behavior while fixing preview behavior.
8. Do not silently alter imported subtitle timings.
9. Keep seconds internally; use frame conversion at UI boundaries.
10. Preserve the v5.0.1 burned subtitle ASS rendering model unless specifically asked to change it.
11. Update `SESSION_CONTEXT.txt` and regenerate clean export at handoff.

---

## Standard Test Gate

Use as applicable:

```powershell
python -m py_compile main.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_edit.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_export.py
python -m py_compile tabs\tab_logs.py
python -m py_compile core\ffmpeg.py
python -m py_compile core\state.py
python -m py_compile core\parser.py
python run_tests.py
```

Only compile files that exist and/or were touched.

## Burned Subtitle Rendering Regression Test

Because v5.0.1 fixed visual burned subtitle rendering, any future export-related work should test:

```text
"Hello," she said.
'Hello,' he replied.
It's John's file.
He said, "It's called 'Subtitle Studio.'"

"This is the wider top line"
short bottom

short top
"This is the much wider bottom line"

"First quoted line"
"Second quoted line"
```

Expected:

```text
“Hello,” she said.
‘Hello,’ he replied.
It’s John’s file.
He said, “It’s called ‘Subtitle Studio.’”
```

Visual expectations:

```text
No overlapping transparent subtitle boxes.
Two-line subtitles use one square-corner merged stepped background shape.
Top wider line creates T-shaped box.
Bottom wider line creates inverted-T-shaped box.
Text remains vertically centered.
Padding remains visually uniform.
Left, center, and right alignment work.
```
